    return at > 0 ? h.slice(0, at) + blk + h.slice(at) : h + blk;
  };

  /* ---------- Send itineraries ---------- */
  async function emailsFor(D) {
    var ids = (D.roster || []).map(function (r) { return r.person_id; }).filter(Boolean);
    var by = {};
    if (ids.length) {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('volunteer_people').select('id,emails,contact_id').in('id', ids);
        var cids = []; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (p) { by[p.id] = (p.emails || [])[0] || ''; if (p.contact_id) cids.push(p.contact_id); });
        if (cids.length) {
          var c = await db.from('crm_contacts').select('id,primary_email').in('id', cids);
          var cm = {}; ((c && c.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { cm[x.id] = x.primary_email; });
          ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (p) { if (p.contact_id && cm[p.contact_id]) by[p.id] = cm[p.contact_id]; });
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { r.__email = by[r.person_id] || (r.detail || {}).email || ''; });
  }
  function dayBefore(ev) { var d = addDays(ev.event_date, -1); var h = Number(S.cfg.sms_itinerary_hour || 17); return d + 'T' + ('0' + h).slice(-2) + ':00'; }
  function torontoIso(local) {
    /* a date and time typed in Toronto, as an ISO instant (the offset of that day) */
    var d = new Date(local + ':00Z'); if (isNaN(d)) return null;
    try { var f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', hour12: false, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(d);
      var g = {}; f.forEach(function (p) { g[p.type] = p.value; });
      var asTor = Date.UTC(+g.year, +g.month - 1, +g.day, +g.hour % 24, +g.minute); var off = asTor - d.getTime();
      return new Date(d.getTime() - off).toISOString(); } catch (e) { return d.toISOString(); }
  }

  }

  }
  async function sendGo8(btn) {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev || S.busy) return;

    if (wh === 'at') { var v = (document.getElementById('w182at') || {}).value; if (!v) { T('Pick a date and time'); return; } at = torontoIso(v); if (!at || new Date(at) < new Date(Date.now() - 60000)) { T('That time has passed'); return; } }
    var note = ((document.getElementById('w182note') || {}).value || '').trim();
    var upd = !!(document.getElementById('w182upd') || {}).checked;
    S.busy = true; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = at ? 'Scheduling\u2026' : 'Sending\u2026';
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa182_send_itineraries', { p_event: ev.id, p_roster: ids, p_channel: ch, p_send_at: at, p_note: note || null, p_update: upd });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r.data || {}, tk = d.tick || {};
      var bits = [];
      if (at) bits.push((d.texts + d.emails) + ' scheduled for ' + fmtWhen(at));
      else {
        if (tk.texts) bits.push(tk.texts + ' text' + (tk.texts === 1 ? '' : 's'));
        if (tk.emails) bits.push(tk.emails + ' email' + (tk.emails === 1 ? '' : 's'));
        if (bits.length) bits = [bits.join(' and ') + ' on the way'];
        if (tk.skipped) bits.push(tk.skipped + ' skipped (see Sent and scheduled)');
        var wn = tk.waiting_for_window != null ? tk.waiting_for_window : tk.waiting_for_8am; if (wn) bits.push(wn + ' text' + (wn === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' wait for 9 am');
      }
      if (d.no_way_to_reach) bits.push(d.no_way_to_reach + ' with no phone or email');
      T(bits.join(', ') || 'Nothing to send');
      closeModal();
      setTimeout(function () { loadSends(ev.id, true).then(function () { var c = document.getElementById('w174card'); if (c) c.__h = ''; paintW174(); decorateCards(); }); }, 2500);
    } catch (e) { T('Could not send: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 110)); btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Send'; }
    S.busy = false;
  }

   Where: a card at the top of Home (#w180card) with the announcements for this person: a poll answered with a tap, a short
   form in a window, the totals once answered (when the poster allows), and for the poster, leaders, HR and admins "See the
   answers" (the totals, who chose what unless anonymous, the text answers, who has not answered, a CSV). "Post an

   the form, anonymous, the totals, when answers close, pinned, when it comes down, and the one Slack post.

   wave180d (30/09/26), Steel: "Actually last question it should be emailed as well": each one is also emailed once to the
   people it is for, from hub@welfareavenue.com with replies to the poster ("Email it to them too", on by default); the link

   Data: hub_announcements (row security: the people it is for, the poster, leaders, HR, admins), hub_announcement_answers
   (own rows), wa180_answer and wa180_results; team-inbox posts the Slack message and sends the email (event announcement).
   ===================================================================== */

  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '?').trim().split(/[\s@._-]+/).filter(Boolean); return ((p[0] || '?').charAt(0) + (p[1] ? p[1].charAt(0) : '')).toUpperCase(); }
  function ago(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '';
    var s = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 1000);
    if (s < 60) return 'just now'; if (s < 3600) return Math.round(s / 60) + ' min ago'; if (s < 86400) return Math.round(s / 3600) + ' h ago';
    var d = Math.round(s / 86400); if (d < 7) return d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d + ' days ago';
    return dayLabel(iso);
  }
  function dayLabel(iso) { try { return new Date(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(iso)) ? iso + 'T12:00:00Z' : iso).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(iso)) ? 'UTC' : 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 10); } }
  function todayIso() { try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); } catch (e) { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function plusDays(n) { var d = new Date(todayIso() + 'T12:00:00Z'); d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + n); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }

    /* the windows (answer, results, all, post) */
    + '#modalBox.w180wide{max-width:760px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180m h3{margin:0 0 4px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:26px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';line-height:1.05;}'

    + '#modalBox .w180f label.l{display:block;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.6px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:14px 0 5px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180f label.l .op{font-weight:500;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-left:6px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180f label.l .req{color:' + FLAG + ';margin-left:3px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180f input[type=text],#modalBox .w180f input[type=date],#modalBox .w180f input[type=number],#modalBox .w180f textarea,#modalBox .w180f select{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;font:inherit;font-size:13.5px;background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + INK + ';}'
    + '#modalBox .w180f textarea{min-height:64px;resize:vertical;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180seg{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180seg button{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.3);background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:999px;padding:6px 13px;font:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180seg button.on{background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';border-color:' + GREEN + ';}#modalBox .w180seg button[disabled]{opacity:.45;cursor:default;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180ck{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:13px;color:' + INK + ';margin:8px 0 0;line-height:1.35;}#modalBox .w180ck input{accent-color:' + GREEN + ';margin-top:2px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180ed{margin:10px 0 0;padding:12px;border-radius:14px;background:' + SAND + ';border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';}'
    + '#modalBox .w180oped{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:0 0 6px;}#modalBox .w180oped input{flex:1;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180x{border:0;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:' + GREEN + ';width:30px;height:30px;flex:0 0 30px;border-radius:50%;font-size:17px;line-height:30px;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180qed{padding:10px 12px;border-radius:12px;background:' + CREAM + ';border:1px solid ' + LINE + ';margin:0 0 8px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180qed .h{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;}#modalBox .w180qed .h input{flex:1;}#modalBox .w180qed .h select{width:auto;flex:0 0 150px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180foot{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:center;margin-top:18px;}#modalBox .w180foot .sp{flex:1;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180ans{margin:0 0 14px;}#modalBox .w180ans .qq{font-weight:700;font-size:14px;color:' + INK + ';margin:0 0 6px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180prog{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:4px 0 12px;font-size:13px;color:' + INK + ';}#modalBox .w180prog .t{flex:1;height:10px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);overflow:hidden;}#modalBox .w180prog .t i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#modalBox .w180rq{margin:0 0 16px;padding:0 0 12px;border-bottom:1px solid ' + LINE + ';}#modalBox .w180rq:last-child{border-bottom:0;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180rq h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:14px;color:' + INK + ';}'
    + '#modalBox .w180names{font-size:11.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-top:3px;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180sent{font-size:12.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border-radius:10px;padding:7px 11px;margin:0 0 12px;line-height:1.45;}#modalBox .w180sent.warn{color:#6b4d0e;background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);}'
    + '#modalBox .w180vals{margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none;}#modalBox .w180vals li{padding:7px 10px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);margin:0 0 5px;font-size:13px;white-space:pre-wrap;word-wrap:break-word;}#modalBox .w180vals li b{display:block;font-size:11.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#modalBox details.w180miss summary{cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';}#modalBox details.w180miss p{font-size:12.5px;color:' + INK + ';margin:6px 0 0;}'
    + '#modalBox .w180all .w180it{cursor:pointer;}#modalBox .w180all .w180it:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.35);}'
    + '@media (max-width:760px){#modalBox .w180row{grid-template-columns:1fr;}#modalBox .w180qed .h{flex-wrap:wrap;}#modalBox .w180qed .h select{flex:1 1 100%;}#w180card .w180hd .w180btn{padding:6px 12px;font-size:12px;}.w180opt{padding:8px 13px;}}';
  if (!document.getElementById('wa180css')) { var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'wa180css'; st.textContent = CSS; document.head.appendChild(st); }

  /* ---------- who is looking (the same rules as the database: wa180_can_post, wa180_for_me, wa180_manages) ---------- */
  function q(p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r || { data: null }; }, function (e) { return { data: null, error: e }; }); }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function profile() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.profile) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff !== 'function' || isStaff(); } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function isAdmin() { return role() === 'admin'; }
  function isLeader() { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function isHr() { var r = role(); return profile() === 'hr' && r !== 'blocked' && r !== 'viewer'; }
  function manager() { return isLeader() || isHr(); }
  function canPost() { return staff() && (isLeader() || isHr()); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function meRow() { try { return (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function meName() { var m = meRow(); if (m && m.name) return m.name; try { return (USER && USER.name) || myEmail().split('@')[0]; } catch (e) { return myEmail().split('@')[0]; } }
  function myEmails() { var out = [myEmail()], m = meRow(); if (m && m.email) out.push(lower(m.email)); return out.filter(Boolean); }
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false; }); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function myTeams() {
    var out = [], em = myEmails();
    members().forEach(function (m) { if (m.department && m.email && em.indexOf(lower(m.email)) >= 0 && out.indexOf(m.department) < 0) out.push(m.department); });
    var me = meRow(); if (me && me.department && out.indexOf(me.department) < 0) out.push(me.department);
    return out;
  }
  function postTeams() { if (isAdmin() || isHr()) return ALL_TEAMS.slice(); if (isLeader()) return myTeams().filter(function (t) { return ALL_TEAMS.indexOf(t) >= 0; }); return []; }
  function teamName(d) {
    if (d === 'exec') return 'Executive team'; if (d === 'board') return 'Board';
    try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; }
  }
  function nameFor(email) {
    var em = lower(email); if (!em) return 'Someone';
    var m = members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0];
    return (m && m.name) || em.split('@')[0].split('.').map(function (s) { return s ? s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s; }).join(' ');
  }
  function mine(a) { return myEmails().indexOf(lower(a.created_by)) >= 0; }
  function manages(a) { return manager() || mine(a); }
  function forMe(a) {
    if (mine(a)) return true;
    if (a.audience === 'everyone' || !a.audience) return staff();   /* everyone is the team: board viewers see what is for the board */
    if (a.audience === 'leaders') return isLeader();
    var t = a.teams || [], mt = myTeams();
    if (t.some(function (x) { return mt.indexOf(x) >= 0; })) return true;
    return t.indexOf('board') >= 0 && !staff();
  }
  function live(a) { return !a.expires_at || String(a.expires_at).slice(0, 10) > todayIso(); }
  function closed(a) { return !!(a.closes_at && new Date(a.closes_at).getTime() <= Date.now()); }
  function openAsk(a) { return !!(a && a.ask && !closed(a) && live(a)); }
  function answered(a) { return !!S.mine[a.id]; }
  function audienceLabel(a) {
    if (a.audience === 'leaders') return 'Leaders';
    if (a.audience === 'teams') return (a.teams || []).map(teamName).join(', ') || 'Some teams';
    return 'Everyone';
  }
  function kindOf(a) { return a.ask ? (a.ask.type === 'poll' ? 'poll' : 'form') : 'news'; }

  /* ---------- the data: the announcements this person may see, and their own answers ---------- */
  var COLS = 'id, kind, title, body, department, link, pinned, happened_at, expires_at, created_by, created_name, created_at, audience, teams, ask, anonymous, show_results, closes_at, post_slack, slack, post_email, emailed';
  function load(force) {
    if (S.listP) return S.listP;
    if (!force && S.list && Date.now() - S.listAt < 45000) return Promise.resolve(S.list);
    S.listP = Promise.all([
      q(db.from('hub_announcements').select(COLS).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(80)),
      q(db.from('hub_announcement_answers').select('announcement_id, answers, updated_at').limit(500))
    ]).then(function (r) {
      if (Array.isArray(r[0].data)) S.list = r[0].data; else if (!S.list) S.list = [];
      if (Array.isArray(r[1].data)) { var m = {}; r[1].data.forEach(function (x) { m[x.announcement_id] = x.answers; }); S.mine = m; }
      S.listAt = Date.now(); S.listP = null;
      return S.list;
    });
    return S.listP;
  }
  function byId(id) { return (S.list || []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function loadOne(id) {
    var a = byId(id); if (a) return Promise.resolve(a);
    return q(db.from('hub_announcements').select(COLS).eq('id', Number(id)).maybeSingle()).then(function (r) {
      var x = r && r.data && !Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : (r && Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data[0] : null);
      if (x) { (S.list = S.list || []).push(x); return x; }
      return null;
    });
  }
  /* where it went (the Slack post, the email), fresh for the poster, leaders, HR and admins */
  var SENT_KEYS = ['post_slack', 'slack', 'post_email', 'emailed'];
  function delivery(a) {
    if (!a || !a.id) return Promise.resolve(a);
    return q(db.from('hub_announcements').select(SENT_KEYS.join(', ')).eq('id', Number(a.id)).maybeSingle()).then(function (r) {
      var x = r && r.data && !Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : null, b = byId(a.id);
      if (x) SENT_KEYS.forEach(function (k) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(x, k)) { a[k] = x[k]; if (b && b !== a) b[k] = x[k]; } });
      return a;
    });
  }
  function cleanErr(m) { return String(m || 'Something went wrong').replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').replace(/\s*CONTEXT:[\s\S]*$/, ''); }
  function results(id, force) {
    var c = S.res[id];
    if (!force && c && Date.now() - c.__at < 20000) return Promise.resolve(c);
    return q(db.rpc('wa180_results', { p_id: Number(id) })).then(function (r) {
      var v = r && r.data; if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = null; } }
      if (v && v.ok) { v.__at = Date.now(); S.res[id] = v; if (v.mine) S.mine[id] = v.mine; return v; }
      return { ok: false, error: (v && v.error) || cleanErr(r && r.error && r.error.message) || 'The results did not load.' };
    });
  }
  function answer(id, answers) {
    return q(db.rpc('wa180_answer', { p_id: Number(id), p_answers: answers })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(cleanErr(r.error.message || r.error.details));
      var v = r && r.data; if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = null; } }
      if (!v || !v.ok) throw new Error('The answer did not go through. Try again.');
      S.mine[id] = v.answers || answers; S.just[id] = true;
      if (v.results && v.results.ok) { v.results.__at = Date.now(); S.res[id] = v.results; }
      return v;
    });
  }
  function patch(id, p) {
    return q(db.from('hub_announcements').update(p).eq('id', Number(id))).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(cleanErr(r.error.message));
      var a = byId(id); if (a) Object.keys(p).forEach(function (k) { a[k] = p[k]; });
      return a;
    });
  }

  /* ---------- the totals (bars) ---------- */
  function barsHtml(qr, mineVal) {
    var opts = qr.options || [], total = opts.reduce(function (s, o) { return s + (Number(o.count) || 0); }, 0), mineIdx = [];
    if (Array.isArray(mineVal)) mineIdx = mineVal.map(Number); else if (typeof mineVal === 'number') mineIdx = [mineVal]; else if (mineVal === true) mineIdx = [0]; else if (mineVal === false) mineIdx = [1];
    return '<div class="w180bars">' + opts.map(function (o, i) {
      var n = Number(o.count) || 0, pc = total ? Math.round(100 * n / total) : 0;
      return '<div class="w180bar' + (mineIdx.indexOf(i) >= 0 ? ' me' : '') + '"><div class="l"><span>' + E(o.label) + (mineIdx.indexOf(i) >= 0 ? ' <span class="w180chip gold">you</span>' : '') + '</span><b>' + n + (total ? ' \u00b7 ' + pc + '%' : '') + '</b></div><div class="t"><i style="width:' + pc + '%"></i></div>'
        + (Array.isArray(o.names) && o.names.length ? '<div class="n w180names">' + E(o.names.join(', ')) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function choiceLabels(a, idx) { var o = (a.ask && a.ask.options) || []; return (Array.isArray(idx) ? idx : [idx]).map(function (i) { return o[i]; }).filter(Boolean).join(', '); }

  /* ---------- a poll, answered with a tap ---------- */
  function pollHtml(a) {
    var ask = a.ask, m = S.mine[a.id], cl = closed(a), res = S.res[a.id], sel = S.sel[a.id], multi = !!ask.multi;
    var editing = !!(sel && sel.edit), canVote = openAsk(a) && forMe(a) && (!m || editing);
    var h = '<div class="w180q">' + E(ask.question) + (multi ? ' <span class="w180chip">pick any</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (canVote) {
      var picks = (sel && sel.picks) || (m && m.choice) || [];
      h += '<div class="w180opts" role="group" aria-label="' + E(ask.question) + '">' + ask.options.map(function (o, i) {
        var on = picks.indexOf(i) >= 0;
        return '<button type="button" class="w180opt' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w180vote="' + E(a.id) + '" data-i="' + i + '" aria-pressed="' + on + '"' + (S.busy['v' + a.id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(o) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
      if (multi || editing) h += '<div class="w180acts">' + (multi ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180send="' + E(a.id) + '"' + (!picks.length || S.busy['v' + a.id] ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy['v' + a.id] ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'Send my answer') + '</button>' : '')
        + (editing ? '<button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180keep="' + E(a.id) + '">Keep my answer</button>' : '') + '</div>';
      return h;
    }
    if (res && res.visible && res.questions && res.questions[0]) h += barsHtml(res.questions[0], m && m.choice);
    if (m) h += '<div class="w180done">\u2713 You chose ' + E(choiceLabels(a, m.choice)) + (openAsk(a) ? ' \u00b7 <button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180change="' + E(a.id) + '">Change my answer</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    else if (cl) h += '<div class="w180done" style="color:' + MUTED + '">Closed ' + E(dayLabel(a.closes_at)) + '</div>';
    if (m && !(res && res.visible) && !a.show_results && !manages(a)) h += '<div class="w180names" style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-top:4px">' + E(first(a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by))) + ' keeps the totals for now.</div>';
    return h;
  }
  /* ---------- a short form: answered in a window ---------- */
  function formStub(a) {
    var qs = (a.ask && a.ask.questions) || [], m = S.mine[a.id], res = S.res[a.id];
    var h = '';
    if (openAsk(a) && forMe(a) && !m) h += '<div class="w180acts"><button type="button" class="w180btn gold" data-w180form="' + E(a.id) + '">Answer (' + plural(qs.length, 'question') + ')</button></div>';
    else if (m) h += '<div class="w180done">\u2713 You answered' + (openAsk(a) ? ' \u00b7 <button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180form="' + E(a.id) + '">Change my answers</button>' : '') + (a.show_results && !manages(a) ? ' \u00b7 <button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180res="' + E(a.id) + '">See the totals</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    else if (closed(a)) h += '<div class="w180done" style="color:' + MUTED + '">Closed ' + E(dayLabel(a.closes_at)) + (a.show_results && !manages(a) ? ' \u00b7 <button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180res="' + E(a.id) + '">See the totals</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    return h;
  }

  /* ---------- one announcement ---------- */
  function itemHtml(a) {
    var ask = a.ask || null, res = S.res[a.id], who = a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by), chips = [];
    chips.push('<span class="w180chip purple">' + E(audienceLabel(a)) + '</span>');
    if (ask) chips.push('<span class="w180chip gold">' + (ask.type === 'poll' ? 'Poll' : 'Short form') + (a.anonymous ? ', anonymous' : '') + '</span>');
    if (ask && closed(a)) chips.push('<span class="w180chip">Closed</span>'); else if (ask && a.closes_at) chips.push('<span class="w180chip">Closes ' + E(dayLabel(a.closes_at)) + '</span>');
    if (a.pinned) chips.push('<span class="w180chip teal">Pinned</span>');
    var h = '<article class="w180it' + (a.pinned ? ' pin' : '') + '" data-w180id="' + E(a.id) + '">'
      + '<div class="top"><span class="who"><span class="w180av" aria-hidden="true">' + E(initials(who)) + '</span><b>' + E(who) + '</b></span><span>' + E(ago(a.created_at)) + '</span>' + chips.join('') + '</div>'
      + '<h4>' + E(a.title) + '</h4>' + (a.body ? '<p class="body">' + E(a.body) + '</p>' : '')
      + (a.link ? '<p style="margin:0 0 6px"><a class="w180lnk" href="' + E(a.link) + '"' + (/^https?:/i.test(a.link) ? ' target="_blank" rel="noopener"' : '') + '>Open the link</a></p>' : '');
    if (ask && ask.type === 'poll') h += pollHtml(a); else if (ask) h += formStub(a);
    if (ask && manages(a)) h += '<div class="w180acts"><button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180res="' + E(a.id) + '">See the answers' + (res && res.manage ? ' (' + res.answered + (res.audience ? ' of ' + res.audience : '') + ')' : '') + '</button></div>';
    if (S.errs[a.id]) h += '<p class="w180err">' + E(S.errs[a.id]) + '</p>';
    return h + '</article>';
  }

  /* ---------- Home: the card ---------- */
  function homeList() {
    return (S.list || []).filter(function (a) { return live(a) && forMe(a); }).sort(function (x, y) {
      var p = (y.pinned ? 1 : 0) - (x.pinned ? 1 : 0); if (p) return p;
      var w = (waiting(y) ? 1 : 0) - (waiting(x) ? 1 : 0); if (w) return w;
      return new Date(y.created_at) - new Date(x.created_at);
    });
  }
  /* what waits on an answer; one answered just now keeps its place until the page reloads (the totals show where it was) */
  function waiting(a) { return (openAsk(a) && !answered(a)) || !!S.just[a.id]; }
  function toAnswer() { return homeList().filter(function (a) { return openAsk(a) && !answered(a); }); }
  function allList() { return (S.list || []).filter(function (a) { return manages(a) || (forMe(a) && live(a)); }).sort(function (x, y) { return new Date(y.created_at) - new Date(x.created_at); }); }
  function cardHtml() {
    var list = homeList(), n = toAnswer().length, cp = canPost();
    if (!list.length && !cp) return '';
    var shown = list.slice(0, 3), all = allList().length;
    var h = '<div class="w180hd"><h3>Announcements</h3>' + (n ? '<span class="w180cnt">' + n + ' to answer</span>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>'
      + (cp ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180post>Post an announcement</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (!list.length) return h + '<div class="w180empty"><span>Nothing posted for you right now. What you post shows here for the people it is for, with one Slack post and an email.</span>' + (all ? '<button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180all>See all announcements (' + all + ')</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    h += shown.map(itemHtml).join('');
    if (all > shown.length) h += '<p class="w180more"><button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180all>See all announcements (' + all + ')</button></p>';
    return h;
  }
  function paintCard() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), el = document.getElementById('w180card');
    var html = S.list ? cardHtml() : '';
    if (!body || !html) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
    /* at the top of Home: right after the line of who is online (before the getting-started list and the other cards) */
    var a = body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-online') || body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-chips') || body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-head');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w180card'; el.className = 'mp-sec'; }
    if (a) { if (a.nextSibling !== el) body.insertBefore(el, a.nextSibling); } else if (el.parentNode !== body) body.insertBefore(el, body.firstChild);
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
    warm();
    /* who may post and manage comes with the person's access (WA40_MINE), which can arrive after Home first draws */
    if ((typeof WA40_MINE === 'undefined' || !WA40_MINE) && !S.waitMine) {
      S.waitMine = 1; var n = 0, tm = setInterval(function () { n++; if ((typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE) || n > 50) { clearInterval(tm); S.waitMine = 0; paintCard(); } }, 400);
    }
  }
  /* the totals for the polls on the card (answered, closed, or the poster's), loaded once */
  function warm() {
    homeList().slice(0, 3).forEach(function (a) {
      if (!a.ask || S.res[a.id] || S.busy['r' + a.id]) return;
      if (!(manages(a) || ((answered(a) || closed(a)) && a.show_results && a.ask.type === 'poll'))) return;
      S.busy['r' + a.id] = 1;
      results(a.id).then(function (v) { S.busy['r' + a.id] = 0; if (!v || !v.ok) S.res[a.id] = { ok: false, failed: true, __at: Date.now() }; paintCard(); },
        function () { S.busy['r' + a.id] = 0; S.res[a.id] = { ok: false, failed: true, __at: Date.now() }; });
    });
  }
  function refreshCard(force) { return load(force).then(function () { paintCard(); }, function () { /* offline */ }); }

  /* ---------- the windows: one announcement, a short form, the answers, all of them ---------- */
  function wide(on) { var b = document.getElementById('modalBox'); if (b) b.classList.toggle('w180wide', !!on); }
  function openWin(html, isWide) { openModal('<div class="w180m">' + html + '</div>'); wide(isWide); }
  function setWin(html) { var m = document.querySelector('#modalBox .w180m'); if (m) m.innerHTML = html; }
  function headHtml(a) {
    var who = a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by);
    return '<h3>' + E(a.title) + '</h3><p class="sub">From ' + E(who) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(ago(a.created_at)) + ' \u00b7 for ' + E(audienceLabel(a))
      + (a.ask && a.closes_at ? (closed(a) ? ' \u00b7 closed ' : ' \u00b7 closes ') + E(dayLabel(a.closes_at)) : '') + (a.anonymous ? ' \u00b7 anonymous' : '') + '</p>'
      + (a.body ? '<p class="body" style="font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.5;white-space:pre-wrap;margin:0 0 10px">' + E(a.body) + '</p>' : '');
  }
  /* where it went: the Slack post and the email (the poster, leaders, HR and admins see it) */
  function deliveryHtml(a) {
    var bits = [], warn = false;
    if (a.post_slack) {
      var sl = a.slack || null, posts = sl && Array.isArray(sl.posts) ? sl.posts : [];
      if (!sl) bits.push('Slack: on its way');
      else if (posts.length) bits.push('Posted in ' + posts.map(function (p) { return '#' + p.channel; }).join(', '));
      else { warn = true; bits.push('Not posted in Slack' + (sl.errors && sl.errors[0] && sl.errors[0].error ? ': ' + sl.errors[0].error : '')); }
    }
    if (a.post_email) {
      var em = a.emailed || null, st = em && em.state, n = Number(em && em.sent) || 0, of = Number(em && em.to_count) || 0;
      if (!em || st === 'sending') bits.push('The email is going out' + (of ? ' to ' + plural(of, 'person', 'people') : ''));
      else if (st === 'sent') bits.push('Emailed to ' + plural(n, 'person', 'people'));
      else if (st === 'partly sent') { warn = true; bits.push('Emailed to ' + n + ' of ' + plural(of, 'person', 'people') + (em.failed && em.failed.length ? '; not to ' + em.failed.join(', ') : '')); }
      else if (st === 'skipped') { warn = true; bits.push('Not emailed: ' + (em.skipped || 'skipped')); }
      else { warn = true; bits.push('The email did not go out' + (em.error ? ': ' + String(em.error).slice(0, 140) : '')); }
    }
    return bits.length ? '<p class="w180sent' + (warn ? ' warn' : '') + '">' + E(bits.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</p>' : '';
  }
  function repaint(id) {
    paintCard();
    var a = byId(id); if (!a) return;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w180slot="' + id + '"]'), function (el) { el.innerHTML = pollHtml(a) + (S.errs[a.id] ? '<p class="w180err">' + E(S.errs[a.id]) + '</p>' : ''); });
  }
  function openItem(id) {
    return load(false).then(function () { return loadOne(id); }).then(function (a) {
      return a && manages(a) ? delivery(a) : a;
    }).then(function (a) {
      if (!a) { T('That announcement is not for you, or it was taken down'); return; }
      if (a.ask && a.ask.type === 'form' && openAsk(a) && forMe(a) && !S.mine[a.id]) return openForm(a);
      if (a.ask && a.ask.type === 'form') return openResults(a);
      if (a.ask && manages(a) && !(openAsk(a) && forMe(a) && !S.mine[a.id])) return openResults(a);
      openWin(headHtml(a) + (manages(a) ? deliveryHtml(a) : '') + (a.ask ? '<div data-w180slot="' + E(a.id) + '">' + pollHtml(a) + '</div>' : '')
        + (a.link ? '<p><a class="w180lnk" href="' + E(a.link) + '"' + (/^https?:/i.test(a.link) ? ' target="_blank" rel="noopener"' : '') + '>Open the link</a></p>' : '')
        + '<div class="w180foot">' + (a.ask && manages(a) ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180res="' + E(a.id) + '">See the answers</button><span class="sp"></span>' : '') + '<button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180close>Close</button></div>', false);
      if (a.ask && a.ask.type === 'poll' && (S.mine[a.id] || closed(a)) && !S.res[a.id]) results(a.id).then(function () { repaint(a.id); });
    });
  }

  /* a short form */
  function pillsHtml(qq, v) {
    var opts = qq.type === 'yesno' ? ['Yes', 'No'] : (qq.options || []), multi = qq.type === 'several';
    var cur = qq.type === 'yesno' ? (v === true ? [0] : v === false ? [1] : []) : (Array.isArray(v) ? v : (typeof v === 'number' ? [v] : []));
    return '<div class="w180opts" role="group" aria-labelledby="w180al_' + E(qq.id) + '">' + opts.map(function (o, i) {
      var on = cur.indexOf(i) >= 0;
      return '<button type="button" class="w180opt' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w180pick="' + E(qq.id) + '" data-i="' + i + '" data-multi="' + (multi ? 1 : 0) + '" aria-pressed="' + on + '">' + E(o) + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function qHtml(qq, v) {
    var id = 'w180a_' + qq.id, mark = qq.required ? '<span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span>' : '<span class="op">optional</span>';
    var lab = '<label class="l" id="w180al_' + E(qq.id) + '"' + (/^(short|long|date|number)$/.test(qq.type) ? ' for="' + id + '"' : '') + '>' + E(qq.label) + mark + (qq.type === 'several' ? '<span class="op">pick any</span>' : '') + '</label>';
    var inp;
    if (qq.type === 'short') inp = '<input type="text" id="' + id + '" data-w180q="' + E(qq.id) + '" maxlength="300" value="' + E(v || '') + '"' + (qq.required ? ' aria-required="true"' : '') + '>';
    else if (qq.type === 'long') inp = '<textarea id="' + id + '" data-w180q="' + E(qq.id) + '" maxlength="3000" rows="4"' + (qq.required ? ' aria-required="true"' : '') + '>' + E(v || '') + '</textarea>';
    else if (qq.type === 'date') inp = '<input type="date" id="' + id + '" data-w180q="' + E(qq.id) + '" value="' + E(v || '') + '" style="max-width:220px">';
    else if (qq.type === 'number') inp = '<input type="number" id="' + id + '" data-w180q="' + E(qq.id) + '" step="any" value="' + E(v == null ? '' : v) + '" style="max-width:220px">';
    else inp = pillsHtml(qq, v);
    return '<div class="w180ans">' + lab + inp + '</div>';
  }
  function openForm(a) {
    var qs = (a.ask && a.ask.questions) || [], who = first(a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by));
    S.formId = a.id; S.formAns = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.mine[a.id] || {}));
    openWin(headHtml(a) + '<form class="w180f" data-w180formbox="' + E(a.id) + '" novalidate>' + qs.map(function (qq) { return qHtml(qq, S.formAns[qq.id]); }).join('')
      + '<p class="w180err" data-w180ferr hidden></p>'
      + '<p class="w180names" style="font-size:12px;margin:14px 0 0">' + (a.anonymous ? 'Anonymous: ' + E(who) + ' sees the totals, not who answered what.' : E(who) + ' sees your name with your answers.') + (a.closes_at ? ' You can change them until ' + E(dayLabel(a.closes_at)) + '.' : ' You can change them later.') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="w180foot" style="margin-top:10px"><button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180close>Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w180btn">' + (S.mine[a.id] ? 'Save my answers' : 'Send my answers') + '</button></div></form>', false);
  }
  function formValues(a) {
    var out = {}, qs = (a.ask && a.ask.questions) || [];
    qs.forEach(function (qq) {
      if (/^(short|long|date|number)$/.test(qq.type)) {
        var el = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180q="' + qq.id + '"]'); if (!el) return;
        var v = String(el.value || '').trim(); if (!v) return;
        if (qq.type === 'number') { var n = Number(v); if (!isNaN(n)) out[qq.id] = n; } else out[qq.id] = v;
      } else if (S.formAns && S.formAns[qq.id] !== undefined && S.formAns[qq.id] !== null && !(Array.isArray(S.formAns[qq.id]) && !S.formAns[qq.id].length)) out[qq.id] = S.formAns[qq.id];
    });
    return out;
  }
  function submitForm(id) {
    var a = byId(id); if (!a) return;
    var vals = formValues(a), err = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180ferr]');
    var miss = ((a.ask && a.ask.questions) || []).filter(function (qq) { return qq.required && vals[qq.id] === undefined; });
    if (miss.length) { if (err) { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = 'Please answer: ' + miss.map(function (qq) { return qq.label; }).join('; '); } return; }
    if (!Object.keys(vals).length) { if (err) { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = 'Answer at least one question.'; } return; }
    var btn = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180formbox] button[type=submit]'); if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026'; }
    answer(id, vals).then(function () {
      closeModal(); T('Thanks, your answers are in'); delete S.errs[id]; paintCard();
    }, function (e) {
      if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = S.mine[id] ? 'Save my answers' : 'Send my answers'; }
      if (err) { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = e.message; }
    });
  }

  /* the answers: the totals, who chose what (unless anonymous), the text answers, who has not answered, a CSV */
  function valText(qr, v) {
    if (qr.type === 'date') return dayLabel(String(v));
    return String(v);
  }
  function valsHtml(qr) {
    if (!Array.isArray(qr.values)) return '<p class="sub" style="margin:0">' + plural(Number(qr.count) || 0, 'answer') + '.</p>';
    if (!qr.values.length) return '<p class="sub" style="margin:0">No answers yet.</p>';
    return '<ul class="w180vals">' + qr.values.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + (x.name ? '<b>' + E(x.name) + '</b>' : '') + E(valText(qr, x.v)) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
  }
  function resultsHtml(a, r) {
    var h = headHtml(a), m = S.mine[a.id] || r.mine || null;
    if (!r.visible) {
      return h + '<p>' + (m ? 'Your answer is in.' : (closed(a) ? 'It closed before you answered.' : 'You have not answered yet.')) + (a.show_results ? '' : ' ' + E(first(a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by))) + ' keeps the totals for now.') + '</p>'
        + '<div class="w180foot">' + (openAsk(a) && forMe(a) && a.ask.type === 'form' ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn gold sm" data-w180form="' + E(a.id) + '">' + (m ? 'Change my answers' : 'Answer') + '</button>' : '') + '<button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180close>Close</button></div>';
    }
    if (r.manage) {
      var pc = r.audience ? Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * r.answered / r.audience)) : 0;
      h += '<div class="w180prog"><span><b>' + r.answered + '</b>' + (r.audience ? ' of ' + r.audience : '') + ' answered</span><span class="t"><i style="width:' + pc + '%"></i></span></div>' + (manages(a) ? deliveryHtml(a) : '');
    } else h += '<p class="sub">' + plural(r.answered, 'person has', 'people have') + ' answered.</p>';
    h += (r.questions || []).map(function (qr) {
      return '<div class="w180rq"><h4>' + E(qr.label) + '</h4>' + (qr.options ? barsHtml(qr, m ? m[qr.id] : undefined) : valsHtml(qr)) + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    if (r.manage) {
      if (a.anonymous) h += '<p class="sub">' + plural(Number(r.missing_count) || 0, 'person has', 'people have') + ' not answered yet. It is anonymous, so the names stay hidden.</p>';
      else if (r.missing && r.missing.length) h += '<details class="w180miss"><summary>' + r.missing.length + ' not answered yet</summary><p>' + E(r.missing.join(', ')) + '</p></details>';
      else if (r.audience) h += '<p class="sub">Everyone it is for has answered.</p>';
    }
    h += '<div class="w180foot">';
    if (openAsk(a) && forMe(a)) h += a.ask.type === 'form' ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180form="' + E(a.id) + '">' + (m ? 'Change my answers' : 'Answer') + '</button>' : '';
    if (r.manage && (mine(a) || isAdmin())) {
      if (Array.isArray(r.rows) && r.rows.length) h += '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180csv="' + E(a.id) + '">Download the answers (CSV)</button>';
      if (openAsk(a)) h += '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180shut="' + E(a.id) + '">Close the answers now</button>';
      if (live(a)) h += '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180down="' + E(a.id) + '">' + (S.busy['down' + a.id] ? 'Press again to take it down' : 'Take it down') + '</button>';
    } else if (r.manage && Array.isArray(r.rows) && r.rows.length) h += '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180csv="' + E(a.id) + '">Download the answers (CSV)</button>';
    h += '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180close>Close</button></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function openResults(a) {
    S.resId = a.id;
    openWin(headHtml(a) + '<p class="sub">Loading the answers\u2026</p>', true);
    return Promise.all([results(a.id, true), manages(a) ? delivery(a) : null]).then(function (both) {
      var r = both[0];
      if (S.resId !== a.id) return;
      if (!r || !r.ok) { setWin(headHtml(a) + '<p class="w180err">' + E((r && r.error) || 'The answers did not load.') + '</p><div class="w180foot"><button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180close>Close</button></div>'); return; }
      setWin(resultsHtml(a, r));
    });
  }
  function csvFor(a, r) {
    var qs = a.ask.type === 'poll' ? [{ id: 'choice', label: a.ask.question, type: a.ask.multi ? 'several' : 'one', options: a.ask.options }] : (a.ask.questions || []);
    var cell = function (s) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s); return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s; };
    var head = (a.anonymous ? [] : ['Name']).concat(['Answered']).concat(qs.map(function (qq) { return qq.label; }));
    var lines = [head.map(cell).join(',')];
    (r.rows || []).forEach(function (row) {
      var ans = row.answers || {};
      var vals = qs.map(function (qq) {
        var v = ans[qq.id]; if (v === undefined || v === null) return '';
        if (qq.type === 'one') return (qq.options || [])[v] || '';
        if (qq.type === 'several') return (Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]).map(function (i) { return (qq.options || [])[i]; }).filter(Boolean).join('; ');
        if (qq.type === 'yesno') return v === true ? 'Yes' : 'No';
        return String(v);
      });
      lines.push((a.anonymous ? [] : [row.name || '']).concat([String(row.at || '').slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ')]).concat(vals).map(cell).join(','));
    });
    return lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
  }
  function downloadCsv(a) {
    var r = S.res[a.id]; if (!r || !r.rows) return;
    try {
      var blob = new Blob(['\ufeff' + csvFor(a, r)], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }), url = URL.createObjectURL(blob), link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url; link.download = todayIso() + ' - ' + String(a.title).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]+/g, ' ').slice(0, 60).trim() + ' - answers.csv';
      document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); link.remove(); }, 1500);
    } catch (e) { T('The download did not start'); }
  }

  /* every announcement this person may see */
  function rowHtml(a) {
    var st = !live(a) ? '<span class="w180chip">Taken down</span>' : !a.ask ? '' : closed(a) ? '<span class="w180chip">Closed</span>' : S.mine[a.id] ? '<span class="w180chip teal">You answered</span>' : forMe(a) ? '<span class="w180chip gold">To answer</span>' : '';
    return '<article class="w180it" data-w180open="' + E(a.id) + '" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="' + E(a.title) + '"><div class="top"><span class="who"><span class="w180av" aria-hidden="true">' + E(initials(a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by))) + '</span><b>' + E(a.created_name || nameFor(a.created_by)) + '</b></span><span>' + E(ago(a.created_at)) + '</span><span class="w180chip purple">' + E(audienceLabel(a)) + '</span>' + (a.ask ? '<span class="w180chip gold">' + (a.ask.type === 'poll' ? 'Poll' : 'Short form') + '</span>' : '') + st + '</div><h4 style="margin-bottom:0">' + E(a.title) + '</h4></article>';
  }
  function openAll() {
    openWin('<h3>Announcements</h3><p class="sub">Loading\u2026</p>', true);
    load(true).then(function () {
      var list = allList();
      setWin('<h3>Announcements</h3><p class="sub">' + (manager() ? 'Everything posted, newest first.' : 'What was posted for you, newest first.') + '</p><div class="w180all">'
        + (list.length ? list.map(rowHtml).join('') : '<p>Nothing posted yet.</p>') + '</div><div class="w180foot">' + (canPost() ? '<button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180post>Post an announcement</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w180btn sm" data-w180close>Close</button></div>');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- posting: who it is for, the poll or the short form, the one Slack post and the email (wave180d) ---------- */
  function newDraft() {
    var own = postTeams(), lead = !isAdmin() && !isHr();
    return { title: '', body: '', link: '', audience: lead && own.length ? 'teams' : 'everyone', teams: lead && own.length ? [own[0]] : [], ask: 'none',
      poll: { question: '', options: ['', ''], multi: false }, form: { questions: [{ id: 'q1', label: '', type: 'short', required: true, options: '' }] },
      anonymous: false, show_results: true, closes: '', pinned: false, expires: '', slack: true, email: true };
  }
  function slackWhere(d) {
    if (d.audience === 'everyone') return '#general';
    if (d.audience === 'leaders') return '#leaders';
    return d.teams.length ? (d.teams.length === 1 ? 'the ' + teamName(d.teams[0]) + ' channel' : 'each team\u2019s channel') : 'the team\u2019s channel';
  }
  /* how it reaches them, in one line: "goes once to Slack and is emailed to them" */
  function reachLine(d) {
    if (d.slack && d.email) return ', goes once to Slack and is emailed to them';
    return d.slack ? ', and goes once to Slack' : d.email ? ', and is emailed to them' : '';
  }
  function reachToast(d) {
    if (d.slack && d.email) return 'Posted, and on its way to ' + slackWhere(d) + ' and by email';
    if (d.slack) return 'Posted, and on its way to ' + slackWhere(d);
    if (d.email) return 'Posted, and on its way by email';
    return 'Posted';
  }
  function editorHtml(d) {
    if (d.ask === 'poll') {
      return '<div class="w180ed"><label class="l" for="w180pq">The question<span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input type="text" id="w180pq" maxlength="300" placeholder="Which start time works for you?" value="' + E(d.poll.question) + '">'
        + '<label class="l">The choices<span class="op">2 to 8</span></label>' + d.poll.options.map(function (o, i) {
          return '<div class="w180oped"><input type="text" data-w180popt="' + i + '" maxlength="120" placeholder="Choice ' + (i + 1) + '" value="' + E(o) + '" aria-label="Choice ' + (i + 1) + '">' + (d.poll.options.length > 2 ? '<button type="button" class="w180x" data-w180delopt="' + i + '" aria-label="Remove choice ' + (i + 1) + '">&times;</button>' : '') + '</div>';
        }).join('')
        + (d.poll.options.length < 8 ? '<button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180addopt>Add a choice</button>' : '')
        + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w180pm"' + (d.poll.multi ? ' checked' : '') + '> People can pick more than one</label></div>';
    }
    if (d.ask === 'form') {
      return '<div class="w180ed">' + d.form.questions.map(function (qq, i) {
        var choice = qq.type === 'one' || qq.type === 'several';
        return '<div class="w180qed"><div class="h"><input type="text" data-w180ql="' + i + '" maxlength="300" placeholder="Question ' + (i + 1) + '" value="' + E(qq.label) + '" aria-label="Question ' + (i + 1) + '">'
          + '<select data-w180qt="' + i + '" aria-label="Kind of answer">' + QTYPES.map(function (t) { return '<option value="' + t[0] + '"' + (qq.type === t[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + t[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
          + (d.form.questions.length > 1 ? '<button type="button" class="w180x" data-w180delq="' + i + '" aria-label="Remove question ' + (i + 1) + '">&times;</button>' : '') + '</div>'
          + (choice ? '<textarea data-w180qo="' + i + '" rows="3" placeholder="The choices, one per line (2 to 10)" aria-label="The choices for question ' + (i + 1) + '" style="margin-top:6px">' + E(qq.options) + '</textarea>' : '')
          + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" data-w180qr="' + i + '"' + (qq.required ? ' checked' : '') + '> Everyone must answer this one</label></div>';
      }).join('') + (d.form.questions.length < 8 ? '<button type="button" class="w180lnk" data-w180addq>Add a question</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    return '';
  }
  function postHtml(d) {
    var own = postTeams(), lead = !isAdmin() && !isHr();
    var aud = [['everyone', 'Everyone'], ['leaders', 'The leaders'], ['teams', lead ? 'My team' + (own.length > 1 ? 's' : '') : 'Some teams']];
    var h = '<h3>Post an announcement</h3><p class="sub">It shows at the top of Home for the people it is for' + reachLine(d) + '. You can add a poll or a short form they answer right there.</p>'
      + '<form class="w180f" data-w180postform novalidate>'
      + '<label class="l" for="w180pt">Headline<span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input type="text" id="w180pt" maxlength="200" placeholder="The gala moves to November 21" value="' + E(d.title) + '">'
      + '<label class="l" for="w180pb">Detail<span class="op">optional</span></label><textarea id="w180pb" maxlength="2000" rows="3" placeholder="A line or two of context.">' + E(d.body) + '</textarea>'
      + '<label class="l">Who it is for</label><div class="w180seg" role="group" aria-label="Who it is for">' + aud.map(function (x) {
        var off = x[0] === 'teams' && !own.length;
        return '<button type="button" data-w180aud="' + x[0] + '"' + (d.audience === x[0] ? ' class="on" aria-pressed="true"' : ' aria-pressed="false"') + (off ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (d.audience === 'teams' ? '<div class="w180seg" role="group" aria-label="The teams" style="margin-top:8px">' + own.map(function (t) {
        var on = d.teams.indexOf(t) >= 0;
        return '<button type="button" data-w180team="' + t + '"' + (on ? ' class="on" aria-pressed="true"' : ' aria-pressed="false"') + '>' + E(teamName(t)) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + '<label class="l">What should they answer?</label><div class="w180seg" role="group" aria-label="What should they answer">' + [['none', 'Nothing, it is news'], ['poll', 'A poll'], ['form', 'A short form']].map(function (x) {
        return '<button type="button" data-w180askt="' + x[0] + '"' + (d.ask === x[0] ? ' class="on" aria-pressed="true"' : ' aria-pressed="false"') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + editorHtml(d)
      + (d.ask !== 'none' ? '<div class="w180row"><div><label class="l" for="w180pc">Answers close on<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="date" id="w180pc" min="' + todayIso() + '" value="' + E(d.closes) + '"></div><div></div></div>'
        + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w180pa"' + (d.anonymous ? ' checked' : '') + '> Anonymous: you see the totals, not who answered what</label>'
        + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w180pr"' + (d.show_results ? ' checked' : '') + '> Show people the totals once they answer</label>' : '')
      + '<label class="l" for="w180pl">A link<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="text" id="w180pl" maxlength="500" placeholder="#team/fundraising or https://\u2026" value="' + E(d.link) + '">'
      + '<div class="w180row"><div><label class="l" for="w180pe">Comes down on<span class="op">optional</span></label><input type="date" id="w180pe" min="' + plusDays(1) + '" value="' + E(d.expires) + '"></div>'
      + '<div><label class="l">&nbsp;</label><label class="w180ck" style="margin-top:6px"><input type="checkbox" id="w180pp"' + (d.pinned ? ' checked' : '') + '> Pin it to the top of Home</label></div></div>'
      + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w180ps"' + (d.slack ? ' checked' : '') + '> Post it once in Slack too, in ' + E(slackWhere(d)) + '</label>'
      + '<label class="w180ck"><input type="checkbox" id="w180pem"' + (d.email ? ' checked' : '') + '> Email it to them too, from hub@welfareavenue.com (replies come to you)</label>'
      + '<p class="w180err" data-w180perr hidden></p>'
      + '<div class="w180foot"><button type="button" class="w180btn ghost sm" data-w180close>Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="w180btn"' + (S.busy.post ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy.post ? 'Posting\u2026' : 'Post it') + '</button></div></form>';
    return h;
  }
  function openPost() {
    if (!canPost()) { T('Leaders, HR and admins post announcements'); return; }
    S.draft = S.draft || newDraft();
    openWin(postHtml(S.draft), false);
    setTimeout(function () { var t = document.getElementById('w180pt'); if (t) { try { t.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 30);
  }
  function v(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value : ''; }
  function ck(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return !!(el && el.checked); }
  /* what is typed goes into the draft before anything redraws the form */
  function syncDraft() {
    var d = S.draft; if (!d || !document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180postform]')) return d;
    d.title = v('w180pt'); d.body = v('w180pb'); d.link = v('w180pl'); d.expires = v('w180pe'); d.pinned = ck('w180pp'); d.slack = ck('w180ps'); d.email = ck('w180pem');
    if (document.getElementById('w180pc')) { d.closes = v('w180pc'); d.anonymous = ck('w180pa'); d.show_results = ck('w180pr'); }
    if (d.ask === 'poll' && document.getElementById('w180pq')) {
      d.poll.question = v('w180pq'); d.poll.multi = ck('w180pm');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#modalBox [data-w180popt]'), function (el) { d.poll.options[Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180popt'))] = el.value; });
    }
    if (d.ask === 'form') {
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#modalBox [data-w180ql]'), function (el) { var i = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180ql')); if (d.form.questions[i]) d.form.questions[i].label = el.value; });
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#modalBox [data-w180qo]'), function (el) { var i = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180qo')); if (d.form.questions[i]) d.form.questions[i].options = el.value; });
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#modalBox [data-w180qr]'), function (el) { var i = Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180qr')); if (d.form.questions[i]) d.form.questions[i].required = el.checked; });
    }
    return d;
  }
  function redrawPost(focusSel) {
    var box = document.querySelector('#modalBox .w180m'); if (!box) return;
    var y = document.getElementById('modalBox') ? document.getElementById('modalBox').scrollTop : 0;
    box.innerHTML = postHtml(S.draft);
    try { document.getElementById('modalBox').scrollTop = y; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (focusSel) { var f = document.querySelector('#modalBox ' + focusSel); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }
  /* end of that day in Toronto (EDT or EST), for "Answers close on" */
  function endOfDay(day) {
    var off = '-04:00';
    try {
      var p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', timeZoneName: 'shortOffset' }).formatToParts(new Date(day + 'T16:00:00Z')).filter(function (x) { return x.type === 'timeZoneName'; })[0];
      var m = p && /GMT([+-])(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?/.exec(p.value); if (m) off = m[1] + ('0' + m[2]).slice(-2) + ':' + (m[3] || '00');
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return new Date(day + 'T23:59:00' + off).toISOString();
  }
  function buildRow(d) {
    var title = String(d.title || '').trim(), body = String(d.body || '').trim(), link = String(d.link || '').trim();
    if (!title) return { err: 'Give it a headline.' };
    if (d.audience === 'teams' && !d.teams.length) return { err: 'Pick the team it is for.' };
    if (link && !/^(#|https?:\/\/)/i.test(link)) return { err: 'A link starts with # (a Hub page) or https://.' };
    var ask = null;
    if (d.ask === 'poll') {
      var opts = d.poll.options.map(function (o) { return String(o || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      if (!String(d.poll.question || '').trim()) return { err: 'Write the poll\u2019s question.' };
      if (opts.length < 2) return { err: 'A poll needs at least two choices.' };
      if (opts.some(function (o, i) { return opts.indexOf(o) !== i; })) return { err: 'Two choices are the same.' };
      ask = { type: 'poll', question: String(d.poll.question).trim(), options: opts, multi: !!d.poll.multi };
    } else if (d.ask === 'form') {
      var qs = [], bad = null;
      d.form.questions.forEach(function (qq, i) {
        if (bad) return;
        var label = String(qq.label || '').trim(); if (!label) { bad = 'Write question ' + (i + 1) + '.'; return; }
        var x = { id: 'q' + (i + 1), label: label, type: qq.type, required: !!qq.required };
        if (qq.type === 'one' || qq.type === 'several') {
          var o = String(qq.options || '').split(/\n+/).map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
          if (o.length < 2 || o.length > 10) { bad = 'Question ' + (i + 1) + ' needs 2 to 10 choices, one per line.'; return; }
          x.options = o;
        }
        qs.push(x);
      });
      if (bad) return { err: bad };
      ask = { type: 'form', questions: qs };
    }
    if (d.closes && d.closes < todayIso()) return { err: 'Answers cannot close in the past.' };
    if (d.expires && d.expires <= todayIso()) return { err: 'It can come down tomorrow at the earliest.' };
    return { row: { kind: 'general', title: title, body: body || null, link: link || null, pinned: !!d.pinned, happened_at: todayIso(), expires_at: d.expires || null,
      created_by: myEmail(), created_name: meName(), audience: d.audience, teams: d.audience === 'teams' ? d.teams.slice() : [],
      department: d.audience === 'teams' && d.teams.length === 1 ? d.teams[0] : null, ask: ask, anonymous: ask ? !!d.anonymous : false,
      show_results: ask ? !!d.show_results : true, closes_at: ask && d.closes ? endOfDay(d.closes) : null, post_slack: !!d.slack, post_email: !!d.email } };
  }
  function submitPost() {
    var d = syncDraft(), b = buildRow(d), err = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180perr]');
    if (b.err) { if (err) { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = b.err; } return; }
    S.busy.post = true; redrawPost();
    q(db.from('hub_announcements').insert(b.row).select('id, created_at').single()).then(function (r) {
      S.busy.post = false;
      if (!r || r.error || !r.data) {
        var m = r && r.error ? String(r.error.message || '') : '';
        redrawPost();
        var e2 = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180perr]');
        if (e2) { e2.hidden = false; e2.textContent = /row-level security|policy/i.test(m) ? 'Leaders post to everyone, the leaders or their own team; HR and admins to anyone. This one is outside what you can post to.' : ('It did not post: ' + cleanErr(m || 'try again')); }
        return;
      }
      var row = Object.assign({}, b.row, { id: r.data.id, created_at: r.data.created_at || new Date().toISOString() });
      (S.list = S.list || []).unshift(row); S.draft = null;
      closeModal(); T(reachToast(d));
      paintCard();
      try { if (window.w116News && w116News.refresh) w116News.refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- voting on a poll ---------- */
  function vote(id, picks) {
    var a = byId(id); if (!a) return;
    S.busy['v' + id] = 1; delete S.errs[id]; repaint(id);
    answer(id, { choice: picks }).then(function () {
      S.busy['v' + id] = 0; delete S.sel[id]; repaint(id); T('Thanks, your answer is in');
    }, function (e) { S.busy['v' + id] = 0; S.errs[id] = e.message; repaint(id); });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el, id;
    /* What's new on the Executive dashboard posts through the same form */
    if (t.closest('#w116news [data-anpost]')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); openPost(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180post]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openPost(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180all]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openAll(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180open]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openItem(el.getAttribute('data-w180open')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180res]'))) { e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180res'); var ar = byId(id); if (ar) openResults(ar); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180form]'))) { e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180form'); var af = byId(id); if (af) openForm(af); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180close]'))) { e.preventDefault(); closeModal(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180vote]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180vote'); var a = byId(id); if (!a || S.busy['v' + id]) return;
      var i = Number(el.getAttribute('data-i'));
      if (a.ask.multi) {
        var s = S.sel[id] || (S.sel[id] = { edit: !!S.mine[id], picks: ((S.mine[id] && S.mine[id].choice) || []).slice() });
        var k = s.picks.indexOf(i); if (k >= 0) s.picks.splice(k, 1); else s.picks.push(i);
        repaint(id); return;
      }
      vote(id, [i]); return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180send]'))) { e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180send'); var sp = S.sel[id]; if (sp && sp.picks.length) vote(id, sp.picks.slice().sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; })); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180change]'))) { e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180change'); S.sel[id] = { edit: true, picks: ((S.mine[id] && S.mine[id].choice) || []).slice() }; repaint(id); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180keep]'))) { e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180keep'); delete S.sel[id]; repaint(id); return; }
    /* a short form's choices */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180pick]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); var qid = el.getAttribute('data-w180pick'), ix = Number(el.getAttribute('data-i')), multi = el.getAttribute('data-multi') === '1';
      var af2 = byId(S.formId), qq = af2 && af2.ask && (af2.ask.questions || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === qid; })[0]; if (!qq) return;
      S.formAns = S.formAns || {};
      if (qq.type === 'yesno') S.formAns[qid] = S.formAns[qid] === (ix === 0) ? undefined : (ix === 0);
      else if (multi) { var cur = Array.isArray(S.formAns[qid]) ? S.formAns[qid] : []; var kk = cur.indexOf(ix); if (kk >= 0) cur.splice(kk, 1); else cur.push(ix); S.formAns[qid] = cur.sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; }); }
      else S.formAns[qid] = S.formAns[qid] === ix ? undefined : ix;
      var fe = document.querySelector('#modalBox [data-w180ferr]'); if (fe) fe.hidden = true;
      var grp = el.parentNode, val = S.formAns[qid];
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(grp.querySelectorAll('[data-w180pick]'), function (b) {
        var j = Number(b.getAttribute('data-i')), on = qq.type === 'yesno' ? (val === true && j === 0) || (val === false && j === 1) : Array.isArray(val) ? val.indexOf(j) >= 0 : val === j;
        b.classList.toggle('on', on); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
      return;
    }
    /* the answers window */
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180csv]'))) { e.preventDefault(); var ac = byId(el.getAttribute('data-w180csv')); if (ac) downloadCsv(ac); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180shut]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180shut'); el.disabled = true;
      patch(id, { closes_at: new Date().toISOString() }).then(function (a2) { T('Answers are closed'); paintCard(); if (a2) openResults(a2); }, function (err) { el.disabled = false; T('Not closed: ' + err.message); });
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180down]'))) {
      e.preventDefault(); id = el.getAttribute('data-w180down');
      if (!S.busy['down' + id]) { S.busy['down' + id] = 1; el.textContent = 'Press again to take it down'; setTimeout(function () { S.busy['down' + id] = 0; }, 6000); return; }
      S.busy['down' + id] = 0; el.disabled = true;
      patch(id, { expires_at: todayIso() }).then(function () { closeModal(); T('Taken down'); paintCard(); try { if (window.w116News && w116News.refresh) w116News.refresh(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }, function (err) { el.disabled = false; T('Not taken down: ' + err.message); });
      return;
    }
    /* the post form */
    if (!t.closest('#modalBox [data-w180postform]')) return;
    var d = S.draft; if (!d) return;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180aud]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); d.audience = el.getAttribute('data-w180aud'); if (d.audience === 'teams' && !d.teams.length) { var own = postTeams(); if (own.length === 1) d.teams = [own[0]]; } redrawPost(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180team]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); var tm = el.getAttribute('data-w180team'), ti = d.teams.indexOf(tm); if (ti >= 0) d.teams.splice(ti, 1); else d.teams.push(tm); redrawPost(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180askt]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); d.ask = el.getAttribute('data-w180askt'); if (d.ask !== 'none' && !d.closes) d.closes = ''; redrawPost(d.ask === 'poll' ? '#w180pq' : d.ask === 'form' ? '[data-w180ql="0"]' : null); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180addopt]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); if (d.poll.options.length < 8) d.poll.options.push(''); redrawPost('[data-w180popt="' + (d.poll.options.length - 1) + '"]'); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180delopt]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); if (d.poll.options.length > 2) d.poll.options.splice(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180delopt')), 1); redrawPost(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180addq]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); if (d.form.questions.length < 8) d.form.questions.push({ id: 'q' + (d.form.questions.length + 1), label: '', type: 'short', required: false, options: '' }); redrawPost('[data-w180ql="' + (d.form.questions.length - 1) + '"]'); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w180delq]'))) { e.preventDefault(); syncDraft(); if (d.form.questions.length > 1) d.form.questions.splice(Number(el.getAttribute('data-w180delq')), 1); redrawPost(); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {   /* a message about what is missing goes once they type */
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var f = t.closest('#modalBox [data-w180formbox], #modalBox [data-w180postform]'); if (!f) return;
    var er = f.querySelector('[data-w180ferr], [data-w180perr]'); if (er && !er.hidden) er.hidden = true;
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#modalBox [data-w180postform]')) return;
    var d = S.draft; if (!d) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w180qt')) { syncDraft(); var i = Number(t.getAttribute('data-w180qt')); if (d.form.questions[i]) d.form.questions[i].type = t.value; redrawPost((t.value === 'one' || t.value === 'several') ? '[data-w180qo="' + i + '"]' : null); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w180ps' || t.id === 'w180pem') { syncDraft(); redrawPost(); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    var f = e.target; if (!f || !f.matches) return;
    if (f.matches('#modalBox [data-w180postform]')) { e.preventDefault(); submitPost(); return; }
    if (f.matches('#modalBox [data-w180formbox]')) { e.preventDefault(); submitForm(f.getAttribute('data-w180formbox')); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('#modalBox [data-w180open]')) { e.preventDefault(); openItem(e.target.getAttribute('data-w180open')); }
  });

  /* ---------- the windows share the Hub's modal: only this wave's are wide ---------- */
  if (typeof openModal === 'function') { var __om180 = openModal; openModal = function () { wide(false); return __om180.apply(this, arguments); }; }
  if (typeof closeModal === 'function') { var __cm180 = closeModal; closeModal = function () { wide(false); return __cm180.apply(this, arguments); }; }

  /* ---------- the address: #announce/<id> (the Slack post's link) and #announce/all open on Home ---------- */
  function hashId() { var m = /^#announce\/(\d+|all)$/.exec(location.hash || ''); return m ? m[1] : null; }
  function fromHash() {
    var id = hashId(); if (!id) return false;
    try { location.replace('#mypage'); } catch (e) { location.hash = '#mypage'; }   /* Home, where announcements live (fires the Hub's own routing) */
    setTimeout(function () { if (id === 'all') openAll(); else openItem(id); }, 350);
    return true;
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { try { fromHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  /* the email's (or Slack's) link through the sign-in: Google brings people back without the address, so this tab keeps it
     until the Hub opens (15 minutes at most) */
  function keepPending() { var id = hashId(); if (!id) return; try { sessionStorage.setItem('w180pend', JSON.stringify({ id: id, t: Date.now() })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function takePending() {
    var v = null;
    try {
      var raw = sessionStorage.getItem('w180pend'); sessionStorage.removeItem('w180pend');
      var o = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
      var age = o ? Date.now() - Number(o.t || 0) : -1e12;
      if (o && /^(\d+|all)$/.test(String(o.id)) && age > -60000 && age < 15 * 60000) v = String(o.id);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return v;
  }
  keepPending();

  /* ---------- Home: the card after every render ---------- */
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm180 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm180.apply(this, arguments);
      try { refreshCard(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa40Load === 'function') {   /* the card redraws once the person's access is known */
    var __wl180 = wa40Load;
    wa40Load = async function () { var r = await __wl180.apply(this, arguments); try { if (S.list) paintCard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return r; };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa180 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var pend = hashId(), kept = takePending();
      if (pend) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#mypage'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      pend = pend || kept;
      var out = await __sa180.apply(this, arguments);
      try { addTour(); if (pend) setTimeout(function () { if (pend === 'all') openAll(); else openItem(pend); }, 600); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  try {
    if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && db.auth) db.auth.onAuthStateChange(function (evt) { if (evt === 'SIGNED_OUT') { S.list = null; S.mine = {}; S.res = {}; S.sel = {}; S.draft = null; var c = document.getElementById('w180card'); if (c) c.remove(); } });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- the Home tour: a stop for the card ---------- */
  function addTour() {
    try {
      var TT = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!TT || !TT.home || !TT.home.steps || TT.home.__w180) return;
      var hs = TT.home.steps, i = -1;

      hs.splice(i >= 0 ? i + 1 : Math.min(2, hs.length), 0, { s: '#w180card', t: 'Announcements', p: 'What your leaders, HR and the admins posted for you: news, and polls or short forms you answer right here (a tap for a poll). Leaders, HR and admins post from here: it shows at the top of Home for the people it is for, goes once to Slack and is emailed to them.', opt: 1 });
      TT.home.__w180 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  addTour(); setTimeout(addTour, 1500);

  window.w180 = { refresh: refreshCard, open: openItem, all: openAll, post: openPost, state: function () { return S; } };
