/* =====================================================================
   WA v177 Confirm your next team meeting from Home (01/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (30/09/26): "When a member is on their home page could you prompt them with confirming their attendance to the
   next team meeting so that the accept button is click on google calendar". His answers: the next team meeting (a meeting
   tied to a team, hub_events.department) in the next 7 days that they have not answered; two choices, Accept and I can't
   make it, with a note to let their leader know at least 24 hours before; Accept switches on their Google Calendar link
   in one go when it is off (said on the card; Settings turns it off). Then (30/09/26): "I'd rather it be a pop up rather
   than a section in the home page. A pop will prompt them to answer it".

   Where: a pop-up over Home (#w177pop holding the dialog #w177card), a moment after Home opens, never on top of another
   window. "Ask me later" (or Escape, or the x) closes it until they next come back to Home. The reply goes to Google
   Calendar as them (hub-connect rsvp, the domain-wide delegation of wave116j), so the organizer sees it like any reply;
   the pop-up then says so and closes. Their leader is the meeting's organizer. Nothing else is sent on their behalf.
   wave238 (14/10/26), the gate. Steel: "I don't want people to exit out of closing their tasks or change due dates on
   the pop up - they must do it to use the hub. Also answering if they are joining meetings too." So the pop-up is now
   part of the gate on opening the Hub: it shows on whatever page the person lands on (not only Home), a moment after
   the Loop's Welcome back (#w238loop) is answered, and it cannot be put off: no x, no Ask me later, no Escape, no click
   outside; every unanswered team meeting of the next seven days is asked in turn. Only a failed reply (Google Calendar
   could not be reached) offers Try again later, so a fault never locks anyone out. The due-date check (#w213pop) waits
   for it.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa177) return; window.__wa177 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', FLAG = '#B3402F', INK = '#1F201E';
  var HEAD = '"Barlow Condensed", "Arial Narrow", sans-serif';
  var DAYS = 7, SKIP = ['hub@welfareavenue.com', 'loom@welfareavenue.com', 'admin@welfareavenue.com', 'wa-hub-drive@welfareavenue.com'];
  var S = { list: null, at: 0, p: null, busy: false, done: null, err: '', later: {}, quiet: false, timer: null };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function tick() { try { return performance.now(); } catch (e) { return Date.now(); } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; }
  function nameOf(em) {
    em = lower(em); var m = members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0];
    return m && m.name ? String(m.name).trim().split(/\s+/)[0] : (em ? em.split('@')[0].split('.')[0].replace(/^./, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }) : '');
  }
  function teamLabel(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || d; } catch (e) { return d; } }
  function when(iso) {
    var d = new Date(iso); if (isNaN(d)) return '';
    var o = { timeZone: 'America/Toronto' };
    var day = d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', Object.assign({ weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }, o));
    var t = d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', Object.assign({ hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }, o)).replace(/\./g, '').replace(/\s?(a|p)m$/i, function (x) { return ' ' + x.trim().toLowerCase(); });
    var k = function (x) { return x.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', o); };
    var now = new Date(), tom = new Date(now.getTime() + 864e5);
    var rel = k(d) === k(now) ? 'Today' : k(d) === k(tom) ? 'Tomorrow' : day;
    return rel + ' at ' + t;
  }
  function myAttendee(e, em) { return (e.attendees || []).filter(function (a) { return lower(a.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function organizer(e) {
    var o = (e.attendees || []).filter(function (a) { return a.organizer; })[0];
    return lower((o && o.email) || e.organizer || '');
  }

  /* the next team meeting this person has not answered (within DAYS) */
  function pick() {
    var em = myEmail(); if (!em || !S.list) return null;
    var now = Date.now();
    var dismissed = S.done && S.done.id;
    return S.list.filter(function (e) {
      if (!e.department || e.status === 'cancelled' || e.all_day) return false;
      if (new Date(e.start_at).getTime() <= now) return false;
      if (String(e.id) === String(dismissed)) return false;
      if (organizer(e) === em) return false;
      var a = myAttendee(e, em); if (!a) return false;
      return !a.status || a.status === 'needsAction';
    })[0] || null;
  }
  async function load(force) {
    var em = myEmail(); if (!em || !staff()) { S.list = null; return null; }
    if (!force && S.list && Date.now() - S.at < 5 * 60000) return S.list;
    if (S.p) return S.p;
    S.p = (async function () {
      try {
        var from = new Date().toISOString(), to = new Date(Date.now() + DAYS * 864e5).toISOString();
        var r = await db.from('hub_events').select('id,summary,start_at,end_at,all_day,status,department,organizer,attendees,attendee_emails,meet_link,html_link')
          .gte('start_at', from).lte('start_at', to).not('department', 'is', null).contains('attendee_emails', [em]).order('start_at').limit(20);
        if (r && !r.error) { S.list = r.data || []; S.at = Date.now(); }
      } catch (e) { /* offline: keep what we had */ }
      S.p = null; return S.list;
    })();
    return S.p;
  }

  function css() {
    if (document.getElementById('w177css')) return;
    var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w177css';
    st.textContent = ''
      + '#w177pop{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;background:rgba(16,32,30,.48);animation:w177in .18s ease-out;}'
      + '@keyframes w177in{from{opacity:0}to{opacity:1}}'
      + '#w177card{position:relative;width:min(520px,100%);max-height:calc(100vh - 32px);overflow:auto;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:20px;background:' + CREAM + ';border-top:6px solid ' + GOLD + ';padding:22px 22px 18px;font-family:inherit;color:' + INK + ';box-shadow:0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.28);}'
      + '#w177card .x{position:absolute;top:10px;right:10px;width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:50%;border:0;background:transparent;color:#5d5b57;font-size:22px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;padding:0;}'
      + '#w177card .x:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);}'
      + '#w177card .k{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1.6px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:800;margin:0 36px 0 0;}'
      + '#w177card h3{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:28px;line-height:1.08;margin:4px 0 6px;color:' + GREEN + ';text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.3px;}'
      + '#w177card .w{margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14.5px;color:' + INK + ';}'
      + '#w177card .w a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;margin-left:8px;}'
      + '#w177card .q{font-weight:700;font-size:16px;margin:0 0 10px;}'
      + '#w177card .row{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;}'
      + '#w177card .row button{border:0;border-radius:999px;padding:11px 20px;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;flex:1 1 160px;}'
      + '#w177card button.yes{background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';}'
      + '#w177card button.no{background:transparent;color:' + FLAG + ';border:1.5px solid rgba(179,64,47,.45);}'
      + '#w177card button[disabled]{opacity:.55;cursor:default;}'
      + '#w177card .note{font-size:12.5px;color:#5d5b57;margin:12px 0 0;line-height:1.45;}'
      + '#w177card .note b{color:' + INK + ';}'
      + '#w177card .err{font-size:12.5px;color:' + FLAG + ';margin:10px 0 0;}'
      + '#w177card .later{display:block;margin:12px auto 0;border:0;background:none;color:#5d5b57;font-weight:600;font-size:12.5px;font-family:inherit;text-decoration:underline;cursor:pointer;}'
      + '#w177card.ok{border-top-color:' + GREEN + ';}'
      + '#w177card.dec{border-top-color:' + FLAG + ';}'
      + '@media (max-width:600px){#w177pop{align-items:flex-end;padding:0;}#w177card{width:100%;border-radius:20px 20px 0 0;padding:20px 18px calc(18px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));}#w177card h3{font-size:24px;}}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }
  function cardHtml(e) {
    var lead = nameOf(organizer(e)); var calOn = !!(window.w116Conn && window.w116Conn.calendar);
    var soon = new Date(e.start_at).getTime() - Date.now() < 864e5;
    /* wave238: no x and no Ask me later: the gate stays until answered */
    return '<p class="k" id="w177k">Your next team meeting \u00b7 ' + E(teamLabel(e.department)) + '</p>'
      + '<h3>' + E(e.summary || 'Team meeting') + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="w">' + E(when(e.start_at)) + (e.meet_link ? '<a href="' + E(e.meet_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Meet link</a>' : '') + '</p>'
      + '<p class="q">Will you be there?</p>'
      + '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="yes" data-w177="accepted"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy === 'accepted' ? 'Accepting\u2026' : 'Accept') + '</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="no" data-w177="declined"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy === 'declined' ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'I can\u2019t make it') + '</button></div>'
      + '<p class="note">Your answer goes to Google Calendar' + (lead ? ', so ' + E(lead) + ' sees it' : '') + '. If you can\u2019t make it, <b>let ' + E(lead || 'your team leader') + ' know at least 24 hours before</b>' + (soon ? ' (this one is less than 24 hours away, so message them now)' : '') + '.'
      + (calOn ? '' : ' Answering here switches on Google Calendar for the Hub, which you can turn off in Settings.') + '</p>'
      + (S.err ? '<p class="err">' + E(S.err) + (e.html_link ? ' <a href="' + E(e.html_link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Answer in Google Calendar</a>' : '') + '</p>'
          + '<button type="button" class="later" data-w177later' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Try again later</button>' : '');
  }
  function doneHtml(d) {
    var lead = d.lead;
    var x = '';   /* wave238: no x; Close (below) moves on to the next meeting, or closes */
    if (d.response === 'accepted') return x + '<p class="k" id="w177k">Thank you</p><h3>See you there</h3><p class="w">' + E(d.summary) + ', ' + E(d.when) + '. Accepted on your Google Calendar.</p>'
      + '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="yes" data-w177close>Close</button></div>';
    return x + '<p class="k" id="w177k">Answer sent</p><h3>Thanks for letting us know</h3><p class="w">' + E(d.summary) + ', ' + E(d.when) + '. Declined on your Google Calendar.</p>'
      + '<p class="note"><b>Please also let ' + E(lead || 'your team leader') + ' know</b>, at least 24 hours before the meeting.</p>'
      + '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button type="button" class="yes" data-w177close>Close</button></div>';
  }
  function onHome() {
    var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), home = document.getElementById('mypage');
    return !!body && (!home || home.classList.contains('active'));
  }
  /* wave238: the gate shows on any page once the Hub has started for a signed-in staff member (the sign-in screen down) */
  function inHub() {
    try { var g = document.getElementById('gate'); if (g && g.style.display !== 'none') return false; } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    return staff() && !!document.getElementById('tabBar');
  }
  /* wave238: the Loop's Welcome back gate comes first */
  function loopGateUp() { var l = document.getElementById('w238loop'); return !!l && l.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0; }
  /* another window is open (the Hub's modal, the Academy's sheet, a tour, any dialog): wait for it to close */
  function busyElsewhere() {
    try {
      var mb = document.getElementById('modalBack'); if (mb && getComputedStyle(mb).display !== 'none') return true;
      var sh = document.getElementById('w122sheet'); if (sh && sh.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0 && getComputedStyle(sh).display !== 'none' && getComputedStyle(sh).visibility !== 'hidden') return true;
      /* the welcome window (84), a tour step (122) */
      if (Array.prototype.some.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w84back, #w122cel, .w122tp'), function (d) { var r = d.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && getComputedStyle(d).display !== 'none' && getComputedStyle(d).visibility !== 'hidden'; })) return true;
      return Array.prototype.some.call(document.querySelectorAll('[aria-modal="true"]'), function (d) { return !d.closest('#w177pop') && d.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0; });
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function close() {
    var p = document.getElementById('w177pop'); if (p) p.remove();
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
  }
  function onKey(ev) {
    /* wave238: Escape does nothing (the gate stays until answered) */
    if (ev.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('w177pop')) { ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation(); }
  }
  function later() {
    var e = pick(); if (e) S.later[e.id] = 1;
    S.err = ''; S.quiet = true; close();
  }
  function paint() {
    var pop = document.getElementById('w177pop');
    var e = S.done ? null : pick();
    if (!inHub()) { if (pop) close(); return; }
    var html = S.done ? doneHtml(S.done) : (e && !S.later[e.id] && !S.quiet) ? cardHtml(e) : '';
    if (!html) { if (pop) close(); return; }
    if (!pop && (busyElsewhere() || loopGateUp())) return;
    css();
    var first = !pop;
    if (!pop) {
      pop = document.createElement('div'); pop.id = 'w177pop';
      pop.innerHTML = '<div id="w177card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w177k"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(pop);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
    }
    var el = document.getElementById('w177card');
    el.className = S.done ? (S.done.response === 'accepted' ? 'ok' : 'dec') : '';
    if (e) el.setAttribute('data-ev', e.id); else el.removeAttribute('data-ev');
    if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; if (!first && !S.busy) { var f = el.querySelector('button.yes'); if (f && S.done) try { f.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }
    if (first) { var b = el.querySelector('button.yes'); if (b) try { b.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* a moment after Home opens, so the page is drawn behind it */
  function soon(ms) { clearTimeout(S.timer); S.timer = setTimeout(function () { refresh(false); }, ms == null ? 4200 : ms); }   /* wave238: after the Loop's gate has had its moment */
  async function refresh(force) { await load(force); paint(); }

  async function answer(resp) {
    var em = myEmail(); var e = pick(); if (!e || S.busy) return;
    S.busy = resp; S.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      /* Accept switches on Google Calendar for the Hub when it is off (Steel, 30/09/26); the card says so */
      if (!(window.w116Conn && window.w116Conn.calendar)) {
        var t = await db.functions.invoke('hub-connect', { body: { action: 'toggle', provider: 'google_calendar', enabled: true } });
        var td = (t && t.data) || {};
        if ((t && t.error) || td.ok === false) throw new Error(td.error && /welfareavenue\.com/.test(td.error) ? 'Replying from the Hub works with a welfareavenue.com account.' : 'Google Calendar could not be switched on for you.');
        window.w116Conn = Object.assign({}, window.w116Conn || {}, { calendar: true });
      }
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-connect', { body: { action: 'rsvp', event_id: e.id, response: resp } });
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      if ((r && r.error) || d.ok === false || d.error) throw new Error(d.error || 'The reply did not go through.');
      var a = myAttendee(e, em); if (a) a.status = resp;
      try { ((window.W116 && window.W116.events) || []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(e.id)) (x.attendees || []).forEach(function (at) { if (lower(at.email) === em) at.status = resp; }); }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      S.done = { id: e.id, response: resp, summary: e.summary || 'Team meeting', when: when(e.start_at), lead: nameOf(organizer(e)), at: tick() };
      S.busy = false; paint();
      toastS(resp === 'accepted' ? 'Accepted on Google Calendar' : 'Declined on Google Calendar');
      var doneAt = S.done; setTimeout(function () { if (S.done === doneAt && resp === 'accepted') finish(); }, 6000);
    } catch (err) {
      S.busy = false; S.err = String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 160); paint();
    }
  }
  /* after an answer: close, then the next unanswered meeting, if any (wave238: the gate asks them all) */
  function finish() { S.done = null; S.err = ''; S.quiet = false; close(); setTimeout(function () { paint(); }, 250); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('#w177card [data-w177]'); if (b) { ev.preventDefault(); answer(b.getAttribute('data-w177')); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w177card [data-w177later]')) { ev.preventDefault(); if (!S.busy && S.err) later(); return; }   /* wave238: only after a failed reply */
    if (t.closest('#w177card [data-w177close]')) { ev.preventDefault(); finish(); }
  });

  /* coming back to Home (from another page, or a fresh load) asks again */
  var wasHome = false;
  function watch() {
    var h = onHome();
    if (h && !wasHome) { S.quiet = false; S.later = {}; soon(); }
    /* wave238: leaving Home no longer closes it; the gate stays until answered */
    wasHome = h;
  }
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rm177 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rm177.apply(this, arguments);
      try { watch(); if (!S.quiet) soon(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(watch, 300); });
  /* another window was open: try again once it closes; look again every ten minutes */
  setInterval(function () { try { watch(); if (!document.hidden && inHub() && !S.quiet && !S.list && !S.p && myEmail()) { refresh(false); return; } if (!document.hidden && inHub() && !document.getElementById('w177pop') && !S.quiet && pick() && !busyElsewhere() && !loopGateUp()) paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 2000);
  setInterval(function () { try { if (!document.hidden && inHub() && !S.quiet) refresh(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 10 * 60000);
  /* wave238: the gate shows on whatever page the Hub opens on, a moment after the start (the Loop's gate first, see paint) */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa177 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { var r = await __sa177.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(function () { try { refresh(false); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 4000); return r; };
  }
  try {
    if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && db.auth) db.auth.onAuthStateChange(function (evt) { if (evt === 'SIGNED_OUT') { S.list = null; S.done = null; S.later = {}; close(); } });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  window.w177 = { refresh: refresh, pick: pick, close: close, state: function () { return S; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v181 The HR centre's Analytics: who has been part of Welfare Avenue, and for how long (30/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (30/09/26), with Slack's Member Analytics export (29/08/2025 to 29/09/2026): "To help you add start dates, add an
   analytics tab to the hr center, show trends, and know who's been a part of the org and for how long ish (we may have
   deactivated their accounts late)".

   Where: a seventh section in the HR centre, Analytics (#hrcentre/analytics; on a computer it is in the app's rail), for
   HR and admins only (wa181_can_see: the HR access list, the HR profile, admins; leaders who open the HR centre do not get
   the tab). It shows:
   - the headline (on the team now, joined and left this fiscal year, how long people stay, everyone since 2021);
   - the team over time (people on the team each month since May 2021, with the month's joiners; hover for the numbers);
   - each fiscal year (October to September): joined, left, on the team at the year's end, and how many of that year's
     joiners are still here;
   - how long people stay (the current team so far, and the people who left);
   - by team (the current team);
   - everyone who has been part of Welfare Avenue, filterable, with a sheet per person (their Slack accounts, link to the
     roster or mark as not a team member) and a CSV;
   - Worth a look (people on Slack but not on the roster, roster and Slack disagreeing, accounts nobody used in a year,
     links made by first name, people with no start date);
   - Import a new Slack export (the same CSV; the page reads it and wa181_import saves it).
   Honest dates: accounts were switched off late and in batches, so a leaving date is "left by" (the latest day they can
   have left), and someone not active once in the export's window left by its first day.
   Data: hr_people_tenure_v (one row per person), hr_slack_accounts (one per Slack account), wa181_import, wa181_link.
   Start dates on the roster now take the day people joined Slack when it is earlier (start_source slack; wave181c).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa181) return; window.__wa181 = true;

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase(); }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : (many || one + 's')); }
  function todayIso() { try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); } catch (e) { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function ym(iso) { return String(iso || '').slice(0, 7); }
  function monLabel(k) { var p = String(k).split('-'); return MON[Number(p[1]) - 1] + ' ' + p[0]; }
  function dayNice(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var p = String(iso).slice(0, 10).split('-'); return Number(p[2]) + ' ' + MON[Number(p[1]) - 1] + ' ' + p[0]; }
  function monthNice(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var p = String(iso).slice(0, 10).split('-'); return MON[Number(p[1]) - 1] + ' ' + p[0]; }
  function months(a, b) { /* whole months between two ISO days */
    if (!a || !b) return null; var x = String(a).split('-').map(Number), y = String(b).split('-').map(Number);
    var m = (y[0] - x[0]) * 12 + (y[1] - x[1]) + (y[2] >= x[2] ? 0 : -1); return Math.max(0, m);
  }
  function dur(m) {
    if (m == null) return '';
    if (m < 1) return 'under a month'; if (m < 12) return plural(m, 'month');
    var y = Math.floor(m / 12), r = m % 12; return y + (y === 1 ? ' year' : ' years') + (r ? ' ' + r + ' mo' : '');
  }
  function fyOf(iso) { var y = Number(String(iso).slice(0, 4)), m = Number(String(iso).slice(5, 7)); return m >= 10 ? y : y - 1; }   /* the fiscal year starting 1 October of that year */
  function fyLabel(y) { return y + '-' + String((y + 1) % 100).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function median(a) { if (!a.length) return null; var s = a.slice().sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; }); var h = Math.floor(s.length / 2); return s.length % 2 ? s[h] : Math.round((s[h - 1] + s[h]) / 2); }
  function teamName(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function teamShort(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || teamName(d); } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function phone() { return window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia('(max-width: 760px)').matches : window.innerWidth <= 760; }
  function hrOn() { var pn = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); return !!pn && pn.classList.contains('active'); }
  function curView() { var on = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab.on'); return on ? on.getAttribute('data-w47v') : ''; }

  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w181css'; st.textContent = CSS; document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---------- who may see it, and the data ---------- */
  function canSee() {
    if (A.can !== null) return Promise.resolve(A.can);
    if (A.canP) return A.canP;
    A.canP = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.rpc('wa181_can_see'); A.can = !!(r && !r.error && r.data === true); }
      catch (e) { A.can = false; }
      A.canP = null; return A.can;
    })();
    return A.canP;
  }
  async function pageAll(q) {   /* PostgREST answers 1,000 rows a call */
    var out = [], from = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      var r = await q().range(from, from + 999);
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      out = out.concat(r.data || []); if (!r.data || r.data.length < 1000) break; from += 1000;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function load(force) {
    if (A.loading) return A.loading;
    if (A.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve();
    A.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var res = await Promise.all([
          pageAll(function () { return db.from('hr_people_tenure_v').select('*').order('person_key'); }),
          pageAll(function () { return db.from('hr_slack_accounts').select('slack_id,full_name,display_name,email,title,account_type,created_on,claimed_on,deactivated_on,days_active,messages,team_member_id,person_key,kind,link_by,period_from,period_to').order('slack_id'); }),
          pageAll(function () { return db.from('team_members').select('id,name,department,active,email').order('name'); })
        ]);
        A.rows = res[0]; A.accts = res[1]; A.roster = res[2]; A.err = ''; A.loaded = true;
      } catch (e) { A.err = (e && e.message) || String(e); }
      A.loading = null;
    })();
    return A.loading;
  }

  /* ---------- the numbers ---------- */
  function today() { return todayIso(); }
  function period() {   /* the export's window */
    var f = null, t = null;
    A.accts.forEach(function (a) { if (a.period_from && (!f || a.period_from > f)) f = a.period_from; if (a.period_to && (!t || a.period_to > t)) t = a.period_to; });
    return { from: f, to: t };
  }
  function people() { return A.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'never_joined'; }); }
  function endOf(p) {   /* the day counted as their last: today while on the team, else the latest day they can have left */
    if (p.status === 'current') return today();
    if (p.left_by) return p.left_by;
    if (p.accounts > 0 && p.open_accounts > 0 && p.period_to) return p.period_to;   /* still on Slack and used it in the window */
    return null;
  }
  function tenureOf(p) { var e = endOf(p); return p.started_on && e ? months(p.started_on, e) : null; }
  function curFy() { return fyOf(today()); }
  function stats() {
    var all = people(), cur = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'current'; });
    var t = today(), y1 = (Number(t.slice(0, 4)) - 1) + t.slice(4);   /* the last 12 months */
    var joined = all.filter(function (p) { return p.started_on && p.started_on > y1 && p.started_on <= t; });
    var left = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'current' && p.left_by && p.left_by > y1 && !(p.days_active === 0 && p.left_by === p.period_from); });
    var gone = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'current' && tenureOf(p) != null; });
    var first = null; all.forEach(function (p) { if (p.started_on && (!first || p.started_on < first)) first = p.started_on; });
    return {
      all: all, cur: cur, since: y1, joined: joined, left: left,
      curMed: median(cur.map(tenureOf).filter(function (x) { return x != null; })),
      goneMed: median(gone.map(tenureOf)),
      first: first,
      noStart: cur.filter(function (p) { return !p.started_on; }).length
    };
  }
  function monthsRange(a, b) { var out = [], y = Number(a.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(a.slice(5, 7)); var ey = Number(b.slice(0, 4)), em = Number(b.slice(5, 7)); while (y < ey || (y === ey && m <= em)) { out.push(y + '-' + String(m).padStart(2, '0')); m++; if (m > 12) { m = 1; y++; } } return out; }
  function series() {   /* people on the team at the end of each month, and the month's joiners */
    var all = people().filter(function (p) { return p.started_on; }); var t = today();
    var first = all.reduce(function (a, p) { return !a || p.started_on < a ? p.started_on : a; }, null); if (!first) return [];
    return monthsRange(ym(first), ym(t)).map(function (k) {
      var y = Number(k.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(k.slice(5, 7)); var last = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).toISOString().slice(0, 10); if (last > t) last = t;
      var on = 0, joined = [], unsure = 0;
      all.forEach(function (p) {
        if (ym(p.started_on) === k) joined.push(p.name);
        var e = endOf(p);
        if (p.started_on <= last && (e === null ? p.status === 'current' : e >= last)) { on++; if (p.status !== 'current' && p.days_active === 0) unsure++; }
      });
      return { k: k, on: on, joined: joined, unsure: unsure };
    });
  }
  function byFy() {
    var all = people(); var t = today(); var fys = {};
    all.forEach(function (p) { if (p.started_on) fys[fyOf(p.started_on)] = 1; });
    return Object.keys(fys).map(Number).sort().map(function (y) {
      var s = y + '-10-01', e = (y + 1) + '-09-30', end = e > t ? t : e;
      var j = all.filter(function (p) { return p.started_on && p.started_on >= s && p.started_on <= e; });
      var l = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'current' && p.left_by && p.left_by >= s && p.left_by <= e; });
      var on = all.filter(function (p) { var x = endOf(p); return p.started_on && p.started_on <= end && (x === null ? p.status === 'current' : x >= end); });
      return { y: y, joined: j.length, still: j.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'current'; }).length, left: l.length, on: on.length, running: e > t };
    });
  }
  var BUCKETS = [['Under 3 months', 0, 3], ['3 to 6 months', 3, 6], ['6 to 12 months', 6, 12], ['1 to 2 years', 12, 24], ['2 years or more', 24, 1e9]];
  function buckets(list) { return BUCKETS.map(function (b) { return [b[0], list.filter(function (m) { return m >= b[1] && m < b[2]; }).length]; }); }
  function byTeam() {
    var cur = people().filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'current'; }); var g = {};
    cur.forEach(function (p) { var k = p.department || '_none'; (g[k] = g[k] || []).push(p); });
    var order = ((window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order) || []).concat(['exec', 'finance', '_none']);
    return Object.keys(g).sort(function (a, b) { var x = order.indexOf(a), y = order.indexOf(b); return (x < 0 ? 99 : x) - (y < 0 ? 99 : y); }).map(function (k) {
      return { k: k, label: k === '_none' ? 'Not on the roster' : teamName(k), n: g[k].length, med: median(g[k].map(tenureOf).filter(function (x) { return x != null; })) };
    });
  }
  function acctsOf(p) { var ids = p.slack_ids || []; return A.accts.filter(function (a) { return ids.indexOf(a.slack_id) >= 0 || (p.team_member_id && a.team_member_id === p.team_member_id); }); }
  function flags(p) {
    var f = [], acc = acctsOf(p);
    if (!p.on_roster && p.status === 'current') f.push(['Not on the roster', 'warn']);
    if (p.on_roster && !p.roster_active && p.open_accounts > 0) f.push(['Left on the roster, Slack still open', 'warn']);
    if (p.on_roster && p.roster_active && p.accounts > 0 && p.open_accounts === 0) f.push(['Slack switched off', 'bad']);
    if (p.status === 'quiet') f.push(['Slack open, not used in a year', 'warn']);
    if (p.accounts > 1) f.push([p.accounts + ' Slack accounts', '']);
    if (acc.some(function (a) { return a.link_by === 'name'; })) f.push(['Linked by first name', 'warn']);
    if (p.status === 'current' && !p.started_on) f.push(['No start date', 'bad']);
    return f;
  }
  function look() {
    var all = A.rows;
    var unusedDup = A.accts.filter(function (a) {
      if (a.kind !== 'team' || !a.claimed_on || a.deactivated_on || a.days_active > 0) return false;
      return A.accts.some(function (b) { return b.slack_id !== a.slack_id && b.person_key === a.person_key && b.days_active > 0; });
    });
    return [
      { k: 'offroster', h: 'On Slack this year, not on the roster', p: 'They used Slack in the last year but have no roster row, so the Hub leaves them out of the team. Add them to the roster, or switch their Slack off if they have gone.', list: all.filter(function (p) { return !p.on_roster && p.status === 'current'; }) },
      { k: 'leftopen', h: 'Left on the roster, Slack still open', p: 'The roster says they left; their Slack account is still on. Switch it off, or put them back on the roster.', list: all.filter(function (p) { return p.on_roster && !p.roster_active && p.open_accounts > 0; }) },
      { k: 'off', h: 'On the roster, Slack switched off', p: 'Still on the team in the Hub, but their Slack account was deactivated. Have they left?', list: all.filter(function (p) { return p.on_roster && p.roster_active && p.accounts > 0 && p.open_accounts === 0; }) },
      { k: 'quiet', h: 'Slack open, not used once in a year', p: 'Accounts still on that nobody used in the export\u2019s window. Usually safe to switch off.', list: all.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'quiet'; }), extra: unusedDup.map(function (a) { var o = all.filter(function (p) { return (p.slack_ids || []).indexOf(a.slack_id) >= 0 || (p.team_member_id && p.team_member_id === a.team_member_id); })[0]; return { a: a, p: o }; }) },
      { k: 'name', h: 'Linked by first name: please confirm', p: 'These Slack accounts were matched to the roster by first name only. Open one to confirm or change it.', list: all.filter(function (p) { return acctsOf(p).some(function (a) { return a.link_by === 'name'; }); }) },
      { k: 'nostart', h: 'On the team, no start date', p: 'No Workspace or Slack date to go on. Set it on the People view (click Started).', list: all.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'current' && !p.started_on; }) }
    ];
  }

  /* ---------- painting ---------- */
  function host() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w181');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w181'; var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs'); if (tabs) tabs.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', h); else body.insertBefore(h, body.firstChild); }
    return h;
  }
  function tile(n, label, small) { return '<div class="w181tile"><b>' + E(n) + '</b><span>' + E(label) + '</span>' + (small ? '<small>' + small + '</small>' : '') + '</div>'; }
  function paint() {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    if (A.err) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w181card"><h3>Analytics</h3><p class="w181empty">Could not load the numbers: ' + E(A.err) + '</p><button class="w181btn sm" data-w181="reload">Try again</button></div>'; return; }
    if (!A.loaded) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w181card"><p class="w181empty">Loading who has been part of Welfare Avenue\u2026</p></div>'; return; }
    if (!A.rows.length) { h.innerHTML = '<div class="w181card"><h3>Analytics</h3><p class="w181empty">Nobody to show yet. Import Slack\u2019s member export to see who has been part of Welfare Avenue and for how long.</p><button class="w181btn sm" data-w181="import">Import a Slack export</button></div>'; return; }
    var s = stats(), pr = period(); A.noStart = s.noStart;
    var top = '<div class="w181top"><p>Who has been part of Welfare Avenue, and for how long: the roster and every Slack account since ' + E(monthNice(s.first)) + '. ' +
      (pr.from ? 'Slack\u2019s export covers ' + E(dayNice(pr.from)) + ' to ' + E(dayNice(pr.to)) + '. ' : '') +
      'Accounts were often switched off late, so a leaving date is the latest day they can have left.</p>' +
      '<div class="acts"><button class="w181btn ghost sm" data-w181="csv">Download (CSV)</button><button class="w181btn sm" data-w181="import">Import a new Slack export</button></div></div>';
    var tiles = '<div class="w181tiles">' +
      tile(s.cur.length, 'on the team now', s.cur.filter(function (p) { return !p.on_roster; }).length ? plural(s.cur.filter(function (p) { return !p.on_roster; }).length, 'is', 'are') + ' on Slack but not on the roster' : 'on the roster') +
      tile(s.joined.length, 'joined in the last 12 months', 'by the day they joined Slack or got a Workspace account') +
      tile(s.left.length, 'left in the last 12 months', 'Slack active in the year, then switched off') +
      tile(s.curMed == null ? '\u2013' : dur(s.curMed), 'typical time with us so far', 'the median of the team today') +
      tile(s.goneMed == null ? '\u2013' : dur(s.goneMed), 'typical stay of people who left', 'at most: they may have left earlier') +
      tile(s.all.length, 'people since ' + monthNice(s.first).split(' ')[1], 'on the team at some point, staff and volunteers') +
      '</div>';
    h.innerHTML = top + tiles + overTime() + '<div class="w181two">' + fyTable() + stayCard() + '</div><div style="height:14px"></div>' + teamCard() + lookCard() + peopleCard();
    wireChart();
  }

  function overTime() {
    var ser = series(); if (!ser.length) return '';
    var W = 760, H = 230, L = 34, R = 10, Tp = 12, B = 26, n = ser.length, cw = (W - L - R) / n;
    var max = Math.max(4, Math.max.apply(null, ser.map(function (d) { return Math.max(d.on, d.joined.length); })));
    var step = max > 60 ? 20 : max > 24 ? 10 : 5; var top = Math.ceil(max / step) * step;
    var y = function (v) { return Tp + (H - Tp - B) * (1 - v / top); };
    var grid = ''; for (var g = 0; g <= top; g += step) grid += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + y(g) + '" y2="' + y(g) + '" stroke="' + LINE + '" stroke-width="1"/><text x="' + (L - 6) + '" y="' + (y(g) + 4) + '" text-anchor="end" font-size="10" fill="' + MUTED + '">' + g + '</text>';
    var bars = ser.map(function (d, i) { var v = d.joined.length; if (!v) return ''; var x = L + i * cw + cw * 0.18; return '<rect x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + y(v).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + (cw * 0.64).toFixed(1) + '" height="' + (y(0) - y(v)).toFixed(1) + '" rx="1.5" fill="' + GOLD + '"/>'; }).join('');
    var pts = ser.map(function (d, i) { return (L + i * cw + cw / 2).toFixed(1) + ',' + y(d.on).toFixed(1); });
    var area = '<path d="M' + (L + cw / 2).toFixed(1) + ',' + y(0) + ' L' + pts.join(' L') + ' L' + (L + (n - 1) * cw + cw / 2).toFixed(1) + ',' + y(0) + ' Z" fill="rgba(16,63,57,.10)"/>';
    var line = '<polyline points="' + pts.join(' ') + '" fill="none" stroke="' + GREEN + '" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';
    var yrs = ser.map(function (d, i) { return d.k.slice(5) === '01' ? '<text x="' + (L + i * cw).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (H - 8) + '" font-size="10.5" fill="' + MUTED + '">' + d.k.slice(0, 4) + '</text><line x1="' + (L + i * cw).toFixed(1) + '" x2="' + (L + i * cw).toFixed(1) + '" y1="' + y(0) + '" y2="' + (y(0) + 4) + '" stroke="' + MUTED + '"/>' : ''; }).join('');
    var hits = ser.map(function (d, i) { return '<rect class="hit" data-i="' + i + '" x="' + (L + i * cw).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + Tp + '" width="' + cw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + (H - Tp - B) + '" fill="transparent" pointer-events="all"/>'; }).join('');
    var cur = '<line class="cur" x1="0" x2="0" y1="' + Tp + '" y2="' + y(0) + '" stroke="' + INK + '" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="3 3" style="display:none"/>';
    A.ser = ser; A.serGeom = { L: L, cw: cw, W: W };
    var last = ser[ser.length - 1], peak = ser.reduce(function (a, d) { return d.on > a.on ? d : a; }, ser[0]);
    return '<div class="w181card"><h3>The team over time</h3><p class="sub">People on the team at the end of each month (green) and the month\u2019s joiners (gold). ' + E(last.on) + ' now' + (A.noStart ? ' (' + plural(A.noStart, 'person', 'people') + ' on the team ' + (A.noStart === 1 ? 'has' : 'have') + ' no start date, so ' + (A.noStart === 1 ? 'is' : 'are') + ' left out)' : '') + '; the most was ' + E(peak.on) + ' in ' + E(monLabel(peak.k)) + '. Before September 2025 the line is at most: people not active once in Slack\u2019s export are counted until it starts, since we cannot see when they stopped.</p>' +
      '<div class="w181chart" id="w181over"><svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="People on the team each month since ' + E(monLabel(ser[0].k)) + '">' + grid + area + bars + line + yrs + cur + hits + '</svg><div class="w181tip"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w181key"><span><i style="background:' + GREEN + '"></i>On the team</span><span><i style="background:' + GOLD + '"></i>Joined that month</span><span>Hover, tap or use the arrow keys for a month\u2019s numbers</span></div></div>';
  }
  function wireChart() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w181over'); if (!box || box.dataset.w) return; box.dataset.w = '1';
    var svg = box.querySelector('svg'), tip = box.querySelector('.w181tip'), cur = svg.querySelector('.cur'); box.tabIndex = 0;
    function show(i) {
      var d = A.ser[i]; if (!d) return; A.serI = i; var g = A.serGeom; var r = svg.getBoundingClientRect(), sc = r.width / g.W;
      var x = (g.L + i * g.cw + g.cw / 2); cur.setAttribute('x1', x); cur.setAttribute('x2', x); cur.style.display = '';
      var j = d.joined.length;
      tip.innerHTML = '<b>' + E(monLabel(d.k)) + '</b><br>On the team: ' + d.on + (d.unsure ? ' (at most)' : '') + '<br>Joined: ' + j + (j && j <= 4 ? ' (' + E(d.joined.join(', ')) + ')' : '');
      tip.style.display = 'block'; var left = x * sc; left = Math.max(80, Math.min(r.width - 80, left)); tip.style.left = left + 'px'; tip.style.top = '6px';
    }
    function hide() { tip.style.display = 'none'; cur.style.display = 'none'; }
    svg.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) { var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.hit'); if (t) show(Number(t.getAttribute('data-i'))); });
    svg.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.hit'); if (t) show(Number(t.getAttribute('data-i'))); });
    svg.addEventListener('mouseleave', hide);
    box.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); var i = (A.serI == null ? A.ser.length : A.serI) + (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 1); show(Math.max(0, Math.min(A.ser.length - 1, i))); } if (e.key === 'Escape') hide(); });
    box.addEventListener('blur', hide);
  }

  function fyTable() {
    var rows = byFy();
    var tr = rows.map(function (r) {
      return '<tr><td>' + E(fyLabel(r.y)) + (r.running ? ' <span class="w181tag lg">so far</span>' : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + r.joined + '</td><td class="n">' + r.left + '</td><td class="n">' + r.on + '</td><td class="n">' + (r.joined ? r.still + ' <span style="color:' + MUTED + '">(' + Math.round(100 * r.still / r.joined) + '%)</span>' : '\u2013') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w181card"><h3>Each fiscal year</h3><p class="sub">October to September. Left counts the day an account was switched off, so a batch can land in the year after people stopped (21 October 2025 switched off 22 at once).</p>' +
      '<table class="w181t w181fy"><thead><tr><th>Year</th><th class="n">Joined</th><th class="n">Left by</th><th class="n"><span class="lg">On the team at the end</span><span class="sm">Year end</span></th><th class="n"><span class="lg">Joiners still here</span><span class="sm">Still here</span></th></tr></thead><tbody>' + tr + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }
  function barsHtml(list, color) {
    var max = Math.max(1, Math.max.apply(null, list.map(function (x) { return x[1]; })));
    return list.map(function (x) { return '<div class="r"><span>' + E(x[0]) + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + (100 * x[1] / max).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + color + '"></i></span><b>' + x[1] + '</b></div>'; }).join('');
  }
  function stayCard() {
    var all = people(), cur = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'current'; }).map(tenureOf).filter(function (x) { return x != null; });
    var gone = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'current'; }).map(tenureOf).filter(function (x) { return x != null; });
    return '<div class="w181card w181bars"><h3>How long people stay</h3><p class="sub">From the day they started to today, or to the latest day they can have left.</p>' +
      '<h4>The team today (' + cur.length + ')</h4>' + barsHtml(buckets(cur), GREEN) +
      '<h4>People who left (' + gone.length + ', at most)</h4>' + barsHtml(buckets(gone), GOLD) + '</div>';
  }
  function teamCard() {
    var t = byTeam(); if (!t.length) return '';
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, t.map(function (x) { return x.n; }));
    return '<div class="w181card w181bars"><h3>The team today, by team</h3><p class="sub">People on the team now and the typical (median) time they have been with us.</p>' +
      t.map(function (x) { return '<div class="r" style="grid-template-columns:minmax(120px,190px) 1fr 40px 110px"><span>' + E(x.label) + '</span><span class="tr"><i style="width:' + (100 * x.n / max).toFixed(1) + '%;background:' + (x.k === '_none' ? GOLD : TEAL) + '"></i></span><b>' + x.n + '</b><span style="color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:12px">' + (x.med == null ? '' : dur(x.med)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function lookCard() {
    var L = look().filter(function (g) { return g.list.length || (g.extra && g.extra.length); }); if (!L.length) return '';
    var nm = function (p) { return '<button class="w181lnk" data-w181p="' + E(p.person_key) + '">' + E(p.name) + '</button>'; };
    return '<div class="w181card"><h3>Worth a look</h3><p class="sub">Where the roster and Slack disagree, and what to tidy. Click a name for their sheet.</p><div class="w181look">' +
      L.map(function (g) {
        var names = g.list.map(nm).join(', ');
        if (g.extra && g.extra.length) names += (names ? '; ' : '') + 'and unused second accounts: ' + g.extra.map(function (x) { return x.p ? '<button class="w181lnk" data-w181p="' + E(x.p.person_key) + '">' + E(x.p.name) + '</button> (' + E(x.a.email || x.a.slack_id) + ')' : E(x.a.full_name); }).join(', ');
        return '<div data-w181look="' + g.k + '"><b class="h">' + E(g.h) + ' (' + (g.list.length + (g.extra ? g.extra.length : 0)) + ')</b><p>' + E(g.p) + '</p>' + names + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  var FILTERS = [['current', 'On the team now'], ['left', 'Left'], ['quiet', 'Slack open, unused'], ['never_joined', 'Never joined'], ['all', 'Everyone']];
  function filtered() {
    var q = lower(A.q);
    var rows = A.rows.filter(function (p) {
      if (A.f !== 'all' && p.status !== A.f) return false;
      if (A.team && (p.department || '_none') !== A.team) return false;
      if (q && lower(p.name + ' ' + (p.slack_name || '') + ' ' + (p.title || '') + ' ' + (p.email || '')).indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    var key = A.sort;
    rows.sort(function (a, b) {
      if (key === 'name') return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name));
      if (key === 'long') return (tenureOf(b) || -1) - (tenureOf(a) || -1);
      if (key === 'left') return String(b.left_by || '').localeCompare(String(a.left_by || ''));
      return String(a.started_on || '9').localeCompare(String(b.started_on || '9'));
    });
    return rows;
  }
  function statusTag(p) {
    if (p.status === 'current') return p.on_roster ? '<span class="w181tag ok">On the team</span>' : '<span class="w181tag ok">On Slack</span>';
    if (p.status === 'left') return '<span class="w181tag">Left</span>';
    if (p.status === 'quiet') return '<span class="w181tag warn">Slack unused</span>';
    return '<span class="w181tag">Invited, never joined</span>';
  }
  function leftCell(p) {
    if (p.status === 'current') return '';
    if (p.left_by) return 'by ' + monthNice(p.left_by);
    return p.status === 'never_joined' ? '' : 'not known';
  }
  function timeCell(p) { var m = tenureOf(p); if (m == null) return ''; return (p.status === 'current' ? '' : 'up to ') + dur(m); }
  function peopleCard() {
    var counts = {}; A.rows.forEach(function (p) { counts[p.status] = (counts[p.status] || 0) + 1; }); counts.all = A.rows.length;
    var teams = {}; A.rows.forEach(function (p) { teams[p.department || '_none'] = 1; });
    var rows = filtered();
    var chips = FILTERS.map(function (f) { return '<button class="w181chip' + (A.f === f[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w181f="' + f[0] + '">' + E(f[1]) + ' ' + (counts[f[0]] || 0) + '</button>'; }).join('');
    var tsel = '<select id="w181team" aria-label="Team"><option value="">Every team</option>' + Object.keys(teams).sort().map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + E(k) + '"' + (A.team === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(k === '_none' ? 'Not on the roster' : teamName(k)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
    var ssel = '<select id="w181sort" aria-label="Sort"><option value="start"' + (A.sort === 'start' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Longest-standing first</option><option value="long"' + (A.sort === 'long' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Longest time with us</option><option value="left"' + (A.sort === 'left' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most recently left</option><option value="name"' + (A.sort === 'name' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Name</option></select>';
    var body = rows.map(function (p) {
      var fl = flags(p).map(function (f) { return '<span class="w181tag ' + f[1] + '">' + E(f[0]) + '</span>'; }).join('');
      return '<tr data-w181p="' + E(p.person_key) + '" tabindex="0"><td><b>' + E(p.name) + '</b>' + (p.title ? '<span class="t">' + E(p.title) + '</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td data-l="Team">' + E(p.department ? teamShort(p.department) : '') + '</td><td data-l="Started">' + E(p.started_on ? monthNice(p.started_on) : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td data-l="Left">' + E(leftCell(p)) + '</td><td data-l="Time with us">' + E(timeCell(p)) + '</td><td data-l="Slack, last year" class="n">' + (p.accounts ? p.days_active + ' days' : '') + '</td><td>' + statusTag(p) + fl + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="w181card" id="w181people"><h3>Everyone who has been part of Welfare Avenue</h3><p class="sub">Started is the earliest of their Workspace account and the day they joined Slack. Slack, last year is the days they were active in the export\u2019s window.</p>' +
      '<div class="w181bar">' + chips + '</div><div class="w181bar"><input type="search" id="w181q" placeholder="Search by name, role or email" value="' + E(A.q) + '">' + tsel + ssel + '</div>' +
      (body ? '<table class="w181t w181ppl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Team</th><th>Started</th><th>Left</th><th>Time with us</th><th class="n">Slack, last year</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>' + body + '</tbody></table>' : '<p class="w181empty">Nobody matches.</p>') +
      '<p class="w181note">' + plural(rows.length, 'person', 'people') + '. Click a person for their Slack accounts, to link an account to the roster, or to mark it as not a team member.</p></div>';
  }
  function repaintPeople() {
    var c = document.getElementById('w181people'); if (!c) return paint();
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = peopleCard(); c.replaceWith(d.firstChild);
  }

  /* ---------- a person's sheet ---------- */
  function personBy(key) { return A.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.person_key === key; })[0]; }
  function openPerson(key) {
    var p = personBy(key); if (!p || typeof openModal !== 'function') return;
    var acc = acctsOf(p).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.claimed_on || a.created_on || '').localeCompare(String(b.claimed_on || b.created_on || '')); });
    var opts = function (sel) { return '<option value="">Not on the roster</option>' + A.roster.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + m.id + '"' + (String(sel) === String(m.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + (m.active === false ? ' (left)' : '') + (m.department ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(teamShort(m.department)) : '') + '</option>'; }).join(''); };
    var accHtml = acc.length ? acc.map(function (a) {
      var bits = [];
      bits.push(a.claimed_on ? 'Joined Slack ' + dayNice(a.claimed_on) : 'Invited ' + dayNice(a.created_on) + ', never joined');
      bits.push(a.deactivated_on ? 'switched off ' + dayNice(a.deactivated_on) : (a.claimed_on ? 'still on' : ''));
      bits.push(a.days_active + ' active days in the export');
      return '<div class="acc" data-w181acc="' + E(a.slack_id) + '"><b>' + E(a.full_name) + '</b> <span style="color:' + MUTED + '">' + E(a.email || '') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(a.account_type) + '</span>' +
        (a.link_by === 'name' ? ' <span class="w181tag warn">linked by first name</span>' : '') + (a.kind === 'external' ? ' <span class="w181tag">not a team member</span>' : '') +
        '<div style="color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:12.5px">' + E(bits.filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + (a.title ? ' \u00b7 \u201c' + E(a.title) + '\u201d' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="row"><label style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700">Roster person <select data-w181m="' + E(a.slack_id) + '">' + opts(a.team_member_id) + '</select></label>' +
        '<label style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700"><input type="checkbox" data-w181x="' + E(a.slack_id) + '"' + (a.kind === 'external' ? ' checked' : '') + '> Not a team member (a consultant, a guest)</label>' +
        '<button class="w181btn sm" data-w181save="' + E(a.slack_id) + '">Save</button></div></div>';
    }).join('') : '<p style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + '">No Slack account found for them. Their start date comes from the roster.</p>';
    var m = tenureOf(p);
    openModal('<div class="w181sheet"><h3>' + E(p.name) + '</h3><p class="sub">' + E([p.title, p.department ? teamName(p.department) : (p.on_roster ? '' : 'Not on the roster')].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</p>' +
      '<div class="facts"><div><small>Status</small>' + statusTag(p) + '</div><div><small>Started</small>' + E(p.started_on ? dayNice(p.started_on) : 'not known') + (p.started_on ? '<br><span style="font-size:11.5px;color:' + MUTED + '">' + E(p.joined_slack && p.started_on === p.joined_slack ? 'joined Slack' : (p.start_source === 'manual' ? 'set by HR' : 'Workspace account')) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div><small>Left</small>' + E(leftCell(p) || (p.status === 'current' ? 'still here' : '')) + '</div><div><small>Time with us</small>' + E(m == null ? 'not known' : timeCell(p)) + '</div></div>' +
      '<b style="font-size:13px;color:' + GREEN + '">Slack accounts</b>' + accHtml + '<p class="err" id="w181serr"></p>' +
      '<div class="foot">' + (p.team_member_id ? '<a class="w181btn ghost sm" href="#person/' + p.team_member_id + '">Open their profile</a>' : '') + '<button class="w181btn ghost sm" data-w181="close">Close</button></div></div>');
  }
  async function saveLink(id) {
    var sel = document.querySelector('[data-w181m="' + id + '"]'), x = document.querySelector('[data-w181x="' + id + '"]'), err = document.getElementById('w181serr');
    var btn = document.querySelector('[data-w181save="' + id + '"]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa181_link', { p_slack_id: id, p_member: sel && sel.value ? Number(sel.value) : null, p_kind: x && x.checked ? 'external' : 'team' });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      T('Saved'); if (typeof closeModal === 'function') closeModal();
      await load(true); paint();
    } catch (e) { if (err) err.textContent = 'Could not save: ' + ((e && e.message) || e); if (btn) btn.disabled = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- the CSV ---------- */
  function csvCell(v) { v = v == null ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(v) ? '"' + v.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : v; }
  function downloadCsv() {
    var head = ['Name', 'Team', 'Title', 'Status', 'On the roster', 'Started', 'Left by', 'Months with us', 'Slack accounts', 'Slack active days (export)', 'Notes'];
    var lines = [head.join(',')].concat(filtered().map(function (p) {
      return [p.name, p.department ? teamName(p.department) : '', p.title || '', p.status, p.on_roster ? 'yes' : 'no', p.started_on || '', p.left_by || '', tenureOf(p) == null ? '' : tenureOf(p), p.accounts, p.days_active, flags(p).map(function (f) { return f[0]; }).join('; ')].map(csvCell).join(',');
    }));
    try {
      var blob = new Blob(['\ufeff' + lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }); var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = todayIso().split('-').reverse().map(function (x, i) { return i === 2 ? x.slice(2) : x; }).join('-') + ' - Welfare Avenue people and tenure.csv';
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 500);
    } catch (e) { T('Could not make the file'); }
  }

  /* ---------- importing a new export ---------- */
  function parseCsv(text) {
    var rows = [], row = [], cell = '', q = false, i = 0, c;
    text = String(text || '').replace(/^\ufeff/, '');
    for (; i < text.length; i++) {
      c = text[i];
      if (q) { if (c === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; i++; } else q = false; } else cell += c; }
      else if (c === '"') q = true;
      else if (c === ',') { row.push(cell); cell = ''; }
      else if (c === '\n' || c === '\r') { if (c === '\r' && text[i + 1] === '\n') i++; row.push(cell); rows.push(row); row = []; cell = ''; }
      else cell += c;
    }
    if (cell !== '' || row.length) { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); }
    return rows.filter(function (r) { return r.length > 1 || (r[0] || '').trim(); });
  }
  function slackDay(s) {   /* "Jan 8, 2025" */
    s = String(s || '').trim(); if (!s) return null; var m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z]{3})[a-z]*\.? (\d{1,2}),? (\d{4})$/); if (!m) return null;
    var mi = MON.indexOf(m[1].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + m[1].slice(1, 3).toLowerCase()); if (mi < 0) return null;
    return m[3] + '-' + String(mi + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(m[2]).padStart(2, '0');
  }
  function fromExport(text) {
    var rows = parseCsv(text); if (rows.length < 2) return { err: 'The file has no rows.' };
    var h = rows[0].map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }), ix = function (n) { return h.indexOf(n); };
    var need = ['Name', 'User ID', 'Account type', 'Account created (UTC)', 'Days active'];
    var miss = need.filter(function (n) { return ix(n) < 0; }); if (miss.length) return { err: 'This does not look like Slack\u2019s Member Analytics export (missing ' + miss.join(', ') + ').' };
    var g = function (r, n) { var k = ix(n); return k < 0 ? '' : (r[k] || '').trim(); }, num = function (v) { var x = parseInt(String(v).replace(/[^\d]/g, ''), 10); return isNaN(x) ? 0 : x; };
    var out = rows.slice(1).map(function (r) {
      return { slack_id: g(r, 'User ID'), full_name: g(r, 'Name') || g(r, 'Display name'), display_name: g(r, 'Display name'), email: lower(g(r, 'Email')), title: g(r, 'What I Do'), account_type: g(r, 'Account type'),
        created_on: slackDay(g(r, 'Account created (UTC)')), claimed_on: slackDay(g(r, 'Claimed Date (UTC)')), deactivated_on: slackDay(g(r, 'Deactivated date (UTC)')),
        days_active: num(g(r, 'Days active')), messages: num(g(r, 'Messages posted')), channel_messages: num(g(r, 'Messages posted in channels')), reactions: num(g(r, 'Reactions added')) };
    }).filter(function (r) { return /^[UW][A-Z0-9]{6,}$/.test(r.slack_id) && r.full_name; });
    return { rows: out };
  }
  function windowFromName(name) {   /* "Welfare Avenue Member Analytics Aug 29 2025 - Sep 29 2026.csv" */
    var m = String(name || '').match(/([A-Za-z]{3}) (\d{1,2}),? (\d{4})\s*-\s*([A-Za-z]{3}) (\d{1,2}),? (\d{4})/); if (!m) return null;
    return { from: slackDay(m[1] + ' ' + m[2] + ', ' + m[3]), to: slackDay(m[4] + ' ' + m[5] + ', ' + m[6]) };
  }
  function openImport() {
    if (typeof openModal !== 'function') return; A.imp = null;
    openModal('<div class="w181sheet"><h3>Import a new Slack export</h3><p class="sub">In Slack: Tools and settings, Workspace settings, Analytics, Members, then Export to CSV (all members, the dates you want).       '<label class="f" for="w181file">The CSV file</label><input type="file" id="w181file" accept=".csv,text/csv">' +
      '<div id="w181impmore" style="display:none"><label class="f">The dates the export covers</label><input type="date" id="w181ifrom"> to <input type="date" id="w181ito"><p id="w181isum" style="font-size:13px;margin:10px 0 0"></p></div>' +
      '<p class="err" id="w181ierr"></p><div class="foot"><button class="w181btn ghost sm" data-w181="close">Cancel</button><button class="w181btn sm" data-w181="doimport" disabled>Import</button></div></div>');
  }
  function readImportFile(f) {
    var err = document.getElementById('w181ierr'), more = document.getElementById('w181impmore'), go = document.querySelector('[data-w181="doimport"]');
    if (!f) return; if (err) err.textContent = '';
    var rd = new FileReader();
    rd.onload = function () {
      var x = fromExport(rd.result); if (x.err) { if (err) err.textContent = x.err; if (go) go.disabled = true; return; }
      A.imp = x.rows; var w = windowFromName(f.name) || {};
      document.getElementById('w181ifrom').value = w.from || ''; document.getElementById('w181ito').value = w.to || '';
      var known = {}; A.accts.forEach(function (a) { known[a.slack_id] = 1; });
      var nw = x.rows.filter(function (r) { return !known[r.slack_id]; }).length;
      document.getElementById('w181isum').textContent = plural(x.rows.length, 'account') + ' in the file: ' + plural(nw, 'new one') + ', ' + (x.rows.length - nw) + ' already in the Hub.' + (w.from ? '' : ' Fill in the dates the export covers.');
      if (more) more.style.display = ''; if (go) go.disabled = false;
    };
    rd.onerror = function () { if (err) err.textContent = 'Could not read the file.'; };
    rd.readAsText(f);
  }
  async function doImport() {
    var err = document.getElementById('w181ierr'), go = document.querySelector('[data-w181="doimport"]');

      T('Imported: ' + (d['new'] || 0) + ' new, ' + (d.updated || 0) + ' updated, ' + plural(d.start_dates || 0, 'start date') + ' filled in');
      await load(true); paint();
    } catch (e) { if (err) err.textContent = 'Could not import: ' + ((e && e.message) || e); if (go) go.disabled = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- the tab in the HR centre ---------- */
  function setShown(on) {
    var h = document.getElementById('w181'); if (h) h.classList.toggle('on', !!on);

  }
  async function decorate() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body) return;
    var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs'); if (!tabs) return;
    if (!(await canSee())) return;
    tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs'); if (!tabs) return;

    var sub = String(location.hash || '').split('/')[1] || '';

  }
  var pend = null;
  function schedule() { clearTimeout(pend); pend = setTimeout(function () { try { decorate(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 160); }
  function watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel || panel.dataset.w181mo) return; panel.dataset.w181mo = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { var t = muts[i].target; if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#w181') || (muts[i].type === 'attributes' && t.closest('.w47tab')))) continue; schedule(); return; }
    }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    schedule();
  }
  if (typeof wa46Render === 'function') {

  }

  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre/analytics') === 0) { A.on = true; schedule(); } });

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {

    var tb = t.closest('#w46body .w47tab');
    if (tb) { A.on = tb.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'analytics'; setTimeout(function () { setShown(A.on); }, 0); return; }

  });

  /* ---------- the tour ---------- */
  function addTour() {

/* =====================================================================
   WA v190 The reading pass: trainings and onboarding that are pleasant to read and on brand (01/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (30/09/26): "Can you revist some of the trainings and onboarding to ensure everything is please to read and that it's
   well branded". Numbered 190: other sessions ship wave181 onwards tonight.

   This segment: the onboarding steps (the plan in the Avenue Academy, the HR centre's master and board, the onboarding window)
   read better. The step text is larger and darker, "To complete this step:" (or the older "To complete the task:", "To complete
   this task:", "Mini task:", "Your task:") becomes a gold box that says what to do, and a line written all in italics (how the
   step ticks itself) is a quiet note. wa37Md (wave37, the steps' Markdown) is wrapped: the wrapper only reshapes the paragraphs
   it returns, so every page that draws a step gets the same look.

   The Avenue Academy's own changes (the brand's faces, the gold rule, numbered steps and headings in readings) live in
   122_wave122; the words (the nine Hub modules, the master onboarding, the Slack imports and the ClickUp leftovers) live in the
   database (migration wa190_reading_pass, build/data/wa190_reading_pass.py).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa190) return; window.__wa190 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', MUTED = '#797773';
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var DO = /^<p>(?:<b>)?\s*(?:to complete (?:this|the) (?:step|task)|mini task|your task)\s*:?\s*(?:<\/b>)?\s*:?\s*([\s\S]*)<\/p>$/i;

  /* reshape what wa37Md returns: a step's to-do as a box, an all-italic line as a note */
  function decorate(html) {
    return String(html || '').replace(/<p>[\s\S]*?<\/p>/g, function (p) {
      var m = DO.exec(p);
      if (m && m[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()) return '<div class="w190do"><b>To complete this step</b><span>' + m[1].replace(/^\s+/, '').replace(/^[a-z]/, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }) + '</span></div>';
      if (p.indexOf('<p><i>') === 0 && p.indexOf('</i>') === p.length - 8) return '<p class="w190tk">' + p.slice(6, -8) + '</p>';
      return p;
    });
  }
  if (typeof window.wa37Md === 'function' && !window.wa37Md.__w190) {
    var base = window.wa37Md;
    var wrapped = function (s) { return decorate(base(s)); };
    wrapped.__w190 = true;
    window.wa37Md = wrapped;
  }

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa190css';
  css.textContent = ''
    + '.w37step .nm{font-size:14.5px;}'
    + '.w37step .ds{font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.55;color:#45443f;margin-top:3px;}'
    + '.w37step .ds p{margin:0 0 6px;}'
    + '.w37step .ds p:last-child{margin-bottom:0;}'
    + '.w37step .ds ul{margin:2px 0 8px;padding-left:18px;}'
    + '.w37step .ds li{margin:0 0 3px;}'
    + '.w37step .ds li::marker{color:' + GOLD + ';}'
    + '.w37step .ds a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w37step .ds .vl-h{font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:8px 0 4px;}'
    + '.w190do{margin:8px 0 6px;padding:8px 12px 9px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(220,165,39,.14);border-left:3px solid ' + GOLD + ';color:#3d3008;}'
    + '.w190do b{display:block;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:13px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8A5A0B;margin:0 0 1px;}'
    + '.w190do a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;}'
    + 'p.w190tk{font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:4px 0 0;}'
    + '.w37step.done .w190do{background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border-left-color:rgba(16,63,57,.22);color:' + MUTED + ';}'
    + '.w37step.done .w190do b{color:' + MUTED + ';}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  window.w190 = { decorate: decorate };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v191 the Volunteer Manager's flow (01/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (01/10/26): "our operations team needs the Volunteer manager to be easy to use and make each part of the event
   planning process as seamless as possible. There's a lot of information everywhere and I'd like to clean it up and
   better show the flow of our event planning process. Please do a full review of the volunteer manager to ensure it has
   Apple UI, but with Clickup's robustness and Welfare Avenue's Montreal operations processes in place."

   The review found two systems side by side: the checklist (the master SOP, 63 steps) still described the manual work
   (the Excel sheet, the Canva itinerary, the email drafts, the WhatsApp asks) while the Hub now does most of it (waves 171
   to 182), nothing ticked when it did, no step had ever been ticked on a real serving, past servings stayed "planned", no
   upcoming serving had its format, and the serving page was one long column of cards from eight waves.

   This wave puts the flow first. Montreal's six stages: Plan, Recruit, Confirm, Prepare, Day of, Wrap up.
   - The serving page: a calm header (date, countdown, format, the stage bar), Next up (the three steps that need a person
     now), then one section per stage, each hosting the cards and the steps that belong to it (the plan and the recipes in
     Plan, the roster board in Recruit, the Texts card in Confirm, the itinerary card in Prepare, the day's contacts in
     Day of, the survey and the recap in Wrap up). The current stage is open; the others fold to one line. Every card the
     earlier waves paint keeps its handlers: this wave only places them.
   - Steps the Hub does tick themselves (the database: wa191_autotick from wa191_signal; sop_template.hub_signal) and say
     so; a person's untick is never overridden. The SOP shows each step's stage, the Hub-done badge, and the Hub's
     proposals for outdated wording (approve or dismiss, leaders, HR, admins; wa191_sop_proposal).
   - The Calendar opens on This week (wa191_this_week): the servings by stage with their next step, overdue steps, steps
     with no owner, and the signed-in person's own steps.
   - Finished servings close themselves the day after (wa191_close_past, cron wa-serving-flow).
   - Retired from the page: the Canva itinerary link, "Build the driver and team leader itinerary" and "Check Quo replies"
     (the Hub's itineraries and the Texts card replaced them). The page's title names the view. "Add an event" shows on
     the calendar only (106 patched). Buttons share one style.
   The database decides who may do what (staff read; leaders, HR and admins decide proposals); sms_mode and mail_mode are
   never switched here; nothing is sent by this wave.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa191) return; window.__wa191 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773';

  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var STAGES = [
    { k: 'plan', l: 'Plan', s: 'The date, the format, the meals and the plan', when: 'More than two weeks out' },
    { k: 'recruit', l: 'Recruit', s: 'Fill the roster for every role', when: 'Two weeks out' },
    { k: 'confirm', l: 'Confirm', s: 'Everyone confirms by text', when: 'Seven days out' },
    { k: 'prepare', l: 'Prepare', s: 'Itineraries, the WhatsApp group, the kit', when: 'The days before' },
    { k: 'day_of', l: 'Day of', s: 'The serving', when: 'The day' },
    { k: 'wrap_up', l: 'Wrap up', s: 'The survey, the thanks, the numbers', when: 'After' }
  ];
  var STAGE_IX = {}; STAGES.forEach(function (s, i) { STAGE_IX[s.k] = i; });
  var FORMATS = { home_cooked: 'Home-cooked', community_kitchen: 'Community kitchen' };
  var ROLE_ORDER = ['team_leader', 'cook', 'driver', 'server', 'dishwasher', 'photographer', 'videographer', 'community_debrief'];   /* wave239: videographer */
  var S = { ev: null, evAt: 0, guides: null, week: null, weekAt: 0, weekFor: null, open: {}, showAll: false, pend: null, laid: null, ticking: false, ticked: {}, sopPend: null };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function todayIso() { try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()); } catch (e) { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function daysTo(iso) { var a = new Date(todayIso() + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime(), b = new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime(); return Math.round((b - a) / 86400000); }
  function fmtDay(iso, long) { if (!iso) return ''; try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', long ? { timeZone: 'UTC', weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' } : { timeZone: 'UTC', weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso); } }
  function fmtDue(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var d = daysTo(iso); if (d === 0) return 'today'; if (d === 1) return 'tomorrow'; if (d === -1) return 'yesterday'; if (d < 0) return fmtDay(iso); if (d < 7) return fmtDay(iso).split(',')[0]; return fmtDay(iso); }
  function hm12(s) { var m = String(s || '').match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); if (!m) return ''; var h = Number(m[1]); return (h % 12 || 12) + ':' + m[2] + (h < 12 ? ' am' : ' pm'); }
  function roleLabel(r) { try { return typeof waVolRoleLabel === 'function' ? waVolRoleLabel(r) : r; } catch (e) { return r; } }
  function one(r) { var ONE = { team_leader: 'Team leader', cook: 'Cook', driver: 'Driver', server: 'Server', dishwasher: 'Dishwasher', photographer: 'Photographer', videographer: 'Videographer', community_talker: 'Community lead', community_debrief: 'Community lead' }; return ONE[r] || String(r || 'volunteer').replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/^./, function (m) { return m.toUpperCase(); }); }
  function isConf(r) { return lower(r.status) === 'confirmed' || r.confirm_status === 'confirmed'; }
  function isDecl(r) { return lower(r.status) === 'unavailable' || r.confirm_status === 'declined'; }
  function isOff(r) { return lower(r.status) === 'cancelled'; }
  function curEv() { var ev = null; try { (WA_VOL_EVENTS || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return ev; }
  function curD() { try { return (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function tmpl() { try { return WA_VOL_TMPL || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function stageOf(date, status) {
    if (status === 'cancelled') return 'cancelled'; if (status === 'completed') return 'done';
    var d = daysTo(date);
    if (d < 0) return 'wrap_up'; if (d === 0) return 'day_of'; if (d <= 2) return 'prepare'; if (d <= 7) return 'confirm'; if (d <= 14) return 'recruit'; return 'plan';
  }
  function stageLabel(k) { var s = STAGES[STAGE_IX[k]]; return s ? s.l : k === 'done' ? 'Done' : k === 'cancelled' ? 'Cancelled' : ''; }
  function countdown(ev) { var d = daysTo(ev.event_date); if (ev.status === 'cancelled') return 'Cancelled'; if (ev.status === 'completed') return 'Done'; if (d === 0) return 'Today'; if (d === 1) return 'Tomorrow'; if (d > 1) return d + ' days to go'; if (d === -1) return 'Yesterday'; return Math.abs(d) + ' days ago'; }
