      '.w191rec .flt input[type=search]{flex:1 1 100%;}.w191rec .flt select{flex:1 1 40%;}.w191rec .cmp{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w191rec .cmp .prev{position:static;max-height:none;}' +
      '.w191rec .srv{padding:8px 12px 6px;}.w191rec .sr .d{gap:0 8px;}.w191rec .sr .d span{flex-basis:100%;}.w191rec .seg.sm button{padding:5px 8px;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(rcss);
  /* ---------- wave191m: the run (Prepare): where each driver goes, in order, with the times and Google Maps ----------
     Steel (01/10/26): the driver's itinerary and start time with the team leader "will always depend on how many cooks to
     pick up from" (3 to 6 cooks, the storage unit, the serving; after it the dishes at the Old Brewery Mission and back to the
     storage unit), and drivers need "a working google maps trajectory link of a starting point, all the stops on the way and
     how long in order to calculate their starting time". The serving-run edge function works the run out (the faster of the
     storage unit first and the cooks first, OpenStreetMap driving times) and stores it (volunteer_event_runs); the itineraries
     carry each person's part. This card shows it to the team: each driver's start, every stop with its time and the drive
     before it, the Maps link, their itinerary (a test link), after the serving, and what needs a look (a cook with no
     address, an address the map placed roughly). It works the run out again when the stops changed (the cron does too,
     every 15 minutes) or on Work it out again. Staff only (wa191_run_get). */
  var RUN = { by: {}, at: {}, busy: {}, err: {}, tried: {}, seen: {} };
  window.WA191RUN = { get: function (id) { var x = RUN.by[id]; return x && x.plan ? x.plan : null; } };
  var RUN_KIND = { both: 'Only driver', main: 'Main driver', pickup: 'Pickup driver', none: 'No driver yet' };
  function runUpcoming(ev) { return ev && ev.status !== 'cancelled' && ev.status !== 'completed' && daysTo(ev.event_date) >= 0; }
  function fmtAt(iso) { try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }).replace(/\./g, ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  async function loadRun(ev, force) {
    if (!ev || !staff()) return null;
    if (!force && RUN.by[ev.id] && Date.now() - (RUN.at[ev.id] || 0) < 30000) return RUN.by[ev.id];
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa191_run_get', { p_event: ev.id });
      if (r && r.error) RUN.err[ev.id] = 'Could not load the run: ' + String(r.error.message || r.error).slice(0, 120);
      else if (r && r.data) { RUN.by[ev.id] = r.data; RUN.err[ev.id] = RUN.err[ev.id] && /^Could not load/.test(RUN.err[ev.id]) ? null : RUN.err[ev.id]; }
    } catch (e) { RUN.err[ev.id] = 'Could not load the run'; }
    RUN.at[ev.id] = Date.now();
    return RUN.by[ev.id] || null;
  }
  async function planRun(ev, force) {
    if (!ev || RUN.busy[ev.id]) return;
    RUN.busy[ev.id] = true; RUN.tried[ev.id] = Date.now(); paintRun();
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('serving-run', { body: { action: 'plan', event_id: ev.id, force: !!force } });
      var d = r && r.data;
      if (r && r.error) RUN.err[ev.id] = 'Could not work it out: ' + String(r.error.message || r.error).slice(0, 120);
      else if (d && d.error) RUN.err[ev.id] = String(d.error).slice(0, 160);
      else RUN.err[ev.id] = null;
    } catch (e) { RUN.err[ev.id] = 'Could not work it out: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120); }
    RUN.busy[ev.id] = false;
    await loadRun(ev, true);
    paintRun();
  }
  function runWarn(w) {
    var n = w.name || 'A cook';
    if (w.why === 'no address') return n + ': no pickup address yet. Their page asks for it; or set them to drop off.';
    if (w.why === 'address not found on the map') return n + ': the map could not find their address. Check it on their card.';
    if (w.why === 'placed by its postal code') return n + ': placed by the postal code only, so their time is approximate.';
    if (w.why === 'placed on its street, not the house') return n + ': placed on the street, not the house number, so their time may be a few minutes off.';
    return String(w.why || '');
  }
  function runCard(r, byId, tl) {
    var names = (r.drivers || []).map(function (id) { var x = byId[id]; return x ? x.person_name : null; }).filter(Boolean);
    if (!names.length) names = (r.names || []).filter(Boolean);
    var part = RUN_KIND[r.kind] || 'Driver';
    var withTl = r.with_tl ? (tl ? ', with ' + (tl.person_name || 'the team leader') + ' (team leader)' : ', with the team leader (none on the roster yet)') : '';
    var stops = r.stops || [];
    var first = stops[0] || {};
    var where = r.at === 'storage' ? 'at the storage unit' : r.at === 'serving' ? 'at the serving' : first.k === 'kitchen' ? 'at the kitchen' : first.label ? 'at ' + first.label + '\u2019s' : 'at the first pickup';
    var h = '<div class="rn" data-w191runkind="' + E(r.kind) + '"><div class="rh"><div class="rw"><b>' + E(names.length ? names.join(', ') : 'No driver yet') + '</b><span>' + E(part + withTl) + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="rt"><b>' + E(hm12(r.start)) + '</b><span>' + E('starts ' + where) + '</span></div></div>';
    h += '<ol class="rs">' + stops.map(function (s, i) {
      var label = s.k === 'storage' ? 'Storage unit' : s.k === 'serving' ? 'The serving' : s.k === 'kitchen' ? 'The kitchen' : (s.label || 'Cook');
      var meta = [];
      if (i && s.drive_min) meta.push(E(s.drive_min + ' min drive'));
      if (s.leave) meta.push(E('leaves ' + hm12(s.leave)));
      if (s.approx) meta.push('<em>placed roughly on the map</em>');
      return '<li><span class="t">' + E(hm12(s.arrive)) + '</span><span class="p"><b>' + E(label) + '</b>' + (s.hood ? ' <i>' + E(s.hood) + '</i>' : '')
        + (s.k !== 'serving' && s.address ? '<small>' + E(s.address) + '</small>' : '') + (meta.length ? '<small>' + meta.join(' \u00b7 ') + '</small>' : '') + '</span></li>';
    }).join('') + '</ol>';
    var tok = (r.drivers || []).map(function (id) { return byId[id] && byId[id].confirm_token; }).filter(Boolean)[0] || (r.with_tl && tl && tl.confirm_token) || '';
    var foot = (stops.length > 1 ? '<span class="rl">' + E((r.total_min || 0) + ' min in all, ' + (r.drive_min || 0) + ' driving') + '</span>' : '')
      + (stops.length > 1 && /^https:\/\//.test(r.maps_url || '') ? '<a class="w191btn ghost" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="' + E(r.maps_url) + '">Open in Google Maps</a>' : '')
      + (tok ? '<a class="w191btn quiet" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="https://hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + E(tok) + '?test=1&it=1">Their itinerary</a>' : '');
    return h + (foot ? '<div class="ra">' + foot + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function afterCard(p, byId, tl) {
    var a = p.after; if (!a || !(a.stops || []).length) return '';
    var r = (p.runs || []).filter(function (x) { return x.with_storage; })[0];
    var names = r ? (r.drivers || []).map(function (id) { var x = byId[id]; return x ? x.person_name : null; }).filter(Boolean) : [];
    var who = (names.length ? names.join(', ') : 'The driver') + (tl ? ' and ' + (tl.person_name || 'the team leader') : ' and the team leader');
    var li = function (t, b, sm) { return '<li><span class="t">' + E(hm12(t)) + '</span><span class="p"><b>' + E(b) + '</b>' + (sm ? '<small>' + E(sm) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></li>'; };
    var rows = a.stops.map(function (s) {
      if (s.k === 'serving') return li(s.leave, 'Leave the serving', 'after packing up');
      if (s.k === 'dish') return li(s.arrive, 'Old Brewery Mission: the dishes', s.drive_min + ' min drive \u00b7 until ' + hm12(s.leave));
      return li(s.arrive, 'Storage unit: put away, then the inventory check', s.drive_min + ' min drive \u00b7 done ' + hm12(s.leave));
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="rn after"><div class="rh"><div class="rw"><b>After the serving</b><span>' + E(who) + '</span></div><div class="rt"><b>' + E(hm12(a.end)) + '</b><span>back and locked up</span></div></div>'
      + '<ol class="rs">' + rows + '</ol>' + (/^https:\/\//.test(a.maps_url || '') ? '<div class="ra"><a class="w191btn ghost" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="' + E(a.maps_url) + '">Open in Google Maps</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function runHtml(ev, D, x) {
    var busy = !!RUN.busy[ev.id], err = RUN.err[ev.id], up = runUpcoming(ev);
    var p = x && x.plan;
    var btn = up ? '<button class="w191btn ghost" data-w191run="again"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (busy ? 'Working it out\u2026' : 'Work it out again') + '</button>' : '';
    var h = '<h4>The run</h4>';
    if (!p) {
      return h + '<p class="w191rsub">' + (busy ? 'Working out the drivers\u2019 run from the cooks\u2019 addresses\u2026' : err ? E(err) : up ? 'The Hub works out the drivers\u2019 run from the cooks\u2019 addresses and the drivers\u2019 parts.' : 'No run was worked out for this serving.') + '</p>'
        + (btn && !busy ? '<div class="w191line">' + btn.replace('Work it out again', 'Work it out') + '</div>' : '');
    }
    var byId = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { byId[r.id] = r; });
    var tl = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return lower(r.role) === 'team_leader' && !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r); })[0] || null;
    var other = p.order === 'cooks_first' ? 'storage_first' : 'cooks_first';
    var pills = [];
    if (p.order) pills.push('<span class="pill">' + E((p.order === 'cooks_first' ? 'Cooks first, then the storage unit' : 'The storage unit first, then the cooks') + (p.compared && p.compared[other] != null ? ': ' + p.compared[p.order] + ' min, against ' + p.compared[other] + ' the other way' : '')) + '</span>');
    if (p.arrive_by) pills.push('<span class="pill">Everyone at the serving by ' + E(hm12(p.arrive_by)) + '</span>');
    if (x.stale) pills.push('<span class="pill short">' + (busy ? 'The stops changed: working it out again' : 'The stops changed since it was worked out') + '</span>');
    else pills.push('<span class="pill ok">Up to date' + (x.computed_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtAt(x.computed_at)) : '') + '</span>');
    if (p.source === 'estimate') pills.push('<span class="pill short">The map service did not answer: the times are estimates</span>');
    var f = p.minutes && p.minutes.factor ? Math.round((Number(p.minutes.factor) - 1) * 100) : 0;
    h += '<p class="w191rsub">Who drives where, in what order, and when to leave. Driving times from OpenStreetMap' + (f > 0 ? ', plus ' + f + '% for traffic' : '') + '. Each driver and the team leader see their part, with the Google Maps link, on their itinerary.</p>'
      + '<div class="w191line">' + pills.join('') + btn + '</div>';
    if (err && !busy) h += '<p class="w191rerr">' + E(err) + '</p>';
    var w = p.warnings || [];
    if (w.length) h += '<div class="w191runw"><b>Needs a look</b>' + w.map(function (y) { return '<div>' + E(runWarn(y)) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (!tl) h += '<div class="w191runw"><b>No team leader yet</b><div>The run has a seat for them: add a team leader in The team.</div></div>';
    h += '<div class="w191runs">' + (p.runs || []).map(function (r) { return runCard(r, byId, tl); }).join('') + afterCard(p, byId, tl) + '</div>';
    return h;
  }
  /* each driver's start for The day card */
  function runStartFor(evId, rid, role) {
    var x = RUN.by[evId]; var p = x && x.plan; if (!p) return '';
    var r = (p.runs || []).filter(function (y) { return role === 'team_leader' ? y.with_tl : (y.drivers || []).indexOf(rid) >= 0; })[0];
    return r ? r.start : '';
  }
  function runBox() {
    var b = stageBody('prepare'); if (!b) return null;
    var box = document.getElementById('w191run');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w191run'; box.className = 'cardy w191run'; }
    var it = b.querySelector(':scope > #w174card');
    if (it) { if (it.nextElementSibling !== box) it.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box); }
    else if (box.parentNode !== b) b.insertBefore(box, stepsBox('prepare'));
    return box;
  }
  async function paintRun() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D || !root() || !staff()) return;
    if (ev.kind && ev.kind !== 'service') return;
    var x = await loadRun(ev, false);
    if (curEv() !== ev || !root()) return;
    var box = runBox(); if (!box) return;
    var html = runHtml(ev, D, x);
    if (box.__h !== html) { box.innerHTML = html; box.__h = html; }
    /* the day card and the itinerary card follow a new run */
    var stamp = x && x.plan ? String(x.computed_at || x.plan.computed_at || '') : '';
    if (stamp && RUN.seen[ev.id] !== stamp) {
      RUN.seen[ev.id] = stamp;
      var row = S.ev && S.ev.id === ev.id ? S.ev : null;
      var dc = root().querySelector('.w191stage[data-stage="day_of"] .w191own'); if (dc) dc.innerHTML = dayCard(ev, D, row);
      var c4 = document.getElementById('w174card'); if (c4) c4.__h = '';
      try { if (window.w171 && typeof w171.paintW174 === 'function') w171.paintW174(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    if (runUpcoming(ev) && x && (!x.plan || x.stale) && !RUN.busy[ev.id] && Date.now() - (RUN.tried[ev.id] || 0) > 60000) planRun(ev, false);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w191run]'); if (!b || !b.closest('#w191run')) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (b.getAttribute('data-w191run') === 'again') planRun(curEv(), true);
  }, true);

  /* ---------- wave191n: the team leader's storage check (Wrap up), and the last check on the Inventory ----------
     After the serving the team leader checks the storage unit on their page (volunteer_inventory_submit): whether everything
     is back in its place, and each item all back, running low or missing; the Inventory's Reorder notes follow. Wrap up shows
     the check and what to restock; before it is in, what to expect and the team leader's link as a test. Staff read. */
  var INVC = { by: {}, at: {}, last: null, lastAt: 0 };
  async function loadInvCheck(ev, force) {
    if (!ev || !staff()) return null;
    if (!force && INVC.at[ev.id] && Date.now() - INVC.at[ev.id] < 30000) return INVC.by[ev.id];
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_inventory_checks').select('*').eq('event_id', ev.id).maybeSingle(); if (r && !r.error) INVC.by[ev.id] = r.data || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    INVC.at[ev.id] = Date.now();
    return INVC.by[ev.id] || null;
  }
  function invCardHtml(ev, D, c) {
    var tl = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return lower(r.role) === 'team_leader' && !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r); })[0] || null;
    var d = daysTo(ev.event_date);
    var h = '<h4>Storage check</h4>';
    var acts = '<div class="w191line">' + (tl && tl.confirm_token ? '<a class="w191btn ghost" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="https://hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + E(tl.confirm_token) + '?test=1#inv">' + (c ? 'Their check (test link)' : 'The team leader\u2019s check (test link)') + '</a>' : '') + '<button class="w191btn quiet" data-w191go="inventory">Open the Inventory</button></div>';
    if (!c) {
      h += '<p class="w191rsub">' + (d <= 0 && ev.status !== 'cancelled'
        ? 'The team leader\u2019s check is not in yet. It opens on their page when the serving starts, for three days: whether everything is back in its place, and what is running low or missing.'
        : 'After the serving the team leader checks the storage unit on their page: whether everything is back in its place, and what is running low or missing. What to restock shows here and in the Inventory.') + '</p>';
      return h + acts;
    }
    var items = c.items || [];
    var lo = items.filter(function (x) { return x.state === 'low'; }), mi = items.filter(function (x) { return x.state === 'missing'; });
    h += '<p class="w191rsub">' + E((c.by_name || 'The team leader') + ' checked the storage unit' + (c.updated_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + fmtAt(c.updated_at) : '')) + '</p>'
      + '<div class="w191line">' + (c.in_place ? '<span class="pill ok">Everything back in its place</span>' : '<span class="pill short">Not everything back in its place</span>')
      + '<span class="pill' + (lo.length ? ' short' : ' ok') + '">' + lo.length + ' running low</span><span class="pill' + (mi.length ? ' short' : ' ok') + '">' + mi.length + ' missing</span></div>';
    var row = function (x, k) { return '<div><span class="tg ' + k + '">' + (k === 'mi' ? 'Missing' : 'Running low') + '</span><b>' + E(x.item || '') + '</b>' + (x.unit ? ' <i>' + E(x.unit) + '</i>' : '') + (x.category ? ' <i>\u00b7 ' + E(x.category) + '</i>' : '') + '</div>'; };
    h += lo.length || mi.length ? '<div class="w191restock"><b>To restock</b>' + mi.map(function (x) { return row(x, 'mi'); }).join('') + lo.map(function (x) { return row(x, 'lo'); }).join('') + '</div>'
      : '<p class="w191rsub" style="margin-top:8px">Nothing to restock.</p>';
    if (c.note) h += '<div class="w191ctx"><b>Their note</b><span>' + E(c.note) + '</span></div>';
    return h + acts;
  }
  function invBox() {
    var b = stageBody('wrap_up'); if (!b) return null;
    var box = document.getElementById('w191inv');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w191inv'; box.className = 'cardy w191run w191inv'; }
    var sv = b.querySelector(':scope > #w174survey');
    if (sv) { if (sv.nextElementSibling !== box) sv.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box); }
    else if (box.parentNode !== b) b.insertBefore(box, stepsBox('wrap_up'));
    return box;
  }
  async function paintInv() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D || !root() || !staff()) return;
    if (ev.kind && ev.kind !== 'service') return;
    var c = await loadInvCheck(ev, false);
    if (curEv() !== ev || !root()) return;
    var box = invBox(); if (!box) return;
    var html = invCardHtml(ev, D, c);
    if (box.__h !== html) { box.innerHTML = html; box.__h = html; }
  }
  /* the Inventory view: the last check, above the table */
  async function invBanner() {
    try {
      if (!staff() || typeof WA_VOL_VIEW === 'undefined' || WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'inventory') return;
      var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
      var wrap = sec.querySelector('.vl-tblwrap'); if (!wrap) return;
      if (!INVC.lastAt || Date.now() - INVC.lastAt > 30000) {
        INVC.lastAt = Date.now();
        var r = await db.from('volunteer_inventory_checks').select('event_id,by_name,updated_at,low,missing,in_place').order('updated_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1);
        INVC.last = r && r.data && r.data[0] ? r.data[0] : null;
      }
      var x = INVC.last; var el = document.getElementById('w191invlast');
      if (!x) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
      var ev = null; try { (WA_VOL_EVENTS || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === x.event_id) ev = e; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      var html = '<b>Last storage check</b><span>' + E((x.by_name || 'The team leader') + (ev ? ', after the serving of ' + fmtDay(ev.event_date) : '') + ' \u00b7 ' + fmtAt(x.updated_at)) + '</span>'
        + '<span>' + E(x.low + ' running low, ' + x.missing + ' missing' + (x.in_place ? ', everything back in its place' : ', not everything back in its place')) + '. Their flags are in the Reorder column.</span>';
      if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w191invlast'; el.className = 'w191ctx'; }
      if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
      var anchor = sec.querySelector('#w63bar') || wrap;
      if (el.nextElementSibling !== anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', el);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* ---------- wave191o: a serving with a partner organization (Plan) ----------
     Steel (01/10/26): collaborators like Oberoi Financial Group "work differently": is their group bringing the food, how
     many are there, which roles do we still fill (cooks, servers, drivers, photographers), "a team leader and community lead
     on our end always need to be there", the kitchen model with DESTA or their own space; their people by "headcount and
     coordinator or each person by name - allow our team to choose", the form route so "we get more people in to our system".
     The card in Plan shows the collaboration (wa191_partner_save keeps it in volunteer_event_partners); the editor sets it.
     What they cover counts toward each role on the serving (the header, Next up, Recruit, the team), so Find volunteers and
     Plan the roster fill only the gap; signed-up people from their form count as themselves. */
  var PART = { by: {}, at: {}, list: null };
  var FOOD = {
    ours: ['Our cooks', 'Our home cooks make the food, as usual.'],
    they_bring: ['They bring the food', 'Their group brings the meals ready to serve: no cooks or pickups from us.'],
    desta_kitchen: ['They cook with us in DESTA\u2019s kitchen', 'Community kitchen at DESTA: their people cook, our driver picks the food up there.'],
    their_kitchen: ['They cook in their own space', 'Their people cook at their place: our driver picks the food up there.']
  };
  var PROLES = [['server', 'Servers'], ['cook', 'Cooks'], ['driver', 'Drivers'], ['photographer', 'Photographers'], ['dishwasher', 'Dishwashing']];
  function partnerOf(evId) { var x = PART.by[evId]; return x && x.active ? x : null; }
  async function loadPartner(ev, force) {
    if (!ev || !staff()) return null;
    if (!force && PART.at[ev.id] && Date.now() - PART.at[ev.id] < 30000) return partnerOf(ev.id);
    try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_event_partners').select('*').eq('event_id', ev.id).maybeSingle(); if (r && !r.error) PART.by[ev.id] = r.data || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    PART.at[ev.id] = Date.now();
    return partnerOf(ev.id);
  }
  /* what we still bring, role by role: what they cover comes off our part; our team leader and community lead are always
     ours; when they make the food we bring no cooks, and when they bring it ready, one driver for the equipment */
  function partnerOurs(evId, base) {
    var P = partnerOf(evId); if (!P) return null;
    var out = Object.assign({}, base); var cov = P.covers || {};
    Object.keys(out).forEach(function (r) { if (r === 'team_leader' || r === 'community_debrief') return; out[r] = Math.max(0, (out[r] || 0) - (cov[r] || 0)); });
    if (P.food && P.food !== 'ours') out.cook = 0;
    if (P.food === 'they_bring' && (out.driver || 0) > 1) out.driver = 1;
    return out;
  }
  /* Plan the roster counts everyone on the list (their people signed up by name too): those come back onto the target */
  function partnerTargets(evId, base, roster) {
    var out = partnerOurs(evId, base); if (!out) return null;
    var P = partnerOf(evId), cov = P.covers || {}, signed = {};
    (roster || []).forEach(function (r) { if ((r.detail || {}).source === 'partner form' && !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r)) signed[r.role] = (signed[r.role] || 0) + 1; });
    Object.keys(out).forEach(function (r) { if (r === 'team_leader' || r === 'community_debrief') return; out[r] += Math.min(signed[r] || 0, cov[r] || 0); });
    return out;
  }
  window.WA191TARGETS = function (evId, base) { var D = curD(); var b2 = withSpots(evId, base); return partnerTargets(evId, b2, D && D.id === evId ? D.roster : []) || (b2 !== base ? b2 : null); };   /* wave232: the serving's own spot counts first */
  function coversText(P) {
    var c = P.covers || {}; var bits = PROLES.filter(function (x) { return c[x[0]]; }).map(function (x) { return c[x[0]] + ' ' + (c[x[0]] === 1 ? x[1].replace(/s$/, '').replace(/^Dishwashing$/, 'dishwasher').toLowerCase() : x[1].toLowerCase()); });
    return bits.join(', ');
  }
  function partnerCardHtml(ev, D, P) {
    var signed = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return (r.detail || {}).source === 'partner form' && !isOff(r) && !isDecl(r); });
    var base = targets(ev.id); var T = partnerOurs(ev.id, base) || base;
    var ours = ROLE_ORDER.filter(function (r) { return (T[r] || 0) > 0; }).map(function (r) { return (T[r] > 1 ? T[r] + ' ' : '') + one(r).toLowerCase() + (T[r] > 1 ? 's' : ''); });
    var link = P.route === 'everyone' && P.signup_token ? 'https://hub.welfareavenue.com/c/' + P.signup_token : '';
    var h = '<h4>Collaboration</h4><div class="w191pt"><div class="nm"><b>' + E(P.partner_name) + '</b>' + (P.headcount ? '<span>' + E(P.headcount + ' people') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<dl><dt>Food</dt><dd>' + E((FOOD[P.food] || FOOD.ours)[0]) + (P.kitchen_place && (P.food === 'desta_kitchen' || P.food === 'their_kitchen') ? ' <i>\u00b7 ' + E(P.kitchen_place) + '</i>' : '') + '</dd>'
      + '<dt>They cover</dt><dd>' + (coversText(P) ? E(coversText(P)) : '<i>no roles yet</i>') + '</dd>'
      + '<dt>We bring</dt><dd>' + E(ours.join(', ') || 'nobody else') + ' <i>(our team leader and community lead, always)</i></dd>'
      + '<dt>Their people</dt><dd>' + (P.route === 'everyone' ? 'Everyone signs up with the form \u00b7 <b>' + signed.length + '</b> signed up so far' : 'A coordinator gives us the headcount')
      + (P.coordinator_name || P.coordinator_email || P.coordinator_phone ? '<br><span class="ct">' + E([P.coordinator_name, P.coordinator_email, P.coordinator_phone].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</dd>'
      + (P.note ? '<dt>Note</dt><dd>' + E(P.note) + '</dd>' : '') + '</dl></div>'
      + '<div class="w191line">' + (staff() ? '<button class="w191btn ghost" data-w191go="partner">Edit the collaboration</button>' : '')
      + (link ? '<button class="w191btn quiet" data-w191pcopy="' + E(link) + '">Copy the form link</button><a class="w191btn quiet" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="' + E(link) + '?test=1">See the form</a>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (link && !P.signup_open ? '<p class="w191rsub" style="margin-top:6px">The form is closed to new sign-ups.</p>' : '');
    return h;
  }
  function partnerBox() {
    var b = stageBody('plan'); if (!b) return null;
    var box = document.getElementById('w191partner');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w191partner'; box.className = 'cardy w191run w191partner'; }
    var own = b.querySelector(':scope > .w191own');
    if (own) { if (own.nextElementSibling !== box) own.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box); }
    else if (box.parentNode !== b) b.insertBefore(box, b.firstChild);
    return box;
  }
  async function paintPartner() {
    var ev = curEv(), D = curD(); if (!ev || !D || !root() || !staff()) return;
    if (ev.kind && ev.kind !== 'service') return;
    var had = !!partnerOf(ev.id);
    var P = await loadPartner(ev, false);
    if (curEv() !== ev || !root()) return;
    var box = document.getElementById('w191partner');
    if (!P) { if (box) box.remove(); if (had) refresh(false); return; }
    box = partnerBox(); if (!box) return;
    var html = partnerCardHtml(ev, D, P);
    if (box.__h !== html) { box.innerHTML = html; box.__h = html; }
    if (!had) refresh(false);
  }
  async function partnerList() {
    if (PART.list) return PART.list;
    try { var r = await db.from('wa_partners').select('id,name,category,status').order('name'); PART.list = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'inactive'; }); } catch (e) { PART.list = []; }
    return PART.list;
  }
  async function partnerEditor() {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev || !staff()) return;
    var P = await loadPartner(ev, true) || {}; var list = await partnerList();
    var c = P.covers || {}; var food = P.food || 'ours'; var route = P.route || 'coordinator';
    var known = P.partner_id && list.some(function (x) { return x.id === P.partner_id; });
    var opts = '<option value="">Choose the organization\u2026</option>' + list.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (P.partner_id === x.id ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option value="other"' + (P.partner_name && !known ? ' selected' : '') + '>Another organization (type its name)</option>';
    var html = '<div class="w171m w191pm"><h3>Collaboration</h3><p class="sub">' + E(fmtDay(ev.event_date, true)) + ' \u00b7 a partner organization serves with us. Our team leader and community lead are always ours.</p>'
      + '<label class="f">The organization</label><select id="w191pp">' + opts + '</select>'
      + '<input type="text" id="w191pn" placeholder="The organization\u2019s name" value="' + E(P.partner_name && !known ? P.partner_name : '') + '" style="margin-top:6px' + (P.partner_name && !known ? '' : ';display:none') + '">'
      + '<label class="f">The food</label><div class="modes">' + Object.keys(FOOD).map(function (k) { return '<label class="' + (food === k ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w191pf" value="' + k + '"' + (food === k ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>' + E(FOOD[k][0]) + '</b><small>' + E(FOOD[k][1]) + '</small></span></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div id="w191pkw"' + (food === 'desta_kitchen' || food === 'their_kitchen' ? '' : ' style="display:none"') + '><label class="f">The kitchen\u2019s address (the drivers\u2019 run picks the food up there)</label><input type="text" id="w191pk" placeholder="Street address, Montr\u00e9al" value="' + E(P.kitchen_place || ev.kitchen_place || '') + '"></div>'
      + '<label class="f">Their people</label><div class="modes">'
      + '<label class="' + (route === 'coordinator' ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w191pr" value="coordinator"' + (route === 'coordinator' ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>A coordinator gives us a headcount</b><small>One contact for their group; we count their people by role.</small></span></label>'
      + '<label class="' + (route === 'everyone' ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w191pr" value="everyone"' + (route === 'everyone' ? ' checked' : '') + '><span><b>Everyone signs up with the form</b><small>Each person gives their name and contact: they join our volunteer list and get their own page. You get a link to share.</small></span></label></div>'
      + '<div class="two3"><div><label class="f">Coordinator</label><input type="text" id="w191pcn" placeholder="Name" value="' + E(P.coordinator_name || '') + '"></div><div><label class="f">Email</label><input type="text" id="w191pce" placeholder="name@company.com" value="' + E(P.coordinator_email || '') + '"></div><div><label class="f">Phone</label><input type="text" id="w191pcp" placeholder="514-555-0100" value="' + E(P.coordinator_phone || '') + '"></div></div>'
      + '<label class="f">How many of theirs, by role (they cover these; we fill the rest)</label><div class="w191pnum">' + PROLES.map(function (x) { return '<label><span>' + E(x[1]) + '</span><input type="number" min="0" max="100" data-w191pc="' + x[0] + '" value="' + E(c[x[0]] || '') + '" placeholder="0"></label>'; }).join('')
      + '<label><span>In all</span><input type="number" min="0" max="500" id="w191ph" value="' + E(P.headcount == null ? '' : P.headcount) + '" placeholder="people"></label></div>'
      + '<label class="f">Note (for the team)</label><textarea id="w191pnote" placeholder="Anything the team should know">' + E(P.note || '') + '</textarea>'
      + (P.route === 'everyone' ? '<label class="row" style="border:none"><input type="checkbox" id="w191pso"' + (P.signup_open === false ? '' : ' checked') + '> The form is open to new sign-ups</label>' : '')
      + '<div class="warn" id="w191perr" style="display:none"></div>'
      + '<div class="foot">' + (P.event_id && P.active ? '<button class="w191btn ghost" data-w191pend="1" style="margin-right:auto">No longer a collaboration</button>' : '') + '<button class="w191btn ghost" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="w191btn gold" data-w191psave="1">Save the collaboration</button></div></div>';
    openModal(html);
  }
  async function partnerSave(btn, end) {
    var ev = curEv(); if (!ev) return;
    var q = function (id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); return el ? el.value : ''; };
    var p;
    if (end) p = { active: false };
    else {
      var sel = q('w191pp'); var covers = {};
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-w191pc]'), function (el) { var v = String(el.value || '').trim(); if (v !== '') covers[el.getAttribute('data-w191pc')] = Number(v); });
      var f = document.querySelector('input[name="w191pf"]:checked'), r = document.querySelector('input[name="w191pr"]:checked');
      var so = document.getElementById('w191pso');
      p = { partner_id: sel && sel !== 'other' ? Number(sel) : null, partner_name: sel === 'other' ? q('w191pn') : '', food: f ? f.value : 'ours', kitchen_place: q('w191pk'), route: r ? r.value : 'coordinator',
        coordinator_name: q('w191pcn'), coordinator_email: q('w191pce'), coordinator_phone: q('w191pcp'), headcount: q('w191ph') === '' ? null : Number(q('w191ph')), covers: covers, note: q('w191pnote'), signup_open: so ? so.checked : true };
    }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var res = await db.rpc('wa191_partner_save', { p_event: ev.id, p: p });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      PART.by[ev.id] = res.data && res.data.active === false ? Object.assign({}, PART.by[ev.id] || {}, { active: false }) : res.data; PART.at[ev.id] = Date.now();
      if (!end && (p.food === 'desta_kitchen' || p.food === 'their_kitchen')) { ev.format = 'community_kitchen'; try { await loadEvRow(ev, true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      closeModal(); T(end ? 'No longer a collaboration' : 'The collaboration is saved');
      var box = document.getElementById('w191partner'); if (box) box.__h = '';
      refresh(false); paintPartner();
    } catch (e) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      var w = document.getElementById('w191perr'); var msg = String((e && e.message) || e).replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').slice(0, 200);
      if (w) { w.style.display = ''; w.textContent = msg; } else T('Could not save: ' + msg);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w191pm')) return;
    if (t.id === 'w191pp') { var n = document.getElementById('w191pn'); if (n) n.style.display = t.value === 'other' ? '' : 'none'; }
    if (t.name === 'w191pf') { var kw = document.getElementById('w191pkw'); if (kw) kw.style.display = t.value === 'desta_kitchen' || t.value === 'their_kitchen' ? '' : 'none'; }
    if (t.name === 'w191pf' || t.name === 'w191pr') { var grp = t.closest('.modes'); if (grp) grp.querySelectorAll('label').forEach(function (l) { l.classList.toggle('on', !!l.querySelector('input:checked')); }); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var s = t.closest('[data-w191psave]'); if (s) { e.preventDefault(); partnerSave(s, false); return; }
    var x = t.closest('[data-w191pend]'); if (x) { e.preventDefault(); partnerSave(x, true); return; }
    var c = t.closest('[data-w191pcopy]'); if (c) { e.preventDefault(); var link = c.getAttribute('data-w191pcopy'); try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(function () { T('The form link is copied'); }, function () { T(link); }); } catch (err) { T(link); } }
  }, true);
  /* ---------- wave191p: choose your servings in the onboarding ----------
     Steel (01/10/26): "in the onboarding, I want onboardees to view the next upcoming volunteering events and select 1 or more
     so that they have confirmed date and to communicate with the operations manager"; volunteering is "required 4 times a
     year". Week 3's lesson (a 'servings' block, wave122) and Week 3's page in the onboarding journey (wave167) carry a
     placeholder (.w191onb); this fills it: how many servings this fiscal year of the four, the next six servings (the ones
     they are on already say so), what they would like to do, a message for the operations lead, Confirm my servings
     (wa191_onb_signup: Confirmed on each, a Request to Operations Montreal with the dates and the message, their onboarding
     step ticked). Team members only. */
  var ONB = { data: null, at: 0, busy: false, err: '', done: null, pick: {}, role: 'server', msg: '' };
  var ONB_ROLES = [['server', 'Serve', 'Set up, serve the meal, pack up'], ['photographer', 'Take photos', 'Capture the day for our stories']];
  function onbFmt(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'UTC', weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso); } }
  function onbMonth(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: 'UTC', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso); } }
  async function onbLoad(force) {
    if (!force && ONB.data && Date.now() - ONB.at < 30000) return ONB.data;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa191_onb_servings'); if (r && r.error) { ONB.err = /Team members only/.test(r.error.message || '') ? 'team' : 'load'; } else if (r) { ONB.data = r.data; ONB.err = ''; } } catch (e) { ONB.err = 'load'; }
    ONB.at = Date.now();
    return ONB.data;
  }
  function onbHtml() {
    var x = ONB.data;
    if (ONB.err === 'team') return '<p class="w191om">Choosing servings here is for team members. Your lead can put you on one.</p>';
    if (!x) return '<p class="w191om">' + (ONB.err ? 'The servings could not load. Try again in a moment.' : 'Loading the next servings\u2026') + '</p>';
    var ops = x.ops && x.ops.name ? x.ops.name : 'the operations team', opsT = x.ops && x.ops.title ? x.ops.title : 'Operations, Montreal';
    var yr = x.year || { done: 0, target: 4 }, pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * (yr.done || 0) / (yr.target || 4)));
    var h = '<div class="yr"><div><b>Your servings this year</b><small>' + E(onbMonth(yr.from) + ' to ' + onbMonth(yr.to)) + '</small></div><span class="n"><b>' + (yr.done || 0) + '</b> of ' + (yr.target || 4) + '</span><i><s style="width:' + pct + '%"></s></i></div>';
    if (ONB.done) {
      return h + '<div class="okb"><b>You are confirmed for ' + E(ONB.done.dates || '') + '.</b><span>' + E(ops) + ' has your message in the Operations Montreal inbox' + (ONB.done.step ? ', and your step Mandatory volunteering is done.' : '.') + ' Thank you for showing up for our community.</span></div>'
        + '<button class="w191btn ghost" data-w191onb="more">Choose more servings</button>';
    }
    var sv = x.servings || [];
    if (!sv.length) return h + '<p class="w191om">No servings are open right now. ' + E(ops) + ' adds the next ones soon; come back to this page.</p>';
    h += '<div class="sl">' + sv.map(function (s) {
      var mine = s.mine && s.mine.confirmed; var on = !!ONB.pick[s.id];
      return '<label class="sv' + (mine ? ' mine' : on ? ' on' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-w191onbev="' + s.id + '"' + (mine ? ' checked disabled' : on ? ' checked' : '') + '>'
        + '<span class="d"><b>' + E(onbFmt(s.date)) + '</b><small>' + E((s.start ? hm12(s.start) + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + (s.location || 'Montreal') + (s.partner ? ' \u00b7 with ' + s.partner : '') + (s.title && !s.partner ? ' \u00b7 ' + s.title : '')) + '</small>'
        + (mine ? '<span class="st">You are on it</span>' : '') + '</span></label>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="lbl">What would you like to do?</div><div class="roles">' + ONB_ROLES.map(function (r) { return '<label class="' + (ONB.role === r[0] ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w191onbrole" value="' + r[0] + '"' + (ONB.role === r[0] ? ' checked' : '') + '><b>' + E(r[1]) + '</b><small>' + E(r[2]) + '</small></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="lbl">A message for ' + E(ops) + ' <span>(' + E(opsT) + ', optional)</span></div><textarea data-w191onbmsg="1" maxlength="1500" placeholder="Hi ' + E(ops) + ', I am ' + E((x.me && x.me.name) || '') + ', new on the team. Anything I should know before my first serving?">' + E(ONB.msg || '') + '</textarea>'
      + (ONB.err && ONB.err !== 'load' ? '<div class="er">' + E(ONB.err) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="ac"><button class="w191btn gold" data-w191onb="go"' + (ONB.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (ONB.busy ? 'Confirming\u2026' : 'Confirm my servings') + '</button><span>They are confirmed right away, and ' + E(ops) + ' gets your message with the dates.</span></div>';
    return h;
  }
  function onbPaint() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.w191onb').forEach(function (el) {
      var ta = el.querySelector('[data-w191onbmsg]'); if (ta && document.activeElement === ta) return;   /* never under the cursor */
      var html = onbHtml(); if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
      el.classList.add('on');
    });
  }
  async function onbFill() {
    var els = document.querySelectorAll('.w191onb:not(.on)'); if (!els.length) return;
    els.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add('on'); el.innerHTML = onbHtml(); el.__h = el.innerHTML; });
    await onbLoad(false); onbPaint();
  }
  async function onbGo() {
    if (ONB.busy) return;
    var ids = Object.keys(ONB.pick).filter(function (k) { return ONB.pick[k]; }).map(Number);
    if (!ids.length) { ONB.err = 'Choose at least one serving.'; onbPaint(); return; }
    ONB.busy = true; ONB.err = ''; onbPaint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa191_onb_signup', { p_events: ids, p_role: ONB.role, p_message: ONB.msg || '' });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      ONB.done = { dates: (r.data && r.data.dates) || '', step: !!(r.data && r.data.step) }; ONB.pick = {}; ONB.msg = '';
      T('You are confirmed. Thank you!');
      await onbLoad(true);
    } catch (e) { ONB.err = String((e && e.message) || e).replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, '').slice(0, 200) || 'Could not confirm. Try again.'; }
    ONB.busy = false; onbPaint();
  }
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.w191onb')) return;
    if (t.getAttribute('data-w191onbev')) { ONB.pick[t.getAttribute('data-w191onbev')] = t.checked; var l = t.closest('label'); if (l) l.classList.toggle('on', t.checked); if (ONB.err) { ONB.err = ''; onbPaint(); } }
    if (t.name === 'w191onbrole') { ONB.role = t.value; t.closest('.roles').querySelectorAll('label').forEach(function (l) { l.classList.toggle('on', !!l.querySelector('input:checked')); }); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w191onbmsg')) ONB.msg = t.value; });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('.w191onb [data-w191onb]'); if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (b.getAttribute('data-w191onb') === 'go') onbGo();
    if (b.getAttribute('data-w191onb') === 'more') { ONB.done = null; onbPaint(); }
  }, true);
  try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (ONB.pend) return; ONB.pend = setTimeout(function () { ONB.pend = null; if (document.querySelector('.w191onb:not(.on)')) onbFill(); }, 150); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  /* ---------- the page's title names the view (inside the Volunteer Manager) ---------- */
  var TITLES = { calendar: ['Servings', 'This week and every upcoming serving'], sop: ['The SOP', 'The master checklist every serving starts from, in six stages'], people: ['Volunteers', 'Everyone who volunteers in Montreal, one record with the CRM'], inventory: ['Inventory', 'The kit in storage, counted and reordered'], recipes: ['Recipes', 'What the cooks make'], event: ['Serving', ''],
    /* wave259: the places wave195, 209, 256 and 259 added, so the title never falls back to Servings */
    applicants: ['Applicants received', 'Volunteers who applied, waiting for a reply'], signoffs: ['Sign-offs', 'Letters and forms volunteers ask us to sign'], phone: ['Messages', 'The ops line: every conversation, group texts and calls'], setup: ['Setup', 'The SOP, recipes, inventory, the usual spot counts, role owners and the text schedule'] };
  function title() {
    try {
      if (!document.body.classList.contains('w153volunteer')) return;
      var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
      var mast = sec.querySelector('.w118mast'); if (!mast) return;
      var h1 = mast.querySelector('h1'), p = mast.querySelector(':scope > p');
      var v = typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_VIEW : 'calendar'; var t = TITLES[v] || TITLES.calendar;
      var ev = v === 'event' ? curEv() : null;
      var h = ev ? 'Serving' : t[0]; var sub = ev ? [fmtDay(ev.event_date, true), ev.code, ev.title].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') : t[1];
      if (h1 && h1.textContent !== h) h1.textContent = h;
      if (p && p.textContent !== sub) p.textContent = sub;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  var __sched191 = schedule;
  schedule = function () { __sched191(); setTimeout(title, 100); setTimeout(title, 700); };
  if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') { var __vr191 = waVolRender; waVolRender = async function () { var out = await __vr191.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(title, 120); setTimeout(title, 900); setTimeout(focusStep, 1200); setTimeout(invBanner, 400); setTimeout(invBanner, 1500); return out; }; }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(title, 300); });
  setTimeout(title, 1500);

  /* the page tour: the cards of the earlier waves now sit in stages, so their steps open the stage first; the masthead pill is gone */
  function fixTour() {
    try {
      var TT = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; var vs = TT && TT.volunteering && TT.volunteering.steps; if (!vs || vs.__w191) return;
      var by = {}; vs.forEach(function (x) { by[x.s] = x; });
      var stage = function (k) { return function () { openStage(k, false); }; };
      var ord = ['#volunteering > .w118mast', '#w191week', '#w106add', '#w119cal', '#w145home', '#volunteering .vl-grid', '#w191head', '#w191next', '#w191team', '#w191stages'];
      var out = ord.map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).filter(Boolean);
      out.push({ s: '#w171card', t: 'Confirm: the texts', p: 'Volunteers get a text from the ops line 7 days before the serving with their own link to confirm. When they tap Confirm (or reply YES), their card turns confirmed within half a minute. Replies that need a person show here.', opt: 1, go: stage('confirm') });
      out.push({ s: '#w174card', t: 'Prepare: the itinerary', p: 'When the serving starts and when each role arrives, a preview of each role\u2019s itinerary, and sending the itineraries by text or email.', opt: 1, go: stage('prepare') });
      out.push({ s: '#w174survey', t: 'Wrap up: the survey', p: 'What volunteers said after the serving: overall, the needs people told us about, what slowed things down, the meal, and every answer in full.', opt: 1, go: stage('wrap_up') });
      if (by['#w145fb']) { var fb = Object.assign({}, by['#w145fb'], { opt: 1, go: stage('wrap_up') }); out.push(fb); }
      out.push({ s: '#w191stages [data-w191go="find"]', t: 'Recruit: Find volunteers', p: 'Looks through the CRM for who signed up and never came, regulars we miss, who came once and who loved it. Tick people, read the texts each one gets, and invite them: they go on the roster as Waiting For Response and get a personal text from the ops line between 9 am and 7 pm. A YES by text or a tap on their link confirms them. Replies show in Conversations.', opt: 1, go: stage('recruit') });
      if (by['#w191extra']) out.push(by['#w191extra']);
      /* any step another wave adds later keeps its place at the end */
      vs.forEach(function (x) { if (out.indexOf(x) < 0 && ['#w171card', '#w174card', '#w119 .mast .w171pill', '#w145fb'].indexOf(x.s) < 0) out.push(x); });
      vs.length = 0; out.forEach(function (x) { vs.push(x); }); vs.__w191 = true;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  setTimeout(fixTour, 1800); setTimeout(fixTour, 5000);

  window.w191 = { S: S, STAGES: STAGES, model: model, layout: layout, refresh: refresh, stageOf: stageOf, paintWeek: paintWeek, decorateSop: decorateSop, title: title, adoptRail: adoptRail, openRecruit: openRecruit, R: R, counts: rosterCounts };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v192 The Loop: I'm in, close the loop (01/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (01/10/26): "I'm questioning whether our checkins should be in our slack channels for everyone to see, I
   really don't want people to feel like they are tracked but more that work that's being done is shared to keep
   everyone in the loop. Also when people check out, it might be helpful to track their clicks in the hub in order to
   help give them a summary of what they did, the voice to text is likely also very helpful. [...] redo the onboarding
   module for check-in-out and instead position it differently, brand it, make it easy, and rethink its processes,
   values and intended purpose."
   His choices (01/10/26): the name The Loop (I'm in to start, Close the loop to finish, #the-loop for everyone); a
   wrap-up goes to the person's team channel by default, or to everyone or just the Hub, their choice each time; the
   shared post carries only what moved (no clock time, no hours); the note drafts itself from the records the person
   changed (checkin-post draft, loop_trail), never from clicks or pages. Hours stay with the person and HR.
   - The header button is #w192go. Wave116j's #w116jck stays underneath, hidden: it still owns the session row (its
     toggle inserts the row; I'm in uses it) and the presence ring. Clicks on #w192go, #w116jck and the person page's
     link are taken at the window, before wave116q's handlers, so its sheet never opens.
   - I'm in starts a session (wave212: a line in #check-in-out-wa says so). Close the loop opens the wrap-up sheet: What moved (lines the Hub drafted
     from the person's own records; private work never pre-filled), Anything the Hub could not see (say it or type it,
     Add to the note folds it in through the model and names the tasks worth closing), the tasks to tick, Where it goes
     (#check-in-out-wa since wave214; until then Who sees it: My team, Everyone, Just the Hub), Close the loop or Close
     without a note. The minutes show to the person only.
   - Home: In the loop, the week's notes from everyone, read through loop_feed() (no times, no hours). The person page:
     Their loop (Your loop on your own). Hours on someone else's page are for HR, leaders and admins (wave116k).
   - The walkthrough: the page tour "loop" (the Academy row tour:loop). It opens the sheet in preview (nothing read or
     written) to show each part, and the preview closes with the tour. The chrome tour's step lives in 122.
   - window.w192: start, open, openRow (wave118b's "as of" close), preview, endPreview, feed, state.
   wave212 (06/10/26, checkin-post v5): I'm in posts a line in #check-in-out-wa ("is in the loop", never the time).
   wave214 (06/10/26), Steel: "All closed loops should be done in the check in out channel"; asked where a note goes, he
   chose only #check-in-out-wa. Who sees it is gone: the sheet's Where it goes names the channel (app_config
   checkinout_channel, or the draft's everyone_channel), every note posts there (checkin-post v6 ignores share_to), and
   Close without a note posts the one line "closed the loop" there. The tour and the button's words say so.
   ===================================================================== */
