  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; }
  function me() { var em = myEmail(); return members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function ck() { return window.w116jCk || null; }
  function fmtMins(m) { m = Math.max(0, Math.round(m)); var h = Math.floor(m / 60), r = m % 60; return h ? h + 'h ' + (r < 10 ? '0' : '') + r + 'm' : r + 'm'; }
  function teamLabel(d) {
    if (!d) return '';
    try { if (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) return WA_TEAMS.label[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) return WA_TEAMS.labels[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) return DEPT_LABEL[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return d === 'exec' ? 'Executive team' : d;
  }
  function teamColor(d) { try { if (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.color && WA_TEAMS.color[d]) return WA_TEAMS.color[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { if (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) return DEPT_COLOR[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return GREEN; }
  /* a day key (YYYY-MM-DD, Toronto) and how it reads: Today, Yesterday, Mon, Sep 29 */
  function dayKey(d) { return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { timeZone: TZ }); }
  function dayLabel(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    var key = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(v)) ? String(v) : dayKey(new Date(v));
    var now = new Date(), yest = new Date(now.getTime() - 864e5);
    if (key === dayKey(now)) return 'Today';
    if (key === dayKey(yest)) return 'Yesterday';
    return new Date(key + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' }).replace(/\./g, '');
  }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed'); }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error && r.data.ok !== true) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }
  function SR() { return window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition || null; }
  /* wave214: every closed loop goes to the check-in-out channel (app_config checkinout_channel; the draft's everyone_channel) */
  function loopName() { return Q.allCh || Q.loopName || 'check-in-out-wa'; }
  (async function () { try { var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'checkinout_channel').maybeSingle(); var v = r && r.data && String(r.data.value || '').replace(/^#/, '').trim(); if (v) Q.loopName = v; } catch (e) { /* default */ } })();
  function onPerson() { return (/^#person\/(\d+)/.exec(location.hash || '') || [])[1] || null; }
  function repaintPerson() { var id = onPerson(); if (id && typeof window.w116Person === 'function') { try { window.w116Person(id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }

  /* ---------- the button (repainted only when what it says changes) ---------- */
  function mount() {
    if (!staff()) return null;
    var pill = document.querySelector('.wa-toolbar .pill.w115pill'); if (!pill) return null;
    var b = document.getElementById('w192go');
    if (!b) { b = document.createElement('button'); b.id = 'w192go'; b.type = 'button'; pill.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', b); }
    return b;
  }
  function render() {
    var b = mount(); if (!b) return;
    var c = ck(); var open = c && c.open();
    var cls, html, title, label;
    if (open) {
      var mins = (Date.now() - new Date(open.started_at).getTime()) / 60000;
      cls = 'in'; html = '<span class="dot"></span>Close the loop <span class="t">&middot; ' + E(fmtMins(mins)) + '</span>';
      title = 'You are in. Your time (' + fmtMins(mins) + ') is for your eyes only. Press to close the loop and share what moved.';
      label = 'Close the loop';
    } else {
      cls = ''; html = '<span class="dot"></span>I’m in';
      title = 'Starting work for Welfare Avenue? Press I’m in. A line in #' + loopName() + ' says you are in (never the time); the ring on your photo shows teammates you are around.';
      label = 'I’m in';
    }
    if (b.className !== cls) b.className = cls;
    if (b.__h !== html) { b.innerHTML = html; b.__h = html; }
    if (b.title !== title) b.title = title;
    if (b.getAttribute('aria-label') !== label) b.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
  }
  setInterval(render, 2000);
  render(); setTimeout(render, 800); setTimeout(render, 2500);

  /* ---------- I'm in: wave116j's toggle inserts the row; checkin-post start posts "is in the loop" in the check-in-out channel (wave212) ---------- */
  async function start() {
    var c = ck(); if (!c || Q.busy || c.open()) return; Q.busy = true;
    try {
      await c.toggle();
      var row = c.open();
      render(); try { c.presence(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (row) {
        toastS('You’re in. Close the loop when you finish.');
        if (row.id) { try { fn('checkin-post', { action: 'start', checkin_id: row.id }); } catch (e) { /* the session runs either way */ } }
        repaintPerson();
      }
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not start your session: ' + (e.message || e)); }
    Q.busy = false;
  }

  /* ---------- Close the loop: the sheet ---------- */
  function stopRec() { try { if (Q.rec) Q.rec.stop(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } Q.listening = false; var b = document.getElementById('w192mic'); if (b) { b.classList.remove('on'); b.innerHTML = '<span class="ic"></span>Talk instead'; } }
  function startRec() {
    var R = SR(); if (!R) return;
    try {
      var rec = new R(); rec.lang = (navigator.language && /^fr/i.test(navigator.language)) ? 'fr-CA' : 'en-CA'; rec.continuous = true; rec.interimResults = true;
      var ta = document.getElementById('w192say'); Q.finalText = ta ? ta.value : '';
      rec.onresult = function (ev) {
        var interim = '';
        for (var i = ev.resultIndex; i < ev.results.length; i++) { var t = ev.results[i][0].transcript; if (ev.results[i].isFinal) Q.finalText = (Q.finalText + ' ' + t).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); else interim += t; }
        var box = document.getElementById('w192say'); if (box) { box.value = (Q.finalText + (interim ? ' ' + interim : '')).trim(); box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight; }
      };
      rec.onerror = function (ev) { stopRec(); var h = document.getElementById('w192hint'); if (h) h.textContent = ev.error === 'not-allowed' ? 'The browser blocked the microphone. Allow it in the address bar, or type instead.' : 'Listening stopped (' + (ev.error || 'error') + '). Type or try again.'; };
      rec.onend = function () { if (Q.listening) { try { rec.start(); } catch (e) { stopRec(); } } };
      rec.start(); Q.rec = rec; Q.listening = true;
      var b = document.getElementById('w192mic'); if (b) { b.classList.add('on'); b.innerHTML = '<span class="ic"></span>Listening&hellip; tap to stop'; }
    } catch (e) { stopRec(); }
  }
  function readBullets() { var out = []; document.querySelectorAll('[data-w192b]').forEach(function (i) { var v = i.value.trim(); if (v) out.push(v.slice(0, 220)); }); return out; }
  function readPicked() { var out = []; document.querySelectorAll('[data-w192t]').forEach(function (i) { if (i.checked) out.push(Number(i.getAttribute('data-w192t'))); }); return out; }
  /* what is on the sheet now, kept before a repaint */
  function keep() {
    if (!document.getElementById('w192sheet')) return;
    var raw = []; document.querySelectorAll('[data-w192b]').forEach(function (i) { raw.push(i.value); }); Q.bullets = raw;
    Q.picked = {}; readPicked().forEach(function (id) { Q.picked[id] = true; });
    var ta = document.getElementById('w192say'); if (ta) Q.text = ta.value;
  }
  function sheetHtml() {
    var bul = Q.bullets.map(function (b, i) { return '<div class="b"><span class="dot"></span><input type="text" data-w192b="' + i + '" value="' + E(b) + '" aria-label="Line ' + (i + 1) + ' of your note"><button type="button" class="rm" data-w192rm="' + i + '" title="Take this line out" aria-label="Take this line out">&#10005;</button></div>'; }).join('');
    var draft = !Q.loaded ? '<p class="empty">Reading what moved…</p>'
      : (Q.bullets.length ? '<div class="bul" id="w192bul">' + bul + '</div>' : '<p class="empty">Nothing on record for this session yet. Say or type what you got done below.</p>');
    var hidden = Q.hidden ? '<p class="quiet">' + Q.hidden + (Q.hidden === 1 ? ' private item is' : ' private items are') + ' left out (confidential, board or one-on-one work). Add a line yourself if it should be shared.</p>' : '';
    var sug = Q.suggest.map(function (s) { return '<label class="sug"><input type="checkbox" data-w192t="' + s.id + '"' + (Q.picked[s.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(s.title) + '</b>' + (s.why ? '<span>' + E(s.why) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></label>'; }).join('');
    var oth = Q.others.map(function (t) { return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w192t="' + t.id + '"' + (Q.picked[t.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b>' + (t.owner ? '<span>' + E(t.owner) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></label>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w113modal w192m" id="w192sheet" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="w192h"><h3 id="w192h">Close the loop' + (Q.preview ? '<span class="pv">Walkthrough</span>' : '') + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub"><span class="hrs">' + E(fmtMins(Q.minutes)) + '</span> on the Hub, for your eyes only. Here is what moved while you were in: keep what matters, add what the Hub could not see, then share.</p>' +
      '<div id="w192draftbox"><h4>What moved <span class="k">From your records</span></h4>' + draft + hidden +
      '<button type="button" class="addl" id="w192add">+ add a line</button></div>' +
      '<div id="w192saybox"><h4>Anything the Hub could not see?</h4>' +
      '<textarea id="w192say" aria-label="Anything the Hub could not see" placeholder="e.g. met Amanda about the flyers, she prints Friday; called the shelter back about Sunday">' + E(Q.text || '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<div class="sayrow">' + (SR() ? '<button type="button" class="mic" id="w192mic"><span class="ic"></span>Talk instead</button>' : '') + '<button type="button" class="addb" id="w192tidy">Add to the note</button><span class="quiet" id="w192hint">' + (SR() ? 'Talking works in Chrome, Edge and Safari. Say it the way you would tell a teammate.' : 'This browser cannot listen; typing works the same.') + '</span></div></div>' +
      (Q.suggest.length ? '<h4>Finished these? Tick to close them</h4><div class="tk" id="w192sug">' + sug + '</div>' : '') +
      (Q.others.length ? '<button type="button" class="more" id="w192more">' + (Q.suggest.length ? 'Close other tasks too' : 'Close a task you finished') + '</button><div class="tk" id="w192oth" style="display:none;margin-top:8px">' + oth + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div id="w192sharebox"><h4>Where it goes</h4><p class="where">#' + E(loopName()) + ' <small>&middot; in Slack, for the whole team</small></p>' +
      '<p class="quiet">Every closed loop goes there, with no clock time and no hours. Every note also shows on Home, in the loop.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w113acts"><span class="w113ok" id="w192ok"></span><span style="flex:1"></span><button type="button" class="skip" id="w192skip">Close without a note</button><button class="btn primary" id="w192close">Close the loop</button></div></div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var sig = sheetHtml();
    if (!document.getElementById('w192sheet') || Q.sig !== sig) { openModal(sig); Q.sig = sig; }
  }
  function begin(row) {
    Q.row = row; Q.preview = false; Q.bullets = []; Q.suggest = []; Q.others = []; Q.picked = {}; Q.finalText = ''; Q.text = ''; Q.hidden = 0; Q.loaded = false;
    Q.teamCh = null; Q.allCh = null; Q.touched = false; Q.share = 'everyone';   /* wave214: one place for every note */
    var endMs = row.ended_at ? new Date(row.ended_at).getTime() : Date.now();
    Q.minutes = Math.max(1, Math.round((endMs - new Date(row.started_at).getTime()) / 60000));
  }
  async function openSheet(row) {
    begin(row); paint();
    try {
      var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'draft', checkin_id: row.id });
      if (Q.row !== row || Q.preview) return;
      keep();
      var mine = Q.bullets.map(function (b) { return String(b || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var drafted = (r.bullets || []).slice();
      Q.bullets = drafted.concat(mine.filter(function (b) { return drafted.indexOf(b) < 0; }));
      Q.hidden = Number(r.hidden || 0); Q.minutes = r.minutes || Q.minutes;
      Q.others = (r.open_tasks || []).slice();
      Q.allCh = r.everyone_channel || null;
      Q.loaded = true; paint();
    } catch (e) {
      if (Q.row !== row) return;
      keep(); Q.loaded = true; Q.bullets = Q.bullets.filter(function (b) { return String(b || '').trim(); }); paint();
      var ok = document.getElementById('w192ok'); if (ok) ok.textContent = 'The Hub could not read what moved (' + (e.message || e) + '). Say or type it instead.';
    }
  }
  async function tidy() {
    if (Q.preview) return;
    keep();
    var text = String(Q.text || '').trim();
    var ok = document.getElementById('w192ok');
    if (text.length < 3) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Say or type a line first, then Add to the note.'; return; }
    stopRec();
    if (Q.busy) return; Q.busy = true; if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Folding that into your note…';
    var btn = document.getElementById('w192tidy'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'tidy', checkin_id: Q.row ? Q.row.id : null, text: text, bullets: readBullets() });
      Q.bullets = (r.bullets || []).slice(); Q.suggest = r.suggest || [];
      Q.others = (r.open_tasks || []).filter(function (t) { return !Q.suggest.some(function (s) { return s.id === t.id; }); });
      Q.suggest.forEach(function (s) { Q.picked[s.id] = true; });
      Q.text = ''; Q.finalText = ''; Q.loaded = true; Q.busy = false; paint();
      return;
    } catch (e) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Could not fold that in (' + (e.message || e) + '). Add it as a line instead.'; if (btn) btn.disabled = false; }
    Q.busy = false;
  }
  async function finish(skipped) {
    var c = ck(); if (!c || !Q.row || Q.busy || Q.preview) return;
    keep(); stopRec();
    var bullets = skipped ? [] : readBullets(), closeIds = skipped ? [] : readPicked(), share = skipped ? 'hub' : 'everyone';
    if (!skipped && String(Q.text || '').trim().length >= 3) bullets.push(String(Q.text).trim().slice(0, 220));   /* words typed but not folded in still go */
    var ok = document.getElementById('w192ok');
    if (!skipped && !bullets.length) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Add a line, or close without a note.'; return; }
    Q.busy = true;
    if (ok) ok.textContent = skipped ? 'Closing…' : 'Sharing…';
    var go = document.getElementById('w192close'); if (go) go.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'close', checkin_id: Q.row.id, bullets: bullets, close_task_ids: closeIds, share_to: share, skipped: !!skipped });
      c.clear(); closeModal(); Q.sig = '';
      var where = skipped ? (r.posted ? 'Loop closed. #' + r.channel + ' shows you closed it, with no note.' : 'Loop closed, no note shared.')
        : r.posted ? 'Loop closed and shared in #' + r.channel + '.'
        : 'Loop closed. Your note is on Home; Slack did not take it (' + (r.error || 'unknown') + ').';
      var n = (r.closed_ids || []).length;
      toastS(where + (n ? ' ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' task closed.' : ' tasks closed.') : ''));
      try {
        var shut = (r.closed_ids || []).map(String);
        if (shut.length && typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' && DB_TASKS) { var nowIso = new Date().toISOString(); DB_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { if (shut.indexOf(String(t.id)) >= 0) { t.done = true; t.closed_at = t.closed_at || nowIso; } }); if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function' && document.getElementById('mpBody')) renderMyPage(); }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      Q.row = null; render(); try { c.presence(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      F.at = 0; injectFeed(true); repaintPerson();
    } catch (e) {
      if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Could not close the loop: ' + (e.message || e);
      if (go) go.disabled = false;
    }
    Q.busy = false;
  }

  /* ---------- the walkthrough's preview of the sheet (nothing is read or written) ---------- */
  function preview() {
    begin({ id: 0, started_at: new Date(Date.now() - 95 * 60000).toISOString() });
    Q.preview = true; Q.loaded = true; Q.hidden = 1; Q.share = 'everyone';
    Q.bullets = ['Closed: Book the kitchen for Sunday; Send the volunteer reminder', 'Commented on: Flyers for the gift basket drive', 'Joined: Operations Montreal meeting'];
    Q.suggest = [{ id: -1, title: 'Confirm the drivers for Sunday', why: 'you said the drivers are confirmed' }]; Q.picked = { '-1': true }; Q.others = [];
    paint();
  }
  function endPreview() { if (!Q.preview) return; Q.preview = false; Q.row = null; Q.sig = ''; try { if (document.getElementById('w192sheet')) closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  /* the tour leaving (Finish, Esc or the x) closes the preview with it */
  try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (Q.preview && !document.querySelector('.w122tp')) endPreview(); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- wiring: taken at the window, before wave116q's document handlers ---------- */
  window.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('#w192go') || t.closest('#w116jck') || t.closest('[data-w116ka="ck"]');
    if (!b) return;
    var c = ck(); if (!c) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if (c.busy() || Q.busy) return;
    if (Q.preview) endPreview();
    if (c.open()) openSheet(c.open()); else start();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !document.getElementById('w192sheet')) return;
    if (t.closest('#w192mic')) { if (Q.preview) return; if (Q.listening) stopRec(); else startRec(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w192tidy')) { tidy(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w192skip')) { if (Q.preview) { endPreview(); return; } finish(true); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w192close')) { if (Q.preview) { endPreview(); return; } finish(false); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w192add')) { keep(); Q.bullets.push(''); Q.loaded = true; paint(); var last = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w192b]'); if (last.length) last[last.length - 1].focus(); return; }
    var rm = t.closest('[data-w192rm]'); if (rm) { keep(); Q.bullets.splice(Number(rm.getAttribute('data-w192rm')), 1); paint(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w192more')) { var o = document.getElementById('w192oth'); if (o) o.style.display = o.style.display === 'none' ? '' : 'none'; return; }
  });

  /* ---------- the shared notes: loop_feed() returns what moved, never a time or hours ---------- */
  async function feedRows(days, email) {
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('loop_feed', email ? { p_days: days, p_email: email } : { p_days: days });
      if (r && !r.error && Array.isArray(r.data)) return r.data;
    } catch (e) { /* the table, for an older database */ }
    try {
      var q = db.from('hub_checkins').select('id, email, name, department, ended_at, summary, share_to');
      if (email) q = q.eq('email', lower(email));
      var t = await q.order('ended_at', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
      return ((t && t.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.ended_at && x.summary && Array.isArray(x.summary.bullets) && x.summary.bullets.length; })
        .map(function (x) { return { id: x.id, email: x.email, name: x.name, department: x.department, day: dayKey(new Date(x.ended_at)), bullets: x.summary.bullets, closed: (x.summary.closed || []).length, closed_titles: [], share_to: x.share_to }; });
    } catch (e) { return []; }
  }
  async function loadFeed(force) {
    if (!force && F.rows && Date.now() - F.at < 3 * 60000) return F.rows;
    if (F.p) return F.p;
    F.p = (async function () { var rows = await feedRows(7, null); F.rows = rows; F.at = Date.now(); F.p = null; return rows; })();
    return F.p;
  }
  function avatar(p) {
    try { if (window.w116Avatar) { var h = window.w116Avatar(p, 30, ''); if (h) return h; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var ini = String(p.name || '?').trim().split(/\s+/).map(function (x) { return x.charAt(0); }).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
    return '<span class="w192av">' + (p.avatar_url ? '<img src="' + E(p.avatar_url) + '" alt="">' : E(ini)) + '</span>';
  }
  function bulletsOf(x) { var b = x.bullets; if (typeof b === 'string') { try { b = JSON.parse(b); } catch (e) { b = []; } } return Array.isArray(b) ? b : []; }
  function feedHtml(rows, all) {
    if (!rows) return '<p class="mp-empty">Loading the loop…</p>';
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="mp-empty">No notes this week yet. Press I’m in when you start and Close the loop when you finish: your note shows here.</p>';
    var shown = all ? rows : rows.slice(0, 5);
    return '<div class="w192l">' + shown.map(function (x) {
      var em = lower(x.email); var m = members().filter(function (y) { return lower(y.email) === em; })[0] || { name: x.name || em, email: em };
      var dept = x.department || m.department;
      var titles = Array.isArray(x.closed_titles) ? x.closed_titles : [];
      var n = Number(x.closed || 0);
      return '<div class="w192e">' + avatar(m) + '<div><div class="who"><b>' + E(m.name || x.name || em) + '</b>' + (dept ? '<span class="pl" style="background:' + E(teamColor(dept)) + '">' + E(teamLabel(dept)) + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="d">' + E(dayLabel(x.day)) + '</span></div>' +
        '<ul>' + bulletsOf(x).slice(0, 6).map(function (b) { return '<li>' + E(b) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' +
        (n ? '<div class="cl">Closed ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') + (titles.length ? ': ' + E(titles.slice(0, 3).join(', ')) : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' + (rows.length > shown.length ? '<button type="button" class="w192more" id="w192feedmore">Show all ' + rows.length + ' from this week</button>' : '');
  }
  function drawFeed() { var s = document.getElementById('w192feed'); if (!s) return; s.innerHTML = '<h3>In the loop</h3><p class="w192sub">What moved this week, from everyone’s notes. Times and hours stay with each person.</p>' + feedHtml(F.rows, s.__all); }
  function injectFeed(force) {
    var page = document.getElementById('mypage'); if (!page || !staff()) return;
    var sec = document.getElementById('w192feed');
    if (!sec) {
      sec = document.createElement('div'); sec.className = 'mp-sec'; sec.id = 'w192feed';
      var anchor = document.getElementById('w116home') || page.querySelector('.mp-online') || page.querySelector('.mp-chips') || page.querySelector('.mp-head');
      if (anchor && anchor.parentNode) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sec);
      else { var grid = page.querySelector('.mp-grid'); var col = grid ? (grid.querySelector('.w74col') || grid) : null; if (!col) return; col.insertBefore(sec, col.firstChild); }
    }
    drawFeed();
    loadFeed(force).then(drawFeed);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w192feedmore') { var s = document.getElementById('w192feed'); if (s) { s.__all = true; drawFeed(); } } });
  if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') {
    var __rmp192 = renderMyPage;
    renderMyPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rmp192.apply(this, arguments);
      try { injectFeed(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
  setTimeout(function () { try { if (document.getElementById('mpBody')) injectFeed(false); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 2500);

  /* ---------- the person page: Their loop (Your loop on your own) ---------- */
  function personCard() {
    var id = onPerson(); if (!id) return;
    var right = document.getElementById('w116kright'); if (!right || document.getElementById('w192their')) return;
    var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!m || !m.email) return;
    var self = lower(m.email) === myEmail();
    var card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = 'w116card'; card.id = 'w192their';
    card.innerHTML = '<h3>' + (self ? 'Your loop' : 'Their loop') + '</h3><p class="empty">Loading…</p>';
    var after = document.getElementById('w116khours') || document.getElementById('w116kabout');
    if (after && after.parentNode === right) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', card); else right.insertBefore(card, right.firstChild);
    feedRows(60, m.email).then(function (rows) {
      var el = document.getElementById('w192their'); if (!el) return;
      rows = (rows || []).slice(0, 5);
      el.innerHTML = '<h3>' + (self ? 'Your loop' : 'Their loop') + '</h3>' + (rows.length ? '<ul>' + rows.map(function (x) { return '<li><span class="d">' + E(dayLabel(x.day)) + '</span>' + bulletsOf(x).slice(0, 4).map(function (b) { return '<span class="b">' + E(b) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="empty">' + (self ? 'No notes yet. Close the loop with a few lines and they show here.' : 'No notes shared yet.') + '</p>');
    });
  }
  try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (onPerson() && staff()) personCard(); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- the walkthrough: the page tour "loop" ---------- */
  function tours() {
    try {
      var T = window.w122 && window.w122.tours; if (!T) return false;
      if (T.loop) return true;
      T.loop = { tab: 'mypage', icon: 'refresh', hash: '#mypage', steps: [
        { c: 1, t: 'The Loop', p: 'Two buttons, one habit: I’m in when you start working for Welfare Avenue, Close the loop when you finish. This one-minute walkthrough shows both. It is not a timeclock: the note you share carries what moved, never your hours.' },
        { s: '#w192go', t: 'I’m in', p: 'Press it when you start: a meeting, your onboarding, a task. A short line in #check-in-out-wa says you are in, never the time. The ring on your photo shows teammates you are around, and your time counts for you, on your own page and for HR.', go: function () { endPreview(); } },
        { s: '#w192draftbox', t: 'What moved', p: 'When you close the loop, the Hub drafts your note from your own records: tasks closed or opened, requests, meetings joined, Academy modules finished. Keep what matters and change any line. Private work (confidential, board, one-on-ones) is never pre-filled.', go: function () { preview(); } },
        { s: '#w192saybox', t: 'Add what the Hub could not see', p: 'A call, an email thread, a conversation. Say it or type it, and Add to the note turns it into lines and spots the tasks you finished.' },
        { s: '#w192sug', t: 'Tick what is finished', p: 'The tasks your words show are done are suggested here. Ticked tasks close themselves when you close the loop.', opt: 1 },
        { s: '#w192sharebox', t: 'Where it goes', p: 'Every closed loop goes to #check-in-out-wa in Slack, for the whole team. Every note also shows on Home, so nothing is lost.' },
        { s: '#w192close', t: 'Close the loop', p: 'Your note goes to #check-in-out-wa, with no clock time and no hours. Close without a note ends your session and posts only that you closed the loop.' },
        { c: 1, t: 'Your hours are yours', p: 'They show on your own page and to HR, for your records and your balance. They never go in the post. That is the whole thing: I’m in, close the loop.' }
      ] };
      return true;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  if (!tours()) { setTimeout(tours, 1500); setTimeout(tours, 5000); }

  window.w192 = {
    start: start,
    open: function () { var c = ck(); if (c && c.open()) openSheet(c.open()); },
    openRow: function (row) { if (row && row.id) openSheet(row); },
    preview: preview, endPreview: endPreview,
    feed: function (f) { return injectFeed(!!f); },
    state: function () { return { row: Q.row, bullets: Q.bullets.slice(), suggest: Q.suggest.slice(), share: Q.share, teamCh: Q.teamCh, allCh: Q.allCh, preview: Q.preview, loaded: Q.loaded, minutes: Q.minutes, feed: F.rows }; }
  };
})();

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }

  function today() { var d = new Date(); return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()); }
  function addDays(d, n) { var x = new Date(d.getTime()); x.setDate(x.getDate() + n); return x; }
  function daysUntil(s) { var d = parseDay(s); if (!d) return null; return Math.round((d.getTime() - today().getTime()) / 86400000); }
  function fmtDate(s, opt) { var d = typeof s === 'string' ? parseDay(s) : s; if (!d) return ''; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', opt || { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); }
  function fmtWhen(ts) { if (!ts) return ''; var d = new Date(ts); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ''; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ', ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }).replace(/\s?([ap])\.?m\.?/i, function (x, p) { return p.toLowerCase() + 'm'; }); }
  function fyStart() { var t = today(); return t.getMonth() >= 9 ? mk(t.getFullYear(), 9, 1) : mk(t.getFullYear() - 1, 9, 1); }
  function fyLabel() { var s = fyStart(); return s.getFullYear() + '-' + String(s.getFullYear() + 1).slice(2); }

  function dueText(s) { var n = daysUntil(s); if (n === null) return ''; if (n === 0) return 'today'; if (n === 1) return 'tomorrow'; if (n < 0) return (-n) + (n === -1 ? ' day ago' : ' days ago'); return 'in ' + n + ' days'; }

  async function loadAll(force) {
    if (F.loading) return F.loading;
    if (F.loaded && !force && Date.now() - F.loaded < 45000) return;
    F.loading = (async function () {
      var a = await Promise.all([
        q(db.from('fund_grants').select('*').order('deadline', { ascending: true, nullsFirst: false }).order('updated_at', { ascending: false })),
        q(db.from('fund_funders').select('*').order('name')),
        q(db.from('fund_scan_finds').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(400)),
        q(db.from('fund_scan_runs').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(8)),
        q(db.from('fund_facts').select('*').order('sort').order('title')),
        q(db.from('fund_documents').select('id, grant_id, funder_id, title, kind, lang, doc_date, words, source_url, source, created_at').order('doc_date', { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false })),
        q(db.from('fund_drafts').select('*').order('updated_at', { ascending: false }).limit(100)),
        q(db.from('fund_settings').select('*')),
        q(db.from('fund_events').select('*').order('sort').order('start_date')),
        q(db.from('trainings').select('*').eq('kind', 'sop').eq('active', true).order('sort').order('title')),
        q(db.rpc('fund_giving_summary')),
        q(db.from('fund_sources').select('*').order('kind').order('name')),
        q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'fund_goals').maybeSingle()),
        q(db.from('fund_grant_log').select('*').order('at', { ascending: false }).limit(12))
      ]);
      F.err = a[0].error ? String(a[0].error.message || a[0].error) : null;
      if (a[0].data) F.grants = a[0].data;
      if (a[1].data) F.funders = a[1].data;
      if (a[2].data) F.finds = a[2].data;
      if (a[3].data) F.runs = a[3].data;
      if (a[4].data) F.facts = a[4].data;
      if (a[5].data) F.docs = a[5].data;
      if (a[6].data) F.drafts = a[6].data;
      if (a[7].data) { F.settings = {}; a[7].data.forEach(function (r) { F.settings[r.key] = r.value; }); }
      if (a[8].data) F.events = a[8].data;
      if (a[9].data) F.sops = a[9].data;
      if (a[10].data && typeof a[10].data === 'object') F.giving = a[10].data;
      if (a[11].data) F.sources = a[11].data;
      F.goals = (a[12].data && a[12].data.value) || F.goals || { minimum: 10000, healthy: 15000, target: 20000 };
      if (a[13].data) F.recent = a[13].data;
      F.loaded = Date.now();
    })();
    try { await F.loading; } finally { F.loading = null; }
  }
  async function loadLog(grantId) {
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_grant_log').select('*').eq('grant_id', grantId).order('at', { ascending: false }).limit(60));
    F.log[grantId] = r.data || [];
    return F.log[grantId];
  }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body || {} });
      if (r && r.error) {
        var msg = '';
        try { var ctx = r.error.context; if (ctx && typeof ctx.json === 'function') { var j = await ctx.json(); msg = j && (j.error || j.message) || ''; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        return { ok: false, error: msg || String(r.error.message || r.error) };
      }
      var d = r && r.data; if (d && d.error && !d.ok) return { ok: false, error: d.error };
      return d || { ok: true };
    } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: String(e && e.message || e) }; }
  }

  /* ---------- the page frame: one panel (#fund), sections by address ---------- */
  var SECT = {
    overview: ['Fundraising', 'Where the money comes from: events, donors and grants, with what is due next.'],
    calendar: ['Fundraising calendar', 'Events, grant deadlines, reports and next actions, month by month.'],
    donors: ['Donor program', 'Monthly givers: the goals, the tiers, the stewardship rhythm and how giving is going.'],
    sops: ['Event SOPs', 'How we run each fundraising event, step by step, with the lessons from last time.'],
    grants: ['Grants', 'The pipeline, what the scanner found, the funders, the writer, the playbook and the fact base.']
  };
  var GVIEW = { pipeline: ['Grants', 'Every opportunity and application, by stage: what is open, what is due, who owns it.'], found: ['Found for you', 'What the scanner found since the last run: keep what fits, dismiss the rest.'], funders: ['Funders', 'Every funder we know: the watchlist, the ones we applied to, the ones screened out.'], write: ['Grant writer', 'Drafts a funder\u2019s questions from our fact base and past applications; you edit, review and export.'], playbook: ['How we win grants', 'The approach: the fit screen, the proposal, the budget, the writing rules and the pipeline discipline.'], facts: ['Fact base', 'Welfare Avenue in its own words, each fact with its source and whether it is verified. The writer drafts from these.'] };
  function mast(title, blurb) {
    var sec = document.getElementById('fund'); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w193', '1'); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<div class="crumb">Fundraising</div><h1>' + E(title) + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
  }
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('fund'); if (!sec) return null;
    var t = F.sec === 'grants' ? GVIEW[F.view] || GVIEW.pipeline : SECT[F.sec] || SECT.overview;
    mast(t[0], t[1]);
    try { document.title = t[0] + ' \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var h = document.getElementById('w193host');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w193host'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function onFund() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'fund'); }
  function go(h) { closeSheet(); if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h; }
  function gTabs() {
    var waiting = F.finds.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'new'; }).length;
    var T = [['pipeline', 'Pipeline', '#fund/grants'], ['found', 'Found for you', '#fund/grants/found'], ['funders', 'Funders', '#fund/grants/funders'], ['write', 'Writer', '#fund/grants/write'], ['playbook', 'Playbook', '#fund/grants/playbook'], ['facts', 'Fact base', '#fund/grants/facts']];
    return '<div class="w193tabs">' + T.map(function (t) { return '<a data-w193go="' + t[2] + '" class="' + (F.view === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(t[1]) + (t[0] === 'found' && waiting ? ' <span class="n">(' + waiting + ')</span>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function fitClass(n) { n = Number(n); if (!isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return ''; return n >= 70 ? 'go' : n >= 55 ? 'maybe' : 'no'; }
  function fitHtml(n, title) { n = Number(n); if (!isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return ''; return '<span class="w193fit ' + fitClass(n) + '" title="' + E(title || 'Fit score') + '">' + n + '</span>'; }
  function stagePill(st) { var s = STAGE[st] || { l: st }; return '<span class="w193pill" data-st="' + E(st) + '" title="' + E(s.d || '') + '">' + E(s.l) + '</span>'; }
  function dueHtml(s, label) { if (!s) return ''; return '<span class="w193due ' + dueClass(s) + '">' + E((label ? label + ' ' : '') + fmtDate(s, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + ' <small>(' + E(dueText(s)) + ')</small></span>'; }
  function amountText(g) { if (g.amount_awarded != null && g.amount_awarded !== '') return money(g.amount_awarded) + ' awarded'; if (g.amount_ask != null && g.amount_ask !== '') return money(g.amount_ask); var f = funderOf(g); if (f && (f.amount_max || f.amount_min)) return (f.amount_min && f.amount_max ? kMoney(f.amount_min) + ' to ' + kMoney(f.amount_max) : 'up to ' + kMoney(f.amount_max || f.amount_min)); return ''; }
  function grantTitle(g) { var f = funderName(g), t = g.title || g.program || ''; if (!f) return t; if (!t || lower(t) === lower(f)) return f; return f + ': ' + t; }

  /* ---------- what is due: grants and events with a date, soonest first ---------- */
  function dueItems(days) {
    var t = today(), out = [];
    F.grants.forEach(function (g) {
      if (ACTIVE.indexOf(g.stage) < 0 && g.stage !== 'watching') return;
      if (g.deadline && OPEN.concat(['watching']).indexOf(g.stage) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'deadline', day: g.deadline, t: grantTitle(g), s: 'Deadline' + (g.deadline_note ? ' \u00b7 ' + g.deadline_note : ''), h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id, g: g });
      if (g.report_due && ['awarded', 'reporting'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'report', day: g.report_due, t: grantTitle(g), s: 'Report due', h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id, g: g });
      if (g.renewal_due) out.push({ k: 'report', day: g.renewal_due, t: grantTitle(g), s: 'Renewal', h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id, g: g });
      if (g.next_action_due && OPEN.concat(['watching', 'awarded', 'reporting']).indexOf(g.stage) >= 0) out.push({ k: 'action', day: g.next_action_due, t: g.next_action || 'Next step', s: grantTitle(g), h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id, g: g });
    });
    F.events.forEach(function (f) {
      if (!f.start_date || f.status === 'cancelled' || f.status === 'done') return;
      out.push({ k: 'event', day: f.start_date, t: f.title, s: 'Event' + (f.partners ? ' \u00b7 ' + f.partners : ''), h: '#fundevents/' + f.id, f: f });
    });
    out = out.filter(function (x) { var n = daysUntil(x.day); return n !== null && n >= -14 && (days == null || n <= days); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.day < b.day ? -1 : a.day > b.day ? 1 : 0; });
    return out;
  }
  var LAYERC = { event: ['Events', ORANGE, 'rgba(200,122,47,.16)', '#8a4d12'], deadline: ['Grant deadlines', GREEN, 'rgba(16,63,57,.12)', GREEN], report: ['Reports and renewals', PURPLE, 'rgba(111,100,171,.16)', '#4a4180'], action: ['Next actions', TEAL, 'rgba(42,140,122,.16)', '#1d6b5d'] };

  /* ---------- the overview ---------- */
  function fyAwarded() { var s = ymd(fyStart()); return F.grants.filter(function (g) { return ['awarded', 'reporting', 'closed'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 && g.amount_awarded && (g.decided_on || '') >= s; }).reduce(function (a, g) { return a + Number(g.amount_awarded || 0); }, 0); }
  function fyEvents() { var s = fyStart(), e = mk(s.getFullYear() + 1, 9, 1); return F.events.filter(function (f) { var d = parseDay(f.start_date); return d && d >= s && d < e; }); }
  function overviewHtml() {
    var open = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return OPEN.indexOf(g.stage) >= 0; });
    var ask = open.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + Number(g.amount_ask || 0); }, 0);
    var sub = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.stage === 'submitted'; });
    var due = dueItems(30);
    var waiting = F.finds.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'new'; });
    var awarded = fyAwarded();
    var ev = fyEvents(), raised = ev.reduce(function (a, f) { return a + Number(f.raised || 0); }, 0);
    var goals = F.goals || {}, G = F.giving || {};
    var sc = setting('scanner') || {}, last = sc.last_run, lastRun = F.runs[0] || null;
    var html = '<div class="w193kpis">'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fund/grants"><small>Open grants</small><b>' + open.length + '</b><span>' + (ask ? E(kMoney(ask)) + ' asked' : 'in the pipeline') + (sub.length ? ' \u00b7 ' + sub.length + ' submitted' : '') + '</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fund/calendar"><small>Due in 30 days</small><b class="' + (due.some(function (x) { return daysUntil(x.day) < 0; }) ? 'flag' : '') + '">' + due.length + '</b><span>deadlines, reports, actions and events</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fund/grants/found"><small>Found for you</small><b class="orange">' + waiting.length + '</b><span>' + (waiting.length ? 'waiting for a keep or dismiss' : last ? 'nothing new; last scan ' + E(fmtWhen(last.at)) : 'the scanner has not run yet') + '</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fund/grants"><small>Grants won, FY ' + E(fyLabel()) + '</small><b>' + E(awarded ? money(awarded) : '$0') + '</b><span>' + (F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.stage === 'reporting'; }).length ? F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.stage === 'reporting'; }).length + ' active with reports due' : 'cash grants awarded this year') + '</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fundevents"><small>Events, FY ' + E(fyLabel()) + '</small><b>' + E(money(raised)) + '</b><span>of the ' + E(kMoney(goals.target || 20000)) + ' target \u00b7 ' + ev.filter(function (f) { return f.status !== 'cancelled' && f.status !== 'idea'; }).length + ' planned</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#fund/donors"><small>Monthly donors</small><b>' + (G.monthly_active != null ? G.monthly_active : '\u2013') + '</b><span>' + (G.monthly_amount ? E(money(G.monthly_amount)) + ' a month \u00b7 ' : '') + 'goal ' + ((setting('donor_program') || {}).goals || {}).monthly_donors + '</span></a>'
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193hero"><div class="w193card"><h3>Up next<span class="sp"></span><span class="lnk" data-w193go="#fund/calendar">the calendar</span></h3>'
      + (due.length ? '<ul class="w193list">' + due.slice(0, 9).map(function (x) { var L = LAYERC[x.k]; return '<li data-w193go="' + E(x.h) + '"><span class="w193tag" style="--bg:' + L[2] + ';--ink:' + L[3] + '">' + E(L[0].replace(/s$/, '').replace('Reports and renewal', x.s === 'Renewal' ? 'Renewal' : 'Report')) + '</span><div class="t"><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><small>' + E(x.s) + '</small></div>' + dueHtml(x.day) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">Nothing dated in the next 30 days. Add deadlines and next actions on the grants, and the events keep their dates.</p>')
      + '</div><div class="w193stack"><div class="w193card"><h3>Where the grants stand</h3><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">'
      + STAGES.filter(function (s) { return F.grants.some(function (g) { return g.stage === s[0]; }); }).map(function (s) { var n = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.stage === s[0]; }).length; return '<a class="w193chip off" data-w193go="#fund/grants" data-w193grp="' + stageGroup(s[0]) + '" title="' + E(s[2]) + '">' + E(s[1]) + ' <span class="n">' + n + '</span></a>'; }).join('')
      + '</div><p class="w193note">' + E(F.funders.filter(function (f) { return f.watch && !f.screened_out; }).length) + ' funders on the watchlist, ' + E(F.funders.filter(function (f) { return f.screened_out; }).length) + ' screened out, ' + E(F.docs.length) + ' past applications and letters on file.</p></div>'
      + '<div class="w193card"><h3>The scanner<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193scan="1"' + (F.scanBusy || (lastRun && lastRun.status === 'running') ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (lastRun && lastRun.status === 'running' ? 'Scanning\u2026' : 'Scan now') + '</a>' : '') + '</h3>'
      + (lastRun ? '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px">' + (lastRun.status === 'running' ? 'A scan is running (started ' + E(fmtWhen(lastRun.started_at)) + ').' : 'Last run ' + E(fmtWhen(lastRun.finished_at || lastRun.started_at)) + ': ' + E(lastRun.summary || lastRun.status)) + '</p>' : '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px">No run yet.</p>')
      + '<p class="w193note">Every Monday at 9:30 (Toronto) it re-reads ' + E(F.sources.filter(function (s) { return s.active && s.kind !== 'search'; }).length) + ' funder pages and runs ' + E(F.sources.filter(function (s) { return s.active && s.kind === 'search'; }).length) + ' web searches; new finds land in Found for you and are posted to #' + E(sc.slack_channel_name || 'grants') + '.</p></div></div></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>In the app</h3><div class="w193quick">'
      + [['#fund/grants', 'list', 'Pipeline', 'the grants by stage'], ['#fund/grants/found', 'search', 'Found for you', waiting.length ? waiting.length + ' waiting' : 'the scanner\u2019s finds'], ['#fund/grants/write', 'pen', 'Grant writer', 'draft answers from the fact base'], ['#fund/grants/playbook', 'book', 'How we win grants', 'the approach, in one page'], ['#fund/grants/funders', 'db', 'Funders', F.funders.length + ' known'], ['#fund/grants/facts', 'star', 'Fact base', F.facts.length + ' facts'], ['#fundevents', 'heart', 'Events', 'the year\u2019s plan, season by season'], ['#fund/calendar', 'cal', 'Calendar', 'everything with a date'], ['#fund/donors', 'people', 'Donor program', 'monthly giving'], ['#fund/sops', 'clip', 'Event SOPs', F.sops.length + ' on file']].map(function (q) { return '<a data-w193go="' + q[0] + '">' + I(q[1]) + '<span>' + E(q[2]) + '<small>' + E(q[3]) + '</small></span></a>'; }).join('')
      + '</div></div>';
    if (F.recent && F.recent.length) {
      html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Recently on the grants</h3><ul class="w193list w193log">' + F.recent.slice(0, 8).map(function (l) { var g = byId(F.grants, l.grant_id); return '<li data-w193go="#fund/grants/' + l.grant_id + '"><span class="k">' + E(l.kind) + '</span><div class="t"><b>' + E(g ? grantTitle(g) : 'Grant ' + l.grant_id) + '</b><small>' + E(clip(l.text, 160)) + (l.who ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(l.who.split('@')[0]) : '') + '</small></div><span class="when">' + E(fmtWhen(l.at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    }
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the Fundraising calendar (#fund/calendar): events, grant deadlines, reports and next actions ---------- */
  function calAnchor() { if (!F.calAnchor) { var t = today(); F.calAnchor = mk(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), 1); } return F.calAnchor; }
  function monthStart(d) { return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1); }
  function addMonths(d, n) { return mk(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + n, 1); }
  function spanDays(f) { var s = parseDay(f.start_date), e = parseDay(f.end_date) || s; if (!s) return 0; return Math.round((e.getTime() - s.getTime()) / 86400000) + 1; }
  /* dated items in a window: grant dates one by one, events of up to four days on their days, longer events as a band */
  function calItems(from, to) {
    var a = ymd(from), b = ymd(to), out = [];
    var inWin = function (d) { return d && d >= a && d <= b; };
    F.grants.forEach(function (g) {
      var tt = grantTitle(g);
      if (F.calLayers.deadlines && g.deadline && OPEN.concat(['watching']).indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 && inWin(g.deadline)) out.push({ id: 'd' + g.id, k: 'deadline', day: g.deadline, title: tt, sub: 'Deadline' + (g.deadline_note ? ' \u00b7 ' + g.deadline_note : ''), h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id });
      if (F.calLayers.reports) {
        if (g.report_due && ['awarded', 'reporting'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 && inWin(g.report_due)) out.push({ id: 'r' + g.id, k: 'report', day: g.report_due, title: tt, sub: 'Report due', h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id });
        if (g.renewal_due && inWin(g.renewal_due)) out.push({ id: 'n' + g.id, k: 'report', day: g.renewal_due, title: tt, sub: 'Renewal', h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id });
        if (g.submitted_on && g.stage === 'submitted' && inWin(g.submitted_on)) out.push({ id: 's' + g.id, k: 'report', day: g.submitted_on, title: tt, sub: 'Submitted', h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id, past: true });
      }
      if (F.calLayers.actions && g.next_action_due && OPEN.concat(['watching', 'awarded', 'reporting']).indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 && inWin(g.next_action_due)) out.push({ id: 'a' + g.id, k: 'action', day: g.next_action_due, title: g.next_action || 'Next step', sub: tt, h: '#fund/grants/' + g.id });
    });
    if (F.calLayers.events) F.events.forEach(function (f) {
      if (!f.start_date || f.status === 'cancelled' || f.precision === 'none') return;
      var n = spanDays(f); if (n > 4) return;
      var s = parseDay(f.start_date);
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var d = ymd(addDays(s, i)); if (inWin(d)) out.push({ id: 'e' + f.id + '-' + i, k: 'event', day: d, title: f.title, sub: (f.partners ? f.partners + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + (f.status || ''), h: '#fundevents/' + f.id, past: f.status === 'done' }); }
    });
    out.sort(function (x, y) { return x.day < y.day ? -1 : x.day > y.day ? 1 : (x.k === 'event' ? -1 : 1); });
    return out;
  }
  function calBands(from, to) {
    if (!F.calLayers.events) return [];
    var a = ymd(from), b = ymd(to);
    return F.events.filter(function (f) { if (!f.start_date || f.status === 'cancelled' || f.precision === 'none') return false; if (spanDays(f) <= 4) return false; var e = f.end_date || f.start_date; return f.start_date <= b && e >= a; });
  }
  function evPill(it) {
    var L = LAYERC[it.k], t = today(), past = it.past || (parseDay(it.day) < t);
    return '<div class="w193ev' + (past ? ' past' : '') + '" data-w193go="' + E(it.h) + '" style="--c:' + L[1] + ';--bg:' + L[2] + ';--ink:' + L[3] + '" title="' + E(it.title + (it.sub ? ' \u00b7 ' + it.sub : '')) + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><span>' + E(it.title) + '</span></div>';
  }
  function calendarHtml() {
    var A = calAnchor(), s = monthStart(A), dow = (s.getDay() + 6) % 7, start = addDays(s, -dow), end = addDays(start, 41), t = today();
    var items = calItems(start, end), byDay = {};
    items.forEach(function (it) { (byDay[it.day] = byDay[it.day] || []).push(it); });
    var counts = {}; calItems(mk(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth() - 1, 1), mk(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth() + 12, 1)).forEach(function (it) { counts[it.k] = (counts[it.k] || 0) + 1; });
    var html = '<div class="w193calbar"><a class="w193btn ghost icon" data-w193nav="-1" aria-label="Previous month">' + I('chevl') + '</a><a class="w193btn ghost icon" data-w193nav="1" aria-label="Next month">' + I('chevr') + '</a><h2>' + E(MONTHS[A.getMonth()] + ' ' + A.getFullYear()) + '</h2><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193nav="0">Today</a><span class="sp"></span>'
      + '<a class="w193lnk" data-w193go="#calendar" style="font-size:12.5px">The master calendar</a></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193layers">' + Object.keys(LAYERC).map(function (k) { var L = LAYERC[k], key = k === 'event' ? 'events' : k === 'deadline' ? 'deadlines' : k === 'report' ? 'reports' : 'actions'; return '<a class="w193chip' + (F.calLayers[key] ? '' : ' off') + '" data-w193layer="' + key + '" style="--c:' + L[1] + '" role="switch" aria-checked="' + (F.calLayers[key] ? 'true' : 'false') + '"><i></i>' + E(L[0]) + ' <span class="n">' + (counts[k] || 0) + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    var bands = calBands(s, addMonths(s, 1));
    if (bands.length) html += '<div class="w193band"><span class="lb">This month</span>' + bands.map(function (f) { return '<a data-w193go="#fundevents/' + f.id + '">' + E(f.title) + (f.start_date ? ' <small>' + E(fmtDate(f.start_date, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + (f.end_date && f.end_date !== f.start_date ? ' to ' + fmtDate(f.end_date, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) : '')) + '</small>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193side"><div><div class="w193grid">' + WD.map(function (d) { return '<div class="w193dow">' + d + '</div>'; }).join('');
    for (var i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      var d = addDays(start, i), key = ymd(d), list = byDay[key] || [], out = d.getMonth() !== A.getMonth();
      html += '<div class="w193cell' + (out ? ' out' : '') + (ymd(t) === key ? ' today' : '') + '"><span class="dn">' + d.getDate() + '</span>' + list.slice(0, 4).map(evPill).join('') + (list.length > 4 ? '<span class="w193more">+' + (list.length - 4) + ' more</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    html += '</div></div>';
    var next = dueItems(120).slice(0, 14);
    html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>Up next</h3>' + (next.length ? '<ul class="w193list">' + next.map(function (x) { var L = LAYERC[x.k]; return '<li data-w193go="' + E(x.h) + '"><span class="w193tag" style="--bg:' + L[2] + ';--ink:' + L[3] + '">' + E(fmtDate(x.day, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + '</span><div class="t"><b>' + E(x.t) + '</b><small>' + E(x.s) + '</small></div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">Nothing dated in the next four months.</p>') + '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the donor program (#fund/donors): fund_settings.donor_program with the giving totals (fund_giving_summary) ---------- */
  function donorsHtml() {
    var D = setting('donor_program') || {}, G = F.giving || {}, goals = D.goals || {};
    var lead = isLeader();
    var monthly = Number(G.monthly_active || 0), goalN = Number(goals.monthly_donors || 40), pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, monthly / goalN * 100));
    var ladder = goals.ladder || [];
    var html = '<div class="w193kpis">'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm/gifts"><small>Monthly donors</small><b class="orange">' + monthly + '</b><span>goal ' + goalN + (goals.by ? ' by ' + E(fmtDate(goals.by)) : '') + '</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm/gifts"><small>Monthly giving</small><b>' + E(money(G.monthly_amount || 0)) + '</b><span>a month \u00b7 goal ' + E(money(goals.monthly_revenue || 400)) + '</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm/reports"><small>Raised, FY ' + E(fyLabel()) + '</small><b>' + E(money(G.fy_raised || 0)) + '</b><span>' + E(G.fy_gifts || 0) + ' gifts from ' + E(G.fy_donors || 0) + ' donors</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm/reports"><small>Last fiscal year</small><b>' + E(money(G.last_fy_raised || 0)) + '</b><span>' + E(G.last_fy_donors || 0) + ' donors</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm/reports"><small>Lapsed donors</small><b class="' + (Number(G.lapsed || 0) > 0 ? 'flag' : '') + '">' + E(G.lapsed || 0) + '</b><span>gave last year, not yet this year</span></a>'
      + '<a class="w193kpi" data-w193go="#crm"><small>Donors on file</small><b>' + E(G.donors_all || 0) + '</b><span>in the CRM' + (G.online_live === false ? ' \u00b7 online giving is off' : '') + '</span></a>'
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193hero"><div class="w193card"><h3>' + E(D.name || 'Monthly donor program') + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (lead ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193dgoals="1">' + I('pen') + 'Edit the goals</a>' : '') + '</h3>'
      + '<div class="w193big">' + monthly + '<small>monthly donors of the ' + goalN + ' goal</small></div>'
      + '<div class="w193meter" title="Monthly donors against the goal"><div class="fill" style="width:' + pct.toFixed(1) + '%"></div>' + ladder.filter(function (l) { return Number(l.donors) <= goalN; }).map(function (l) { return '<div class="tick" style="left:' + Math.min(100, Number(l.donors) / goalN * 100) + '%"><span>' + E(l.donors) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(l.when) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + (ladder.length ? '<p class="w193note">The ladder: ' + ladder.map(function (l) { return E(l.donors) + ' donors at about $' + E(l.avg) + ' (' + E(l.when) + ')'; }).join('; ') + '. Year goal ' + E(money(goals.year_revenue || 3000)) + '.</p>' : '')
      + (D.audience ? '<p class="w193p" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Who we ask:</b> ' + E(D.audience) + '</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="w193acts"><a class="w193btn orange sm" href="https://hub.welfareavenue.com/give" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + 'The donation page</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#crm/gifts">' + I('heart') + 'Gifts in the CRM</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#crm/reports">' + I('list') + 'Giving reports</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#crm/automations">' + I('bolt') + 'The thank-you automation</a></div></div>'
      + '<div class="w193stack"><div class="w193card"><h3>The tiers</h3><div class="w193tiers">' + (D.tiers || []).map(function (t) { return '<div class="w193tier"><b>' + E(t.label || ('$' + t.amount + ' a month')) + '</b><small>buys</small><p>' + E(t.buys || '') + '</p></div>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="w193card"><h3>The team</h3><div class="w193roles">' + (D.roles || []).map(function (r) { return '<b>' + E(r.who) + '</b><span>' + E(r.role) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div></div></div></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193two"><div class="w193card"><h3>The stewardship rhythm' + (lead ? '<span class="sp"></span><span class="lnk">tick what is in place</span>' : '') + '</h3><ul class="w193checks">' + (D.cadence || []).map(function (c, i) { return '<li class="' + (c.done ? 'on' : '') + '"><span class="bx" data-w193dtick="cadence:' + i + '" role="checkbox" aria-checked="' + (c.done ? 'true' : 'false') + '" tabindex="0">' + (c.done ? I('check') : '') + '</span><div><span><b>' + E(c.l) + '</b></span>' + (c.how ? '<div class="w193note" style="margin-top:2px">' + E(c.how) + '</div>' : '') + '</div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>'
      + '<div class="w193card"><h3>The launch plan' + (lead ? '<span class="sp"></span><span class="lnk">tick what is done</span>' : '') + '</h3><ul class="w193checks">' + (D.launch || []).map(function (c, i) { return '<li class="' + (c.done ? 'on' : '') + '"><span class="bx" data-w193dtick="launch:' + i + '" role="checkbox" aria-checked="' + (c.done ? 'true' : 'false') + '" tabindex="0">' + (c.done ? I('check') : '') + '</span><span>' + E(c.step) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' + (D.launched ? '<p class="w193note">Launched ' + E(fmtDate(D.launched)) + '.</p>' : '<p class="w193note">Not launched yet' + (lead ? ': set the date with Edit the goals when it goes live' : '') + '.</p>') + '</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>How we grow monthly giving</h3><ul class="w193ul">' + (D.playbook || []).map(function (p) { return '<li>' + E(p) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' + (D.sources && D.sources.length ? '<p class="w193note">From ' + E(D.sources.join('; ')) + '.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  async function donorTick(ref) {
    if (!isLeader()) { toastS('Leaders and admins tick these.'); return; }
    var p = String(ref).split(':'), list = p[0], i = Number(p[1]);
    var D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(setting('donor_program') || {}));
    var arr = D[list]; if (!arr || !arr[i]) return;
    arr[i].done = !arr[i].done;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_settings').update({ value: D, updated_by: me().email }).eq('key', 'donor_program'));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    F.settings.donor_program = D; paint();
  }
  async function saveDonorGoals(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(setting('donor_program') || {}));
    D.goals = D.goals || {};
    D.goals.monthly_donors = Number(fd.get('monthly_donors')) || D.goals.monthly_donors;
    D.goals.monthly_revenue = Number(fd.get('monthly_revenue')) || D.goals.monthly_revenue;
    D.goals.year_revenue = Number(fd.get('year_revenue')) || D.goals.year_revenue;
    D.goals.by = String(fd.get('by') || '') || null;
    D.launched = String(fd.get('launched') || '') || null;
    D.audience = String(fd.get('audience') || '').trim() || D.audience;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_settings').update({ value: D, updated_by: me().email }).eq('key', 'donor_program'));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    F.settings.donor_program = D; closeSheet(); paint(); toastS('Saved.');
  }

  /* ---------- the event SOPs (#fund/sops[/<id>]): trainings of kind sop, read in the app, edited in the Avenue Academy ---------- */
  function sopTitle(t) { return String((t && t.title) || '').replace(/^SOP:\s*/i, ''); }
  function sopDepts(t) { var b = sopBody(t), d = Array.isArray(b.depts) ? b.depts : (b.dept ? [b.dept] : []); return d; }
  function teamName(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || d; } catch (e) { return d; } }
  function sopEvents(t) { return F.events.filter(function (f) { return t && String(f.sop_training_id) === String(t.id) && f.status !== 'cancelled'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.start_date || '9999').localeCompare(String(b.start_date || '9999')); }); }
  /* the fundraising SOPs first (the team's own and the ones an event uses), then the rest of the library */
  function sopsSorted() {
    var used = {}; F.events.forEach(function (f) { if (f.sop_training_id) used[String(f.sop_training_id)] = 1; });
    var mine = function (t) { return sopDepts(t).indexOf('fundraising') >= 0 || used[String(t.id)]; };
    return F.sops.slice().sort(function (a, b) { var ma = mine(a) ? 0 : 1, mb = mine(b) ? 0 : 1; return ma - mb || (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); });
  }
  function sopCard(t) {
    var b = sopBody(t), ev = sopEvents(t), up = ev.filter(function (f) { return f.status !== 'done'; })[0];
    return '<div class="w193sop" data-w193go="#fund/sops/' + t.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><h4>' + E(sopTitle(t)) + '</h4><p>' + E(clip(b.intro || t.summary || '', 170)) + '</p><div class="ft">'
      + sopDepts(t).map(function (d) { return '<span class="w193tag">' + E(teamName(d)) + '</span>'; }).join('')
      + (b.owner ? '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:rgba(200,122,47,.14);--ink:#8a4d12">' + E(b.owner) + '</span>' : '')
      + (up ? '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:rgba(42,140,122,.14);--ink:#1d6b5d">' + I('clock') + E(up.title) + (up.start_date ? ', ' + E(fmtDate(up.start_date, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) : '') + '</span>' : '')
      + (b.reviewed ? '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:transparent;--ink:var(--wa-muted)">reviewed ' + E(fmtDate(b.reviewed)) + '</span>' : '')
      + '</div></div>';
  }
  function sopsHtml() {
    var list = sopsSorted();
    var html = '<div class="w193bar"><span class="w193empty">' + list.length + ' standard operating procedure' + (list.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '. Each one is a reading in the Avenue Academy; open it there to edit it or to count it as read.</span><span class="sp"></span><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#training">' + I('book') + 'The Avenue Academy</a></div>';
    html += list.length ? '<div class="w193sops">' + list.map(sopCard).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">No SOP on file yet. Leaders write them in the Avenue Academy (Trainings, Add an SOP).</p></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function li(arr, fmt) { return (arr || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + (fmt ? fmt(x) : E(x)) + '</li>'; }).join(''); }
  function safeUrl(u) { u = String(u || '').trim(); return /^https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+$/i.test(u) ? u : ''; }
  function sopReaderHtml(t) {
    var b = sopBody(t), ev = sopEvents(t);
    var html = '<div class="w193bar"><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#fund/sops">' + I('chevl') + 'All SOPs</a><span class="sp"></span><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#training/t/' + t.id + '">' + I('book') + 'Open in the Avenue Academy</a></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193side"><div class="w193card w193reader"><h2>' + E(sopTitle(t)) + '</h2>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 12px">' + sopDepts(t).map(function (d) { return '<span class="w193tag">' + E(teamName(d)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + (b.owner ? '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:rgba(200,122,47,.14);--ink:#8a4d12">Owner: ' + E(b.owner) + '</span>' : '') + (b.reviewed ? '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:transparent;--ink:var(--wa-muted)">reviewed ' + E(fmtDate(b.reviewed)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (b.intro ? '<p class="intro">' + nl(b.intro) + '</p>' : '')
      + (b.glance && b.glance.length ? '<div class="w193glance">' + b.glance.map(function (g) { return '<div><small>' + E(g[0]) + '</small><b>' + E(g[1]) + '</b></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + (b.format && b.format.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>The format</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.format) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.roles && b.roles.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Who does what</h4><div class="w193roles">' + b.roles.map(function (r) { return '<b>' + E(r[0]) + '</b><span>' + E(r[1]) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' : '')
      + (b.phases && b.phases.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Step by step</h4>' + b.phases.map(function (p) { return '<div class="w193phase"><h4 style="font-size:15.5px;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:var(--wa-text)">' + E(p.title || '') + '</h4>' + (p.when ? '<small>' + E(p.when) + '</small>' : '') + '<ol>' + li(p.steps) + '</ol></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + (b.dayof && b.dayof.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>On the day</h4><ol class="w193ul">' + li(b.dayof) + '</ol></div>' : '')
      + (b.money && b.money.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>The money</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.money) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.confirm && b.confirm.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Before you call it done</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.confirm) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.lessons && b.lessons.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Lessons from last time</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.lessons) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.editions && b.editions.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Past editions</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.editions, function (e) { return '<b>' + E(e[0]) + '</b>' + (e[1] ? ' (' + E(e[1]) + ')' : '') + (e[2] ? ': ' + E(e[2]) : ''); }) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.links && b.links.length ? '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Links</h4><ul class="w193ul">' + li(b.links, function (l) { var u = safeUrl(l[1]); return u ? '<a class="w193lnk" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(l[0] || u) + '</a>' : E(l[0] || ''); }) + '</ul></div>' : '')
      + (b.sources ? '<p class="w193note">Sources: ' + E(Array.isArray(b.sources) ? b.sources.join('; ') : b.sources) + '</p>' : '')
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193stack"><div class="w193card"><h3>On the events calendar</h3>' + (ev.length ? '<ul class="w193list">' + ev.map(function (f) { return '<li data-w193go="#fundevents/' + f.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(f.title) + '</b><small>' + E(f.status || '') + (f.raised != null && f.status === 'done' ? ' \u00b7 raised ' + E(money(f.raised)) : '') + '</small></div>' + (f.start_date ? '<span class="when">' + E(fmtDate(f.start_date, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + '</span>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">No event is linked to this SOP yet. Link one from the event\u2019s form on the Events page.</p>') + '</div></div></div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the pipeline (#fund/grants[/<id>]) ---------- */
  function matches(g, qq) {
    if (!qq) return true;
    var f = funderOf(g) || {};
    var hay = fold([grantTitle(g), g.program, g.title, f.name, (f.aliases || []).join(' '), g.owner_name, g.owner_email, g.partner, g.program_area, g.next_action, g.notes, g.stage].join(' '));
    return qq.split(/\s+/).every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; });
  }
  function grantSort(a, b) {
    var da = a.deadline || a.next_action_due || a.report_due || '9999', dbb = b.deadline || b.next_action_due || b.report_due || '9999';
    if (da !== dbb) return da < dbb ? -1 : 1;
    return String(b.updated_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.updated_at || ''));
  }
  function grantRow(g) {
    var f = funderOf(g) || {}, closed = ['declined', 'not_eligible', 'dropped', 'closed'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0;
    var when = g.stage === 'submitted' ? (g.submitted_on ? '<span class="w193due">submitted ' + E(fmtDate(g.submitted_on, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + '</span>' : '') : ['awarded', 'reporting'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 ? dueHtml(g.report_due, 'report') : closed ? (g.decided_on ? '<span class="w193due">' + E(fmtDate(g.decided_on, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })) + '</span>' : '') : dueHtml(g.deadline);
    var sub = [KINDS[f.kind] || '', g.program_area || '', g.owner_name || (g.owner_email ? g.owner_email.split('@')[0] : ''), g.partner ? 'with ' + g.partner : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ');
    return '<div class="w193row' + (closed ? ' closed' : '') + '" data-w193grant="' + g.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><div class="nm"><b>' + E(grantTitle(g)) + '</b><small>' + E(sub || (g.title && g.program ? g.program : '')) + '</small></div>' + stagePill(g.stage) + '<div>' + when + '</div><div class="amt">' + E(amountText(g)) + '</div><div class="nx">' + (g.next_action ? '<b>' + E(g.next_action) + '</b>' + (g.next_action_due ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(dueText(g.next_action_due)) : '') : (g.decision_note && closed ? E(clip(g.decision_note, 60)) : '')) + '</div><div>' + fitHtml(g.fit_score) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function pipelineHtml() {
    var FL = F.filters, qq = fold(FL.q);
    var grp = GROUPS.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === FL.group; })[0];
    var stages = FL.group === 'all' ? STAGES.map(function (s) { return s[0]; }) : grp ? grp[2] : OPEN;
    var list = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return stages.indexOf(g.stage) >= 0 && matches(g, qq) && (!FL.region || (funderOf(g) || {}).region === FL.region) && (!FL.owner || lower(g.owner_email) === FL.owner); });
    var owners = uniq(F.grants.map(function (g) { return lower(g.owner_email); }));
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193bar"><div class="w193seg">' + GROUPS.concat([['all', 'All', []]]).map(function (x) { var n = x[0] === 'all' ? F.grants.length : F.grants.filter(function (g) { return x[2].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0; }).length; return '<a data-w193grp="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (FL.group === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + ' <span class="n">' + n + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<input type="search" placeholder="Search the grants: funder, program, owner, partner\u2026" value="' + E(FL.q) + '" data-w193q="1" aria-label="Search the grants">'
      + '<select data-w193region="1" aria-label="Region"><option value="">Every region</option>' + Object.keys(REGIONS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (FL.region === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(REGIONS[k]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + (owners.length > 1 ? '<select data-w193owner="1" aria-label="Owner"><option value="">Every owner</option>' + owners.map(function (o) { var g = F.grants.filter(function (x) { return lower(x.owner_email) === o; })[0]; return '<option value="' + E(o) + '"' + (FL.owner === o ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(g.owner_name || o.split('@')[0]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '')
      + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn sm" data-w193gadd="1">' + I('plus') + 'Add a grant</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (!list.length) return html + '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">' + (qq || FL.region || FL.owner ? 'Nothing matches that search.' : FL.group === 'open' ? 'Nothing open right now. The watchlist holds the next cycles; Found for you has the scanner\u2019s finds.' : 'Nothing here yet.') + '</p></div>';
    var byStage = {}; list.forEach(function (g) { (byStage[g.stage] = byStage[g.stage] || []).push(g); });
    STAGES.forEach(function (s) {
      var rows = byStage[s[0]]; if (!rows || !rows.length) return;
      rows.sort(grantSort);
      var ask = rows.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + Number(g.amount_ask || 0); }, 0), won = rows.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + Number(g.amount_awarded || 0); }, 0);
      html += '<div class="w193stage"><h3>' + E(s[1]) + '</h3><span>' + rows.length + (s[0] === 'awarded' || s[0] === 'reporting' || s[0] === 'closed' ? (won ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(money(won)) + ' awarded' : '') : (ask ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(kMoney(ask)) + ' asked' : '')) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(s[2]) + '</span></div><div class="w193rows">' + rows.map(grantRow).join('') + '</div>';
    });
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- found for you (#fund/grants/found) ---------- */
  function findCard(x) {
    var mf = x.matched_funder_id ? byId(F.funders, x.matched_funder_id) : null, done = x.status !== 'new';
    var meta = [];
    if (x.amount_min || x.amount_max) meta.push('<b>' + E(x.amount_min && x.amount_max ? kMoney(x.amount_min) + ' to ' + kMoney(x.amount_max) : 'up to ' + kMoney(x.amount_max || x.amount_min)) + '</b>');
    if (x.deadline) meta.push('deadline <b>' + E(fmtDate(x.deadline)) + '</b> (' + E(dueText(x.deadline)) + ')'); else if (x.deadline_text) meta.push(E(x.deadline_text));
    if (x.region) meta.push(E(REGIONS[x.region] || x.region));
    if (x.kind) meta.push(E(KINDS[x.kind] || x.kind));
    var src = x.raw && x.raw.source_id ? byId(F.sources, x.raw.source_id) : null;
    return '<div class="w193find' + (done ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w193find="' + x.id + '"><div class="hd">' + fitHtml(x.fit_score, x.fit_note || 'Fit score') + (mf ? '<span class="w193tag" title="Already in Funders">' + I('db') + E(clip(mf.name, 28)) + '</span>' : '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:rgba(200,122,47,.14);--ink:#8a4d12">New funder</span>') + (done ? '<span class="w193pill" data-st="' + E(x.status) + '">' + E(x.status) + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><span class="w193due">' + E(fmtWhen(x.created_at)) + '</span></div>'
      + '<h4>' + E(x.funder_name) + '<small>' + E(x.program || '') + '</small></h4>'
      + (x.summary ? '<p>' + E(x.summary) + '</p>' : '')
      + (meta.length ? '<div class="meta">' + meta.join('<span>\u00b7</span>') + '</div>' : '')
      + (x.eligibility ? '<p class="w193note" style="margin:0"><b>Eligibility:</b> ' + E(clip(x.eligibility, 240)) + '</p>' : '')
      + (x.fit_note ? '<p class="w193note" style="margin:0"><b>Why ' + E(x.fit_score) + ':</b> ' + E(x.fit_note) + '</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="meta">' + (x.url && safeUrl(x.url) ? '<a class="w193lnk" href="' + E(x.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' The call</a>' : '') + (src ? '<span>from ' + E(src.name) + '</span>' : '') + (x.decision_note ? '<span>' + E(x.decision_note) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (!done && canEdit() ? '<div class="acts"><a class="w193btn sm" data-w193keep="' + x.id + '">' + I('check') + 'Keep</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193dismiss="' + x.id + '">Dismiss</a><a class="w193btn warn sm" data-w193screen="' + x.id + '" title="Dismiss, and stop proposing this funder">Not for us</a></div><div class="w193dform" data-w193dform="' + x.id + '" style="display:none"><textarea class="w193reason" placeholder="Why (optional, kept with the decision)" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:inherit;font-size:13px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:10px;min-height:60px"></textarea><div class="acts"><a class="w193btn sm" data-w193dgo="' + x.id + '">Confirm</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193dcancel="' + x.id + '">Cancel</a></div></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function foundHtml() {
    var fresh = F.finds.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'new'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return Number(b.fit_score || 0) - Number(a.fit_score || 0); });
    var older = F.finds.filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'new'; }).slice(0, 60);
    var lastRun = F.runs[0] || null, running = lastRun && lastRun.status === 'running';
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193bar"><span class="w193empty">' + (fresh.length ? fresh.length + ' find' + (fresh.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' waiting. Keep puts the grant in the pipeline at Found; Not for us also stops the scanner proposing the funder.' : 'Nothing waiting. New finds land here after every scan' + (lastRun && !running ? '; the last one ran ' + E(fmtWhen(lastRun.finished_at || lastRun.started_at)) : '') + '.') + '</span><span class="sp"></span>'
      + '<a class="w193chip' + (F.foundAll ? ' on' : ' off') + '" data-w193foundall="1">Show decided and known</a>'
      + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn sm" data-w193scan="1"' + (F.scanBusy || running ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('refresh') + (running ? 'Scanning\u2026' : 'Scan now') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (running) html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p class="w193empty">' + I('clock') + ' A scan is running (started ' + E(fmtWhen(lastRun.started_at)) + ', step ' + E(lastRun.steps || 1) + '). It reads the funder pages first, then runs the web searches; a few minutes. This page refreshes itself.</p></div>';
    html += fresh.length ? '<div class="w193finds">' + fresh.map(findCard).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">The watchlist (' + F.sources.filter(function (s) { return s.active && s.kind !== 'search'; }).length + ' pages) and the ' + F.sources.filter(function (s) { return s.active && s.kind === 'search'; }).length + ' web searches run every Monday morning; Scan now runs them today.</p></div>';
    if (F.foundAll && older.length) html += '<div class="w193stage"><h3>Decided and known</h3><span>the last ' + older.length + '</span></div><div class="w193finds">' + older.map(findCard).join('') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-top:16px"><h3>The runs</h3>' + (F.runs.length ? '<ul class="w193list w193runs">' + F.runs.map(function (r) { return '<li><span class="w193pill" data-st="' + (r.status === 'ok' ? 'awarded' : r.status === 'running' ? 'drafting' : r.status === 'partial' ? 'screened' : 'declined') + '">' + E(r.status) + '</span><div class="t"><b>' + E(r.summary || (r.status === 'running' ? 'Running\u2026' : r.status)) + '</b><small>' + E(r.trigger === 'schedule' ? 'the Monday schedule' : r.trigger === 'test' ? 'a test' : (r.by_email ? r.by_email.split('@')[0] : 'a person')) + (r.cost ? ' \u00b7 $' + Number(r.cost).toFixed(2) + ' in model calls' : '') + (r.slack_ts ? ' \u00b7 posted to Slack' : '') + (r.errors && r.errors.length ?
