' \u00b7 ' + r.errors.length + ' problem' + (r.errors.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + (isAdmin() ? ': ' + E(r.errors.slice(0, 3).map(function (e) { return e.where + ' (' + clip(e.error, 60) + ')'; }).join('; ')) : '') : '') + '</small></div><span class="when">' + E(fmtWhen(r.started_at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">No run yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  async function scanNow() {
    if (F.scanBusy) return;
    F.scanBusy = true; paint();
    var r = await fn('grant-scan', { action: 'scan', trigger: 'person' });
    F.scanBusy = false;
    if (!r || r.ok === false || r.error) { toastS(r && r.error ? r.error : 'The scan could not start.'); paint(); return; }
    toastS('Scanning: the funder pages first, then the web searches. A few minutes.');
    await loadAll(true); paint();
    watchRun();
  }
  var runT = null;
  function watchRun() {
    clearTimeout(runT);
    runT = setTimeout(async function () {
      await loadAll(true);
      var lastRun = F.runs[0];
      if (onFund()) paint();
      if (lastRun && lastRun.status === 'running' && onFund()) watchRun();
      else if (lastRun && lastRun.status !== 'running') toastS('The scan finished: ' + (lastRun.summary || lastRun.status));
    }, 15000);
  }
  async function decideFind(id, action, note) {
    var r = await q(db.rpc('fund_find_decide', { p_find: Number(id), p_action: action, p_note: note || null }));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    await loadAll(true);
    if (action === 'keep' && r.data) { toastS('Kept: it is in the pipeline at Found.'); go('#fund/grants/' + r.data); return; }
    toastS(action === 'screen_out' ? 'Dismissed, and the funder will not be proposed again.' : 'Dismissed.');
    paint();
  }

  /* ---------- the funders (#fund/grants/funders[/<id>]) ---------- */
  function funderRow(f) {
    var gs = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.funder_id === f.id; }), open = gs.filter(function (g) { return OPEN.indexOf(g.stage) >= 0; }).length;
    var fit = FIT[f.fit] || FIT.unknown;
    return '<div class="w193frow' + (f.screened_out ? ' out' : '') + '" data-w193funder="' + f.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button"><div class="nm"><b>' + E(f.name) + '</b><small>' + E([f.funds_what ? clip(f.funds_what, 90) : '', f.aliases && f.aliases.length ? 'also ' + f.aliases.slice(0, 2).join(', ') : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</small></div><div class="kd">' + E(KINDS[f.kind] || f.kind) + '<br><small>' + E(REGIONS[f.region] || f.region) + '</small></div><div>' + (f.screened_out ? '<span class="w193pill" data-st="not_eligible" title="' + E(f.screened_reason || '') + '">Screened out</span>' : '<span class="w193tag" style="--bg:' + fit[1] + ';--ink:' + fit[2] + '" title="' + E(f.fit_note || '') + '">' + E(fit[0]) + '</span>') + '</div><div>' + (f.watch && !f.screened_out ? '<span class="w193tag">' + I('search') + 'watched</span>' : '') + '</div><div class="cy">' + E(f.next_deadline ? 'next ' + fmtDate(f.next_deadline, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }) : (f.cycle || '')) + (gs.length ? '<br><small>' + gs.length + ' grant' + (gs.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + (open ? ', ' + open + ' open' : '') + '</small>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function fundersHtml() {
    var FL = F.filters, qq = fold(FL.q);
    var list = F.funders.filter(function (f) {
      if (FL.watch === 'watch' && (!f.watch || f.screened_out)) return false;
      if (FL.watch === 'out' && !f.screened_out) return false;
      if (FL.watch === 'applied' && !F.grants.some(function (g) { return g.funder_id === f.id && ['drafting', 'submitted', 'awarded', 'declined', 'not_eligible', 'reporting', 'closed'].indexOf(g.stage) >= 0; })) return false;
      if (FL.watch === '' && f.screened_out && !qq) return false;
      if (FL.fkind && f.kind !== FL.fkind) return false;
      if (FL.region && f.region !== FL.region) return false;
      if (FL.fit && f.fit !== FL.fit) return false;
      if (qq) { var hay = fold([f.name, (f.aliases || []).join(' '), f.funds_what, f.eligibility, f.notes, f.relationship, f.contact].join(' ')); if (!qq.split(/\s+/).every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; })) return false; }
      return true;
    }).sort(function (a, b) { var o = { good: 0, possible: 1, unknown: 2, weak: 3 }; return (a.screened_out ? 1 : 0) - (b.screened_out ? 1 : 0) || o[a.fit] - o[b.fit] || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193bar"><div class="w193seg">' + [['', 'Active'], ['watch', 'Watchlist'], ['applied', 'Applied to'], ['out', 'Screened out']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w193watch="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (FL.watch === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<input type="search" placeholder="Search the funders\u2026" value="' + E(FL.q) + '" data-w193q="1" aria-label="Search the funders">'
      + '<select data-w193fkind="1" aria-label="Kind"><option value="">Every kind</option>' + Object.keys(KINDS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (FL.fkind === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(KINDS[k]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<select data-w193region="1" aria-label="Region"><option value="">Every region</option>' + Object.keys(REGIONS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (FL.region === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(REGIONS[k]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<select data-w193fit="1" aria-label="Fit"><option value="">Any fit</option>' + Object.keys(FIT).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (FL.fit === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(FIT[k][0]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn sm" data-w193fadd="1">' + I('plus') + 'Add a funder</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    html += '<p class="w193note" style="margin:0 0 10px">' + list.length + ' of ' + F.funders.length + ' funders' + (FL.watch === '' ? ' (screened-out funders are under their own tab)' : '') + '.</p>';
    html += list.length ? '<div class="w193rows">' + list.map(funderRow).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">Nothing matches.</p></div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the grant writer (#fund/grants/write[/<draft>]) ---------- */
  function writerGrants() { return F.grants.filter(function (g) { return OPEN.concat(['watching', 'awarded', 'reporting']).indexOf(g.stage) >= 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return OPEN.indexOf(b.stage) - OPEN.indexOf(a.stage) || String(grantTitle(a)).localeCompare(String(grantTitle(b))); }); }
  function parseQuestions(text) {
    return String(text || '').split(/\n+/).map(function (l) { return l.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).map(function (l) {
      var m = /\[(\d{2,5})\s*(?:words?|mots?)?\]\s*$/i.exec(l) || /\((\d{2,5})\s*(?:words?|mots?)\)\s*$/i.exec(l);
      return { q: l.replace(/\s*[\[(]\d{2,5}\s*(?:words?|mots?)?[\])]\s*$/i, '').replace(/^\d+[.)]\s*/, '').trim(), limit: m ? Number(m[1]) : null };
    }).filter(function (x) { return x.q; });
  }
  function questionsText(qs) { return (qs || []).map(function (x) { return x.q + (x.limit ? ' [' + x.limit + ']' : ''); }).join('\n'); }
  function factTitle(key) { var f = F.facts.filter(function (x) { return x.key === key; })[0]; return f ? f.title : key; }
  function factStatusOf(key) { var f = F.facts.filter(function (x) { return x.key === key; })[0]; return f ? f.status : ''; }
  function docTitle(id) { var d = byId(F.docs, id); return d ? d.title : 'document ' + id; }
  function writerHtml() {
    var W = F.writer, d = F.draft, busy = F.busy.write;
    var gs = writerGrants();
    var verified = F.facts.filter(function (f) { return f.status === 'verified'; }).length, check = F.facts.filter(function (f) { return f.status === 'needs_check'; }).length, conflict = F.facts.filter(function (f) { return f.status === 'conflict'; }).length;
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193writer"><div class="w193stack"><div class="w193card w193form"><h3>' + (d ? 'This draft' : 'A new draft') + (d ? '<span class="sp"></span><a class="lnk" data-w193go="#fund/grants/write/new">start another</a>' : '') + '</h3>'
      + '<label>The grant</label><select data-w193wgrant="1"><option value="">No grant: a general draft</option>' + gs.map(function (g) { return '<option value="' + g.id + '"' + (String(W.grantId) === String(g.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(grantTitle(g)) + ' (' + E((STAGE[g.stage] || {}).l || g.stage) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<label>Title</label><input type="text" data-w193wtitle="1" value="' + E(W.title || '') + '" placeholder="' + E(W.grantId && byId(F.grants, W.grantId) ? grantTitle(byId(F.grants, W.grantId)) : 'Draft') + '">'
      + '<div class="row"><div><label>Language</label><select data-w193wlang="1"><option value="en"' + (W.lang !== 'fr' ? ' selected' : '') + '>English</option><option value="fr"' + (W.lang === 'fr' ? ' selected' : '') + '>French</option></select></div><div><label>Status</label><div style="padding-top:8px">' + (d ? '<span class="w193pill" data-st="' + (d.status === 'final' ? 'awarded' : 'drafting') + '">' + E(d.status) + '</span>' : '<span class="w193empty">not saved yet</span>') + '</div></div></div>'
      + '<label>The funder\u2019s questions</label><textarea data-w193wq="1" placeholder="One question per line. A word limit in brackets: What problem do you address? [250]" style="min-height:150px">' + E(W.text) + '</textarea><p class="hint">One per line, in the funder\u2019s words. Add the word limit in brackets. Up to 25.</p>'
      + '<label>Notes for the writer</label><textarea data-w193wnotes="1" placeholder="The project, the amount, the angle, anything the answers must say or avoid">' + E(W.notes || '') + '</textarea>'
      + '<div class="acts">' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn' + (busy ? '" disabled' : '"') + ' data-w193wdraft="1">' + I('pen') + (busy === 'draft' ? 'Drafting\u2026' : d ? 'Draft again' : 'Draft the answers') + '</a>' : '') + (d && canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193wsave="1"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Save</a><a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193wreview="1"' + (busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (busy === 'review' ? 'Reviewing\u2026' : 'Review it') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (busy === 'draft' ? '<p class="hint">The assistant reads the fact base, the playbook and the closest past applications, then writes every answer. One to two minutes.</p>' : busy === 'review' ? '<p class="hint">A program officer\u2019s read of the draft: a score per answer, what is weak, what is unsupported. About a minute.</p>' : '<p class="hint">The writer uses only verified facts (' + verified + ' now; ' + check + ' need a check' + (conflict ? ', ' + conflict + ' in conflict' : '') + ') and ' + F.docs.length + ' past applications and letters. Every number it cannot prove becomes a [confirm: \u2026] placeholder.</p>')
      + '</div>';
    if (d) html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>Keep and share</h3><div class="w193acts" style="margin:0">' + '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193wcopy="1">' + I('copy') + 'Copy all the answers</a>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193wdoc="1" title="Keeps the answers with the past applications the writer learns from">' + I('doc') + 'Save as a past application</a>' + (d.status !== 'final' ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193wfinal="1">' + I('check') + 'Mark final</a>' : '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193wreopen="1">Reopen</a>') : '') + (canDelete() ? '<a class="w193btn warn sm" data-w193wdel="1">Delete</a>' : '') + '</div>' + (d.grant_id ? '<p class="w193note">On the grant: <a class="w193lnk" data-w193go="#fund/grants/' + d.grant_id + '">' + E(byId(F.grants, d.grant_id) ? grantTitle(byId(F.grants, d.grant_id)) : 'open it') + '</a>.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>Your drafts</h3>' + (F.drafts.length ? '<ul class="w193list w193drafts">' + F.drafts.slice(0, 30).map(function (x) { return '<li data-w193go="#fund/grants/write/' + x.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(x.title) + '</b><small>' + E((x.questions || []).length) + ' answer' + ((x.questions || []).length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.lang === 'fr' ? 'French' : 'English') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.status) + (x.updated_by ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(x.updated_by).split('@')[0]) : '') + '</small></div><span class="when">' + E(fmtWhen(x.updated_at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">No draft yet.</p>') + '</div></div>';
    /* the answers */
    html += '<div>';
    if (!d) {
      html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>How the writer works</h3><p class="w193p">Paste the funder\u2019s questions on the left, pick the grant, and the assistant drafts every answer in Welfare Avenue\u2019s voice: plain, concrete, a number in every paragraph that makes a claim, within the word limit. It writes only from the fact base and from what we have sent funders before, and it marks what you must check. You edit the answers here, ask for a program officer\u2019s review, then copy them into the funder\u2019s form.</p>'
        + '<ul class="w193ul"><li><b>Verified facts</b> are used as written and cited under each answer.</li><li><b>Facts that need a check</b> can be used, with a warning naming them.</li><li><b>Figures in conflict</b> (the meals total, the volunteer count, the founding year) are never stated: the draft leaves a [confirm: \u2026] placeholder.</li><li><b>Past applications</b> show how we answered before; the writer reuses what fits, in today\u2019s words, and names them under the answer.</li><li>The rules come from the playbook: lead with the answer, mirror the funder\u2019s words, outputs apart from outcomes, no jargon, no em dashes.</li></ul>'
        + '<div class="w193acts"><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#fund/grants/facts">' + I('star') + 'The fact base</a><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193go="#fund/grants/playbook">' + I('book') + 'The playbook</a></div></div>';
    } else {
      var qs = d.questions || [];
      if (W.result && W.result.reviewer_note) html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-bottom:12px"><h3>The writer\u2019s note</h3><p class="w193p" style="margin:0">' + E(W.result.reviewer_note) + '</p>' + (W.result.consistency && W.result.consistency.length ? '<ul class="w193ul" style="margin-top:8px">' + W.result.consistency.map(function (c) { return '<li>' + E(c) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') + (W.result.cost ? '<p class="w193note">Model ' + E(W.result.model || '') + ', $' + Number(W.result.cost).toFixed(2) + '.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
      if (W.review) {
        var R = W.review;
        html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-bottom:12px;border-color:rgba(200,122,47,.4)"><h3>The program officer\u2019s review<span class="sp"></span><a class="lnk" data-w193wreviewx="1">hide</a></h3><p class="w193p">' + E(R.overall || '') + '</p>' + (R.missing && R.missing.length ? '<p class="w193p"><b>Missing:</b> ' + E(R.missing.join('; ')) + '</p>' : '') + (R.consistency && R.consistency.length ? '<p class="w193p"><b>Consistency:</b> ' + E(R.consistency.join('; ')) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
      }
      qs.forEach(function (x, i) {
        var over = x.limit && x.words > x.limit, rv = W.review && W.review.answers ? (W.review.answers[i] || W.review.answers.filter(function (a) { return a.q === x.q; })[0]) : null;
        html += '<div class="w193ans" data-w193ans="' + i + '"><div class="q"><span>' + (i + 1) + '. ' + E(x.q) + '</span>' + (x.limit ? '<small>' + x.limit + ' words</small>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<textarea data-w193answer="' + i + '"' + (canEdit() ? '' : ' readonly') + '>' + E(x.answer || '') + '</textarea>'
          + '<div class="ft"><span class="cnt' + (over ? ' over' : '') + '" data-w193count="' + i + '">' + words(x.answer) + (x.limit ? ' of ' + x.limit : '') + ' words</span>' + (x.refs && x.refs.length ? '<span>\u00b7 from ' + E(x.refs.map(docTitle).join(', ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
          + (x.facts && x.facts.length ? '<div class="facts">' + x.facts.map(function (k) { var st = factStatusOf(k); return '<span class="w193tag' + (st === 'verified' ? '' : '') + '" style="' + (st === 'needs_check' ? '--bg:rgba(220,165,39,.2);--ink:#6b4d05' : st === 'conflict' ? '--bg:rgba(179,64,47,.12);--ink:' + FLAG : '') + '" title="' + E(k) + '">' + E(factTitle(k)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
          + (x.warnings && x.warnings.length ? '<div class="warn"><b>Check before sending:</b> ' + E(x.warnings.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>' : '')
          + (rv ? '<div class="warn" style="background:rgba(111,100,171,.1);color:#4a4180"><b>Review ' + E(rv.score) + '/10.</b> ' + E(rv.what_works || '') + (rv.what_is_weak ? ' <b>Weak:</b> ' + E(rv.what_is_weak) : '') + (rv.unsupported && rv.unsupported.length ? ' <b>Unsupported:</b> ' + E(rv.unsupported.join('; ')) : '') + (rv.suggestion ? ' <b>Do this:</b> ' + E(rv.suggestion) : '') + '</div>' : '')
          + '</div>';
      });
      if (d.notes && !W.result) html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>Notes on this draft</h3><p class="w193p" style="white-space:pre-wrap;margin:0;font-size:13px">' + E(d.notes) + '</p></div>';
    }
    html += '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function readWriterForm() {
    var h = document.getElementById('w193host'); if (!h) return;
    var g = h.querySelector('[data-w193wgrant]'), t = h.querySelector('[data-w193wtitle]'), l = h.querySelector('[data-w193wlang]'), qq = h.querySelector('[data-w193wq]'), n = h.querySelector('[data-w193wnotes]');
    if (g) F.writer.grantId = g.value || null;
    if (t) F.writer.title = t.value;
    if (l) F.writer.lang = l.value;
    if (qq) F.writer.text = qq.value;
    if (n) F.writer.notes = n.value;
  }
  function readAnswers() {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return;
    (d.questions || []).forEach(function (x, i) { var ta = document.querySelector('#w193host [data-w193answer="' + i + '"]'); if (ta) { x.answer = ta.value; x.words = words(ta.value); } });
  }
  function openDraft(id) {
    var d = id ? byId(F.drafts, id) : null;
    F.draft = d ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d)) : null;
    F.writer.review = null; F.writer.result = null;
    if (d) { F.writer.grantId = d.grant_id || null; F.writer.lang = d.lang || 'en'; F.writer.text = questionsText(d.questions); F.writer.title = d.title || ''; F.writer.notes = ''; }
    else if (!id) { F.writer.text = F.writer.text || ''; F.writer.title = ''; }
  }
  async function draftNow() {
    if (F.busy.write) return;
    readWriterForm();
    var qs = parseQuestions(F.writer.text);
    if (!qs.length) { toastS('Paste the funder\u2019s questions first, one per line.'); return; }
    F.busy.write = 'draft'; paint();
    var r = await fn('grant-write', { action: 'draft', grant_id: F.writer.grantId ? Number(F.writer.grantId) : null, title: F.writer.title || null, lang: F.writer.lang, notes: F.writer.notes, questions: qs, draft_id: F.draft ? F.draft.id : null });
    F.busy.write = null;
    if (!r || r.ok === false || r.error) { toastS('The draft did not come back: ' + (r && r.error ? r.error : 'try again')); paint(); return; }
    await loadAll(true);
    F.draft = byId(F.drafts, r.draft_id) ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(byId(F.drafts, r.draft_id))) : { id: r.draft_id, title: r.title, lang: r.lang, questions: r.questions, status: 'draft', grant_id: F.writer.grantId };
    F.writer.result = { reviewer_note: r.reviewer_note, consistency: r.consistency, cost: r.cost, model: r.model };
    F.writer.review = null;
    toastS('Drafted ' + (r.questions || []).length + ' answers. Read every warning before you send.');
    if (location.hash !== '#fund/grants/write/' + r.draft_id) { F.sub = String(r.draft_id); history.replaceState(null, '', '#fund/grants/write/' + r.draft_id); }
    paint();
  }
  async function saveDraft(quiet) {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return false;
    readAnswers(); readWriterForm();
    var rec = { title: F.writer.title || d.title, lang: F.writer.lang || d.lang, questions: d.questions, grant_id: F.writer.grantId ? Number(F.writer.grantId) : null, status: d.status, updated_by: me().email };
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_drafts').update(rec).eq('id', d.id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return false; }
    d.title = rec.title; d.lang = rec.lang; d.grant_id = rec.grant_id;
    var i = F.drafts.findIndex(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(d.id); }); if (i >= 0) F.drafts[i] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d));
    if (!quiet) toastS('Saved.');
    return true;
  }
  async function reviewNow() {
    if (F.busy.write || !F.draft) return;
    if (!(await saveDraft(true))) return;
    F.busy.write = 'review'; paint();
    var r = await fn('grant-write', { action: 'review', draft_id: F.draft.id });
    F.busy.write = null;
    if (!r || r.ok === false || r.error) { toastS('The review did not come back: ' + (r && r.error ? r.error : 'try again')); paint(); return; }
    F.writer.review = r.review || null; paint();
  }
  function copyAnswers() {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return; readAnswers();
    var text = (d.questions || []).map(function (x, i) { return (i + 1) + '. ' + x.q + (x.limit ? ' [' + x.limit + ' words]' : '') + '\n\n' + (x.answer || ''); }).join('\n\n\n');
    var done = function () { toastS('Copied: ' + (d.questions || []).length + ' answers.'); };
    try { if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, function () { fallbackCopy(text); done(); }); return; } } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    fallbackCopy(text); done();
  }
  function fallbackCopy(text) { try { var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function saveAsDocument() {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return; readAnswers();
    var g = d.grant_id ? byId(F.grants, d.grant_id) : null;
    var text = (d.questions || []).map(function (x) { return 'Q: ' + x.q + (x.limit ? ' [' + x.limit + ' words]' : '') + '\nA: ' + (x.answer || ''); }).join('\n\n');
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_documents').insert({ grant_id: d.grant_id || null, funder_id: g ? g.funder_id : null, title: d.title, kind: 'application', lang: d.lang || 'en', doc_date: ymd(today()), text: text, words: words(text), source: 'The grant writer (draft ' + d.id + ')', created_by: me().email }));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    if (d.grant_id) await q(db.from('fund_grant_log').insert({ grant_id: d.grant_id, who: me().email, kind: 'draft', text: 'Saved draft ' + d.id + ' as a past application (' + d.title + ').' }));
    await loadAll(true); paint(); toastS('Saved with the past applications: the writer learns from it now.');
  }
  async function setDraftStatus(st) {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return; d.status = st;
    if (await saveDraft(true)) { toastS(st === 'final' ? 'Marked final.' : 'Reopened.'); paint(); }
  }
  async function deleteDraft() {
    var d = F.draft; if (!d) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_drafts').delete().eq('id', d.id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    F.draft = null; await loadAll(true); go('#fund/grants/write'); toastS('Deleted.');
  }

  /* ---------- the playbook (#fund/grants/playbook): markdown with tables ---------- */
  function inlineMd(s) {
    var h = E(s);
    h = h.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
    h = h.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>').replace(/(^|[\s(])\*([^*\n]+)\*/g, '$1<i>$2</i>').replace(/(^|[\s(])_([^_\n]+)_/g, '$1<i>$2</i>');
    h = h.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
    return h;
  }
  function mdToHtml(md) {
    var lines = String(md || '').replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n'), out = [], i = 0, toc = [];
    var slug = function (t) { return 'w193h-' + fold(t).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 60); };
    var closeList = function (L) { while (L.length) out.push(L.pop() === 'ol' ? '</ol>' : '</ul>'); };
    var L = [];
    while (i < lines.length) {
      var ln = lines[i];
      if (/^\s*$/.test(ln)) { closeList(L); i++; continue; }
      var hm = /^(#{1,4})\s+(.*)$/.exec(ln);
      if (hm) { closeList(L); var lvl = hm[1].length, t = hm[2].trim(), id = slug(t); if (lvl <= 2) toc.push({ id: id, t: t, lvl: lvl }); out.push('<h' + lvl + ' id="' + id + '">' + inlineMd(t) + '</h' + lvl + '>'); i++; continue; }
      if (/^\s*(-{3,}|\*{3,})\s*$/.test(ln)) { closeList(L); out.push('<hr>'); i++; continue; }
      if (/^\s*\|.*\|\s*$/.test(ln) && i + 1 < lines.length && /^\s*\|?\s*:?-{2,}/.test(lines[i + 1])) {
        closeList(L);
        var cells = function (r) { return r.trim().replace(/^\|/, '').replace(/\|$/, '').split('|').map(function (c) { return c.trim(); }); };
        var head = cells(ln); i += 2; var rows = [];
        while (i < lines.length && /^\s*\|.*\|\s*$/.test(lines[i])) { rows.push(cells(lines[i])); i++; }
        out.push('<table><thead><tr>' + head.map(function (c) { return '<th>' + inlineMd(c) + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<tr>' + r.map(function (c) { return '<td>' + inlineMd(c) + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table>');
        continue;
      }
      if (/^\s*>\s?/.test(ln)) { closeList(L); var qb = []; while (i < lines.length && /^\s*>\s?/.test(lines[i])) { qb.push(lines[i].replace(/^\s*>\s?/, '')); i++; } out.push('<blockquote>' + inlineMd(qb.join(' ')) + '</blockquote>'); continue; }
      var lm = /^(\s*)([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$/.exec(ln);
      if (lm) {
        var kind = /\d/.test(lm[2]) ? 'ol' : 'ul', depth = Math.floor(lm[1].replace(/\t/g, '  ').length / 2) + 1;
        while (L.length > depth) out.push(L.pop() === 'ol' ? '</ol>' : '</ul>');
        while (L.length < depth) { L.push(kind); out.push(kind === 'ol' ? '<ol>' : '<ul>'); }
        var item = lm[3]; i++;
        while (i < lines.length && /^\s{2,}\S/.test(lines[i]) && !/^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+/.test(lines[i])) { item += ' ' + lines[i].trim(); i++; }
        out.push('<li>' + inlineMd(item) + '</li>');
        continue;
      }
      closeList(L);
      var para = [ln.trim()]; i++;
      while (i < lines.length && !/^\s*$/.test(lines[i]) && !/^(#{1,4})\s/.test(lines[i]) && !/^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+/.test(lines[i]) && !/^\s*\|/.test(lines[i]) && !/^\s*>/.test(lines[i])) { para.push(lines[i].trim()); i++; }
      out.push('<p>' + inlineMd(para.join(' ')) + '</p>');
    }
    closeList(L);
    return { html: out.join('\n'), toc: toc };
  }
  function playbookHtml() {
    var P = setting('playbook') || {}, md = String(P.markdown || '');
    if (!md) return gTabs() + '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">The playbook is not written yet.</p></div>';
    var r = mdToHtml(md);
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193side"><div class="w193card"><div class="w193md">' + r.html + '</div>' + (P.written ? '<p class="w193note">Written ' + E(fmtDate(P.written)) + ' from the sector\u2019s guidance and our own record; the full sources are at the end.' + (isLeader() ? ' Leaders change it in the database (fund_settings.playbook) for now.' : '') + '</p>' : '') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w193stack"><div class="w193card w193toc"><h3>On this page</h3>' + r.toc.filter(function (t) { return t.lvl === 2; }).map(function (t) { return '<a data-w193anchor="' + t.id + '">' + E(t.t.replace(/^\d+\.\s*/, '')) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w193card"><h3>Use it</h3><ul class="w193ul"><li><b>Screen a grant</b> in its sheet: Score the fit runs the gates and the seven dimensions for you.</li><li><b>Draft</b> in the writer: the rules and the section skeletons are in the prompt.</li><li><b>Keep the pipeline</b> at eight to twelve applications a year, with three in the pipeline for every one you need to win.</li></ul></div></div></div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the fact base (#fund/grants/facts) ---------- */
  function factsHtml() {
    var FL = F.filters, qq = fold(FL.q);
    var list = F.facts.filter(function (f) { return (!FL.fstatus || f.status === FL.fstatus) && (!FL.fcat || f.category === FL.fcat) && (!qq || qq.split(/\s+/).every(function (w) { return fold([f.title, f.body, f.key, f.source, f.notes].join(' ')).indexOf(w) >= 0; })); });
    var counts = {}; F.facts.forEach(function (f) { counts[f.status] = (counts[f.status] || 0) + 1; });
    var html = gTabs() + '<div class="w193bar"><div class="w193seg">' + [['', 'All'], ['verified', 'Verified'], ['needs_check', 'Needs a check'], ['conflict', 'In conflict'], ['retired', 'Retired']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w193fstatus="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (FL.fstatus === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + (x[0] ? ' <span class="n">' + (counts[x[0]] || 0) + '</span>' : '') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<input type="search" placeholder="Search the facts\u2026" value="' + E(FL.q) + '" data-w193q="1" aria-label="Search the facts">'
      + '<select data-w193fcat="1" aria-label="Category"><option value="">Every category</option>' + FACT_CATS.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + c[0] + '"' + (FL.fcat === c[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn sm" data-w193factadd="1">' + I('plus') + 'Add a fact</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (counts.conflict) html += '<div class="w193card" style="margin-bottom:14px;border-color:rgba(179,64,47,.3)"><p class="w193empty"><b>' + counts.conflict + ' fact' + (counts.conflict > 1 ? 's are' : ' is') + ' in conflict:</b> the documents disagree, so the writer never states those numbers. Settle each one (the sheet shows what the sources say), then mark it verified.</p></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193facts">';
    FACT_CATS.forEach(function (c) {
      var rows = list.filter(function (f) { return f.category === c[0]; }); if (!rows.length) return;
      html += '<div class="w193card"><h3>' + E(c[1]) + '<span class="sp"></span><span class="lnk">' + rows.length + '</span></h3>' + rows.map(function (f) {
        var st = FACT_ST[f.status] || FACT_ST.needs_check;
        return '<div class="w193fact" data-w193fact="' + f.id + '"><div class="k">' + E(f.title) + '<span class="w193pill ' + (f.status === 'verified' ? 'v' : f.status === 'conflict' ? 'x' : f.status === 'retired' ? 'r' : 'c') + '">' + E(st[0]) + '</span><code>' + E(f.key) + '</code></div><div class="acts">' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193factedit="' + f.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit</a>' + (f.status !== 'verified' && f.status !== 'retired' ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193factok="' + f.id + '">' + I('check') + 'Verified</a>' : '') : '') + '</div><div class="b">' + E(f.body) + '</div>' + (f.body_fr ? '<div class="b" style="color:var(--wa-muted)">FR: ' + E(f.body_fr) + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="src">' + (f.source ? 'Source: ' + E(f.source) : '') + (f.verified_on ? ' \u00b7 verified ' + E(fmtDate(f.verified_on)) + (f.verified_by ? ' by ' + E(String(f.verified_by).split('@')[0]) : '') : '') + (f.notes ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(f.notes) : '') + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    if (!list.length) html += '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">Nothing matches.</p></div>';
    return html;
  }
  async function verifyFact(id) {
    var f = byId(F.facts, id); if (!f) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_facts').update({ status: 'verified', verified_on: ymd(today()), verified_by: me().email, updated_by: me().email }).eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    f.status = 'verified'; f.verified_on = ymd(today()); f.verified_by = me().email; paint(); toastS('Marked verified.');
  }

  /* ---------- the side sheet: a grant, a funder, a document, and the forms ---------- */
  function sheetEl() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w193sheet');
    if (!s) {
      var sc = document.createElement('div'); sc.className = 'w193scrim'; sc.id = 'w193scrim'; sc.setAttribute('data-w193close', '1'); document.body.appendChild(sc);
      s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w193sheet'; s.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); s.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); document.body.appendChild(s);
    }
    return s;
  }
  function showSheet(title, sub, body) {
    var s = sheetEl(), sc = document.getElementById('w193scrim');
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><h2>' + title + (sub ? '<small>' + sub + '</small>' : '') + '</h2><button type="button" class="x" data-w193close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div><div class="bd">' + body + '</div>';
    s.classList.add('open'); if (sc) sc.classList.add('open');
    F.open = true;
    try { var f = s.querySelector('input,select,textarea,button:not(.x)'); if (f && window.innerWidth > 900) f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w193sheet'), sc = document.getElementById('w193scrim');
    if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); if (sc) sc.classList.remove('open');
    F.open = null; F.sheet = null;
    if (/^#fund\/grants\/\d+$/.test(location.hash) || /^#fund\/grants\/funders\/\d+$/.test(location.hash)) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', location.hash.replace(/\/\d+$/, '')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } F.sub = ''; }
  }
  function kv(rows) { return '<div class="w193kv">' + rows.filter(function (r) { return r[1] !== '' && r[1] != null; }).map(function (r) { return '<small>' + E(r[0]) + '</small><div>' + (r[2] ? r[1] : E(r[1])) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }
  function linkHtml(l) { var u = safeUrl(l.url || l); if (!u) return ''; return '<a class="w193lnk" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' ' + E(l.label || u.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').slice(0, 60)) + '</a>'; }
  function gateIcon(r) { return r === 'pass' ? '<span class="ok">' + I('check') + '</span>' : r === 'fail' ? '<span class="no">' + I('x') + '</span>' : '<span class="unk">' + I('warn') + '</span>'; }
  function fitSection(g) {
    var fit = g.fit || {}, gates = Array.isArray(g.gates) ? g.gates : [], dims = Array.isArray(fit.dimensions) ? fit.dimensions : [];
    var html = '<div class="w193sec"><h4>The fit' + (g.fit_score ? ' <span class="w193fit ' + fitClass(g.fit_score) + '">' + g.fit_score + '</span>' + (fit.verdict ? ' <span class="w193pill" data-st="' + (fit.verdict === 'go' ? 'awarded' : fit.verdict === 'conditional' ? 'screened' : 'declined') + '">' + E(fit.verdict) + '</span>' : '') : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193fit="' + g.id + '"' + (F.busy.fit ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('target') + (F.busy.fit ? 'Scoring\u2026' : g.fit_score ? 'Score it again' : 'Score the fit') + '</a>' : '') + '</h4>';
    if (!g.fit_score && !gates.length) html += '<p class="w193empty">Not scored yet. Score the fit runs the playbook\u2019s screen: the hard gates, then eligibility, alignment, geography, amount, capacity, relationship and odds (70 and up: go; 55 to 69: conditional).</p>';
    if (fit.scanner_note) html += '<p class="w193note" style="margin:0 0 8px"><b>The scanner:</b> ' + E(fit.scanner_note) + '</p>';
    if (gates.length) html += '<ul class="w193gates" style="list-style:none;margin:0 0 8px;padding:0">' + gates.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + gateIcon(x.result) + '<span><b>' + E(x.gate) + '</b>' + (x.note ? ': ' + E(x.note) : '') + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>';
    if (dims.length) html += '<div class="w193dims">' + dims.map(function (d) { return '<span>' + E(String(d.name || '').replace(/^\w/, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); })) + ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">(' + E(d.weight) + ')</span></span><b>' + E(d.score) + '/5</b>' + (d.note ? '<small>' + E(d.note) + '</small>' : ''); }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (fit.first_step) html += '<p class="w193p" style="margin:8px 0 0;font-size:13.5px"><b>First step:</b> ' + E(fit.first_step) + (fit.hours ? ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">(about ' + E(fit.hours) + ' volunteer hours)</span>' : '') + '</p>';
    if (fit.scored_at) html += '<p class="w193note">Scored ' + E(fmtWhen(fit.scored_at)) + (fit.by ? ' by ' + E(String(fit.by).split('@')[0]) : '') + '.</p>';
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function grantSheetHtml(g) {
    var f = funderOf(g) || {}, log = F.log[g.id], docs = F.docs.filter(function (d) { return String(d.grant_id) === String(g.id) || (!d.grant_id && d.funder_id && String(d.funder_id) === String(g.funder_id)); }), drafts = F.drafts.filter(function (d) { return String(d.grant_id) === String(g.id); });
    var links = Array.isArray(g.links) ? g.links : [];
    var html = '<div class="w193acts" style="margin:0 0 14px">' + (canEdit() ? '<select data-w193stage="' + g.id + '" aria-label="Stage" style="font-family:inherit;font-size:13px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:10px;background:var(--wa-card)">' + STAGES.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s[0] + '"' + (g.stage === s[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(s[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193gedit="' + g.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit</a><a class="w193btn sm" data-w193go="#fund/grants/write/new?grant=' + g.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Write answers</a>' : '') + '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193funder="' + g.funder_id + '">' + I('db') + 'The funder</a></div>';
    html += kv([
      ['Funder', f.name || ''], ['Program', g.program], ['Our project', g.title || ''], ['Ask', g.amount_ask != null ? money(g.amount_ask) : (f.amount_min || f.amount_max ? 'They give ' + (f.amount_min && f.amount_max ? kMoney(f.amount_min) + ' to ' + kMoney(f.amount_max) : 'up to ' + kMoney(f.amount_max || f.amount_min)) : '')], ['Awarded', g.amount_awarded != null ? money(g.amount_awarded) : ''],
      ['Deadline', g.deadline ? fmtDate(g.deadline) + ' (' + dueText(g.deadline) + ')' + (g.deadline_note ? ' \u00b7 ' + g.deadline_note : '') : (g.deadline_note || (f.cycle ? 'Cycle: ' + f.cycle : ''))],
      ['Submitted', g.submitted_on ? fmtDate(g.submitted_on) : ''], ['Decided', g.decided_on ? fmtDate(g.decided_on) + (g.decision_note ? ': ' + g.decision_note : '') : (g.decision_note || '')],
      ['Report due', g.report_due ? fmtDate(g.report_due) + ' (' + dueText(g.report_due) + ')' : ''], ['Renewal', g.renewal_due ? fmtDate(g.renewal_due) : ''],
      ['Next step', g.next_action ? g.next_action + (g.next_action_due ? ' \u00b7 ' + fmtDate(g.next_action_due) + ' (' + dueText(g.next_action_due) + ')' : '') : ''],
      ['Owner', g.owner_name || g.owner_email || ''], ['Partner', g.partner || ''], ['For', g.program_area || ''], ['Project', g.project_id ? '<a class="w193lnk" data-w193go="#project/' + g.project_id + '">The project board</a>' : '', true], ['Hours spent', g.hours_spent != null ? String(g.hours_spent) : ''], ['Source', [g.source, g.source_ref].filter(Boolean).join(', ')]
    ]);
    if (links.length || f.url || f.portal) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Links</h4><div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;font-size:13.5px">' + links.map(linkHtml).join('') + (f.url ? linkHtml({ label: f.name + ' (website)', url: f.url }) : '') + (f.portal ? linkHtml({ label: 'The application portal', url: f.portal }) : '') + '</div></div>';
    html += fitSection(g);
    if (f.eligibility || f.funds_what || f.fit_note) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>About the funder</h4>' + (f.funds_what ? '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>They fund:</b> ' + E(f.funds_what) + '</p>' : '') + (f.eligibility ? '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>Eligibility:</b> ' + E(f.eligibility) + '</p>' : '') + (f.fit_note ? '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>Fit:</b> ' + E(f.fit_note) + '</p>' : '') + (f.relationship ? '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>Our history:</b> ' + E(f.relationship) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (g.notes) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Notes</h4><p class="w193p" style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13.5px">' + E(g.notes) + '</p></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Applications and letters on file<span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193docadd="' + g.id + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add text</a>' : '') + '</h4>' + (docs.length ? '<ul class="w193list">' + docs.map(function (d) { return '<li data-w193doc="' + d.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(d.title) + '</b><small>' + E(d.kind) + (d.doc_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDate(d.doc_date)) : '') + (d.words ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(d.words) + ' words' : '') + '</small></div>' + I('chevr') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">Nothing on file for this grant yet. Add the application text so the writer can learn from it.</p>') + '</div>';
    if (drafts.length) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Drafts</h4><ul class="w193list">' + drafts.map(function (d) { return '<li data-w193go="#fund/grants/write/' + d.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(d.title) + '</b><small>' + E((d.questions || []).length) + ' answers \u00b7 ' + E(d.status) + '</small></div><span class="when">' + E(fmtWhen(d.updated_at)) + '</span></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>What happened</h4>' + (canEdit() ? '<form class="w193form" data-w193logform="' + g.id + '" style="margin:0 0 10px"><div class="row" style="grid-template-columns:120px 1fr"><select name="kind"><option value="note">Note</option><option value="call">Call</option><option value="email">Email</option><option value="report">Report</option></select><input type="text" name="text" placeholder="What happened, in a line" required maxlength="1000"></div><div class="acts" style="margin-top:8px"><button type="submit" class="w193btn sm">Add</button></div></form>' : '')
      + (log === undefined ? '<p class="w193empty">Loading\u2026</p>' : log.length ? '<ul class="w193list w193log">' + log.map(function (l) { return '<li><span class="k">' + E(l.kind) + '</span><div class="t"><span>' + E(l.text) + '</span><small>' + E(fmtWhen(l.at)) + (l.who ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(l.who).split('@')[0]) : '') + '</small></div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">Nothing logged yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    if (canDelete()) html += '<div class="w193acts" style="margin-top:18px"><a class="w193btn warn sm" data-w193gdel="' + g.id + '">Delete this grant</a></div>';
    return html;
  }
  async function openGrant(id) {
    var g = byId(F.grants, id); if (!g) { toastS('That grant is not here any more.'); return; }
    F.sheet = { kind: 'grant', id: g.id };
    showSheet(E(grantTitle(g)), (g.title && g.program ? E(g.program) + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + stagePill(g.stage) + ' ' + fitHtml(g.fit_score), grantSheetHtml(g));
    if (F.log[g.id] === undefined) { await loadLog(g.id); if (F.sheet && F.sheet.kind === 'grant' && String(F.sheet.id) === String(g.id)) refreshSheet(); }
  }
  function refreshSheet() {
    var s = F.sheet; if (!s) return;
    if (s.kind === 'grant') { var g = byId(F.grants, s.id); if (g) { var bd = document.querySelector('#w193sheet .bd'); if (bd) bd.innerHTML = grantSheetHtml(g); var h = document.querySelector('#w193sheet .hd h2'); if (h) h.innerHTML = E(grantTitle(g)) + '<small>' + (g.title && g.program ? E(g.program) + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + stagePill(g.stage) + ' ' + fitHtml(g.fit_score) + '</small>'; } }
    if (s.kind === 'funder') { var f = byId(F.funders, s.id); if (f) openFunder(f.id); }
  }
  async function setStage(id, stage) {
    var g = byId(F.grants, id); if (!g || g.stage === stage) return;
    var r = await q(db.rpc('fund_grant_stage', { p_grant: Number(id), p_stage: stage, p_note: null }));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not move it: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); refreshSheet(); return; }
    if (stage === 'awarded' && g.amount_awarded == null && g.amount_ask != null) await q(db.from('fund_grants').update({ amount_awarded: g.amount_ask, updated_by: me().email }).eq('id', id));
    await loadAll(true); await loadLog(id);
    toastS('Moved to ' + (STAGE[stage] || {}).l + (['awarded', 'declined', 'not_eligible', 'dropped'].indexOf(stage) >= 0 ? '. Add a line on the decision below.' : '.'));
    if (onFund()) paint(); refreshSheet();
  }
  async function scoreFit(id) {
    if (F.busy.fit) return;
    F.busy.fit = true; refreshSheet();
    var r = await fn('grant-write', { action: 'fit', grant_id: Number(id) });
    F.busy.fit = false;
    if (!r || r.ok === false || r.error) { toastS('The score did not come back: ' + (r && r.error ? r.error : 'try again')); refreshSheet(); return; }
    await loadAll(true); await loadLog(id);
    toastS('Fit ' + r.fit_score + ' (' + (r.fit && r.fit.verdict ? r.fit.verdict : '') + ').');
    if (onFund()) paint(); refreshSheet();
  }
  async function addLog(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), id = form.getAttribute('data-w193logform'), text = String(fd.get('text') || '').trim(); if (!text) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_grant_log').insert({ grant_id: Number(id), who: me().email, kind: String(fd.get('kind') || 'note'), text: text }));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    await loadLog(id); await loadAll(true); refreshSheet();
  }
  async function deleteGrant(id) {
    var g = byId(F.grants, id); if (!g) return;
    var ok = true; try { ok = window.confirm('Delete "' + grantTitle(g) + '" and its log? This cannot be undone.'); } catch (e) { ok = true; }
    if (!ok) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_grants').delete().eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    closeSheet(); await loadAll(true); paint(); toastS('Deleted.');
  }

  /* ---------- a document (the text of a past application) ---------- */
  async function openDocument(id, back) {
    var d = byId(F.docs, id); if (!d) return;
    F.sheet = { kind: 'doc', id: id };
    showSheet(E(d.title), E(d.kind) + (d.doc_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDate(d.doc_date)) : '') + (d.words ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(d.words) + ' words' : ''), '<p class="w193empty">Loading\u2026</p>');
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_documents').select('text, source_url, source, created_by').eq('id', id).maybeSingle());
    if (!F.sheet || F.sheet.kind !== 'doc' || String(F.sheet.id) !== String(id)) return;
    var bd = document.querySelector('#w193sheet .bd'); if (!bd) return;
    var t = r.data || {};
    bd.innerHTML = '<div class="w193acts" style="margin:0 0 12px">' + (back ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193grant="' + back + '">' + I('chevl') + 'Back to the grant</a>' : '') + (t.source_url && safeUrl(t.source_url) ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" href="' + E(t.source_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + 'The original</a>' : '') + '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193doccopy="' + id + '">' + I('copy') + 'Copy the text</a>' + (canDelete() ? '<a class="w193btn warn sm" data-w193docdel="' + id + '">Delete</a>' : '') + '</div>'
      + (t.source ? '<p class="w193note" style="margin:0 0 10px">Source: ' + E(t.source) + '</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="w193p" style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.6" data-w193doctext="1">' + E(t.text || '(no text)') + '</div>';
  }
  async function deleteDocument(id) {
    var ok = true; try { ok = window.confirm('Delete this document? The writer will no longer learn from it.'); } catch (e) { ok = true; }
    if (!ok) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_documents').delete().eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    closeSheet(); await loadAll(true); paint(); toastS('Deleted.');
  }

  /* ---------- a funder ---------- */
  function funderSheetHtml(f) {
    var gs = F.grants.filter(function (g) { return g.funder_id === f.id; }).sort(grantSort), docs = F.docs.filter(function (d) { return String(d.funder_id) === String(f.id); });
    var fit = FIT[f.fit] || FIT.unknown;
    var html = '<div class="w193acts" style="margin:0 0 14px">' + (canEdit() ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193fedit="' + f.id + '">' + I('pen') + 'Edit</a><a class="w193btn sm" data-w193gadd="' + f.id + '">' + I('plus') + 'Add a grant here</a>' + (!f.screened_out ? '<a class="w193btn ghost sm" data-w193fwatch="' + f.id + '">' + I('search') + (f.watch ? 'Stop watching' : 'Watch it') + '</a>' : '') + '<a class="w193btn ' + (f.screened_out ? 'ghost' : 'warn') + ' sm" data-w193fscreen="' + f.id + '">' + (f.screened_out ? 'Back in play' : 'Screen out') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 12px"><span class="w193tag">' + E(KINDS[f.kind] || f.kind) + '</span><span class="w193tag">' + E(REGIONS[f.region] || f.region) + '</span><span class="w193tag" style="--bg:' + fit[1] + ';--ink:' + fit[2] + '">' + E(fit[0]) + '</span>' + (f.watch && !f.screened_out ? '<span class="w193tag">' + I('search') + 'on the watchlist</span>' : '') + (f.screened_out ? '<span class="w193pill" data-st="not_eligible">Screened out</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (f.screened_out && f.screened_reason) html += '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>Why not for us:</b> ' + E(f.screened_reason) + '</p>';
    if (f.fit_note) html += '<p class="w193p" style="font-size:13.5px"><b>Fit:</b> ' + E(f.fit_note) + '</p>';
    html += kv([['Website', f.url && safeUrl(f.url) ? linkHtml({ label: f.url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''), url: f.url }) : '', true], ['Portal', f.portal && safeUrl(f.portal) ? linkHtml({ label: f.portal.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''), url: f.portal }) : '', true], ['Contact', f.contact || ''], ['They fund', f.funds_what || ''], ['Eligibility', f.eligibility || ''], ['Cycle', f.cycle || ''], ['Next deadline', f.next_deadline ? fmtDate(f.next_deadline) + ' (' + dueText(f.next_deadline) + ')' : ''], ['Amounts', f.amount_min || f.amount_max ? (f.amount_min && f.amount_max ? money(f.amount_min) + ' to ' + money(f.amount_max) : 'up to ' + money(f.amount_max || f.amount_min)) : ''], ['Our history', f.relationship || ''], ['Also called', (f.aliases || []).join(', ')], ['Source', f.source || '']]);
    if (f.notes) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Notes</h4><p class="w193p" style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13.5px">' + E(f.notes) + '</p></div>';
    html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>Grants with this funder</h4>' + (gs.length ? '<ul class="w193list">' + gs.map(function (g) { return '<li data-w193grant="' + g.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(g.program) + (g.title ? ': ' + E(g.title) : '') + '</b><small>' + E(amountText(g)) + (g.deadline ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDate(g.deadline)) : '') + (g.decided_on ? ' \u00b7 decided ' + E(fmtDate(g.decided_on)) : '') + '</small></div>' + stagePill(g.stage) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w193empty">None yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    if (docs.length) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>On file</h4><ul class="w193list">' + docs.map(function (d) { return '<li data-w193doc="' + d.id + '"><div class="t"><b>' + E(d.title) + '</b><small>' + E(d.kind) + (d.doc_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDate(d.doc_date)) : '') + '</small></div>' + I('chevr') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    var srcs = F.sources.filter(function (s) { return String(s.funder_id) === String(f.id); });
    if (srcs.length) html += '<div class="w193sec"><h4>The scanner reads</h4><ul class="w193list static">' + srcs.map(function (s) { return '<li><div class="t"><b>' + E(s.name) + '</b><small>' + (s.last_checked ? 'checked ' + E(fmtWhen(s.last_checked)) : 'not read yet') + (s.last_error ? ' \u00b7 <span style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(clip(s.last_error, 80)) + '</span>' : '') + (s.last_summary ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(clip(s.last_summary, 140)) : '') + '</small></div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
    if (canDelete() && !gs.length) html += '<div class="w193acts" style="margin-top:18px"><a class="w193btn warn sm" data-w193fdel="' + f.id + '">Delete this funder</a></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function openFunder(id) {
    var f = byId(F.funders, id); if (!f) { toastS('That funder is not here any more.'); return; }
    F.sheet = { kind: 'funder', id: f.id };
    showSheet(E(f.name), E(KINDS[f.kind] || f.kind) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(REGIONS[f.region] || f.region), funderSheetHtml(f));
  }
  async function toggleWatch(id) {
    var f = byId(F.funders, id); if (!f) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_funders').update({ watch: !f.watch, updated_by: me().email }).eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    await loadAll(true); if (onFund()) paint(); openFunder(id); toastS(!f.watch ? 'On the watchlist.' : 'Off the watchlist.');
  }
  async function toggleScreen(id) {
    var f = byId(F.funders, id); if (!f) return;
    if (!f.screened_out) { var c = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(f)); c.screened_out = true; c.watch = false; openFunderForm(c); toastS('Say why it is not for us, then Save.'); return; }
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_funders').update({ screened_out: false, updated_by: me().email }).eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    await loadAll(true); if (onFund()) paint(); openFunder(id); toastS('Back in play.');
  }
  async function deleteFunder(id) {
    var f = byId(F.funders, id); if (!f) return;
    var ok = true; try { ok = window.confirm('Delete "' + f.name + '"?'); } catch (e) { ok = true; }
    if (!ok) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_funders').delete().eq('id', id));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not delete: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    closeSheet(); await loadAll(true); paint(); toastS('Deleted.');
  }

  /* ---------- forms ---------- */
  function inp(name, label, value, type, extra) { return '<label>' + E(label) + '</label><input type="' + (type || 'text') + '" name="' + name + '" value="' + E(value == null ? '' : value) + '"' + (extra || '') + '>'; }
  function sel(name, label, value, opts) { return '<label>' + E(label) + '</label><select name="' + name + '">' + opts.map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + E(o[0]) + '"' + (String(value) === String(o[0]) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(o[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'; }
  function ta(name, label, value, ph) { return '<label>' + E(label) + '</label><textarea name="' + name + '" placeholder="' + E(ph || '') + '">' + E(value == null ? '' : value) + '</textarea>'; }
  function num(v) { v = String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[$,\s]/g, ''); return v === '' ? null : (isFinite(Number(v)) ? Number(v) : null); }
  function str(v) { v = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); return v === '' ? null : v; }
  function openGrantForm(g, funderId) {
    var isNew = !g; g = g || { funder_id: funderId || '', stage: funderId ? 'screened' : 'watching', links: [] };
    var funders = F.funders.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.screened_out ? 1 : 0) - (b.screened_out ? 1 : 0) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var ms = members().filter(function (m) { return m.email; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var links = (Array.isArray(g.links) ? g.links : []).map(function (l) { return (l.label ? l.label + ' | ' : '') + (l.url || ''); }).join('\n');
    var body = '<form class="w193form" data-w193gform="' + (g.id || '') + '">'
      + sel('funder_id', 'Funder', g.funder_id, [['', 'Choose the funder\u2026']].concat(funders.map(function (f) { return [f.id, f.name + (f.screened_out ? ' (screened out)' : '')]; }))) + '<p class="hint">Missing? Add the funder first (Funders, Add a funder).</p>'
      + inp('program', 'Program or call', g.program, 'text', ' required maxlength="300" placeholder="Seed Grant, Community Fund, Employee giving\u2026"')
      + inp('title', 'Our project (optional)', g.title, 'text', ' maxlength="200" placeholder="Community kitchen 2027"')
      + '<div class="row">' + sel('stage', 'Stage', g.stage, STAGES.map(function (s) { return [s[0], s[1]]; })) + '<div>' + inp('fit_score', 'Fit score (0 to 100)', g.fit_score, 'number', ' min="0" max="100"') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('amount_ask', 'Our ask ($)', g.amount_ask, 'text', ' inputmode="decimal"') + '</div><div>' + inp('amount_awarded', 'Awarded ($)', g.amount_awarded, 'text', ' inputmode="decimal"') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('deadline', 'Deadline', g.deadline, 'date') + '</div><div>' + inp('deadline_note', 'Deadline note', g.deadline_note, 'text', ' maxlength="200" placeholder="rolling, expected March\u2026"') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('submitted_on', 'Submitted on', g.submitted_on, 'date') + '</div><div>' + inp('decided_on', 'Decided on', g.decided_on, 'date') + '</div></div>'
      + inp('decision_note', 'Decision note', g.decision_note, 'text', ' maxlength="500"')
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('report_due', 'Report due', g.report_due, 'date') + '</div><div>' + inp('renewal_due', 'Renewal due', g.renewal_due, 'date') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('next_action', 'Next step', g.next_action, 'text', ' maxlength="300"') + '</div><div>' + inp('next_action_due', 'By', g.next_action_due, 'date') + '</div></div>'
      + sel('owner_email', 'Owner', lower(g.owner_email || ''), [['', 'Nobody yet']].concat(ms.map(function (m) { return [lower(m.email), m.name || m.email]; })))
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('partner', 'Partner', g.partner, 'text', ' maxlength="200"') + '</div><div>' + inp('program_area', 'What the money is for', g.program_area, 'text', ' maxlength="200" placeholder="kitchen equipment, Sunday servings\u2026"') + '</div></div>'
      + inp('hours_spent', 'Hours spent', g.hours_spent, 'text', ' inputmode="decimal"')
      + ta('links', 'Links (one per line: Label | URL)', links, 'The call | https://\u2026')
      + ta('notes', 'Notes', g.notes, '')
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="submit" class="w193btn">' + (isNew ? 'Add the grant' : 'Save') + '</button><a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193cancel="1">Cancel</a></div></form>';
    F.sheet = { kind: 'gform', id: g.id || null };
    showSheet(isNew ? 'Add a grant' : 'Edit the grant', isNew ? 'An opportunity or an application' : E(grantTitle(g)), body);
  }
  async function saveGrant(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), id = form.getAttribute('data-w193gform'), old = id ? byId(F.grants, id) : null;
    var owner = lower(fd.get('owner_email')), om = members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === owner; })[0];
    var links = String(fd.get('links') || '').split(/\n+/).map(function (l) { return l.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).map(function (l) { var p = l.split('|').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }); var url = p.length > 1 ? p[1] : p[0]; return safeUrl(url) ? { label: p.length > 1 ? p[0] : '', url: url } : null; }).filter(Boolean);
    var rec = { funder_id: num(fd.get('funder_id')), program: str(fd.get('program')), title: str(fd.get('title')), fit_score: num(fd.get('fit_score')), amount_ask: num(fd.get('amount_ask')), amount_awarded: num(fd.get('amount_awarded')), deadline: str(fd.get('deadline')), deadline_note: str(fd.get('deadline_note')), submitted_on: str(fd.get('submitted_on')), decided_on: str(fd.get('decided_on')), decision_note: str(fd.get('decision_note')), report_due: str(fd.get('report_due')), renewal_due: str(fd.get('renewal_due')), next_action: str(fd.get('next_action')), next_action_due: str(fd.get('next_action_due')), owner_email: owner || null, owner_name: om ? om.name : null, partner: str(fd.get('partner')), program_area: str(fd.get('program_area')), hours_spent: num(fd.get('hours_spent')), links: links, notes: str(fd.get('notes')), updated_by: me().email };
    if (!rec.funder_id) { toastS('Choose the funder.'); return; }
    if (!rec.program) { toastS('Name the program or call.'); return; }
    var stage = String(fd.get('stage') || 'watching'), r, gid = id ? Number(id) : null;
    if (old) {
      r = await q(db.from('fund_grants').update(rec).eq('id', id));
      if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
      if (stage !== old.stage) { var rs = await q(db.rpc('fund_grant_stage', { p_grant: gid, p_stage: stage, p_note: null })); if (rs.error) toastS('Saved, but the stage did not move: ' + (rs.error.message || rs.error)); }
    } else {
      rec.stage = stage; rec.created_by = me().email; rec.source = 'hub';
      r = await q(db.from('fund_grants').insert(rec).select('id').single());
      if (r.error || !r.data) { toastS('Could not add it: ' + (r.error && (r.error.message || r.error) || '')); return; }
      gid = r.data.id;
      await q(db.from('fund_grant_log').insert({ grant_id: gid, who: me().email, kind: 'note', text: 'Added to the pipeline at ' + (STAGE[stage] || {}).l.toLowerCase() + '.' }));
    }
    await loadAll(true); if (onFund()) paint();
    toastS(old ? 'Saved.' : 'Added.');
    openGrant(gid);
  }
  function openFunderForm(f) {
    var isNew = !f; f = f || { kind: 'foundation', region: 'quebec', fit: 'unknown', watch: true, aliases: [] };
    var body = '<form class="w193form" data-w193fform="' + (f.id || '') + '">'
      + inp('name', 'Name', f.name, 'text', ' required maxlength="200"')
      + inp('aliases', 'Also called (comma separated)', (f.aliases || []).join(', '), 'text', ' maxlength="400"')
      + '<div class="row">' + sel('kind', 'Kind', f.kind, Object.keys(KINDS).map(function (k) { return [k, KINDS[k]]; })) + sel('region', 'Region', f.region, Object.keys(REGIONS).map(function (k) { return [k, REGIONS[k]]; })) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('url', 'Website', f.url, 'url', ' placeholder="https://"') + '</div><div>' + inp('portal', 'Application portal', f.portal, 'url', ' placeholder="https://"') + '</div></div>'
      + inp('contact', 'Contact', f.contact, 'text', ' maxlength="300" placeholder="Name, role, email"')
      + ta('funds_what', 'What they fund', f.funds_what) + ta('eligibility', 'Eligibility', f.eligibility)
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('cycle', 'Cycle', f.cycle, 'text', ' maxlength="200" placeholder="rolling; spring and fall; once a year in March"') + '</div><div>' + inp('next_deadline', 'Next deadline', f.next_deadline, 'date') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('amount_min', 'Amounts from ($)', f.amount_min, 'text', ' inputmode="decimal"') + '</div><div>' + inp('amount_max', 'to ($)', f.amount_max, 'text', ' inputmode="decimal"') + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="row">' + sel('fit', 'Fit', f.fit, Object.keys(FIT).map(function (k) { return [k, FIT[k][0]]; })) + '<div>' + inp('fit_note', 'Why', f.fit_note, 'text', ' maxlength="500"') + '</div></div>'
      + ta('relationship', 'Our history with them', f.relationship) + ta('notes', 'Notes', f.notes)
      + '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:13.5px;margin-top:14px"><input type="checkbox" name="watch"' + (f.watch ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="width:auto"> The scanner watches this funder\u2019s page</label>'
      + '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:13.5px"><input type="checkbox" name="screened_out"' + (f.screened_out ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="width:auto"> Not for us (screened out)</label>'
      + inp('screened_reason', 'Why not for us', f.screened_reason, 'text', ' maxlength="500"')
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="submit" class="w193btn">' + (isNew ? 'Add the funder' : 'Save') + '</button><a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193cancel="1">Cancel</a></div></form>';
    F.sheet = { kind: 'fform', id: f.id || null };
    showSheet(isNew ? 'Add a funder' : 'Edit the funder', isNew ? 'A foundation, a program, a company' : E(f.name), body);
  }
  async function saveFunder(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), id = form.getAttribute('data-w193fform');
    var rec = { name: str(fd.get('name')), aliases: String(fd.get('aliases') || '').split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean), kind: String(fd.get('kind')), region: String(fd.get('region')), url: str(fd.get('url')), portal: str(fd.get('portal')), contact: str(fd.get('contact')), funds_what: str(fd.get('funds_what')), eligibility: str(fd.get('eligibility')), cycle: str(fd.get('cycle')), next_deadline: str(fd.get('next_deadline')), amount_min: num(fd.get('amount_min')), amount_max: num(fd.get('amount_max')), fit: String(fd.get('fit')), fit_note: str(fd.get('fit_note')), relationship: str(fd.get('relationship')), notes: str(fd.get('notes')), watch: fd.get('watch') === 'on', screened_out: fd.get('screened_out') === 'on', screened_reason: str(fd.get('screened_reason')), updated_by: me().email };
    if (!rec.name) { toastS('Name the funder.'); return; }
    if (rec.screened_out) rec.watch = false;
    var r, fid = id ? Number(id) : null;
    if (id) r = await q(db.from('fund_funders').update(rec).eq('id', id));
    else { rec.created_by = me().email; rec.source = 'hub'; r = await q(db.from('fund_funders').insert(rec).select('id').single()); if (r.data) fid = r.data.id; }
    if (r.error) { toastS(/duplicate|unique/i.test(String(r.error.message)) ? 'A funder with that name is already here.' : 'Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    await loadAll(true); if (onFund()) paint(); toastS(id ? 'Saved.' : 'Added.');
    if (fid) openFunder(fid);
  }
  function openFactForm(f) {
    var isNew = !f; f = f || { category: 'numbers', status: 'needs_check', sort: 100 };
    var body = '<form class="w193form" data-w193factform="' + (f.id || '') + '">'
      + inp('key', 'Key (short, letters and dashes)', f.key, 'text', (isNew ? ' required' : ' readonly') + ' maxlength="60" placeholder="meals-2025"')
      + sel('category', 'Category', f.category, FACT_CATS)
      + inp('title', 'Title', f.title, 'text', ' required maxlength="200"')
      + ta('body', 'The fact, in our words', f.body) + ta('body_fr', 'In French (optional)', f.body_fr)
      + inp('source', 'Source', f.source, 'text', ' maxlength="300" placeholder="Impact Tracker 2025; the Hub"')
      + '<div class="row">' + sel('status', 'Status', f.status, Object.keys(FACT_ST).map(function (k) { return [k, FACT_ST[k][0]]; })) + '<div>' + inp('sort', 'Order', f.sort, 'number') + '</div></div>'
      + ta('notes', 'Notes (what to check, what the sources disagree on)', f.notes)
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="submit" class="w193btn">' + (isNew ? 'Add the fact' : 'Save') + '</button><a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193cancel="1">Cancel</a></div></form>';
    F.sheet = { kind: 'factform', id: f.id || null };
    showSheet(isNew ? 'Add a fact' : 'Edit the fact', isNew ? 'Something the writer may say about us' : E(f.title), body);
  }
  async function saveFact(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), id = form.getAttribute('data-w193factform');
    var rec = { category: String(fd.get('category')), title: str(fd.get('title')), body: str(fd.get('body')), body_fr: str(fd.get('body_fr')), source: str(fd.get('source')), status: String(fd.get('status')), sort: num(fd.get('sort')) == null ? 100 : num(fd.get('sort')), notes: str(fd.get('notes')), updated_by: me().email };
    if (!rec.title || !rec.body) { toastS('A title and the fact itself.'); return; }
    if (rec.status === 'verified') { rec.verified_on = ymd(today()); rec.verified_by = me().email; }
    var r;
    if (id) r = await q(db.from('fund_facts').update(rec).eq('id', id));
    else { rec.key = fold(fd.get('key')).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '').slice(0, 60); if (!rec.key) { toastS('Give it a key.'); return; } r = await q(db.from('fund_facts').insert(rec)); }
    if (r.error) { toastS(/duplicate|unique/i.test(String(r.error.message)) ? 'That key is taken.' : 'Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    closeSheet(); await loadAll(true); paint(); toastS(id ? 'Saved.' : 'Added.');
  }
  function openDocForm(grantId) {
    var g = byId(F.grants, grantId);
    var body = '<form class="w193form" data-w193docform="' + (grantId || '') + '">'
      + inp('title', 'Title', g ? grantTitle(g) + ' application' : '', 'text', ' required maxlength="200"')
      + '<div class="row">' + sel('kind', 'Kind', 'application', [['application', 'Application'], ['loi', 'Letter of intent'], ['letter', 'Letter'], ['budget', 'Budget'], ['confirmation', 'Confirmation'], ['report', 'Report'], ['answers', 'Standard answers'], ['other', 'Other']]) + '<div>' + inp('doc_date', 'Date', ymd(today()), 'date') + '</div></div>'
      + sel('lang', 'Language', 'en', [['en', 'English'], ['fr', 'French']])
      + inp('source_url', 'Where the original lives (optional)', '', 'url', ' placeholder="https://docs.google.com/\u2026"')
      + '<label>The text</label><textarea name="text" required style="min-height:220px" placeholder="Paste the questions and answers as sent"></textarea>'
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="submit" class="w193btn">Keep it</button><a class="w193btn ghost" data-w193cancel="1">Cancel</a></div></form>';
    F.sheet = { kind: 'docform', id: grantId };
    showSheet('Add a past application', g ? E(grantTitle(g)) : '', body);
  }
  async function saveDoc(form) {
    var fd = new FormData(form), gid = num(form.getAttribute('data-w193docform')), g = gid ? byId(F.grants, gid) : null, text = str(fd.get('text'));
    if (!text) return;
    var r = await q(db.from('fund_documents').insert({ grant_id: gid, funder_id: g ? g.funder_id : null, title: str(fd.get('title')) || 'Document', kind: String(fd.get('kind')), lang: String(fd.get('lang')), doc_date: str(fd.get('doc_date')), text: text, words: words(text), source_url: str(fd.get('source_url')), source: 'added in the Hub', created_by: me().email }));
    if (r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    if (gid) await q(db.from('fund_grant_log').insert({ grant_id: gid, who: me().email, kind: 'note', text: 'Added a document: ' + (str(fd.get('title')) || 'Document') + '.' }));
    await loadAll(true); if (onFund()) paint(); toastS('Kept: the writer learns from it.');
    if (gid) { await loadLog(gid); openGrant(gid); } else closeSheet();
  }

  /* ---------- paint and route ---------- */
  var VIEWS = { pipeline: pipelineHtml, found: foundHtml, funders: fundersHtml, write: writerHtml, playbook: playbookHtml, facts: factsHtml };
  function paint() {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var active = document.activeElement, keep = null;
    if (active && h.contains(active) && active.matches && active.matches('[data-w193q]')) keep = { sel: '[data-w193q]', pos: active.selectionStart };
    var html;
    if (!staffOk()) html = '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">The Fundraising app is for the staff: the Fundraising team, the executives and the admins.</p></div>';
    else if (F.err && !F.grants.length && !F.funders.length) html = '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">The Fundraising app is not open to you' + (F.err ? ' (' + E(clip(F.err, 120)) + ')' : '') + '. Ask an admin to add it to your access profile.</p></div>';
    else if (F.sec === 'calendar') html = calendarHtml();
    else if (F.sec === 'donors') html = donorsHtml();
    else if (F.sec === 'sops') { var t = F.sub ? byId(F.sops, F.sub) : null; html = t ? sopReaderHtml(t) : sopsHtml(); }
    else if (F.sec === 'grants') html = (VIEWS[F.view] || pipelineHtml)();
    else html = overviewHtml();
    h.innerHTML = html;
    if (keep) { try { var el = h.querySelector(keep.sel); if (el) { el.focus(); if (keep.pos != null) el.setSelectionRange(keep.pos, keep.pos); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function route(h) {
    var p = String(h || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
    var sec = p[1] || 'overview', sub = p[2] || '', sub2 = p.slice(3).join('/');
    F.sub = '';
    if (sec === 'grants') {
      F.sec = 'grants';
      if (!sub) F.view = 'pipeline';
      else if (/^\d+$/.test(sub)) { F.view = 'pipeline'; F.sub = sub; }
      else if (GVIEWS.indexOf(sub) >= 0) { F.view = sub; F.sub = sub2; }
      else F.view = 'pipeline';
    } else if (SECTIONS.indexOf(sec) >= 0) { F.sec = sec; F.sub = sub; }
    else F.sec = 'overview';
    if (F.sec === 'calendar' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(sub)) { F.calAnchor = mk(Number(sub.slice(0, 4)), Number(sub.slice(5)) - 1, 1); F.sub = ''; }
    if (F.sec !== 'grants' || F.view !== 'pipeline') F.filters.q = F.filters.q && F.lastView === F.sec + '/' + F.view ? F.filters.q : '';
    F.lastView = F.sec + '/' + F.view;
  }
  async function show(h) {
    route(h);
    var hh = host(); if (!hh) return;
    if (!F.loaded) hh.innerHTML = '<div class="w193card"><p class="w193empty">Loading the Fundraising app\u2026</p></div>';
    await loadAll();
    if (F.sec === 'grants' && F.view === 'write') {
      var m = /^new(?:\?grant=(\d+))?$/.exec(F.sub || '');
      if (m) { openDraft(null); F.writer.text = ''; F.writer.notes = ''; if (m[1]) F.writer.grantId = m[1]; try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#fund/grants/write'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } F.sub = ''; }
      else if (/^\d+$/.test(F.sub || '')) { if (!F.draft || String(F.draft.id) !== String(F.sub)) openDraft(F.sub); }
    }
    paint();
    if (F.sec === 'grants' && F.view === 'pipeline' && F.sub) openGrant(F.sub);
    else if (F.sec === 'grants' && F.view === 'funders' && /^\d+$/.test(F.sub || '')) openFunder(F.sub);
    else if (F.open) closeSheet();
    var run = F.runs[0]; if (run && run.status === 'running') watchRun();
  }

  /* whoever opens the app reaches the Events calendar through it: body.w193has takes the page out of their Hub menu (wave153's rule) */

  var hasT = null;
  function watchHas() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar || bar.__w193obs) return;
    bar.__w193obs = true;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(hasT); hasT = setTimeout(markHas, 150); }).observe(bar, { attributes: true, subtree: true, attributeFilter: ['style'] }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function register() {
    if (!window.WA_NAV || typeof WA_NAV.register !== 'function') return;
    if (!WA_NAV.pages.some(function (p) { return p.id === 'fund'; })) WA_NAV.register({ id: 'fund', label: 'Fundraising', group: 'apps', dot: ORANGE, gate: 'member', onShow: function (h) { show(h); } });
    try { var l = JSON.parse(lsGet('w193layers') || 'null'); if (l && typeof l === 'object') Object.keys(F.calLayers).forEach(function (k) { if (typeof l[k] === 'boolean') F.calLayers[k] = l[k]; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa193 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var out = await __sa193.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        register(); addTours(); markHas(); watchHas();
        [400, 1500, 4000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(markHas, ms); });
        var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        if (h.split('/')[0] === 'fund') { showPanel('fund'); show(h); }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave193 boot', e); }
      return out;
    };
  }

  /* ---------- test hook ---------- */
  window.w193 = { state: function () { return F; }, load: loadAll, paint: paint, show: show, route: route, openGrant: openGrant, openFunder: openFunder, openDraft: openDraft, close: closeSheet, md: mdToHtml, questions: parseQuestions, due: dueItems, cal: calItems, sect: SECT, views: GVIEW };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v194 Updated since you finished: open it, then close it. The certificate in the brand, signed (02/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/10/26): "when new onboarding changes are made, I understand that everyone get a section called "Update since
   you finished" and the option to write got it. Instead I'd like for them to haft to open the link of the module to re do
   it and then they can close it." Then: "the certificate members get should be better branded with our branding and I'll
   add my signature" (he sent the signature). His choices: opening the module unlocks Close, and the opening is saved, so
   Close is still there on another device; a step with no module opens in the person's plan with its new instructions,
   the same way; the signature reads Steel MacDonald, Founder and Chair.

   1. Updated since you finished (the Academy's onboarding card in 122, the Classic view in 058). Each changed step shows
      Open the module, Open the avenue or Open the step instead of Got it. Opening is saved (onboarding_update_opens, one
      row per person per version of the step) and brings Close, which records the acknowledgement as before
      (onboarding_update_acks). What opens carries a gold bar: what changed, the step's words when it has no module, and
      Close the update. A step with no module opens the plan at that step (its week chosen, the step shown and flashed).
   2. The certificate (122 calls certHtml and printCert here). The brand: the green band with the cream lockup, AVENUE
      ACADEMY in gold, the gold seal with the E, one gold star; Barlow Condensed and Space Grotesk; the gold rule; the
      name and what was completed; the signature with the name and title under a line, and the date awarded. The
      signature is app_config cert_signature (JSON: image as a PNG data URL, name, title, org); signed-in staff read it,
      only admins write cert_* keys (wa_app_config_guard). Admins change it from the certificate (Signature). Print keeps
      the fonts and the colours (A4 landscape, edge to edge).
   Data: wa194_updates_certificate. Test: test/w194drive.js.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa194) return; window.__wa194 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773';
  var DISP = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function Q(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/["\\]/g, '\\$&'); }

  function md(s) { try { return typeof wa37Md === 'function' ? wa37Md(s) : E(s); } catch (e) { return E(s); } }
  function ts(x) { var n = Date.parse(String(x || '')); return isFinite(n) ? n : 0; }
  function role() {
    try { if (typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board') return 'board'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) return WA40_MINE.role; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return 'member';
  }

  function weekLabel(s) {
    var u = String(s || '').trim(), m = /^WEEK\s+(\d+)$/i.exec(u); if (m) return 'Week ' + m[1];
    m = /^(\d+)\s+(DAYS?|MONTHS?)$/i.exec(u); if (m) return m[1] + ' ' + m[2].toLowerCase();
    return u ? u.charAt(0) + u.slice(1).toLowerCase() : '';
  }
