  /* =================== 1. Updated since you finished =================== */
  var U = { onb: null, opens: {}, loaded: false, loading: null, focus: null, busy: {} };
  function loadOpens(onbId) {
    var id = String(onbId == null ? '' : onbId); if (!id) return Promise.resolve(false);
    if (U.onb === id && U.loaded) return Promise.resolve(false);
    if (U.onb === id && U.loading) return U.loading;
    if (U.onb !== id) { U.onb = id; U.opens = {}; }
    U.loaded = false;
    U.loading = Promise.resolve(db.from('onboarding_update_opens').select('template_id,template_updated_at').eq('onboarding_id', Number(id))).then(function (r) {
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { var k = String(x.template_id); if (!U.opens[k] || ts(U.opens[k]) < ts(x.template_updated_at)) U.opens[k] = x.template_updated_at; });
      U.loaded = true; U.loading = null; return true;
    }).catch(function () { U.loaded = true; U.loading = null; return false; });
    return U.loading;
  }
  function opened(t) { var v = t ? U.opens[String(t.id)] : null; return !!v && ts(v) >= ts(t.updated_at) - 1; }
  function meWho() {
    try { var p = typeof wa28Me === 'function' ? wa28Me() : null; return { name: (p && p.name) || '', email: myEmail(), dept: (p && p.department) || '' }; }
    catch (e) { return { name: '', email: myEmail(), dept: '' }; }
  }
  /* where a changed step opens: its module, its avenue (the team's or any one resolved as wave166 does), or the plan */
  function linkOf(t) {
    if (t.training_id) return { kind: 'module', label: 'Open the module', hash: '#training/t/' + t.training_id, training: String(t.training_id) };
    if (t.academy_path) {
      var key = String(t.academy_path);
      if (key === 'team' || key === 'any') {
        var ps = null; try { if (window.w166 && typeof w166.pathState === 'function') ps = w166.pathState(meWho(), key); } catch (e) { ps = null; }
        key = ps && ps.key && ps.key !== 'team' && ps.key !== 'any' && (ps.done || ps.complete) ? String(ps.key) : '';
      }
      return key ? { kind: 'avenue', label: 'Open the avenue', hash: '#training/p/' + key, path: key } : { kind: 'avenues', label: 'Open the avenues', hash: '#training' };
    }
    return { kind: 'step', label: 'Open the step', hash: '#training/onboarding' };
  }
  function whatOf(L) { return L.kind === 'module' ? 'Avenue Academy module' : (L.kind === 'step' ? 'Onboarding step' : 'Learning Avenue'); }
  function actsHtml(t, onbId) {
    var L = linkOf(t), op = opened(t), a = ' data-up="' + E(t.updated_at) + '" data-onb="' + E(onbId) + '"';
    return '<button type="button" class="w194b' + (op ? ' soft' : '') + '" data-w194open="' + E(t.id) + '"' + a + '>' + (op ? 'Open it again' : E(L.label)) + '</button>'
      + (op ? '<button type="button" class="w194b" data-w194close="' + E(t.id) + '"' + a + '>Close</button>' : '');
  }
  function rowHtml(t, onbId) {
    var L = linkOf(t), wk = weekLabel(t.section), op = opened(t);
    return '<div class="ur w194r' + (op ? ' op' : '') + '" data-w194row="' + E(t.id) + '"><span class="tx"><em>' + E(t.name) + '</em><small>' + E([wk, whatOf(L)].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + (op ? ' \u00b7 opened, ready to close' : '') + '</small></span>'
      + '<span class="w194acts">' + actsHtml(t, onbId) + '</span></div>';
  }
  /* 122's onboarding card calls this (pendingHtml) */
  function pendingHtml(g) {
    var o = g && g.o, list = (g && g.pending) || [], id = o ? String(o.id) : '';
    if (id && !(U.onb === id && U.loaded)) loadOpens(id).then(function (ch) { if (ch) repaint(); });
    var one = list.length === 1;
    return '<div class="upd w194upd"><b>Updated since you finished (' + list.length + ')</b>'
      + '<p class="w194why">HR changed ' + (one ? 'this step' : 'these steps') + ' after you finished your onboarding. Open ' + (one ? 'it' : 'each one') + ' to go through it again, then close it.</p>'
      + list.map(function (t) { return rowHtml(t, id); }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function refreshRows(tid) {
    var t = tmpl(tid); if (!t) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w194row="' + Q(tid) + '"]').forEach(function (r) {
      var acts = r.querySelector('.w194acts'), b = r.querySelector('[data-onb]'), onb = b ? b.getAttribute('data-onb') : U.onb;
      if (acts) acts.innerHTML = actsHtml(t, onb);
    });
  }
  function doOpen(btn) {
    var tid = btn.getAttribute('data-w194open'), up = btn.getAttribute('data-up'), onb = btn.getAttribute('data-onb') || U.onb;
    var t = tmpl(tid); if (!t) { T('That step is not in the onboarding any more'); return; }
    var L = linkOf(t);
    if (!opened(t)) {
      U.opens[String(tid)] = up || t.updated_at;   /* at once: Close is there when they come back */
      try {
        Promise.resolve(db.from('onboarding_update_opens').upsert({ onboarding_id: Number(onb), template_id: String(tid), template_updated_at: up || t.updated_at, opened_by: whoName() },
          { onConflict: 'onboarding_id,template_id,template_updated_at', ignoreDuplicates: true })).catch(function () { /* kept here; saved on the next open */ });
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    U.focus = { tid: String(tid), up: up || t.updated_at, onb: String(onb), kind: L.kind, training: L.training || null, path: L.path || null, scrolled: false, at: Date.now() };
    refreshRows(tid);
    try { if (typeof closeModal === 'function' && btn.closest('.modal-back')) closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (location.hash === L.hash) {
      var R = sheetR();
      if (L.kind === 'module' && window.w122 && typeof w122.openTraining === 'function' && !(R && R.mode === 'view' && R.t && String(R.t.id) === L.training)) w122.openTraining(L.training);
      else decorate(sheetEl(), sheetR());
    } else location.hash = L.hash;
    if (L.kind === 'avenues') T('Pick the Learning Avenue for this step, then close the update from your onboarding card.');
  }
  function doClose(btn) {
    var tid = btn.getAttribute('data-w194close'), up = btn.getAttribute('data-up'), onb = btn.getAttribute('data-onb') || U.onb;
    if (!tid || U.busy[tid]) return; U.busy[tid] = 1;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w194close="' + Q(tid) + '"]').forEach(function (b) { b.disabled = true; });
    Promise.resolve(db.from('onboarding_update_acks').insert({ onboarding_id: Number(onb), template_id: String(tid), template_updated_at: up, acked_by: whoName() })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      try { if (window.w122 && w122.A) { w122.A.acks = w122.A.acks || {}; w122.A.acks[String(tid)] = up; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (window.w58 && w58.state && w58.state.acks) w58.state.acks[String(tid)] = up; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (U.focus && U.focus.tid === String(tid)) U.focus = null;
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w194bar="' + Q(tid) + '"]').forEach(function (b) {
        b.classList.add('done');
        var w = b.querySelector('.w194bw'); if (w) w.textContent = 'Closed. You are up to date on this step.';
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w58row="' + Q(tid) + '"]').forEach(function (r) { var box = r.closest('.w58upd'); r.remove(); if (box && !box.querySelector('.w58st')) box.remove(); });
      T('Closed. You are up to date on this step.');
      repaint();
    }).catch(function () {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w194close="' + Q(tid) + '"]').forEach(function (b) { b.disabled = false; });
      T('Could not close it just now. Try again in a moment.');
    }).then(function () { delete U.busy[tid]; });
  }
  function inPlan(t, onb) { try { var m = typeof wa37StepsFor === 'function' ? wa37StepsFor(onb) : null; return !!(m && m[t.id]); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function barHtml(t, f) {
    var wk = weekLabel(t.section);
    var why = f.kind === 'module' ? 'This module is part of your onboarding and changed after you finished. Go through it again, then close the update.'
      : (f.kind === 'step' ? 'This step changed after you finished. Read what it says now, then close the update.'
        : 'This avenue is part of your onboarding and changed after you finished. Go through it again, then close the update.');
    var words = f.kind === 'step' && t.description ? '<details class="w194txt"' + (inPlan(t, f.onb) ? '' : ' open') + '><summary>What it says now</summary><div class="ds">' + md(t.description) + '</div></details>' : '';
    return '<div class="w194bi"><span class="ov">Updated since you finished' + (wk ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(wk) : '') + '</span><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b><span class="w194bw">' + E(why) + '</span>' + words + '</div>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w194b gold" data-w194close="' + E(t.id) + '" data-up="' + E(f.up) + '" data-onb="' + E(f.onb) + '">Close the update</button>';
  }
  /* 122 calls this after it draws its sheet: the gold bar on what was opened from Updated since you finished */
  function decorate(sh, R) {
    if (!sh || !R || !U.focus) return;
    var f = U.focus, t = tmpl(f.tid);
    if (!t || ts(t.updated_at) > ts(f.up) + 1) { U.focus = null; return; }
    var match = (f.kind === 'module' && R.mode === 'view' && R.t && String(R.t.id) === f.training)
      || (f.kind === 'avenue' && R.mode === 'path' && R.path && String(R.path.key) === f.path)
      || (f.kind === 'step' && R.mode === 'onb' && !window.__w169view);
    if (!match || sh.querySelector('[data-w194bar]')) return;
    var bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'w194bar'; bar.setAttribute('data-w194bar', f.tid); bar.innerHTML = barHtml(t, f);
    var bd = null; [].forEach.call(sh.children, function (c) { if (!bd && c.classList && c.classList.contains('bd')) bd = c; });
    if (bd) sh.insertBefore(bar, bd); else sh.appendChild(bar);
    if (f.kind === 'step' && !f.scrolled) { f.scrolled = true; focusStep(t); }
  }
  /* the plan: choose the step's week (wave167's journey), unfold it, show it and flash it */
  function focusStep(t) {
    var tid = String(t.id), tries = 0;
    (function tick() {
      var R = sheetR(), sh = sheetEl(); if (!R || R.mode !== 'onb' || !sh) return;
      if (!sh.classList.contains('w167') && tries < 12) { tries++; setTimeout(tick, 90); return; }
      try { if (window.w167 && typeof w167.select === 'function' && t.section && sh.classList.contains('w167')) w167.select(String(t.section), false); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      var row = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap [data-w166t="' + Q(tid) + '"]');
      if (!row) { if (tries++ < 30) setTimeout(tick, 90); return; }
      var d = row.closest('details'); if (d && !d.open) d.open = true;
      if (row.style.display === 'none' && t.parent_id) { var tg = sh.querySelector('.onbwrap [data-w57t="' + Q(t.parent_id) + '"]'); if (tg) tg.click(); }
      setTimeout(function () {
        try { row.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        row.classList.add('w194hit'); row.classList.remove('w167flash'); void row.offsetWidth; row.classList.add('w167flash');
      }, 60);
    })();
  }

  /* =================== 2. the certificate =================== */
  var C = { cfg: null, loaded: false, loading: null, edit: null };
  function loadCfg(force) {
    if (C.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve(false);
    if (C.loading) return C.loading;
    var before = JSON.stringify(C.cfg);
    C.loading = Promise.resolve(db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', CFG_KEY).maybeSingle()).then(function (r) {
      var v = null; try { v = r && r.data && r.data.value ? JSON.parse(r.data.value) : null; } catch (e) { v = null; }
      C.cfg = v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : null; C.loaded = true; C.loading = null; return JSON.stringify(C.cfg) !== before;
    }).catch(function () { C.loaded = true; C.loading = null; return false; });
    return C.loading;
  }
  function sig() {
    var c = C.cfg || {}, img = String(c.image || '');
    return { img: /^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+$/.test(img) ? img : '', name: c.name || DEF.name, title: c.title || DEF.title, org: c.org || DEF.org };
  }
  var MONTHS = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
  function longDate(d) { var x = new Date(d); if (isNaN(x.getTime())) return ''; return MONTHS[x.getMonth()] + ' ' + x.getDate() + ', ' + x.getFullYear(); }
  function nameCls(n) { var L = String(n || '').length; return L > 28 ? ' s3' : (L > 19 ? ' s2' : ''); }
  /* 122's certHtml(a) comes here: a is the award row, m is the person (me()), no is the certificate number */
  function certHtml(a, m, no) {
    var s = sig(), name = (m && (m.full || m.name)) || '', when = longDate(a.earned_at);
    var title = String(a.title || '');
    return '<div class="w194cert" role="img" aria-label="' + E('Certificate of completion: ' + name + ' completed ' + title + (when ? ', awarded ' + when : '')) + '"><div class="ci">'
      + '<div class="band"><img class="logo" src="' + IMG.logo + '" alt="Welfare Avenue"><div class="acad"><span>Avenue</span><span>Academy</span><i></i></div>'
      + '<div class="seal"><img src="' + IMG.e + '" alt=""></div><img class="star" src="' + IMG.star + '" alt=""></div>'
      + '<div class="body"><div class="ov">Certificate of completion</div><i class="rule"></i>'
      + '<div class="mid"><div class="lead">This certifies that</div><div class="nm' + nameCls(name) + '">' + E(name) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="lead">has completed</div><div class="what">' + E(title) + '</div></div>'
      + '<div class="foot"><div class="sg"><div class="ink">' + (s.img ? '<img src="' + s.img + '" alt="Signature of ' + E(s.name) + '">' : '') + '</div><div class="ln"></div><b>' + E(s.name) + '</b><span>' + E(s.title + (s.org ? ', ' + s.org : '')) + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="dt"><div class="ink"><em>' + E(when) + '</em></div><div class="ln"></div><b>Date awarded</b><span>' + (no ? 'Certificate ' + E(no) : 'Avenue Academy') + '</span></div></div>'
      + '</div></div></div>';
  }
  /* the print window: the same certificate, A4 landscape edge to edge, once the fonts and the pictures are in */
  function writeCert(w, a, m, no) {
    var body = certHtml(a, m, no);
    w.document.open();
    w.document.write('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + E(a.title) + ' certificate</title>'
      + '<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com"><link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>'
      + '<link id="f194" rel="stylesheet" href="' + FONTS + '"><style>@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:0;}html,body{margin:0;padding:0;background:' + CREAM + ';}'
      + 'body{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:100vh;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact;}'
      + CERT_CSS + '.w194cert{width:297mm;max-width:100vw;border-radius:0;box-shadow:none;}'
      + '@media print{body{min-height:0;display:block;}.w194cert{width:297mm;height:210mm;}.w194cert .ci{height:100%;aspect-ratio:auto;}}</style></head><body>' + body
      + '<script>(function(){var done=false;function go(){if(done)return;done=true;setTimeout(function(){try{window.focus();}catch(e){}window.print();},150);}'
      + 'function pics(){return Promise.all([].slice.call(document.images).map(function(i){return i.complete?0:new Promise(function(r){i.onload=i.onerror=r;});}));}'
      + 'function faces(){try{return Promise.all([document.fonts.load(\'600 40px "Barlow Condensed"\'),document.fonts.load(\'500 40px "Barlow Condensed"\'),document.fonts.load(\'400 16px "Space Grotesk"\'),document.fonts.load(\'700 16px "Space Grotesk"\')]);}catch(e){return Promise.resolve();}}'
      + 'var l=document.getElementById("f194");var css=new Promise(function(r){if(l.sheet)return r();l.onload=l.onerror=r;});'
      + 'css.then(function(){return Promise.all([faces(),pics()]);}).then(go,go);setTimeout(go,4000);})();<\/script></body></html>');
    w.document.close();
  }
  /* the window opens inside the click (pop-up blockers), then fills once the signature is read */
  function printNow(a, m, no) {
    var w = window.open('', '_blank'); if (!w) { T('Allow pop-ups for the Hub to print the certificate'); return false; }
    try { w.document.write('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Preparing the certificate</title><body style="font-family:sans-serif;color:' + GREEN + ';background:' + CREAM + ';padding:40px">Preparing the certificate…</body>'); w.document.close(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var done = false, go = function () { if (done) return; done = true; try { writeCert(w, a, m, no); } catch (e) { /* the window was closed */ } };
    if (C.loaded) go(); else { loadCfg().then(go, go); setTimeout(go, 2500); }
    return true;
  }

  /* the signature, for admins (Signature on the certificate) */
  function sigEditorHtml() {
    var s = sig(), e = C.edit || {};
    var img = e.img != null ? e.img : s.img;
    return '<div id="w194sig"><div class="h"><b>The signature on every certificate</b><span>Only admins can change it. It shows on every certificate in the Avenue Academy, the ones already earned too.</span></div>'
      + '<div class="row"><div class="pv">' + (img ? '<img src="' + img + '" alt="The signature">' : 'No signature yet') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="f"><label>Name<input type="text" id="w194sn" maxlength="60" value="' + E(e.name != null ? e.name : s.name) + '"></label>'
      + '<label>Title<input type="text" id="w194st" maxlength="80" value="' + E(e.title != null ? e.title : s.title) + '"></label>'
      + '<label>A new signature (PNG or JPG)<input type="file" id="w194sf" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"></label>'
      + '<p class="hint">Sign in dark ink on white paper or a clear background, then take a photo or a scan. The Hub trims it and clears the background.</p></div></div>'
      + '<div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w122btn soft sm" data-w194sigx="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w122btn sm" data-w194sigsave="1">Save the signature</button></div></div>';
  }
  function openSigEditor() {
    if (!isAdmin()) { T('Only an admin can change the signature'); return; }
    var sh = sheetEl(); var wrap = sh && sh.querySelector('.main .wrap'); if (!wrap) return;
    var old = document.getElementById('w194sig'); if (old) { old.remove(); C.edit = null; return; }
    C.edit = {};
    wrap.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', sigEditorHtml());
  }
  /* a photo or a scan becomes ink on a clear background, trimmed, at most 900 px wide */
  function cleanSignature(file) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      if (!file || !/^image\//.test(file.type || '')) { reject(new Error('Choose a PNG or JPG picture')); return; }
      if (file.size > 6 * 1024 * 1024) { reject(new Error('That picture is over 6 MB')); return; }
      var rd = new FileReader();
      rd.onerror = function () { reject(new Error('Could not read the picture')); };
      rd.onload = function () {
        var im = new Image();
        im.onerror = function () { reject(new Error('Could not read the picture')); };
        im.onload = function () {
          var W = im.naturalWidth, H = im.naturalHeight, k = Math.min(1, 1800 / Math.max(W, H));
          W = Math.max(1, Math.round(W * k)); H = Math.max(1, Math.round(H * k));
          var cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H;
          var cx = cv.getContext('2d'); cx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, W, H);
          var d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H), p = d.data, i, opaque = true;
          for (i = 3; i < p.length; i += 4 * 97) { if (p[i] < 250) { opaque = false; break; } }
          var x0 = W, y0 = H, x1 = -1, y1 = -1, x, y, a;
          for (y = 0; y < H; y++) for (x = 0; x < W; x++) {
            i = (y * W + x) * 4;
            if (opaque) { var lum = 0.299 * p[i] + 0.587 * p[i + 1] + 0.114 * p[i + 2]; a = lum >= 215 ? 0 : (lum <= 120 ? 255 : Math.round(255 * (215 - lum) / 95)); }
            else a = p[i + 3];
            p[i] = 31; p[i + 1] = 32; p[i + 2] = 30; p[i + 3] = a;
            if (a > 24) { if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y; }
          }
          if (x1 < 0) { reject(new Error('No signature found in that picture')); return; }
          cx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
          var pad = 8; x0 = Math.max(0, x0 - pad); y0 = Math.max(0, y0 - pad); x1 = Math.min(W - 1, x1 + pad); y1 = Math.min(H - 1, y1 + pad);
          var cw = x1 - x0 + 1, ch = y1 - y0 + 1, s2 = Math.min(1, 900 / cw);
          var out = document.createElement('canvas'); out.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cw * s2)); out.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(ch * s2));
          out.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv, x0, y0, cw, ch, 0, 0, out.width, out.height);
          resolve(out.toDataURL('image/png'));
        };
        im.src = rd.result;
      };
      rd.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }
  function saveSig() {
    if (!isAdmin()) { T('Only an admin can change the signature'); return; }
    var s = sig(), e = C.edit || {};
    var name = String((document.getElementById('w194sn') || {}).value || '').trim() || DEF.name;
    var title = String((document.getElementById('w194st') || {}).value || '').trim() || DEF.title;
    var img = e.img != null ? e.img : s.img;
    var v = { image: img || '', name: name, title: title, org: s.org || DEF.org, updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    var btn = document.querySelector('[data-w194sigsave]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    Promise.resolve(db.from('app_config').upsert({ key: CFG_KEY, value: JSON.stringify(v), updated_at: v.updated_at }, { onConflict: 'key' })).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      C.cfg = v; C.loaded = true; C.edit = null;
      T('Saved. Every certificate now carries this signature.');
      rerenderCert();
    }).catch(function (err) {
      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
      T(/admin/i.test(String((err && err.message) || '')) ? 'Only an admin can change the signature' : 'Could not save the signature just now');
    });
  }
  function rerenderCert() {
    var R = sheetR(), sh = sheetEl(), lc = window.w194.lastCert; if (!R || R.mode !== 'cert' || !sh || !lc) return;
    var cur = sh.querySelector('.w194cert'); if (cur) cur.outerHTML = certHtml(lc.a, lc.m, lc.no);
    if (!C.edit) { var ed = document.getElementById('w194sig'); if (ed) ed.remove(); }
  }
  /* 122's renderCert calls this after it draws the certificate sheet */
  function certShown(sh, a, m, no) {
    window.w194.lastCert = { a: a, m: m, no: no };
    if (!C.loaded) loadCfg().then(function (ch) { if (ch) rerenderCert(); });
    if (isAdmin() && sh) {
      var ft = sh.querySelector('.ft'), pr = ft && ft.querySelector('[data-w122print]');
      if (ft && !ft.querySelector('[data-w194sig]')) {
        var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w122btn soft'; b.setAttribute('data-w194sig', '1'); b.textContent = 'Signature';
        if (pr) ft.insertBefore(b, pr); else ft.appendChild(b);
      }
    }
  }
  /* =================== wiring =================== */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w194open]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); doOpen(el); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w194close]'))) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); doClose(el); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w194sig]'))) { e.preventDefault(); openSigEditor(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w194sigx]'))) { e.preventDefault(); C.edit = null; var ed = document.getElementById('w194sig'); if (ed) ed.remove(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w194sigsave]'))) { e.preventDefault(); saveSig(); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || t.id !== 'w194sf') return;
    var f = t.files && t.files[0]; if (!f) return;
    cleanSignature(f).then(function (url) {
      C.edit = C.edit || {}; C.edit.img = url;
      var pv = document.querySelector('#w194sig .pv'); if (pv) pv.innerHTML = '<img src="' + url + '" alt="The new signature">';
    }).catch(function (err) { T(String((err && err.message) || 'Could not read the picture')); t.value = ''; });
  });
  /* the sheet closed or moved on: the bar's job is done */
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if (!U.focus) return;
    var h = location.hash || '';
    if (!/^#training/.test(h)) U.focus = null;
  });

  /* ---------- for 122, 058 and the harness ---------- */
  window.w194 = {
    pendingHtml: pendingHtml, actsHtml: actsHtml, loadOpens: loadOpens, opened: opened, linkOf: linkOf, decorate: decorate,
    certHtml: certHtml, printCert: printNow, certShown: certShown, loadCfg: loadCfg, sig: sig, cleanSignature: cleanSignature,
    state: function () { return { U: U, C: C }; }, lastCert: null
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v195 Sign-offs: volunteers' letters and forms, signed (02/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/10/26): "I'd also like to add my signature to volunteer recognition and volunteer hours sign off requests
   that volunteers can ask for on their profile in the ebsite. This would appear on our volunteer manager as it's own
   page to sign off on hours completed with us." His choices: an hours sign-off letter, a letter of recognition, or the
   volunteer's own form signed; Operations leads, admins and the people an admin assigns sign; the signed PDF goes to the
   volunteer's profile on welfareavenue.com and by email.

   - The Volunteer Manager's Sign-offs page (#volunteering/signoffs; in the rail under People, with the waiting count;
     a chip on phones): Waiting, Signed, Declined, All. Volunteers ask from their profile (wave194's account page).
   - A request opens in a sheet: what they asked for and who it is for, their record, then, for a signer: the servings to
     list with their hours (each can be changed or left out), the signer's own words on a recognition letter, where the
     signature goes on the volunteer's own form (a page, a corner, a size; our hours page after it), a preview of the PDF,
     Sign and send (the volunteer is emailed that it is ready, unless the signer says not to) or Decline with a reason the
     volunteer sees. A signed request keeps its PDF and reference (WA-VS-xxxxxx) to download again.
   - My signature: the name and title printed under it (English, and French for French letters), the signature drawn
     with a finger or the mouse, or uploaded; Steel's can come from the signature he sent for the Academy certificate
     (app_config cert_signature, wave194 of another session). A sample letter shows how it prints.
   - Signers: who can sign (admins, Operations leads, people assigned); admins assign or remove team members.
   - The page tour "signoffs" (the Academy row Page tour: Sign-offs).
   Everything else is in the database (wa195_signoffs: signoff_list, signoff_detail, signoff_decline, signoff_signer_me,
   signoff_signer_save, signoff_signers, signoff_signer_assign, signoff_counts) and the signoff edge function (the PDFs
   and the signatures, in the private signoffs bucket). Nothing here switches mail_mode. The page hooks into the
   Volunteer Manager at run time (waVolShell, waVolRender, WA_APP.apps.volunteer.groups, the masthead), so no other
   segment changes.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa195) return; window.__wa195 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', INK = '#1F201E', MUTED = '#797773', LINE = 'rgba(16,63,57,.14)';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var KINDS = {
    hours: { l: 'Hours sign-off letter', s: 'Their servings and hours, signed' },
    recognition: { l: 'Letter of recognition', s: 'A letter about what they bring to our community, signed' },
    form: { l: 'Their own form', s: 'The form their school or employer gave them, signed' }
  };
  var STATUS = { requested: 'Waiting', signed: 'Signed', declined: 'Declined', all: 'All' };
  /* where the signature goes on their form: the top left corner and the width, as parts of the page */
  var PLACES = [['br', 'Bottom right', 0.58, 0.80], ['bl', 'Bottom left', 0.10, 0.80], ['bc', 'Bottom centre', 0.36, 0.80], ['mr', 'Middle right', 0.58, 0.48], ['tr', 'Top right', 0.62, 0.08]];
  var SIZES = [['s', 'Small', 0.2], ['m', 'Medium', 0.28], ['l', 'Large', 0.38]];
  var S = {
    status: 'requested', list: null, counts: {}, canSign: false, err: '', loading: false, me: null, signers: null, signersBusy: false,
    waiting: null, det: null, items: null, note: '', place: 'br', size: 'm', page: '', addPage: true, caption: true, notify: true,
    preview: null, previewBusy: false, step: '', reason: '', busy: false, sheetErr: '', sigUrl: null, sigAt: 0, cert: undefined,
    mine: null, mineErr: '', mineBusy: false, sampleBusy: false
  };

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function onView() { try { return typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'signoffs'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function first(name) { return String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || ''; }
  function msgOf(err) { var m = String((err && (err.message || err.error_description || err.error)) || err || ''); return m.replace(/^.*?:\s(?=[A-Z])/, '') || 'Something went wrong.'; }
  function fmtDay(iso, long) {
    if (!iso) return '';
    try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', long ? { timeZone: 'UTC', weekday: 'short', year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' } : { timeZone: 'UTC', year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
    catch (e) { return String(iso).slice(0, 10); }
  }
  function ago(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    var d = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(ts).getTime()) / 86400000);
    return d <= 0 ? 'today' : d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d < 14 ? d + ' days ago' : fmtDay(ts);
  }
  function num(n) { var x = Math.round(Number(n || 0) * 100) / 100; return String(x); }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (Number(n) === 1 ? one : many); }
  async function rpc(fn, args) { try { return await db.rpc(fn, args || {}); } catch (e) { return { error: e }; } }
  /* the signoff function, as the signed-in person (their Hub sign-in); an error's own words when it has some */
  async function call(body) {
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('signoff', { body: body });
      if (!r.error) return { ok: true, data: r.data || {} };
      var j = null;
      try { j = r.error.context && typeof r.error.context.json === 'function' ? await r.error.context.json() : null; } catch (e) { j = null; }
      return { ok: false, data: j || {}, error: (j && (j.error === 'server' && j.detail ? j.detail : j.error)) || msgOf(r.error) };
    } catch (e) { return { ok: false, data: {}, error: msgOf(e) }; }
  }
  var WORDS = { not_allowed: 'This is for the Volunteer Manager\u2019s team.', not_found: 'That request is gone.', not_signer: 'Only a signer can do that.', signin: 'Your session ended. Sign in again.' };
  function words(e) { return WORDS[e] || e || 'Something went wrong.'; }

  /* ---------- the page: Waiting, Signed, Declined, All; my signature; the signers ---------- */
  function setWaiting(n) {
    S.waiting = n;
    try {
      var a = document.querySelector('#w153rail a[data-w153go="#volunteering/signoffs"]'); if (!a) return;
      var b = a.querySelector('.w153n');
      if (n) { if (!b) { b = document.createElement('small'); b.className = 'w153n'; a.appendChild(b); } b.textContent = String(n); }
      else if (b) b.parentNode.removeChild(b);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function counts() {
    if (!staff()) return;
    var r = await rpc('signoff_counts');
    if (!r.error && r.data && r.data.requested != null) setWaiting(Number(r.data.requested) || 0);
  }
  async function load() {
    if (S.loading) return;
    S.loading = true;
    var r = await rpc('signoff_list', { p_status: S.status });
    if (r.error) { S.err = msgOf(r.error); S.list = []; }
    else { S.err = ''; S.list = (r.data && r.data.rows) || []; S.counts = (r.data && r.data.counts) || {}; S.canSign = !!(r.data && r.data.can_sign); setWaiting(Number(S.counts.requested || 0)); }
    if (!S.me) { var m = await rpc('signoff_signer_me'); if (!m.error && m.data) { S.me = m.data; S.meErr = false; } else S.meErr = true; }
    S.loading = false;
    paint();
    if (S.me && S.me.can_sign && S.me.has_signature && !S.sigUrl) sigImage();
    if (!S.signers && !S.signersBusy) loadSigners();
  }
  async function loadSigners() {
    S.signersBusy = true;
    var r = await rpc('signoff_signers');
    S.signersBusy = false;
    if (!r.error && r.data) { S.signers = r.data; paintSide(); }
  }
  async function sigImage() {
    var r = await call({ action: 'signature_url' });
    if (r.ok && r.data.url) { S.sigUrl = r.data.url; S.sigAt = Date.now(); paintSide(); }
  }
  function kindL(k) { return (KINDS[k] || {}).l || k; }
  function rowHtml(x) {
    var bits = [];
    if (x.for_org) bits.push('For ' + x.for_org);
    if (x.status === 'requested') bits.push('asked ' + ago(x.requested));
    else if (x.status === 'signed') bits.push('signed ' + ago(x.signed) + (x.signer ? ' by ' + x.signer : ''));
    else if (x.status === 'declined') bits.push('declined ' + ago(x.declined));
    if (x.kind !== 'recognition' && x.status === 'requested') bits.push(plural(Number(x.now_servings || 0), 'serving', 'servings') + (x.period_from || x.period_to ? ' in their dates' : ''));
    if (x.reference) bits.push(x.reference);
    return '<button type="button" class="w195row" data-w195open="' + Number(x.id) + '"><span class="k">' + E(kindL(x.kind)) + (x.lang === 'fr' ? ' \u00b7 en fran\u00e7ais' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<b>' + E(x.name || 'A volunteer') + '</b><span class="m">' + E(bits.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span><span class="w195st ' + E(x.status) + '">' + E(STATUS[x.status] || x.status) + '</span></button>';
  }
  function listHtml() {
    var inner;
    if (S.list === null) inner = '<div class="w195empty">Loading the requests\u2026</div>';
    else if (S.err) inner = '<div class="w195warn">' + E(S.err) + '</div>';
    else if (!S.list.length) {
      inner = '<div class="w195empty">' + E(S.status === 'requested' ? 'Nothing waiting. Volunteers ask for a letter or a signed form from their profile on welfareavenue.com, and their requests show here.'
        : S.status === 'signed' ? 'Nothing signed yet.' : S.status === 'declined' ? 'Nothing declined.' : 'No requests yet.') + '</div>';
    }
    else return '<div class="w195list" id="w195list">' + S.list.map(rowHtml).join('') + '</div>';
    return '<div id="w195list">' + inner + '</div>';
  }
  function filtersHtml() {
    var c = S.counts || {}, all = Object.keys(c).reduce(function (a, k) { return k === 'cancelled' ? a : a + Number(c[k] || 0); }, 0);
    return '<div class="w195f" id="w195filters" role="group" aria-label="Show">' + ['requested', 'signed', 'declined', 'all'].map(function (k) {
      var n = k === 'all' ? all : Number(c[k] || 0);
      return '<button type="button" data-w195status="' + k + '"' + (S.status === k ? ' class="on" aria-pressed="true"' : ' aria-pressed="false"') + '>' + E(STATUS[k]) + (n ? '<small>' + n + '</small>' : '') + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function mineHtml() {
    var me = S.me;
    if (!me) return '<div class="w195card" id="w195mine"><h3>My signature</h3><p class="w195muted">' + (S.meErr ? 'Your signer details did not load. Reload the page to try again.' : 'Loading\u2026') + '</p></div>';
    if (!me.can_sign) {
      return '<div class="w195card" id="w195mine"><h3>Who signs</h3><p>Admins, the Operations leads and the people an admin assigns sign these letters. You can see every request here.</p></div>';
    }
    var ready = me.has_signature && me.name && me.title;
    return '<div class="w195card" id="w195mine"><h3>My signature</h3>' +
      (me.has_signature ? (S.sigUrl ? '<img class="w195sig" src="' + E(S.sigUrl) + '" alt="Your saved signature">' : '<p class="w195muted">Your signature is saved.</p>') : '<p>No signature saved yet. Add it once and every letter you sign carries it.</p>') +
      (me.name ? '<p><span class="w195who">' + E(me.name) + '</span>' + (me.title ? '<br>' + E(me.title) + (me.title_fr ? '<br><span class="w195muted">' + E(me.title_fr) + ' (French letters)</span>' : '') : '') + '</p>' : '') +
      (!ready ? '<div class="w195warn">Before you sign: ' + E([!me.has_signature ? 'your signature' : '', !me.name ? 'your name' : '', !me.title ? 'your title' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ')) + '.</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w195add"><button type="button" class="w195btn' + (me.has_signature ? ' ghost' : '') + '" data-w195mine="1">' + (me.has_signature ? 'Change' : 'Add my signature') + '</button>' +
      (me.has_signature ? '<button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195sample="1"' + (S.sampleBusy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.sampleBusy ? 'Drawing\u2026' : 'See a sample letter') + '</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function signersHtml() {
    var d = S.signers; if (!d) return '';
    var WHY = { admin: 'Admin', lead: 'Operations lead', assigned: 'Assigned' };
    var h = '<div class="w195card" id="w195signers"><h3>Signers</h3><ul class="w195signers">' + (d.signers || []).map(function (s) {
      return '<li><span>' + E(s.name || s.email) + (s.has_signature ? ' <span title="Signature saved">\u2713</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="why">' + E(WHY[s.why] || s.why) + '</span>' +
        (d.admin && s.why === 'assigned' ? '<button type="button" class="w195link" data-w195unassign="' + E(s.email) + '">Remove</button>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul>';
    if (d.admin && (d.people || []).length) {
      h += '<div class="w195add"><select id="w195pick" aria-label="A team member to add as a signer"><option value="">Add a signer\u2026</option>' + d.people.map(function (p) {
        return '<option value="' + E(p.email) + '">' + E(p.name + (p.department ? ' (' + p.department + ')' : '')) + '</option>';
      }).join('') + '</select><button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195assign="1">Add</button></div>';
    }
    return h + '</div>';
  }
  function pageHtml() {
    return '<div id="w195"><p class="w195lead">Letters and forms volunteers ask us to sign from their profile on welfareavenue.com: an hours sign-off letter, a letter of recognition, or their own school or employer form. Check what they ask for, then sign with your saved signature. They get the PDF on their profile and an email that it is ready.</p>' +
      '<div class="w195top"><div>' + filtersHtml() + listHtml() + '</div><div id="w195side">' + mineHtml() + signersHtml() + '</div></div></div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    if (!onView()) return;
    css();
    try { if (typeof waVolPaint === 'function') waVolPaint(pageHtml()); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    masthead();
  }
  function paintSide() { var s = document.getElementById('w195side'); if (s && onView()) s.innerHTML = mineHtml() + signersHtml(); }
  async function sample() {
    if (S.sampleBusy) return;
    S.sampleBusy = true; paintSide();
    var r = await call({ action: 'sample', kind: 'hours', lang: 'en' });
    S.sampleBusy = false; paintSide();
    if (r.ok && r.data.url) { try { window.open(r.data.url, '_blank', 'noopener'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    else T(words(r.error));
  }
  async function assign(email, on) {
    if (!email) { T('Choose someone first'); return; }
    var r = await rpc('signoff_signer_assign', { p_email: email, p_on: !!on });
    if (r.error) { T(msgOf(r.error)); return; }
    T(on ? 'Added as a signer' : 'Removed from the signers');
    S.signers = null; loadSigners();
  }

  /* ---------- a request, in a sheet: what they asked for, their record, then sign or decline ---------- */
  var SUB = 'Letters and forms volunteers ask us to sign from their profile on welfareavenue.com';
  function escKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('w195sheet')) closeSheet(); }
  function closeSheet() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w195sheet'); if (s) s.parentNode.removeChild(s);
    S.det = null; S.preview = null; S.step = ''; S.sheetErr = ''; S.sheetOk = ''; S.mine = null;
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', escKey);
  }
  function sheet(inner) {
    css();
    var s = document.getElementById('w195sheet');
    if (!s) {
      s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w195sheet'; s.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); s.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
      document.body.appendChild(s);
      s.addEventListener('click', onSheetClick); s.addEventListener('input', onSheetInput); s.addEventListener('change', onSheetChange);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', escKey);
    }
    var old = s.querySelector('.w195pane'), keep = old ? old.scrollTop : 0;
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="w195pane"><button type="button" class="w195x" data-w195close="1" aria-label="Close">\u00d7</button>' + inner + '</div>';
    var p = s.querySelector('.w195pane'); if (p) p.scrollTop = keep;
    return s;
  }
  function dt(k, v) { return '<dt>' + E(k) + '</dt><dd>' + E(v) + '</dd>'; }
  async function open(id) {
    S.det = null; S.items = null; S.note = ''; S.place = 'br'; S.size = 'm'; S.page = ''; S.addPage = true; S.caption = true; S.notify = true;
    S.preview = null; S.stale = false; S.step = ''; S.reason = ''; S.sheetErr = ''; S.sheetOk = ''; S.mine = null;
    sheet('<p class="w195muted">Loading\u2026</p>');
    await fill(id);
  }
  async function fill(id) {
    var r = await rpc('signoff_detail', { p_id: id });
    if (r.error || !r.data) { sheet('<div class="w195warn">' + E(r.error ? msgOf(r.error) : 'That request is gone.') + '</div>'); return; }
    S.det = r.data;
    /* 11/10/26: words prepared for the signer ahead of time (signer_note on a waiting request) fill the box */
    if (!S.note && r.data.request && r.data.request.status === 'requested' && r.data.request.signer_note) S.note = r.data.request.signer_note;
    if (!S.me) { var m = await rpc('signoff_signer_me'); if (!m.error && m.data) S.me = m.data; }
    S.items = (r.data.servings || []).map(function (x) { return { roster_id: x.roster_id, date: x.date, role: x.role_en || x.role, location: x.location, hours: Number(x.hours || 0), on: true }; });
    paintSheet();
  }
  function totText() {
    var on = (S.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x.on; });
    return plural(on.length, 'serving', 'servings') + ', ' + num(on.reduce(function (a, x) { return a + Number(x.hours || 0); }, 0)) + ' hours';
  }
  function servingsHtml() {
    var it = S.items || [];
    if (!it.length) return '<h3>Servings to list</h3><div class="w195warn">No confirmed servings in their dates. Decline with a note, or ask them for other dates.</div>';
    return '<h3>Servings to list</h3><p class="w195muted">Every confirmed serving in their dates, with the roster\u2019s hours (the usual 2 when none were recorded). Untick one to leave it out, or change its hours.</p>' +
      '<div class="w195box"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col"><span class="sr">List</span></th><th scope="col">Date</th><th scope="col">Role</th><th scope="col">Where</th><th scope="col">Hours</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      it.map(function (x, i) {
        return '<tr' + (x.on ? '' : ' class="off"') + '><td><input type="checkbox" data-w195on="' + i + '"' + (x.on ? ' checked' : '') + ' aria-label="List ' + E(fmtDay(x.date)) + '"></td><td>' + E(fmtDay(x.date)) + '</td><td>' + E(x.role || '') + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + E(x.location || '') + '</td><td><input type="number" min="0.25" max="24" step="0.25" value="' + E(num(x.hours)) + '" data-w195h="' + i + '" aria-label="Hours on ' + E(fmtDay(x.date)) + '"></td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table><div class="w195tot" id="w195tot">' + E(totText()) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function placementHtml() {
    return '<h3>Where your signature goes on their form</h3><p class="w195muted">Open their form to see where it asks for a signature, then pick the spot. The preview shows it before you sign.</p><div class="w195box">' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><span class="w195k">Page</span><label><input type="radio" name="w195pg" value=""' + (S.page === '' ? ' checked' : '') + '> The last page</label>' +
      '<label><input type="radio" name="w195pg" value="n"' + (S.page !== '' ? ' checked' : '') + '> Page</label><input type="number" min="1" max="200" id="w195pgn" aria-label="Page number" value="' + E(S.page || '1') + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><span class="w195k">Where</span>' + PLACES.map(function (p) { return '<label><input type="radio" name="w195pl" value="' + p[0] + '"' + (S.place === p[0] ? ' checked' : '') + '> ' + E(p[1]) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><span class="w195k">Size</span>' + SIZES.map(function (z) { return '<label><input type="radio" name="w195sz" value="' + z[0] + '"' + (S.size === z[0] ? ' checked' : '') + '> ' + E(z[1]) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><label><input type="checkbox" id="w195cap"' + (S.caption ? ' checked' : '') + '> Your name, title, the date and the reference under the signature</label></div>' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><label><input type="checkbox" id="w195add"' + (S.addPage ? ' checked' : '') + '> Add our hours confirmation page after their form</label></div></div>';
  }
  function placement() {
    var p = PLACES.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === S.place; })[0] || PLACES[0], z = SIZES.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === S.size; })[0] || SIZES[1];
    var o = { x: p[2], y: p[3], w: z[2], caption: !!S.caption };
    if (S.page !== '') o.page = Math.max(0, (Number(S.page) || 1) - 1);
    return o;
  }
  function bodyFor(action) {
    var q = S.det.request, b = { action: action, id: q.id };
    if (q.kind !== 'recognition') b.items = (S.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x.on; }).map(function (x) { return { roster_id: x.roster_id, hours: Number(x.hours) }; });
    if (q.kind !== 'form') b.note = S.note || '';   /* 11/10/26: the signer's comment also goes on an hours letter */
    if (q.kind === 'form') { b.placement = placement(); b.add_page = !!S.addPage; }
    if (action === 'sign') b.notify = !!S.notify;
    return b;
  }
  function ready() { var me = S.me || {}; return !!(me.has_signature && me.name && me.title); }
  function actionsHtml() {
    var d = S.det, q = d.request, me = S.me || {}, fn = d.first_name || first(d.name) || 'They';
    if (S.step === 'confirm') {
      return '<div class="w195confirm" role="alertdialog" aria-label="Sign this request"><p><b>Sign as ' + E(me.name) + ', ' + E(q.lang === 'fr' ? (me.title_fr || me.title) : me.title) + '?</b> ' +
        E(fn) + ' gets the signed PDF on their profile' + E(S.notify ? ', and an email that it is ready.' : '. No email goes out.') + '</p>' +
        '<div class="w195row2"><label><input type="checkbox" id="w195notify"' + (S.notify ? ' checked' : '') + '> Email ' + E(fn) + ' that it is ready</label></div>' +
        '<div class="w195acts"><button type="button" class="w195btn" data-w195sign="1"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Signing\u2026' : 'Yes, sign it') + '</button>' +
        '<button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195step="">Not yet</button></div></div>';
    }
    if (S.step === 'decline') {
      return '<div class="w195confirm"><p><b>Decline this request?</b> ' + E(fn) + ' sees your note on their profile. Say why in a few words, and what would work instead if anything.</p>' +
        '<label class="sr" for="w195reason">Your note to them</label><textarea id="w195reason" maxlength="500" placeholder="For example: this form is for hours with another organization.">' + E(S.reason) + '</textarea>' +
        '<div class="w195acts"><button type="button" class="w195btn flag" data-w195decline="1"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Declining\u2026' : 'Decline the request') + '</button>' +
        '<button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195step="">Back</button></div></div>';
    }
    var none = q.kind !== 'recognition' && !(S.items || []).some(function (x) { return x.on; });
    var why = !ready() ? 'Add your signature, name and title in My signature first' : none ? 'List at least one serving' : '';
    return '<div class="w195acts"><button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195preview="1"' + (S.previewBusy || none ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.previewBusy ? 'Drawing\u2026' : 'Preview') + '</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w195btn" data-w195step="confirm"' + (why ? ' disabled title="' + E(why) + '"' : '') + '>Sign and send</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w195btn flag" data-w195step="decline">Decline</button></div>' + (why && !none ? '<p class="w195muted">' + E(why) + '.</p>' : '');
  }
  function paintSheet() {
    var d = S.det; if (!d) return;
    var q = d.request, waiting = q.status === 'requested', fn = d.first_name || first(d.name);
    var h = '<p class="w195k">' + E(kindL(q.kind)) + (q.lang === 'fr' ? ' \u00b7 the letter is in French' : '') + '</p><h2>' + E(d.name || 'A volunteer') + '</h2>' +
      '<p><span class="w195st ' + E(q.status) + '">' + E(STATUS[q.status] || q.status) + '</span> <span class="w195muted">asked ' + E(fmtDay(q.created_at, true)) + '</span></p>' +
      (S.sheetOk ? '<div class="w195ok" role="status">' + E(S.sheetOk) + '</div>' : '') + (S.sheetErr ? '<div class="w195warn" role="alert">' + E(S.sheetErr) + '</div>' : '');
    h += '<h3>What they asked for</h3><div class="w195box"><dl>' + dt('Kind', kindL(q.kind)) +
      dt('For', q.for_org || 'Not said: the letter is addressed to whom it may concern') + (q.purpose ? dt('What for', q.purpose) : '') +
      (q.kind !== 'recognition' ? dt('Dates', q.period_from || q.period_to ? (q.period_from ? fmtDay(q.period_from) : 'their first serving') + ' to ' + (q.period_to ? fmtDay(q.period_to) : 'today') : 'All their servings') : '') +
      (q.notes ? '<dt>Their note</dt><dd class="w195q">' + E(q.notes) + '</dd>' : '') +
      (q.kind === 'form' ? '<dt>Their form</dt><dd>' + E(q.form_name || 'Their form') + (q.form_bytes ? ' <span class="w195muted">(' + Math.max(1, Math.round(q.form_bytes / 1024)) + ' KB)</span>' : '') +
        ' <button type="button" class="w195link" data-w195form="1">Open it</button></dd>' : '') +
      dt('Email', d.email || 'None on file') + '</dl></div>';
    h += '<h3>Their record</h3><div class="w195box">' + E(plural(Number(d.all_count || 0), 'confirmed serving', 'confirmed servings') + ', about ' + num(d.all_hours) + ' hours' +
      (d.first ? ', from ' + fmtDay(d.first) + ' to ' + fmtDay(d.last) : '') + '.') + ((d.roles || []).filter(Boolean).length ? ' <span class="w195muted">' + E(d.roles.filter(Boolean).join(', ')) + '</span>' : '') +
      (d.request.person_id ? ' <a class="w195link" href="#volunteering/people" data-w195person="' + Number(d.request.person_id) + '">Their volunteer record</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    if (q.status === 'signed') {
      h += '<h3>Signed</h3><div class="w195box"><dl>' + dt('Signed', fmtDay(q.signed_at, true) + (q.signer_name ? ' by ' + q.signer_name + (q.signer_title ? ', ' + q.signer_title : '') : '')) +
        dt('Reference', q.reference || '') + (q.kind !== 'recognition' ? dt('Listed', plural(Number(q.servings || 0), 'serving', 'servings') + ', ' + num(q.hours) + ' hours') : '') +
        dt('Emailed', q.emailed_at ? fmtDay(q.emailed_at, true) : 'Not emailed') + '</dl></div><div class="w195acts"><button type="button" class="w195btn" data-w195letter="1">Open the signed PDF</button></div>';
    } else if (q.status === 'declined') {
      h += '<h3>Declined</h3><div class="w195box"><dl>' + dt('When', fmtDay(q.declined_at, true)) + (q.declined_by ? dt('By', q.declined_by) : '') +
        '<dt>Our note to them</dt><dd class="w195q">' + E(q.decline_reason || '') + '</dd></dl></div>';
    } else if (waiting && !d.can_sign) {
      h += '<div class="w195box">Only a signer can sign or decline. Admins, the Operations leads and the people an admin assigns sign these letters.</div>';
    } else if (waiting) {
      if (!ready()) h += '<div class="w195warn">Add your signature, name and title before you sign. <button type="button" class="w195link" data-w195mine="1">Open My signature</button></div>';
      if (q.kind !== 'recognition') h += servingsHtml();
      if (q.kind !== 'form') {   /* 11/10/26: recognition letters and hours letters both take the signer's own words */
        h += '<h3>' + (q.kind === 'hours' ? 'Your comment' : 'Your words') + '</h3><p class="w195muted">' + (q.kind === 'hours' ? 'Optional. The letter already lists their servings, hours and roles; your comment prints under the table.' : 'The letter already says when ' + E(fn || 'they') + ' started, their servings, hours and roles.') + ' Add a few lines of your own if you like: what they bring, a moment that stood out. A blank line starts a new paragraph.</p>' +
          '<label class="sr" for="w195note">Your words</label><textarea id="w195note" maxlength="1500">' + E(S.note) + '</textarea>';
      }
      if (q.kind === 'form') h += placementHtml();
      h += actionsHtml();
      if (S.preview) {
        h += '<h3 id="w195pv">Preview</h3><p class="w195muted"><span id="w195stale">' + (S.stale ? STALE : '') + '</span>Marked PREVIEW; the signed copy carries its reference instead. ' +
          '<a href="' + E(S.preview.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it in a new tab</a></p><iframe src="' + E(S.preview.url) + '" title="Preview of the PDF"></iframe>';
      }
    }
    sheet(h);
  }
  /* a change after the preview: say so where the preview is, without drawing the sheet again */
  var STALE = '<b>Before your last change.</b> Preview again to see it. ';
  function stale() {
    if (!S.preview || S.stale) return;
    S.stale = true;
    var n = document.getElementById('w195stale'); if (n) n.innerHTML = STALE;
  }
  async function preview() {
    if (S.previewBusy || !S.det) return;
    S.previewBusy = true; S.sheetErr = ''; S.sheetOk = ''; paintSheet();
    var r = await call(bodyFor('preview'));
    S.previewBusy = false;
    if (r.ok && r.data.url) {
      S.preview = { url: r.data.url, pages: r.data.pages }; S.stale = false; paintSheet();
      var f = document.getElementById('w195pv'); if (f) { try { f.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    } else { S.sheetErr = words(r.error); paintSheet(); }
  }
  function mailWords(m) {
    m = m || {};
    if (m.skipped) return 'No email sent.';
    var st = String(m.status || '');
    if (st === 'sent' || st === 'queued') return 'The volunteer was emailed.';
    if (/test/.test(st) || /test/i.test(String(m.note || ''))) return 'The Hub\u2019s email is in test mode, so the email went to the test recipients.';
    return 'The email did not go out (' + (m.note || m.error || st || 'no reason given') + '); the PDF is on their profile.';
  }
  async function sign() {
    if (S.busy || !S.det) return;
    var id = S.det.request.id;
    S.busy = true; S.sheetErr = ''; paintSheet();
    var r = await call(bodyFor('sign'));
    S.busy = false;
    if (r.ok && r.data.ok) {
      T('Signed: ' + r.data.reference);
      S.step = ''; S.preview = null; S.sheetOk = 'Signed. Reference ' + r.data.reference + '. ' + mailWords(r.data.email);
      await fill(id); load(); counts();
    } else { S.step = 'confirm'; S.sheetErr = words(r.error); paintSheet(); }
  }
  async function decline() {
    if (S.busy || !S.det) return;
    var reason = String(S.reason || '').trim(), id = S.det.request.id, fn = first(S.det.name) || 'They';
    if (reason.length < 3) { S.sheetErr = 'Say why in a few words: the volunteer sees it.'; paintSheet(); return; }
    S.busy = true; S.sheetErr = ''; paintSheet();
    var r = await rpc('signoff_decline', { p_id: id, p_reason: reason });
    S.busy = false;
    if (r.error) { S.sheetErr = msgOf(r.error); paintSheet(); return; }
    T('Declined'); S.step = ''; S.sheetOk = 'Declined. ' + fn + ' sees your note on their profile.';
    await fill(id); load(); counts();
  }
  async function openFile(action) {
    var w = null;
    try { w = window.open('about:blank', '_blank'); if (w) w.opener = null; } catch (e) { w = null; }
    var r = await call({ action: action, id: S.det.request.id });
    if (r.ok && r.data.url) { if (w) w.location.href = r.data.url; else window.location.href = r.data.url; }
    else { try { if (w) w.close(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } T(words(r.error)); }
  }
  function onSheetClick(e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && t.id === 'w195sheet') { closeSheet(); return; }
    var el = t && t.closest ? t.closest('button, a[data-w195person]') : null; if (!el) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195close')) closeSheet();
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195form')) openFile('form_url');
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195letter')) openFile('letter_url');
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195preview')) preview();
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195step')) {
      S.step = el.getAttribute('data-w195step'); S.sheetErr = ''; paintSheet();
      var f = document.getElementById(S.step === 'decline' ? 'w195reason' : S.step === 'confirm' ? 'w195notify' : ''); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
    }
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195sign')) sign();
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195decline')) decline();
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195mine')) openMine();
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195person')) closeSheet();
    else mineClick(el);
  }
  function onSheetInput(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w195h') && S.items) {
      var i = Number(t.getAttribute('data-w195h')), v = Number(t.value);
      if (S.items[i] && isFinite(v) && v > 0 && v <= 24) { S.items[i].hours = Math.round(v * 4) / 4; var tot = document.getElementById('w195tot'); if (tot) tot.textContent = totText(); stale(); }
    }
    else if (t.id === 'w195note') { S.note = t.value; stale(); }
    else if (t.id === 'w195reason') S.reason = t.value;
    else if (t.id === 'w195pgn') { S.page = String(Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(t.value) || 1))); var rb = document.querySelector('#w195sheet input[name="w195pg"][value="n"]'); if (rb) rb.checked = true; stale(); }
    else mineInput(t);
  }
  function onSheetChange(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w195on') && S.items) { var i = Number(t.getAttribute('data-w195on')); if (S.items[i]) S.items[i].on = t.checked; stale(); paintSheet(); }
    else if (t.name === 'w195pg') { S.page = t.value === 'n' ? String(Math.max(1, Number((document.getElementById('w195pgn') || {}).value) || 1)) : ''; stale(); }
    else if (t.name === 'w195pl') { S.place = t.value; stale(); }
    else if (t.name === 'w195sz') { S.size = t.value; stale(); }
    else if (t.id === 'w195cap') { S.caption = t.checked; stale(); }
    else if (t.id === 'w195add') { S.addPage = t.checked; stale(); }
    else if (t.id === 'w195notify') { S.notify = t.checked; paintSheet(); }
    else mineChange(t);
  }

  /* ---------- my signature: the name and titles under it, the signature drawn, uploaded or the one Steel sent ---------- */
  async function loadCert() {
    if (S.cert !== undefined) return S.cert;
    S.cert = null;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'cert_signature').maybeSingle();
      var v = r && r.data ? r.data.value : null, j = typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : v;
      if (j && /^data:image\/png;base64,/.test(String(j.image || ''))) S.cert = { image: j.image, name: String(j.name || ''), title: String(j.title || '') };
    } catch (e) { S.cert = null; }
    return S.cert;
  }
  /* the certificate's signature is offered only to the person it belongs to */
  function certOk() {
    var c = S.cert, m = S.mine || {}, me = S.me || {}, own = lower(c && c.name);
    if (!own) return false;
    var mine = [m.name, me.name, (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || ''].map(lower);
    return mine.indexOf(own) >= 0;
  }
  async function openMine() {
    var me = S.me || {};
    S.mine = { name: me.name || (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || '', title: me.title || '', title_fr: me.title_fr || '', tab: me.has_signature ? 'keep' : 'draw', png: null, canvas: null };
    S.mineErr = '';
    sheet('<p class="w195muted">Loading\u2026</p>');
    await loadCert();
    if (certOk()) { if (!me.has_signature) S.mine.tab = 'cert'; if (!S.mine.title && S.cert.title) S.mine.title = S.cert.title; }
    if (me.has_signature && !S.sigUrl) await sigImage();
    paintMine();
  }
  function paintMine() {
    var m = S.mine, me = S.me || {}; if (!m) return;
    var tabs = [];
    if (me.has_signature) tabs.push(['keep', 'Keep my signature']);
    if (certOk()) tabs.push(['cert', 'The one I sent for the certificate']);
    tabs.push(['draw', 'Draw it'], ['upload', 'Upload a picture']);
    var h = '<p class="w195k">Volunteer Manager \u00b7 Sign-offs</p><h2>My signature</h2><p class="w195muted">What prints at the bottom of every letter you sign: your signature, then your name and title, then Welfare Avenue.</p>' +
      (S.mineErr ? '<div class="w195warn" role="alert">' + E(S.mineErr) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w195grid"><div><label for="w195mn">Name</label><input type="text" id="w195mn" maxlength="120" autocomplete="name" value="' + E(m.name) + '"></div>' +
      '<div><label for="w195mt">Title</label><input type="text" id="w195mt" maxlength="120" placeholder="For example Operations Lead" value="' + E(m.title) + '"></div>' +
      '<div><label for="w195mf">Title in French, for French letters (optional)</label><input type="text" id="w195mf" maxlength="120" placeholder="For example Responsable des op\u00e9rations" value="' + E(m.title_fr) + '"></div></div>' +
      '<h3>Signature</h3><div class="w195tabs" role="group" aria-label="Your signature">' + tabs.map(function (x) {
        return '<button type="button" data-w195tab="' + x[0] + '"' + (m.tab === x[0] ? ' class="on" aria-pressed="true"' : ' aria-pressed="false"') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (m.tab === 'keep') h += '<div class="w195box">' + (S.sigUrl ? '<img class="w195big" src="' + E(S.sigUrl) + '" alt="Your saved signature">' : '<span class="w195muted">Your saved signature stays as it is.</span>') + '</div>';
    else if (m.tab === 'cert') h += '<div class="w195cert"><img src="' + E(S.cert.image) + '" alt="The signature you sent"><span class="w195muted">The signature you sent for the Avenue Academy certificate. It prints the same way on volunteer letters.</span></div>';
    else if (m.tab === 'draw') h += '<div class="w195pad" id="w195pad"><canvas aria-label="Draw your signature here"></canvas><span class="w195line"></span></div>' +
      '<div class="w195row2"><button type="button" class="w195link" data-w195clear="1">Clear</button><span class="w195muted">Sign above the line with a finger, a pen or the mouse.</span></div>';
    else h += '<div class="w195box"><label class="sr" for="w195file">A picture of your signature</label><input type="file" id="w195file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg">' +
      '<p class="w195muted">A photo or a scan of your signature on white paper works: the white around it is cleared.</p>' + (m.png ? '<img class="w195big" src="' + m.png + '" alt="Your signature, ready to save">' : '') + '</div>';
    h += '<div class="w195acts"><button type="button" class="w195btn" data-w195save="1"' + (S.mineBusy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.mineBusy ? 'Saving\u2026' : 'Save') + '</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w195btn ghost" data-w195close="1">Cancel</button></div>';
    sheet(h);
    if (m.tab === 'draw') pad();
  }
  function pad() {
    var wrap = document.getElementById('w195pad'); if (!wrap || !S.mine) return;
    var cv = wrap.querySelector('canvas'), r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), dpr = Math.min(3, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    cv.width = Math.max(300, Math.round((r.width || 600) * dpr)); cv.height = Math.max(100, Math.round((r.height || 200) * dpr));
    var cx = cv.getContext('2d'), down = false, last = null;
    cx.lineCap = 'round'; cx.lineJoin = 'round'; cx.strokeStyle = INK; cx.fillStyle = INK; cx.lineWidth = 2.6 * dpr;
    function pt(e) { var b = cv.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: (e.clientX - b.left) * cv.width / b.width, y: (e.clientY - b.top) * cv.height / b.height }; }
    cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { down = true; last = pt(e); try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(last.x, last.y, cx.lineWidth / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2); cx.fill(); e.preventDefault(); });
    cv.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (!down) return; var p = pt(e); cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(last.x, last.y); cx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); cx.stroke(); last = p; e.preventDefault(); });
    function end() { down = false; last = null; }
    cv.addEventListener('pointerup', end); cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
    S.mine.canvas = cv;
  }
  /* the drawing, cropped to its ink with a margin, at most 1200 x 600, as a PNG with a clear background */
  function trimmed(cv) {
    var w = cv.width, h = cv.height, d = cv.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data, x0 = w, y0 = h, x1 = -1, y1 = -1;
    for (var y = 0; y < h; y++) for (var x = 0; x < w; x++) { if (d[(y * w + x) * 4 + 3] > 16) { if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y; } }
    if (x1 < 0) return null;
    var m = 12; x0 = Math.max(0, x0 - m); y0 = Math.max(0, y0 - m); x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x1 + m); y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y1 + m);
    var cw = x1 - x0 + 1, ch = y1 - y0 + 1, s = Math.min(1, 1200 / cw, 600 / ch);
    var out = document.createElement('canvas'); out.width = Math.max(40, Math.round(cw * s)); out.height = Math.max(15, Math.round(ch * s));
    out.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv, x0, y0, cw, ch, 0, 0, out.width, out.height);
    return out.toDataURL('image/png');
  }
  function fromFile(file) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      if (!file || !/^image\/(png|jpeg)$/.test(file.type)) { rej(new Error('Choose a PNG or JPEG picture.')); return; }
      if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { rej(new Error('That picture is over 8 MB.')); return; }
      var fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onload = function () {
        var img = new Image();
        img.onload = function () {
          var s = Math.min(1, 2000 / img.width, 1000 / img.height), cv = document.createElement('canvas');
          cv.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width * s)); cv.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.height * s));
          var cx = cv.getContext('2d'); cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
          var id = cx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height), d = id.data;
          for (var i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) { var l = (d[i] + d[i + 1] + d[i + 2]) / 3; if (l > 215) d[i + 3] = 0; else if (l > 160) d[i + 3] = Math.round(d[i + 3] * (215 - l) / 55); }
          cx.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
          var png = trimmed(cv); if (!png) rej(new Error('We could not find a signature in that picture.')); else res(png);
        };
        img.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('That picture could not be read.')); };
        img.src = fr.result;
      };
      fr.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('That picture could not be read.')); };
      fr.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }
  async function saveMine() {
    var m = S.mine; if (!m || S.mineBusy) return;
    m.name = String(m.name || '').trim(); m.title = String(m.title || '').trim(); m.title_fr = String(m.title_fr || '').trim();
    if (!m.name || !m.title) { S.mineErr = 'Your name and title print under your signature: add both.'; paintMine(); return; }
    var png = null, me = S.me || {};
    if (m.tab === 'draw') { png = m.canvas ? trimmed(m.canvas) : null; if (!png) { S.mineErr = 'Draw your signature first, or choose another way.'; paintMine(); return; } }
    else if (m.tab === 'upload') { if (!m.png) { S.mineErr = 'Choose a picture of your signature first.'; paintMine(); return; } png = m.png; }
    else if (m.tab === 'cert') png = S.cert && S.cert.image;
    else if (!me.has_signature) { S.mineErr = 'Add your signature first.'; paintMine(); return; }
    S.mineBusy = true; S.mineErr = ''; paintMine();
    var r1 = await rpc('signoff_signer_save', { p: { name: m.name, title: m.title, title_fr: m.title_fr } });
    if (r1.error) { S.mineBusy = false; S.mineErr = msgOf(r1.error); paintMine(); return; }
    if (png) {
      var r2 = await call({ action: 'signature_save', png_b64: png });
      if (!r2.ok) { S.mineBusy = false; S.mineErr = words(r2.error); paintMine(); return; }
    }
    S.mineBusy = false;
    var r3 = await rpc('signoff_signer_me'); if (!r3.error && r3.data) S.me = r3.data;
    S.sigUrl = null; closeSheet(); T('Your signature is saved');
    paint(); if (S.me && S.me.has_signature) sigImage();
    S.signers = null; loadSigners();
  }
  function mineClick(el) {
    if (!S.mine) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195tab')) { S.mine.tab = el.getAttribute('data-w195tab'); S.mine.canvas = null; S.mineErr = ''; paintMine(); }
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195clear')) { var cv = S.mine.canvas; if (cv) cv.getContext('2d').clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); }
    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195save')) saveMine();
  }
  function mineInput(t) {
    if (!S.mine) return;
    if (t.id === 'w195mn') S.mine.name = t.value;
    else if (t.id === 'w195mt') S.mine.title = t.value;
    else if (t.id === 'w195mf') S.mine.title_fr = t.value;
  }
  function mineChange(t) {
    if (!S.mine || t.id !== 'w195file') return;
    var f = t.files && t.files[0]; if (!f) return;
    fromFile(f).then(function (png) { S.mine.png = png; S.mineErr = ''; paintMine(); }, function (e) { S.mine.png = null; S.mineErr = e.message; paintMine(); });
  }

  /* ---------- the masthead: the page's own title over wave191's ---------- */
  function masthead() {

  }
  function watch() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec || sec.getAttribute('data-w195mo')) return !!sec;
    sec.setAttribute('data-w195mo', '1');
    var t = null;
    try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (!onView()) return; clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(masthead, 40); }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return true;
  }

  /* ---------- the page's clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var root = document.getElementById('w195'); if (!root || !e.target || !root.contains(e.target)) return;
    var el = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('button') : null; if (!el) return;

    else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w195unassign')) assign(el.getAttribute('data-w195unassign'), false);
  });

  /* ---------- the Volunteer Manager: the view, the chip on phones, the rail item with the waiting count ---------- */
  function wrapVol() {
    var ok = true;
    if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') {
      if (!waVolRender.__w195) {

        waVolRender = async function () {

          }

        };

      }
    } else ok = false;
    if (typeof waVolShell === 'function') {

        waVolShell = function (inner) {

          try {

          } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

        };

      }
    } else ok = false;
    return ok;
  }
  function railItem() {
    var A = window.WA_APP && window.WA_APP.apps;
    if (!A || !A.volunteer || typeof A.volunteer.groups !== 'function') return false;
    if (A.volunteer.__w195) return true;
    var g0 = A.volunteer.groups;
    A.volunteer.groups = function () {
      var gs = g0.apply(this, arguments);
      if (!staff()) return gs;
      try {
        var it = { h: '#volunteering/signoffs', l: 'Sign-offs', ic: 'form', page: 'volunteering', n: S.waiting ? String(S.waiting) : null };
        var people = gs.filter(function (g) { return /people/i.test(g.label || ''); })[0];
        if (people) { if (!people.items.some(function (x) { return x.h === it.h; })) people.items.push(it); }
        else gs.push({ label: 'People', items: [it] });
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return gs;
    };
    A.volunteer.__w195 = true;
    return true;
  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v197 the HR centre, rebuilt for Valex, and page names in every app (03/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/10/26): "The HR center app of the HUB needs a full revamp on how
   it's displayed ... it says HR Center too many times, I think it should say
   just the page name ... that's for all apps." And for the HR centre: buttons
   that are not consistent, sections repeated across pages (last sign-in,
   commitment), pages that hold things unrelated to their name, and "I'd also
   like it to be easy to navigate the recruitment process. The intended purpose
   is to make it easy to manage HR so that Valex can easily navigate this
   center and do more with less time."
   His choices (02/10/26): the layout below; hours from The Loop only (the Slack
   hours table goes; its data stays in the database); French follows in wave198.

   1. Every app: the big title is the page's name (the rail's item), the crumb
      inside the page goes, and on a computer the app bar above the page goes
      (the sidebar already names the app); its buttons (Add a contact, Text
      volunteers) move into the page's header. Phones keep their bar (it holds
      the menu).
   2. The HR centre's rail: Overview; People: Team, Hours, Time off,
      Recognition; Hiring: Applications, Positions, Playbook; Growth:
      Onboarding, Onboarding plan, Training; Care: Policies, Discipline;
      Reports: Analytics (HR and admins). Each address #hrcentre/<page> opens
      exactly that page.
   3. Nothing repeats: the hero, the second intro, and the five sections that
      sat under every page (meeting attendance, quizzes, scope of work, time
      off, Hub engagement) each live on one page. Hub engagement leaves HR
      (the Admin centre's Adoption has sign-ins and use); the Slack scope of
      work becomes what people shared in The Loop.
   4. New: Overview (HR's to-do list and the numbers), Applications as a
      stage board with a candidate sheet, Hours from The Loop (minimums,
      below-minimum first, open sessions counted at most eight hours).
   How it works: the older waves still draw their sections; this wave tags
   each one with the page it belongs to (data-w197s) and shows only the
   current page's (#hrcentre[data-w197]); it clicks the older tab a page
   needs so its data loads. No data model change.
   ===================================================================== */
