  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- data (row security decides what each person reads; HR, leaders and admins see the HR tables) ---------- */
  async function q(p) { try { var r = await p; return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  async function loadCands(force) {
    if (S.cLoading) return S.cLoading;
    if (!force && S.candsAt && Date.now() - S.candsAt < 60000) return S.cands;
    S.cLoading = (async function () {
      S.cands = await q(db.from('hub_candidates').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(300));
      S.candsAt = Date.now(); S.cLoading = null; return S.cands;
    })();
    return S.cLoading;
  }
  /* The Loop's sessions (hub_checkins: the person, admins and HR read them, wave192c) */
  async function loadHours(force) {
    if (!force && S.ckAt && Date.now() - S.ckAt < 60000) return;
    var since = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000 * (S.hoursWeeks + 1)).toISOString();
    var res = await Promise.all([
      q(db.from('hub_checkins').select('email,name,department,started_at,ended_at,minutes,auto_closed').gte('started_at', since).order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3000)),
      q(db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'hours_minimums').limit(1)),
      (async function () { try { var r = await db.rpc('loop_feed', { p_days: 30, p_email: null }); return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } })(),
      q(db.from('slack_hours').select('member_name,week_start,hours,sessions').gte('week_start', since.slice(0, 10)).limit(2000))   /* wave215: Slack check-ins count too (Steel, 07/10/26) */
    ]);
    S.sh = res[3] || [];
    S.ck = res[0]; var mv = res[1][0] && res[1][0].value; if (mv && typeof mv === 'object') S.mins = { member: Number(mv.member) || 3, manager: Number(mv.manager) || 5 };
    S.feed = res[2]; S.ckAt = Date.now();
  }
  /* what Overview counts: one round of small reads */
  async function loadOverview(force) {
    if (S.dLoading) return S.dLoading;
    if (!force && S.data && Date.now() - S.data.at < 60000) return S.data;
    S.dLoading = (async function () {
      var res = await Promise.all([
        loadCands(force),
        q(db.from('wa_timeoff').select('id,name,email,category,start_date,end_date,days,status,created_at').eq('status', 'requested').order('created_at', { ascending: true }).limit(50)),   /* wave212: the requests are 'requested' (was 'pending', so the count was always 0) */
        q(db.from('wa_policies').select('key,title,required,active').eq('active', true)),
        q(db.from('wa_policy_signoffs').select('email,policy_key')),
        q(db.from('onboardings').select('id,member_id,person_name,status,started_at,completed_at').limit(200)),
        q(db.from('training_progress').select('id,person_name,email,status,due_date,assignment_id').neq('status', 'done').limit(3000)),
        loadHours(force)
      ]);
      var d = { at: Date.now(), timeoff: res[1], pol: res[2], sig: res[3], onb: res[4], tp: res[5] };
      S.data = d; S.dLoading = null; return d;
    })();
    return S.dLoading;
  }
  /* sums per person from The Loop: this week (from Monday), last week, the average of the last N full weeks; an open session counts at most eight hours */
  function monday(d) { var x = new Date(d); x.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); var wd = (x.getDay() + 6) % 7; x.setDate(x.getDate() - wd); return x; }
  function sessionMin(c) {
    if (c.minutes != null && c.ended_at) return Number(c.minutes) || 0;
    var start = new Date(c.started_at).getTime(), end = c.ended_at ? new Date(c.ended_at).getTime() : Date.now();
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(480, Math.round((end - start) / 60000)));
  }
  /* full weeks to average over: at most S.hoursWeeks, and only weeks The Loop has run in full (it began 25/09/26) */
  function avgWeeks() {
    var m0 = monday(Date.now()).getTime(), wk = 7 * 86400000, first = null;
    S.ck.forEach(function (c) { if (!first || c.started_at < first) first = c.started_at; });
    if (!first) return 0;
    var fm = monday(first).getTime(); if (new Date(first).getTime() > fm) fm += wk;   /* a week begun part way is not a full week */
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(S.hoursWeeks, Math.floor((m0 - fm) / wk)));
  }
  function hoursBy() {
    var m0 = monday(Date.now()).getTime(), wk = 7 * 86400000, out = {}, aw = avgWeeks(); S.avgW = aw;
    S.ck.forEach(function (c) {
      var em = lower(c.email); if (!em) return;
      var o = out[em] || (out[em] = { email: em, name: c.name, dept: c.department, week: 0, last: 0, past: 0, sessions: 0, open: false, lastAt: null });
      var t = new Date(c.started_at).getTime(), mn = sessionMin(c);
      if (t >= m0) o.week += mn; else if (t >= m0 - wk) o.last += mn;
      if (t < m0 && t >= m0 - wk * aw) o.past += mn;
      o.sessions++; if (!c.ended_at) o.open = true; if (!o.lastAt || c.started_at > o.lastAt) o.lastAt = c.started_at;
    });
    /* wave215 (Steel, 07/10/26: "Slack activity can count toward hours"): the weekly hours hours-pull reads from people's own
       "in" and "out" messages in the Slack check-in channels (slack_hours; the Hub's own Loop posts are never counted there),
       added to the person's Loop time for the same week. o.slackWeek / o.slackPast say how much of it came from Slack. */
    (S.sh || []).forEach(function (r) {
      var mm = members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(r.member_name) || lower(String(m.name).split(' ')[0]) === lower(String(r.member_name).split(' ')[0]); })[0];
      if (!mm || !mm.email) return;
      var em = lower(mm.email), mins = Math.round(Number(r.hours || 0) * 60); if (!mins) return;
      var o = out[em] || (out[em] = { email: em, name: mm.name, dept: mm.department, week: 0, last: 0, past: 0, sessions: 0, open: false, lastAt: null });
      var t = new Date(String(r.week_start) + 'T12:00:00').getTime();
      if (t >= m0) { o.week += mins; o.slackWeek = (o.slackWeek || 0) + mins; } else if (t >= m0 - wk) { o.last += mins; o.slackLast = (o.slackLast || 0) + mins; }
      if (t < m0 && t >= m0 - wk * aw) { o.past += mins; o.slackPast = (o.slackPast || 0) + mins; }
      o.sessions += Number(r.sessions || 0);
    });
    return out;
  }
  /* managers and up get the higher minimum (the same rule as wave54: lead, leader, director, manager, chair or co- in the role; the operational executives 8h); the board is exempt */
  function isManager(m) { return /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i.test(String(m.role || '')); }
  function minFor(m) { if (!m || m.department === 'board') return 0; if (/steel\.macdonald|suhaib\.abdillahi/.test(lower(m.email))) return 8; return isManager(m) ? S.mins.manager : S.mins.member; }

  /* ---------- the hosts this wave draws into ---------- */
  function host(id, cls) {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = id; h.className = 'w197card ' + (cls || ''); var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs'); if (tabs) tabs.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', h); else body.insertBefore(h, body.firstChild); }
    return h;
  }
  function go(k) { location.hash = '#hrcentre/' + k; }

  /* ---------- Overview: HR's to-do list ---------- */
  function overviewHtml() {
    var d = S.data; if (!d) return '<p class="w197note">Loading what needs HR\u2026</p>';
    var ppl = members(), cands = S.cands, hb = hoursBy();
    var newC = cands.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'new'; });
    var nobody = cands.filter(function (c) { return ['new', 'reviewing', 'interview', 'offer'].indexOf(c.status) >= 0 && !c.owner; });
    var inFlight = cands.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'interview' || c.status === 'offer'; });
    var onb = d.onb.filter(function (o) { return !o.completed_at && o.status !== 'done' && o.status !== 'completed' && o.status !== 'removed'; });
    var t0 = today(), overdue = d.tp.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && t.due_date < t0; });
    var overduePeople = {}; overdue.forEach(function (t) { overduePeople[lower(t.email || t.person_name)] = 1; });
    var req = d.pol.filter(function (p) { return p.required; }), signed = {};
    d.sig.forEach(function (s) { signed[lower(s.email) + '|' + s.policy_key] = 1; });
    var unsigned = ppl.filter(function (m) { return m.email && req.some(function (p) { return !signed[lower(m.email) + '|' + p.key]; }); });
    var low = !S.avgW ? [] : ppl.filter(function (m) { var mn = minFor(m); if (!mn) return false; var h = hb[lower(m.email)]; var avg = h ? h.past / S.avgW / 60 : 0; return avg < mn; });
    var inLoop = ppl.filter(function (m) { var h = hb[lower(m.email)]; return h && h.week > 0; });
    var checkIn = Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w144hr .w144sub'), function (s) { return s; }).filter(function (s) { return /worth a check/i.test(s.textContent); })[0];
    var checkList = checkIn && checkIn.nextElementSibling ? Array.prototype.map.call(checkIn.nextElementSibling.querySelectorAll('li'), function (li) { var a = li.querySelector('a') || li.firstChild; return a ? String(a.textContent || '').trim() : ''; }).filter(Boolean) : [];
    function names(arr, f, n) { var a = arr.slice(0, n || 4).map(f); return a.join(', ') + (arr.length > (n || 4) ? ' and ' + (arr.length - (n || 4)) + ' more' : ''); }
    var oldest = newC.length ? newC.reduce(function (a, c) { return c.created_at < a ? c.created_at : a; }, newC[0].created_at) : null;
    var items = [
      { n: newC.length, k: 'applications', t: newC.length === 1 ? 'new application to read' : 'new applications to read', s: newC.length ? 'The oldest came ' + ago(oldest) + '. Read each one and move it to Reviewing, or close it.' : '' },
      { n: nobody.length, k: 'applications', t: nobody.length === 1 ? 'application nobody is handling' : 'applications nobody is handling', s: nobody.length ? names(nobody, function (c) { return c.name; }) : '' },
      { n: inFlight.length, k: 'applications', t: inFlight.length === 1 ? 'candidate at interview or offer' : 'candidates at interview or offer', s: inFlight.length ? names(inFlight, function (c) { return c.name + ' (' + c.status + ')'; }) : '' },
      { n: d.timeoff.length, k: 'timeoff', t: d.timeoff.length === 1 ? 'time-off request to decide' : 'time-off requests to decide', s: d.timeoff.length ? names(d.timeoff, function (r) { return (r.name || r.email) + ', ' + r.start_date; }) : '' },
      { n: onb.length, k: 'onboarding', t: onb.length === 1 ? 'person onboarding' : 'people onboarding', s: onb.length ? names(onb, function (o) { return o.person_name; }) : '' },
      { n: Object.keys(overduePeople).length, k: 'training', t: Object.keys(overduePeople).length === 1 ? 'person with a training past its due date' : 'people with a training past its due date', s: overdue.length ? overdue.length + ' trainings in all' : '' },
      { n: unsigned.length, k: 'policies', t: unsigned.length === 1 ? 'person who has not signed every required policy' : 'people who have not signed every required policy', s: unsigned.length ? names(unsigned, function (m) { return m.name; }, 5) : '' },
      { n: low.length, k: 'hours', t: low.length === 1 ? 'person below their weekly minimum' : 'people below their weekly minimum', s: low.length ? 'Average over the last ' + S.avgW + (S.avgW === 1 ? ' full week' : ' full weeks') + ' in The Loop. ' + names(low, function (m) { return m.name; }, 5) : '' },
      { n: checkList.length, k: 'hours', t: (checkList.length === 1 ? 'person' : 'people') + ' worth a check-in (missed their team\u2019s last meetings)', s: checkList.length ? checkList.join(', ') + '. A kind word first: people step back for many reasons.' : '' }
    ];
    var todo = items.filter(function (i) { return i.n > 0; });
    var tiles = [
      [ppl.length, 'people on the team', 'team'], [inLoop.length, 'in The Loop this week', 'hours'], [newC.length, 'new applications', 'applications', newC.length > 0],
      [onb.length, 'onboarding now', 'onboarding'], [Object.keys(overduePeople).length, 'with a training overdue', 'training', overdue.length > 0], [d.timeoff.length, 'time off to decide', 'timeoff', d.timeoff.length > 0]
    ];
    /* anniversaries in the next 30 days and the newest people */
    var now = new Date(), soon = [];
    ppl.forEach(function (m) {
      if (!m.start_date) return; var sd = new Date(m.start_date + 'T12:00:00'), y = now.getFullYear() - sd.getFullYear();
      var next = new Date(sd); next.setFullYear(now.getFullYear()); if (next < now) { next.setFullYear(now.getFullYear() + 1); y++; }
      var days = Math.round((next - now) / 86400000); if (y >= 1 && days <= 30) soon.push({ m: m, y: y, days: days, next: next });
    });
    soon.sort(function (a, b) { return a.days - b.days; });
    var newest = ppl.filter(function (m) { return m.start_date; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.start_date).localeCompare(String(a.start_date)); }).slice(0, 4);
    function dm(x) { try { return x.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
    return '<div class="w197tiles">' + tiles.map(function (t) { return '<button type="button" class="w197tile' + (t[3] ? ' hot' : '') + '" data-w197go="' + t[2] + '"><b>' + t[0] + '</b><span>' + E(t[1]) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w197grid2"><div class="w197card" style="margin:0"><h2>Needs HR</h2>' +
      (todo.length ? '<ul class="w197todo">' + todo.map(function (i) { return '<li><span class="n">' + i.n + '</span><div class="t"><b>' + E(i.n + ' ' + i.t) + '</b>' + (i.s ? '<small>' + E(i.s) + '</small>' : '') + '</div>' + btn('Open', 'data-w197go="' + i.k + '"', 'sm') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197done">Nothing waiting on HR. Nice.</p>') +
      '</div><div class="w197card" style="margin:0"><h2>Coming up</h2>' +
      (soon.length ? '<ul class="w197todo">' + soon.slice(0, 6).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + av(x.m, 36) + '<div class="t"><b>' + E(x.m.name) + '</b><small>' + x.y + (x.y === 1 ? ' year' : ' years') + ' with us on ' + dm(x.next) + (x.days === 0 ? ' (today)' : '') + '</small></div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note">No work anniversaries in the next 30 days.</p>') +
      (newest.length ? '<h2 style="margin-top:16px">Newest on the team</h2>' : '') + '<ul class="w197todo">' + newest.map(function (m) { return '<li>' + av(m, 36) + '<div class="t"><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b><small>' + E(teamLabel(m.department)) + ' \u00b7 started ' + E(ago(m.start_date + 'T12:00:00')) + '</small></div></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div></div>';
  }
  function paintOverview() { var h = host('w197ov'); if (!h) return; h.className = 'w197ovwrap'; h.innerHTML = overviewHtml(); }

  /* ---------- Applications: the stage board ---------- */
  function stageLabel(s) { var x = STAGES.concat(CLOSED).filter(function (p) { return p[0] === s; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : s; }
  function daysIn(c) { var t = c.stage_at || c.created_at; return t ? Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(t).getTime()) / 86400000) : 0; }
  function roles() { if (window.w199) return w199.filterOptions(S.cands); var r = {}; S.cands.forEach(function (c) { if (c.role) r[c.role] = (r[c.role] || 0) + 1; }); return Object.keys(r).sort().map(function (k) { return { v: k, l: k }; }); }
  function filtered() {
    var qq = lower(S.q), ow = S.filterOwner, ro = S.filterRole || '';
    return S.cands.filter(function (c) {
      if (qq && (lower(c.name) + ' ' + lower(c.role) + ' ' + lower(window.w199 ? w199.roleOf(c) : '') + ' ' + lower(c.email)).indexOf(qq) < 0) return false;
      if (ro && !(window.w199 ? w199.match(c, ro) : c.role === ro)) return false;
      if (window.w199 && w199.notCalledOnly() && !w199.notCalled(c)) return false;   /* wave199: Reviewing, nobody has called */
      if (ow === '__me') return lower(c.owner) === lower(me().name) || lower(c.owner) === lower((me().name || '').split(' ')[0]);
      if (ow === '__none') return !c.owner;
      if (ow) return c.owner === ow;
      return true;
    });
  }
  function candCard(c, small) {
    var still = /still interested/i.test(String(c.summary || '') + ' ' + String(c.notes || ''));
    var d = daysIn(c);
    return '<button type="button" class="w197cand" draggable="' + (small ? 'false' : 'true') + '" data-w197c="' + c.id + '"><b>' + E(c.name || 'Someone') + '</b>' +
      (small ? '' : '<span class="r">' + E(window.w199 ? w199.roleOf(c) : (c.role || 'No role given')) + ' \u00b7 applied ' + E(ago(c.created_at)) + '</span><span class="m">' +
        (c.owner ? '<span class="who">' + E(String(c.owner).split(' ')[0]) + '</span>' : '<span>Nobody yet</span>') +
        (still ? '<span class="ok">Still interested</span>' : '') + (window.w199 ? w199.cardBits(c) : '') + (c.cv_path ? '<span>CV</span>' : '') +
        (d >= 7 ? '<span class="age">' + d + ' days here</span>' : '') + '</span>') + '</button>';
  }
  function boardHtml() {
    var rows = filtered(), people = members().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
    var owners = {}; S.cands.forEach(function (c) { if (c.owner) owners[c.owner] = 1; });
    var bar = '<div class="w197bar"><input type="search" id="w197q" placeholder="Search a name or a role" value="' + E(S.q) + '">' +
      '<select id="w197role" aria-label="Position"><option value="">Every position</option>' + roles().map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + E(r.v) + '"' + (S.filterRole === r.v ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(r.l) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select id="w197own" aria-label="Handled by"><option value="">Anyone handling</option><option value="__me"' + (S.filterOwner === '__me' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Mine</option><option value="__none"' + (S.filterOwner === '__none' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Nobody yet</option>' +
      Object.keys(owners).sort().map(function (o) { return '<option' + (S.filterOwner === o ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(o) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      (window.w199 ? w199.barBits() : '') + btn('Refresh', 'data-w197a="reload"', 'sm') + '</div>';
    var cols = STAGES.map(function (s) {
      var list = rows.filter(function (c) { return c.status === s[0] || (s[0] === 'new' && !c.status); });
      if (s[0] === 'hired') list = list.filter(function (c) { return daysIn(c) <= 90; });
      list.sort(function (a, b) { if (window.w199 && w199.sortGrade()) { var d = w199.gradeAvg(b) - w199.gradeAvg(a); if (d) return d; } if (s[0] === 'interview') return String(a.interview_at || '9').localeCompare(String(b.interview_at || '9')); return String(a.created_at).localeCompare(String(b.created_at)); });   /* wave199: interviews in date order, the ones with no time last */
      return '<div class="w197col" data-w197col="' + s[0] + '"><h4>' + E(s[1]) + ' <small>' + list.length + '</small></h4>' + (list.length ? list.map(function (c) { return candCard(c); }).join('') : '<p class="w197note" style="margin:4px">' + (s[0] === 'new' ? 'No new applications.' : 'Drop a card here.') + '</p>') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var closed = rows.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'declined' || c.status === 'withdrawn' || c.status === 'volunteering'; });
    return bar + '<div class="w197board" id="w197board">' + cols + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w197closed">' + (closed.length ? '<button type="button" class="w197b sm" data-w197a="closed">' + (S.showClosed ? 'Hide' : 'Show') + ' the ' + closed.length + ' closed (declined, withdrawn or volunteering)</button>' + (S.showClosed ? '<div>' + closed.map(function (c) { return candCard(c, true); }).join('') + '</div>' : '') : '') +
      '<p class="w197note">Applications emailed to hub@ (label Hub/Recruitment) and from the job boards land in New. Hired shows the last 90 days.</p></div>';
  }
  function paintBoard() { var h = host('w197apps'); if (!h) return; var a = document.activeElement, qf = a && a.id === 'w197q'; h.innerHTML = boardHtml(); if (qf) { var x = document.getElementById('w197q'); if (x) { x.focus(); try { x.setSelectionRange(x.value.length, x.value.length); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } } }

  /* the candidate sheet */
  var NEXT = {
    'new': 'Read the application, choose who handles it, then move it to Reviewing (or close it as Declined).',
    reviewing: 'Invite them to an interview. The interview question templates are in Playbook.',
    interview: 'After the interview, write your impressions here. If it is a yes, move it to Offer and send the offer letter (Playbook).',
    offer: 'When they accept, move it to Hired, then set up their onboarding.',
    hired: 'Set up their onboarding: Onboarding, Assign an onboarding. The Welcome kit there has the email and the account setup.',
    declined: 'Closed. Move it back to a stage if this changes.', withdrawn: 'Closed. Move it back to a stage if this changes.', volunteering: 'With the Volunteer Manager: Operations welcomes them from Applicants received.'
  };
  function sheetEl() { var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w197sheet'; s.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); s.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); s.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Application'); document.body.appendChild(s); } return s; }
  function openSheet(id) {
    var c = S.cands.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!c) return;
    S.open = c.id;
    var people = members().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
    var positions = []; try { positions = (window.WA46 && WA46.reqs || []).filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === 'hiring-request' && !r.done; }); } catch (e) { positions = []; }
    var pos = positions.map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + r.id + '"' + (String(c.request_id) === String(r.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(r.title || ('Request #' + r.id)) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (c.request_id && !positions.some(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(c.request_id); })) pos += '<option value="' + E(c.request_id) + '" selected>Position #' + E(c.request_id) + '</option>';
    var links = (c.links || []).map(function (l) { return '<a href="' + E(l) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(String(l).replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?/, '').slice(0, 48)) + '</a>'; }).join('<br>');
    var s = sheetEl();
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w197a="shut"></div><div class="pn">' + btn('Close', 'data-w197a="shut"', 'sm x') +
      '<h3>' + E(c.name || 'Someone') + '</h3><p class="role">' + E(window.w199 ? w199.roleOf(c) : (c.role || 'No role given')) + ' \u00b7 applied ' + E(ago(c.created_at)) + (c.source ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(c.source) : '') + '</p>' +
      '<label>Stage</label><div class="stg">' + STAGES.map(function (p) { return '<button type="button" data-w197st="' + p[0] + '" class="' + (c.status === p[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(p[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') +
      CLOSED.map(function (p) { return '<button type="button" data-w197st="' + p[0] + '" class="cl' + (c.status === p[0] ? ' on' : '') + '">' + E(p[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="next"><b>Next:</b> ' + E(NEXT[c.status || 'new'] || '') + (c.status === 'hired' ? ' ' + btn('Go to Onboarding', 'data-w197go="onboarding"', 'sm pri') : '') + '</div>' +
      '<label>Contact</label><div class="ct">' + (c.email ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(c.email) + '">' + E(c.email) + '</a>' : 'No email') + (c.phone ? '<br><a href="tel:' + E(String(c.phone).replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '">' + E(c.phone) + '</a>' : '') + (links ? '<br>' + links : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + (c.cv_path ? btn('Open the CV' + (c.cv_name ? ' (' + E(String(c.cv_name).slice(0, 28)) + ')' : ''), 'data-w197cv="' + E(c.cv_path) + '"', 'pri sm') : '<span class="w197pill mut">No CV attached</span>') + (c.mail_id ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="#hubmail">The original email</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<label for="w197own1">Handled by</label><select id="w197own1" data-w197f="owner"><option value="">Nobody yet</option>' + people.map(function (m) { return '<option' + (c.owner === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      (window.w199 ? w199.sheetBits(c) : '<label for="w197pos1">Position</label><select id="w197pos1" data-w197f="request_id"><option value="">Not linked to a position</option>' + pos + '</select>') +
      (c.summary ? '<label>What they wrote</label><div class="sum">' + E(c.summary) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<label for="w197notes1">Notes</label><textarea id="w197notes1" data-w197f="notes" placeholder="Interview impressions, the next step">' + E(c.notes || '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn('Save the notes', 'data-w197a="notes"', 'pri sm') + '</div></div>';
    s.classList.add('open');
    var f = s.querySelector('.stg button.on') || s.querySelector('.stg button'); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function shutSheet() { var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (!s) return; var t = s.querySelector('[data-w197f="notes"]'); if (t && S.open) { var c = S.cands.filter(function (x) { return x.id === S.open; })[0]; if (c && (c.notes || '') !== t.value) updateCand(c.id, { notes: t.value.slice(0, 2000) }, 'Notes saved'); } s.classList.remove('open'); S.open = null; }
  async function updateCand(id, patch, msg) {
    try {
      var who = me().name || me().email;
      patch.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); patch.updated_by = who;
      if (patch.status) patch.stage_at = patch.updated_at;
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update(patch).eq('id', id);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var c = S.cands.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (c) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; });
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(id), field: Object.keys(patch).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'updated_at' && k !== 'updated_by' && k !== 'stage_at'; })[0] || 'update', old_value: null, new_value: String(patch.status || patch.owner || patch.request_id || (patch.notes ? 'notes' : '')), changed_by: who }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      if (msg) toastS(msg);
      return true;
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (e.message || e)); return false; }
  }
  async function moveTo(id, st) {
    var c = S.cands.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!c || c.status === st) return;
    if (st === 'volunteering') { if (window.w218) w218.open(c); return; }   /* wave218: roles and servings first, in one step */
    var extra = {}; if (window.w211g) { extra = await w211g.gate(c, st); if (!extra) return; }   /* wave211: what the stage needs before the card moves */
    var patch = { status: st }; Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (k) { patch[k] = extra[k]; });
    var ok = await updateCand(c.id, patch, (c.name || 'Moved') + ': ' + stageLabel(st));
    if (ok) {
      paintBoard(); paintRail(); if (S.open === c.id) openSheet(c.id);
      if (st === 'interview' && !c.interview_at && window.w199) { openSheet(c.id); w199.askTime(); }   /* wave199: an interview needs its day and time */
      if (st === 'offer' && !c.offer_sent_at && window.w211) w211.askOffer(c.id);   /* wave211: the offer letter, ready to send */
    }
  }

  /* ---------- Hours: The Loop against the minimums ---------- */
  function hoursHtml() {
    var hb = hoursBy(), ppl = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department !== 'board' || minFor(m) > 0; });
    var rows = ppl.map(function (m) {
      var h = hb[lower(m.email)] || { week: 0, last: 0, past: 0, sessions: 0, open: false, lastAt: null };
      var mn = minFor(m), avg = S.avgW ? h.past / S.avgW / 60 : 0;
      return { m: m, h: h, min: mn, avg: avg, low: !!S.avgW && mn > 0 && avg < mn };
    });
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { if (a.low !== b.low) return a.low ? -1 : 1; if (a.low) return (a.avg / a.min) - (b.avg / b.min); return b.avg - a.avg; });
    var lowN = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.low; }).length, none = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.min > 0 && !r.h.sessions; }).length;
    var weekP = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.h.week > 0; }).length;
    var tot = rows.reduce(function (a, r) { return a + r.h.week; }, 0);
    var html = '<div class="w197tiles">' +
      '<div class="w197tile"><b>' + weekP + '</b><span>people in The Loop this week</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w197tile"><b>' + fmtH(tot) + '</b><span>given this week</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w197tile' + (lowN ? ' hot' : '') + '"><b>' + (S.avgW ? lowN : '\u2013') + '</b><span>' + (S.avgW ? 'below their minimum (' + S.avgW + '-week average)' : 'below their minimum: from the first full week of The Loop') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w197tile"><b>' + none + '</b><span>no session in The Loop yet</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w197card"><h2>Hours by person</h2>' +
      '<div class="w197mins"><b>Weekly minimums:</b> managers and up <input type="number" id="w197mmgr" min="1" max="40" value="' + E(S.mins.manager) + '">h, everyone else <input type="number" id="w197mmem" min="1" max="40" value="' + E(S.mins.member) + '">h' + (isAdmin() || canHr() ? btn('Save', 'data-w197a="mins"', 'sm pri') : '') + '<span class="w197note" style="margin:0">The board is exempt; the operational executives (Steel, Suhaib) 8h.</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w197wrap"><table class="w197tbl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>This week</th><th class="hp">Last week</th><th>Average a week</th><th>Minimum</th><th class="hp"></th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      rows.map(function (r) {
        var pct = r.min ? Math.min(100, Math.round(r.avg / r.min * 100)) : 100;
        return '<tr class="' + (r.low ? 'low' : '') + '"><td><b>' + E(r.m.name) + '</b><br><small style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + E(teamLabel(r.m.department)) + (r.h.open ? ' \u00b7 in now' : '') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td>' + fmtH(r.h.week) + (r.h.slackWeek ? '<br><small style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + fmtH(r.h.slackWeek) + ' from Slack</small>' : '') + '</td><td class="hp">' + fmtH(r.h.last) + (r.h.slackLast ? '<br><small style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + fmtH(r.h.slackLast) + ' from Slack</small>' : '') + '</td>' +
          '<td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px"><div class="bar" style="flex:1"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div><span>' + (Math.round(r.avg * 10) / 10) + 'h</span></div></td>' +
          '<td>' + (r.min ? r.min + 'h' : '<span class="w197pill mut">exempt</span>') + '</td><td class="hp">' + (r.min ? (!S.avgW ? '<span class="w197pill mut">too early</span>' : r.low ? '<span class="w197pill low">below</span>' : '<span class="w197pill">on track</span>') : '') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>' +
      '<p class="w197note">From The Loop and Slack: a Loop session counts from I\u2019m in to Close the loop (a session still open counts at most eight hours), and the hours people log with their own \u201cin\u201d and \u201cout\u201d messages in the Slack check-in channels are added (read every morning; the Hub\u2019s own Loop posts there are not counted twice). Average a week is the last ' + S.hoursWeeks + ' full weeks, counted from The Loop\u2019s first full week (it began on 25 September 2026). Hours stay with the person, HR and admins.</p></div>';
    var feed = S.feed.slice(0, 25);
    html += '<div class="w197card"><h2>What people shared in The Loop</h2><p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 8px">What moved, as people chose to share it when they closed the loop (last 30 days). Never times or hours.</p>' +
      (feed.length ? '<ul class="w197feed">' + feed.map(function (f) {
        var b = Array.isArray(f.bullets) ? f.bullets : [];
        return '<li><b>' + E(f.name || f.email) + '</b> <small>\u00b7 ' + E(teamLabel(f.department)) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(ago(f.day + 'T12:00:00')) + (f.closed ? ' \u00b7 closed ' + f.closed + (f.closed === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') : '') + '</small>' +
          (b.length ? '<ul>' + b.slice(0, 5).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(typeof x === 'string' ? x : (x && (x.text || x.t)) || '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') + '</li>';
      }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note">Nothing shared in the last 30 days yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function paintHours() { var h = host('w197hours'); if (!h) return; h.className = 'w197hourswrap'; h.innerHTML = S.ckAt ? hoursHtml() : '<p class="w197note">Loading The Loop\u2026</p>'; }
  async function saveMins() {
    var mg = Number((document.getElementById('w197mmgr') || {}).value), mm = Number((document.getElementById('w197mmem') || {}).value);
    if (!(mg > 0) || !(mm > 0)) { toastS('Both minimums need a number of hours'); return; }
    var old = S.mins.manager + '/' + S.mins.member;
    var r = await db.from('hub_settings').upsert({ key: 'hours_minimums', value: { manager: mg, member: mm }, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not save the minimums'); return; }
    S.mins = { manager: mg, member: mm };
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'settings', entity_id: 'hours_minimums', field: 'minimums', old_value: old, new_value: mg + '/' + mm, changed_by: me().name || me().email }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    toastS('Minimums saved: ' + mg + 'h managers and up, ' + mm + 'h everyone else'); paintHours();
  }

  /* ---------- the router: tag every section with its page, show the current page's ---------- */
  function tag(el, keys, blk) { if (!el) return; el.setAttribute('data-w197s', keys); if (blk) el.classList.add('w197blk'); }
  function hide(el) { if (el) el.classList.add('w197x'); }
  function secByH3(re) { return Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w46body > .w46sec'), function (s) { var h = s.querySelector(':scope > h3'); return h && re.test(h.textContent.trim()); })[0] || null; }
  function tagAll() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel) return;
    hide(document.getElementById('w116phero'));
    hide(panel.querySelector(':scope > .card > h2'));
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll(':scope > .card > p.w85p, :scope > .card > p.read'), hide);
    hide(document.getElementById('w93eng'));     /* Hub engagement: the Admin centre's Adoption */
    hide(document.getElementById('w103scope'));  /* Slack scope of work: The Loop's notes on Hours */
    hide(document.getElementById('w52hours'));   /* the Slack hours and the old Hub hours table: Hours, from The Loop */
    hide(document.getElementById('w169cardO'));  /* the same list as Training's Trainings assigned to the organization */
    tag(document.getElementById('w197ov'), 'overview', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w197hours'), 'hours', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w197apps'), 'applications', true);
    var pp = document.getElementById('w116ppeople');
    if (pp) {
      tag(pp, 'team recognition', true);
      tag(document.getElementById('w116pshouts'), 'recognition');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(pp.children, function (c) { if (c.id !== 'w116pshouts') tag(c, 'team'); });
    }
    tag(document.getElementById('w144hr'), 'hours', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w145quiz'), 'onboarding', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w105to'), 'timeoff', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w106polsec'), 'policies', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w106discsec'), 'discipline', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w105pol'), 'policies', true); tag(document.getElementById('w105disc'), 'discipline', true);   /* wave105 draws them as cards of their own before wave106 moves them */
    tag(document.getElementById('w181'), 'analytics', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w116pcands'), 'applications-list', true);   /* the old list: the board replaces it */
    var onb = secByH3(/^Onboarding$/), master = secByH3(/^Master onboarding/), across = secByH3(/^Trainings across the team/), rec = secByH3(/^Recruitment$/);
    tag(onb, 'onboarding', true); tag(master, 'plan', true); tag(across, 'training', true);
    tag(document.getElementById('w169cardT'), 'training', true);
    if (onb) { var h = onb.querySelector(':scope > h3'); if (h) h.textContent = 'Who is onboarding'; }
    if (master) { var h2 = master.querySelector(':scope > h3'); if (h2) hide(h2); }
    if (rec) {
      tag(rec, 'playbook', true);   /* wave199 draws Positions (#w199pos); the older hiring-request list is shown on no page */
      var mode = 'positions';
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(rec.children, function (c, i) {
        if (i === 0 && c.tagName === 'H3') { hide(c); return; }
        if (c.classList.contains('w49pb')) mode = 'playbook';
        tag(c, mode === 'positions' ? 'oldpositions' : mode);
        if (c.tagName === 'H3') c.classList.add('w197sub');
      });
    }
    /* anything else inside the page this wave does not know: shown on no page (a newer wave adds its own tag) */
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (body) Array.prototype.forEach.call(body.children, function (c) { if (!c.hasAttribute('data-w197s') && !c.classList.contains('w47tabs') && !c.classList.contains('w197x')) tag(c, 'none'); });
    if (body && body.parentNode && body.parentNode.parentNode === panel) body.parentNode.classList.add('w197main');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(panel.children, function (c) { if (c.classList.contains('w197main') || c.classList.contains('w118mast') || c.hasAttribute('data-w197s') || c.classList.contains('w197x')) return; tag(c, 'none'); });
  }
  function apply() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel) return;
    var k = S.page;
    if (k === 'analytics' && !seesAnalytics()) k = 'overview';
    panel.setAttribute('data-w197', k);
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(panel.querySelectorAll('[data-w197s]'), function (el) {
      var on = (' ' + el.getAttribute('data-w197s') + ' ').indexOf(' ' + k + ' ') >= 0;
      if (on !== el.classList.contains('w197on')) el.classList.toggle('w197on', on);
    });
    mastHr();
  }
  /* the HR centre's masthead: the page's name, its line, its main button */
  var MAIN = {
    /* only where the page's own button sits low or inside a form; pages whose card already leads with its button keep that one */
    team: ['Add someone', 'addperson'], timeoff: ['Log time off', 'logto'], recognition: ['Post a shout-out', 'shout'],
    policies: ['Add a policy', 'addpol'], discipline: ['Record an entry', 'recdisc']
  };
  function mastHr() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel) return;
    var m = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast'); if (!m) return;
    var p = pageDef(S.page) || PAGES[0];
    var same = function (el, en) { return window.w198 && w198.same ? w198.same(el.textContent, en) : el.textContent === en; };   /* wave198: its French counts as the same words */
    var h1 = m.querySelector('h1'); if (h1 && !same(h1, p.l)) h1.textContent = p.l;
    var d = m.querySelector(':scope > p'); if (d && !same(d, p.d)) d.textContent = p.d;
    var acts = m.querySelector('.w197acts'); if (!acts) { acts = document.createElement('div'); acts.className = 'w197acts'; m.appendChild(acts); }
    var help = m.querySelector('.w43help'); if (help && help.parentNode !== acts) acts.appendChild(help);
    if (window.w212) { try { w212.bell(acts); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }   /* wave212: the bell */
    var want = MAIN[S.page] && (S.page !== 'team' || canHr()) ? MAIN[S.page] : null;
    var cur = acts.querySelector('[data-w197main]');
    if (!want && cur) cur.remove();
    if (want && (!cur || cur.getAttribute('data-w197main') !== want[1])) { if (cur) cur.remove(); acts.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button type="button" class="w197pact" data-w197main="' + want[1] + '">' + E(want[0]) + '</button>'); }
  }
  /* the main buttons press the older pages' own controls (one flow each, no copy) */
  function mainAction(k) {
    function click(sel) { var b = document.querySelector(sel); if (b) { try { b.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } b.click(); return true; } return false; }
    function focus(sel) { var b = document.querySelector(sel); if (b) { try { b.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { b.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return true; } return false; }
    if (k === 'reload') { loadCands(true).then(function () { paintBoard(); paintRail(); toastS('Applications up to date'); }); return; }
    if (k === 'addperson') { location.hash = '#adminpanel/people'; return; }
    if (k === 'logto') { if (window.w105LogModal) { w105LogModal(); return; } focus('#w105to select, #w105to input'); return; }   /* wave212: a proper form */
    if (k === 'shout') { focus('#w116pshouts textarea, #w116pshouts input'); return; }
    if (k === 'assignonb') { focus('#w46body .w46sec[data-w197s~="onboarding"] select'); return; }
    if (k === 'preview') { click('#w46body .w169bar button'); return; }
    if (k === 'assigntr') { click('#w169cardT button, #w46body [data-w197s~="training"] button'); return; }
    if (k === 'addpol') { focus('#w106polsec input'); return; }
    if (k === 'recdisc') { focus('#w106discsec select'); return; }
  }

  /* ---------- the rail: replace the HR entry's items ---------- */
  function railCount(k) {
    if (k === 'applications') { var n = S.cands.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'new'; }).length; return n ? String(n) : null; }
    if (k === 'team') { var t = members().length; return t ? String(t) : null; }
    if (k === 'timeoff' && S.data) { return S.data.timeoff.length ? String(S.data.timeoff.length) : null; }
    return null;
  }
  function hrGroups() {
    var pn = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    var notice = !!pn && pn.classList.contains('active') && !document.getElementById('w46body') && /for the HR team|for admins and leaders/i.test(pn.textContent || '');
    if (notice || (window.wa46IsHr && !window.wa46IsHr())) return [{ label: 'HR centre', items: [{ h: '#hrcentre', l: 'HR centre', ic: 'clip', page: 'hrcentre' }] }];
    var groups = [], by = {};
    PAGES.forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.only === 'analytics' && !seesAnalytics()) return;
      var g = p.g || ''; if (!by[g]) { by[g] = { label: g || 'HR centre', solo: !g, items: [] }; groups.push(by[g]); }
      by[g].items.push({ h: '#hrcentre/' + p.k, l: p.l, ic: p.ic, page: 'hrcentre', n: railCount(p.k) });
    });
    return groups;
  }
  function installRail() {
    try { if (window.WA_APP && WA_APP.apps && WA_APP.apps.hr && !WA_APP.apps.hr.__w197) { WA_APP.apps.hr.groups = hrGroups; WA_APP.apps.hr.__w197 = true; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function paintRail() { try { if (window.w153 && w153.paint) w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  /* ---------- every app: the page's name as the title, the app bar's buttons in the header ---------- */
  function appTitles() {
    if (!document.body.classList.contains('w153app')) return;
    var on = document.querySelector('#w153rail .w153it.on span'); var label = on ? on.textContent.trim() : '';
    var panel = document.querySelector('.panel.active') || Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('.w118mast'), function (x) { return x.offsetParent; }).map(function (x) { return x.parentNode; })[0];
    if (!panel) return;
    var m = panel.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    var app0 = window.WA_APP && WA_APP.current && WA_APP.current(), appLabel = app0 && WA_APP.apps && WA_APP.apps[app0] ? WA_APP.apps[app0].label : '';
    if (m && label && panel.id !== 'hrcentre') {
      var h1 = m.querySelector('h1');
      /* only where the title repeats the app's name (CRM, Finance, Fundraising...): a page that names itself keeps its title */
      if (h1 && (lower(h1.textContent) === lower(appLabel) || h1.textContent === h1.getAttribute('data-w197set')) && h1.textContent.trim() !== label) { h1.textContent = label; h1.setAttribute('data-w197set', label); }
      if (h1 && !h1.__w197mo) { h1.__w197mo = 1; new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(h1.__w197t); h1.__w197t = setTimeout(appTitles, 30); }).observe(h1, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true }); }
      /* the CRM's line describes its contacts: on its other tabs the tab says what it is */
      var d = m.querySelector(':scope > p');
      if (d && app0 === 'crm' && panel.id === 'crm') d.style.display = /^(Contacts)$/.test(label) ? '' : 'none';
    }
    if (m) {
      var acts = m.querySelector('.w197acts');
      var src = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w153bar .w153btn[data-w153act]'));
      if (!acts && src.length) { acts = document.createElement('div'); acts.className = 'w197acts'; m.appendChild(acts); var help = m.querySelector('.w43help'); if (help) acts.appendChild(help); }
      if (acts) {
        var want = src.map(function (b) { return b.getAttribute('data-w153act'); }).join('|');
        if (acts.getAttribute('data-w197a') !== want) {
          Array.prototype.forEach.call(acts.querySelectorAll('[data-w197proxy]'), function (x) { x.remove(); });
          src.forEach(function (b) { acts.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button type="button" class="w197pact" data-w197proxy="' + E(b.getAttribute('data-w153act')) + '">' + b.innerHTML + '</button>'); });
          acts.setAttribute('data-w197a', want);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /* ---------- showing a page ---------- */
  function oldTabOn() { var t = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab.on'); return t ? t.getAttribute('data-w47v') : ''; }
  function show(k, quiet) {
    if (!document.getElementById('w46body')) return;
    S.page = k || subNow() || 'overview';
    var p = pageDef(S.page) || PAGES[0];
    if (p.k === 'overview') host('w197ov');
    if (p.k === 'hours') host('w197hours');
    if (p.k === 'applications') host('w197apps');
    tagAll(); apply();
    var want = p.tab && document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab[data-w47v="' + p.tab + '"]');
    if (want && oldTabOn() !== p.tab) {
      S.self = true; try { want.click(); } finally { S.self = false; }
      /* wave47's tab click writes its own key into the address (replaceState): put this page's back */
      if (String(location.hash || '') !== '#hrcentre/' + S.page) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + S.page); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      setTimeout(function () { tagAll(); apply(); paintRail(); }, 60);
    }
    if (quiet) return;
    if (p.k === 'overview') { paintOverview(); loadOverview().then(function () { if (S.page === 'overview') paintOverview(); paintRail(); }); }
    if (p.k === 'applications') { if (S.candsAt) paintBoard(); else { var h = host('w197apps'); if (h) h.innerHTML = '<p class="w197note">Loading the applications\u2026</p>'; } loadCands().then(function () { if (S.page === 'applications') paintBoard(); paintRail(); }); }
    if (p.k === 'hours') { paintHours(); loadHours().then(function () { if (S.page === 'hours') paintHours(); }); }
    if (p.k === 'positions' && window.w199) w199.show();
    if (!S.data) loadOverview().then(paintRail);
  }
  var pend = null;
  /* an older wave redrew the page body: put this page's own section back (from what is already loaded) */
  function ensureMine() {
    if (!document.getElementById('w46body')) return;
    if (S.page === 'overview' && !document.getElementById('w197ov')) paintOverview();
    if (S.page === 'hours' && !document.getElementById('w197hours')) paintHours();
    if (S.page === 'positions' && window.w199 && !document.getElementById('w199pos')) w199.show();
    if (S.page === 'applications' && !document.getElementById('w197apps')) { if (S.candsAt) paintBoard(); else show('applications'); }
  }
  function schedule() { clearTimeout(pend); pend = setTimeout(function () { try { if (onHr()) { ensureMine(); tagAll(); apply(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 30); }
  function watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel || panel.dataset.w197mo) return; panel.dataset.w197mo = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { var t = muts[i].target; if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#w197ov') || t.closest('#w197hours') || t.closest('#w197apps') || t.closest('#w199pos') || t.closest('.w197acts') || (t.closest('.w118mast') && muts[i].type === 'characterData'))) continue; schedule(); return; }
    }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  function fixAddress() {
    var h = String(location.hash || ''); if (h.indexOf('#hrcentre') !== 0) return;
    var s = (h.replace(/^#/, '').split('/')[1] || '').split('?')[0];
    if (s && OLD[s]) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + OLD[s]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    else if (s && !pageDef(s)) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/overview'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function onRoute() {
    installRail();
    if (!onHr()) return;
    fixAddress();
    if (S.want && String(location.hash || '') !== '#hrcentre/' + S.want) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + S.want); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
    watch(); show(S.want || subNow());
    setTimeout(paintRail, 80);
  }
  if (typeof wa46Render === 'function') {
    var __r46 = wa46Render;
    wa46Render = async function () { var out = await __r46.apply(this, arguments); try { installRail(); watch(); if (onHr()) { if (S.want && String(location.hash || '') !== '#hrcentre/' + S.want) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + S.want); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } show(S.want || subNow()); } paintRail(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { if (id === 'hrcentre') S.want = subNow(); var out = __sp.apply(this, arguments); try { installRail(); if (id === 'hrcentre') setTimeout(onRoute, 300); setTimeout(appTitles, 400); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre') === 0) { fixAddress(); S.want = subNow(); if (document.getElementById('w46body')) { show(subNow()); setTimeout(paintRail, 60); } } else shutSheet(); [150, 700].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(appTitles, ms); }); });

  /* every app's title: after the rail repaints */
  (function railWatch() {
    var r = document.getElementById('w153rail');

    new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(railWatch.t); railWatch.t = setTimeout(function () { try { appTitles(); if (onHr()) mastHr(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 40); }).observe(r, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  })();

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  /* an older tab pressed by anything else (a tour, wave169's links, a test): open its page */
  var FROMTAB = { people: 'team', onboarding: 'onboarding', training: 'training', recruitment: 'applications', hours: 'hours', policies: 'policies', discipline: 'discipline', analytics: 'analytics' };
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var ot = t.closest('#w46body .w47tab[data-w47v]');
    if (ot && !S.self) { var nk = FROMTAB[ot.getAttribute('data-w47v')]; if (nk && nk !== S.page) setTimeout(function () { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + nk); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } S.want = nk; show(nk); paintRail(); }, 0); return; }
    var px = t.closest('[data-w197proxy]'); if (px) { var o = document.querySelector('#w153bar .w153btn[data-w153act="' + px.getAttribute('data-w197proxy') + '"]'); if (o) o.click(); return; }
    var mn = t.closest('[data-w197main]'); if (mn) { mainAction(mn.getAttribute('data-w197main')); return; }
    var g = t.closest('[data-w197go]'); if (g) { if (t.closest('#w197sheet')) shutSheet(); go(g.getAttribute('data-w197go')); return; }
    var st = t.closest('#w197sheet [data-w197st]'); if (st && S.open) { moveTo(S.open, st.getAttribute('data-w197st')); return; }
    var cv = t.closest('[data-w197cv]');
    if (cv) { (async function () { try { var s = await db.storage.from('hub-mail').createSignedUrl(cv.getAttribute('data-w197cv'), 600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) window.open(s.data.signedUrl, '_blank', 'noopener'); else toastS('Could not open the CV'); } catch (x) { toastS('Could not open the CV'); } })(); return; }
    var a = t.closest('[data-w197a]');
    if (a) {
      var k = a.getAttribute('data-w197a');
      if (k === 'shut') shutSheet();
      else if (k === 'reload') mainAction('reload');
      else if (k === 'closed') { S.showClosed = !S.showClosed; paintBoard(); }
      else if (k === 'mins') saveMins();
      else if (k === 'notes') { var ta = document.querySelector('#w197sheet [data-w197f="notes"]'); var c = S.cands.filter(function (x) { return x.id === S.open; })[0]; if (ta && c) updateCand(c.id, { notes: ta.value.slice(0, 2000) }, 'Notes saved').then(function () { paintBoard(); }); }
      return;
    }
    var cd = t.closest('[data-w197c]'); if (cd) { openSheet(cd.getAttribute('data-w197c')); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.id === 'w197role') { S.filterRole = t.value; paintBoard(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w197own') { S.filterOwner = t.value; paintBoard(); return; }
    var f = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w197f');
    if (f && S.open && f !== 'notes') {
      var patch = {}; patch[f] = f === 'request_id' ? (t.value ? Number(t.value) : null) : (t.value || null);
      updateCand(S.open, patch, f === 'owner' ? (t.value ? 'Handled by ' + t.value : 'Nobody handling it yet') : (t.value ? 'Linked to the position' : 'Unlinked')).then(function () { paintBoard(); });
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w197q') { S.q = t.value; paintBoard(); } });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && S.open) shutSheet(); });
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) { var c = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w197board .w197cand'); if (!c) return; S.drag = c.getAttribute('data-w197c'); try { e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', S.drag); e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; } catch (x) { /* fine */ } });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w197board .w197col'); if (!col || !S.drag) return; e.preventDefault(); Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w197board .w197col.over'), function (x) { if (x !== col) x.classList.remove('over'); }); col.classList.add('over'); });
  document.addEventListener('dragleave', function (e) { var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w197board .w197col'); if (col && !col.contains(e.relatedTarget)) col.classList.remove('over'); });
  document.addEventListener('drop', function (e) { var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w197board .w197col'); if (!col || !S.drag) return; e.preventDefault(); var id = S.drag; S.drag = null; col.classList.remove('over'); moveTo(id, col.getAttribute('data-w197col')); });
  document.addEventListener('dragend', function () { S.drag = null; Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w197board .w197col.over'), function (x) { x.classList.remove('over'); }); });

  /* ---------- the tour ---------- */
  function addTour() {
