/* =====================================================================
   WA v196 The side menu folds away (02/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/10/26): "to give more space to the apps, I want the ability to colapse the menu of pages on the side. When
   it's closed, you should be able to put your mouse over to all the way to the left side of the page to have it open to
   choose another page/app, when you hover away it should close again. You should also have the functionality to keep it
   open". His answer: every app and the Hub (one menu, nav.wa-nav, holds the Hub's pages and each app's rail), remembered
   for each person on their computer; phones keep their bottom bar.

   On a computer: "Hide the menu" (the button at the top of the menu, or Ctrl or Cmd + \) folds it away and the page takes
   the whole width. Then the far left edge of the window (and the small Menu tab at the top left) opens it over the page;
   moving away closes it again, unless the launcher or a menu inside it is open, or it has the keyboard's focus. "Keep it
   open" (the pin, while it is out) puts it back for good. The choice lives in localStorage (wa196nav) on that computer.
   The automations reshape of wave196 is in parts152 (j_autos.js).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa196) return; window.__wa196 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var KEY = 'wa196nav', WIDE = 901, S = { col: false, peek: false, t: null, quiet: 0 };
  function wide() { return window.innerWidth >= WIDE; }
  function get() { try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY) === 'col'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function put(v) { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, v ? 'col' : 'open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function nav() { return document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav'); }
  var ICON = {
    hide: '<path d="M15 6 9 12l6 6"/><path d="M4.5 4.5v15"/>',
    pin: '<path d="M9 4.5h6l-1 5 3.5 3.5h-11L10 9.5z"/><path d="M12 13v6.5"/>',
    menu: '<path d="M4.5 7h15M4.5 12h15M4.5 17h15"/>'
  };
  function ic(n) { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + ICON[n] + '</svg>'; }

  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w196navcss';
  st.textContent = ''
    + '#w196pin,#w196edge,#w196tab{display:none;}'
    + '@media (min-width:' + WIDE + 'px){'
    + 'nav.wa-nav{transition:transform .22s ease, box-shadow .22s ease;}'
    + 'body{transition:padding-left .22s ease;}'
    + '#w196pin{display:inline-flex;position:absolute;top:14px;right:10px;z-index:4;width:32px;height:32px;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:none;border-radius:10px;background:transparent;color:rgba(16,63,57,.55);cursor:pointer;padding:0;}'
    + '#w196pin:hover,#w196pin:focus-visible{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w196pin:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:1px;}'
    + 'body.w196col{padding-left:0 !important;}'
    + 'body.w196col nav.wa-nav{transform:translateX(-101%);box-shadow:none;}'
    + 'body.w196col.w196peek nav.wa-nav{transform:none;box-shadow:16px 0 46px rgba(16,63,57,.22);z-index:200;}'
    + 'body.w196col.w196peek #w153pop{z-index:201;}'
    + 'body.w196col #w196pin{color:' + GREEN + ';background:rgba(220,165,39,.22);}'
    + 'body.w196col #w196edge{display:block;position:fixed;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:14px;z-index:199;}'
    + 'body.w196col #w196tab{display:inline-flex;position:fixed;left:10px;top:12px;z-index:198;align-items:center;gap:6px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);background:var(--wa-card,' + CREAM + ');color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:12px;padding:7px 11px 7px 9px;font:700 12.5px ' + BODY + ';cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(16,63,57,.10);}'
    + 'body.w196col #w196tab:hover{background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + 'body.w196col.w196peek #w196tab{opacity:0;pointer-events:none;}'
    + 'body.w196col .wa-toolbar{padding-left:112px !important;}'
    + '}'
    + '@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){nav.wa-nav,body{transition:none !important;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function ensure() {
    var n = nav(); if (!n) return false;
    if (!document.getElementById('w196pin')) {
      var b = document.createElement('button'); b.id = 'w196pin'; b.type = 'button';
      n.appendChild(b);
      b.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); set(!S.col); });
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w196edge')) {
      var ed = document.createElement('div'); ed.id = 'w196edge'; ed.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.appendChild(ed);
      ed.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { peek(true); });
    }
    if (!document.getElementById('w196tab')) {
      var tb = document.createElement('button'); tb.id = 'w196tab'; tb.type = 'button'; tb.innerHTML = ic('menu') + '<span>Menu</span>';
      tb.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Show the menu'); tb.title = 'Show the menu (Ctrl or Cmd + \\)';
      document.body.appendChild(tb);
      tb.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); peek(true); var f = nav() && nav().querySelector('#w196pin'); if (f) try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } });
      tb.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { peek(true); });
    }
    paintPin();
    return true;
  }
  function paintPin() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w196pin'); if (!b) return;
    var pinMode = S.col;   /* folded (and out on a hover): the pin keeps it open; otherwise the button hides it */
    b.innerHTML = ic(pinMode ? 'pin' : 'hide');
    var label = pinMode ? 'Keep the menu open' : 'Hide the menu';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', label); b.title = label + ' (Ctrl or Cmd + \\)';
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', pinMode ? 'false' : 'true');
  }
  function apply() {
    var on = S.col && wide();
    document.body.classList.toggle('w196col', on);
    if (!on) { S.peek = false; document.body.classList.remove('w196peek'); }
    paintPin();
  }
  function set(col) {
    S.col = !!col; put(S.col); S.peek = false; document.body.classList.remove('w196peek'); apply();
    /* just hidden with the mouse (or the focus) still on it: it stays hidden until the mouse has left it */
    if (S.col) { S.quiet = Date.now() + 1500; try { var ae = document.activeElement; if (ae && nav() && nav().contains(ae)) ae.blur(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(S.col ? 'Menu hidden: move to the left edge to open it' : 'Menu kept open'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* while it is out: it stays for the launcher, a menu inside it, the keyboard's focus or the mouse over it */
  function holding() {
    var n = nav(); if (!n) return false;
    if (n.matches(':hover')) return true;
    var pop = document.getElementById('w153pop'); if (pop && (pop.classList.contains('open') || pop.matches(':hover'))) return true;
    var ae = document.activeElement; if (ae && ae !== document.body && n.contains(ae) && ae.id !== 'w196pin' && ae.matches(':focus-visible')) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function peek(on) {
    if (!S.col || !wide()) return;
    if (on && S.quiet && Date.now() < S.quiet) return;
    clearTimeout(S.t);
    if (on) { S.peek = true; document.body.classList.add('w196peek'); paintPin(); return; }
    S.peek = false; document.body.classList.remove('w196peek');
  }
  function later(ms) {
    clearTimeout(S.t);
    S.t = setTimeout(function () { if (!S.peek) return; if (holding()) { later(400); return; } peek(false); }, ms == null ? 380 : ms);
  }
  function wire() {
    var n = nav(); if (!n || n.__w196) return; n.__w196 = true;
    n.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { if (S.col) { clearTimeout(S.t); if (!S.peek) peek(true); } });
    n.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { S.quiet = 0; if (S.col && S.peek) later(); });
    n.addEventListener('focusin', function () { if (S.col && !S.peek) peek(true); });
    n.addEventListener('focusout', function () { if (S.col && S.peek) later(250); });
    document.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) { var p = document.getElementById('w153pop'); if (S.peek && p && p.contains(e.target)) clearTimeout(S.t); });
    document.addEventListener('mouseout', function (e) { var p = document.getElementById('w153pop'); if (S.peek && p && p.contains(e.target) && !(e.relatedTarget && (p.contains(e.relatedTarget) || n.contains(e.relatedTarget)))) later(); });
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!wide()) return;
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && !e.altKey && (e.key === '\\' || e.code === 'Backslash')) { e.preventDefault(); set(!S.col); return; }
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && S.col && S.peek) { peek(false); }
  });
  /* choosing a page while it is out closes it */
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (S.col && S.peek) setTimeout(function () { if (!(nav() && nav().matches(':hover'))) peek(false); }, 150); });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () { apply(); });

  function boot() { if (!ensure()) return false; wire(); S.col = get(); apply(); return true; }
  if (!boot()) {
    var tries = 0, tm = setInterval(function () { if (boot() || ++tries > 40) clearInterval(tm); }, 250);
  }
  /* the menu is rebuilt by other waves: keep the button in it */
  setInterval(function () { try { var n = nav(); if (n && !document.getElementById('w196pin')) { ensure(); wire(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 1500);

  window.w196nav = { set: set, peek: peek, state: function () { return { col: S.col, peek: S.peek }; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v198 the Hub in French (03/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (02/10/26): "He's also french so I'd love to make the effort in
   putting the entire hub in french as an option. This is for future
   recruits too." His choices: the interface, the onboarding and the
   Avenue Academy in French (what people type stays as written); Quebec
   French, vous; each person picks their language.

   How it works (no page is rewritten):
   1. Each person's language: the account menu's Fran\u00e7ais / English,
      kept in hub_user_prefs (follows them to any device) and in this
      browser (localStorage wa198lang, so the page starts in it). Changing
      it reloads the page.
   2. The interface: a dictionary of the Hub's own words (hub_i18n:
      English as the page shows it, numbers as {0}, {1}...; read once
      through wa198_dict and kept in this browser until it changes).
      Every piece of text the page draws is looked up as it appears
      (a MutationObserver): whole, then with its numbers set aside, then
      piece by piece around separators (\u00b7, \u2014, |), then as a known
      phrase followed or preceded by a name or a number; dates and
      times read the French way. Text that is not the Hub's own (names,
      tasks, notes, messages) has no entry and stays as written. Text
      with no entry yet is reported (wa198_seen); i18n-translate
      translates it overnight when its words are in the Hub's code.
   3. The onboarding and the Avenue Academy: their French (hub_i18n_
      content, served by wa198_content only while the English is
      unchanged) replaces the English as the page reads those tables,
      except on the HR centre and the Admin centre, where they are
      edited. A save never writes the French over the English.
   Off: English readers get none of this (one localStorage read).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa198) return; window.__wa198 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var S = { lang: 'en', d: null, v: '', cmap: null, rev: {}, q: {}, qn: 0, sent: 0, names: {}, on: false, ready: false, stats: { hit: 0, miss: 0 }, burst: { n: 0, t: 0, hot: false } };
  function ls(k, v) { try { if (v === undefined) return localStorage.getItem(k); if (v === null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { return null; } return null; }
  S.lang = ls('wa198lang') === 'fr' ? 'fr' : 'en';
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  var NBSP = '\u00a0';

  /* French dates: Oct 2 \u2192 2 oct., Fri, Oct 2, 8:47 PM \u2192 ven. 2 oct., 20 h 47 */
  var MON = { jan: 'janv.', feb: 'f\u00e9vr.', mar: 'mars', apr: 'avr.', may: 'mai', jun: 'juin', jul: 'juill.', aug: 'ao\u00fbt', sep: 'sept.', oct: 'oct.', nov: 'nov.', dec: 'd\u00e9c.' };
  var MONL = { january: 'janvier', february: 'f\u00e9vrier', march: 'mars', april: 'avril', may: 'mai', june: 'juin', july: 'juillet', august: 'ao\u00fbt', september: 'septembre', october: 'octobre', november: 'novembre', december: 'd\u00e9cembre' };
  var DAY = { mon: 'lun.', tue: 'mar.', wed: 'mer.', thu: 'jeu.', fri: 'ven.', sat: 'sam.', sun: 'dim.' };
  var DAYL = { monday: 'lundi', tuesday: 'mardi', wednesday: 'mercredi', thursday: 'jeudi', friday: 'vendredi', saturday: 'samedi', sunday: 'dimanche' };

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa198css';
  css.textContent = '' +
    '#w115menu .w198lang{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}' +
    '#w115menu .w198lang small{margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.6px;color:var(--wa-muted);}' +
    '.w198busy{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;background:rgba(16,63,57,.92);color:' + CREAM + ';display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:18px;font-weight:700;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- the lookup ---------- */
  var NUM = /\d+(?:[.,]\d+)*/g;
  function norm(s) { var nums = [], i = 0; var k = s.replace(NUM, function (m) { nums.push(m); return '{' + (i++) + '}'; }); return { k: k, nums: nums }; }
  function frNum(n) { if (/^\d{1,3}(,\d{3})+$/.test(n)) return n.replace(/,/g, '\u202f'); if (/^\d+\.\d+$/.test(n)) return n.replace('.', ','); return n; }
  function fill(t, nums) { return t.replace(/\{(\d+)\}/g, function (m, i) { return nums[+i] != null ? frNum(nums[+i]) : m; }); }
  function look(s) {
    var d = S.d; if (!d) return null;
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d, s)) return d[s];
    var n = norm(s);
    if (n.nums.length && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d, n.k)) return fill(d[n.k], n.nums);
    return null;
  }
  var NAME1 = "[A-Z\\u00c0-\\u00de][A-Za-z\\u00c0-\\u00ff'\\u2019.-]*(?:\\s+(?:de|du|van|von|da|el|al|[A-Z\\u00c0-\\u00de][A-Za-z\\u00c0-\\u00ff'\\u2019.-]*)){0,4}";
  var NAMEISH = new RegExp('^(?:' + NAME1 + '(?:,\\s*(?:and\\s+)?' + NAME1 + '){0,8}|[#$]?\\d[\\d.,:%]*\\s*[A-Za-z%$]{0,4})[.,!?]?$');
  function dates(s) {
    if (!/\b(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec|Mon|Tue|Wed|Thu|Fri|Sat|Sun)[a-z]*\b|\b(?:AM|PM|am|pm)\b/.test(s)) return s;
    var o = s;
    o = o.replace(/\b(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)\s+(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?(,?\s+(\d{4}))?\b/g, function (m, mo, dd, x, yy) { return dd + ' ' + MONL[mo.toLowerCase()] + (yy ? ' ' + yy : ''); });
    o = o.replace(/\b(Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sept?|Oct|Nov|Dec)\.?\s+(\d{1,2})(,?\s+(\d{4}))?\b/g, function (m, mo, dd, x, yy) { return dd + ' ' + MON[mo.slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()] + (yy ? ' ' + yy : ''); });
    o = o.replace(/\b(\d{1,2})\s+(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)\b/g, function (m, dd, mo) { return dd + ' ' + MONL[mo.toLowerCase()]; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)\s+(\d{4})\b/g, function (m, mo, yy) { return MONL[mo.toLowerCase()] + ' ' + yy; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\s+(\d{4})\b/g, function (m, mo, yy) { return MON[mo.toLowerCase()] + ' ' + yy; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(Monday|Tuesday|Wednesday|Thursday|Friday|Saturday|Sunday)\b,?/g, function (m, d) { return DAYL[d.toLowerCase()]; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(Mon|Tue|Wed|Thu|Fri|Sat|Sun)\b,?(?=\s+\d)/g, function (m, d) { return DAY[d.toLowerCase()]; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(AM|PM|am|pm)\b/g, function (m, h, mi, ap) { h = +h % 12 + (/p/i.test(ap) ? 12 : 0); return h + ' h ' + mi; });
    o = o.replace(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(AM|PM|am|pm)\b/g, function (m, h, ap) { h = +h % 12 + (/p/i.test(ap) ? 12 : 0); return h + ' h'; });
    return o;
  }
  /* a date or a time and nothing else (so a task that says Sunday stays as written) */
  var DATEWORD = /\b(?:(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec|Mon|Tue|Wed|Thu|Fri|Sat|Sun)[a-z]*|AM|PM|am|pm|at|to|from|until)\b|\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)?/g;
  function dateOnly(s) { return /\d/.test(s) && !/[A-Za-z]{2}/.test(s.replace(DATEWORD, '')); }
  function datesIf(s) { return dateOnly(s) ? dates(s) : s; }
  /* what may sit beside a known phrase: a number, a date, or people on the roster (Ahmed; Ahmed, Ian and Shima) */
  function fill2(x) {
    x = String(x || '').trim(); if (!x) return false;
    if (/^[#$]?\d[\d.,:%]*\s*[A-Za-z%$]{0,4}[.,!?]?$/.test(x) || dateOnly(x)) return true;
    var toks = x.replace(/[.,!?]$/, '').split(/\s*,\s*|\s+and\s+/);
    return toks.length > 0 && toks.every(function (t) { return !!S.names[t] || !!S.names[t.split(' ')[0]] && /^[A-Z]/.test(t); });
  }
  function punct(p) { return p === ':' ? NBSP + ':' : p; }
  function core(s, depth) {
    if (!s || S.names[s]) return null;
    var t = look(s); if (t != null) return t;
    if (depth > 2) return null;
    /* around separators */
    var parts = s.split(/(\s+[\u00b7\u2022\u2014|]\s+|\s+-\s+)/);
    if (parts.length > 1) {
      var any = false, o = parts.map(function (p, i) { if (i % 2) return p; var x = core(p, depth + 1); if (x != null) { any = true; return x; } var dt = datesIf(p); if (dt !== p) any = true; return dt; });
      if (any) return o.join('');
    }
    /* symbols around the words (+ Add a task, ? How this page works, sparkles Get recommendation, open \u00b7) */
    var sy = /^([^A-Za-z0-9\u00c0-\u00ff({\[]+)?([\s\S]*?)([^A-Za-z0-9\u00c0-\u00ff)}\].!?\u2026]+)?$/.exec(s);
    if (sy && (sy[1] || sy[3]) && sy[2] && sy[2] !== s) { var inner = core(sy[2], depth + 1); if (inner != null) return (sy[1] || '') + inner + (sy[3] || ''); }
    /* words, then a short note in brackets: Daniela (Fundraising), Meeting date (optional) */
    var br = /^([\s\S]*?\S)\s*\(([^()]{1,60})\)$/.exec(s);
    if (br) {
      var bh = core(br[1], depth + 1), bi = core(br[2], depth + 1);
      if (bh != null || bi != null) return (bh != null ? bh : br[1]) + ' (' + (bi != null ? bi : br[2]) + ')';
    }
    /* a phrase and its closing punctuation */
    var c = /^([\s\S]*?\S)\s*([:?!.\u2026])$/.exec(s);
    if (c) { var cx = look(c[1]); if (cx != null) return cx + punct(c[2]); }
    /* a known phrase, then a name or a number (Handled by Ahmed, Welcome Alyx), or the other way round */
    if (s.length < 120) {
      var sp = []; for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) if (s[i] === ' ') sp.push(i);
      for (var j = sp.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        var head = s.slice(0, sp[j]), tail = s.slice(sp[j] + 1);
        if (fill2(tail)) { var h = look(head) || look(head.replace(/[:,]$/, '')); if (h != null) return h + (/[:,]$/.test(head) && !/[:,]$/.test(h) ? punct(head.slice(-1)) : '') + ' ' + datesIf(tail); }
        if (fill2(head)) { var tl = look(tail); if (tl != null) return datesIf(head) + ' ' + tl; }
      }
    }
    var dt2 = datesIf(s); if (dt2 !== s) return dt2;
    return undefined;   /* nothing known: may be the Hub's own words not yet translated */
  }
  /* raw text as the page has it (with its spaces) \u2192 French, or null to leave it */
  function tr(raw) {
    var m = /^(\s*)([\s\S]*?)(\s*)$/.exec(raw); var s = m[2];
    if (s.length < 2 || !/[A-Za-z]{2}/.test(s)) return null;
    var out = core(s, 0);
    if (out === undefined) { S.stats.miss++; queue(s); return null; }
    if (out == null || out === s) return null;
    S.stats.hit++;
    return m[1] + out + m[3];
  }
  function queue(s) {
    if (s.length > 160 || S.qn > 400 || S.names[s]) return;
    if (!/[a-z]{2}/.test(s)) return;                       /* all capitals or a code */
    if (NAMEISH.test(s) && !/\s[a-z]/.test(s)) return;     /* looks like a name */
    var k = norm(s).k; if (S.q[k]) return; S.q[k] = 1; S.qn++;
  }

  /* ---------- the page: every text node and a few attributes ---------- */
  var SKIP = 'script,style,textarea,code,pre,svg,[contenteditable=""],[contenteditable="true"],.notranslate,[translate="no"],[data-w198skip]';
  var ATTRS = ['placeholder', 'title', 'aria-label', 'alt'];
  function skipEl(el) { try { return !!(el && el.closest && el.closest(SKIP)); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  /* Some pages check their own words and put the English back (a title kept in step with its section): the second time an
     element is set back to the English it had, it keeps the English, so the two never take turns. */
  function doText(tn) {
    if (tn.__w198fr !== undefined && tn.data === tn.__w198fr) return;
    var p = tn.parentNode; if (!p || p.nodeType !== 1 || p.__w198stop || skipEl(p)) return;
    var now = Date.now();
    if (p.__w198last && p.__w198last.en === tn.data && now - p.__w198last.t < 4000) {
      p.__w198back = (p.__w198back || 0) + 1;
      if (p.__w198back >= 2) { p.__w198stop = true; tn.__w198fr = tn.data; return; }
    }
    if (++S.burst.n > 20000) { if (now - S.burst.t < 1500) { S.burst.hot = true; return; } S.burst = { n: 0, t: now, hot: false }; }
    var fr = tr(tn.data);
    if (fr != null) { p.__w198last = { en: tn.data, t: now }; tn.__w198en = tn.data; tn.__w198fr = fr; tn.data = fr; }
    else tn.__w198fr = tn.data;
  }
  function doAttrs(el) {
    if (skipEl(el)) return;
    for (var i = 0; i < ATTRS.length; i++) {
      var a = ATTRS[i]; if (!el.hasAttribute(a)) continue;
      var v = el.getAttribute(a), st = el.__w198a || (el.__w198a = {});
      if (st[a] === v) continue;
      var fr = tr(v); if (fr != null) { st[a] = fr; el.setAttribute(a, fr); } else st[a] = v;
    }
    if (el.tagName === 'INPUT' && (el.type === 'button' || el.type === 'submit') && el.value && !el.__w198v) { var fv = tr(el.value); if (fv != null) { el.value = fv; } el.__w198v = 1; }
  }
  function walk(root) {
    if (!root) return;
    if (root.nodeType === 3) { doText(root); return; }
    if (root.nodeType !== 1 && root.nodeType !== 9 && root.nodeType !== 11) return;
    if (root.nodeType === 1) { if (skipEl(root)) return; doAttrs(root); }
    var w = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_ELEMENT | NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, { acceptNode: function (n) { if (n.nodeType === 1) { var tg = n.tagName; return (tg === 'SCRIPT' || tg === 'STYLE' || tg === 'TEXTAREA' || tg === 'CODE' || tg === 'PRE' || n.hasAttribute('data-w198skip') || n.classList.contains('notranslate')) ? NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT : NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT; } return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT; } });
    var n; while ((n = w.nextNode())) { if (n.nodeType === 3) doText(n); else doAttrs(n); }
  }
  var MO = null;
  function start() {
    if (S.on || S.lang !== 'fr' || !S.d) return; S.on = true;
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('lang', 'fr-CA');
    walk(document.body); title();
    MO = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) {
        var m = muts[i];
        if (m.type === 'childList') { for (var j = 0; j < m.addedNodes.length; j++) walk(m.addedNodes[j]); }
        else if (m.type === 'characterData') doText(m.target);
        else if (m.type === 'attributes' && m.target.nodeType === 1) doAttrs(m.target);
      }
    });
    MO.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ATTRS });
    var tEl = document.querySelector('title'); if (tEl) new MutationObserver(title).observe(tEl, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true });
  }
  function title() { var t = document.title; if (t && t !== S.lastTitle) { var parts = t.split(' \u00b7 '), out = parts.map(function (p) { var x = tr(p); return x == null ? p : x; }).join(' \u00b7 '); S.lastTitle = out; if (out !== t) document.title = out; } }

  /* ---------- the dictionary: this browser's copy, refreshed when the Hub's changes ---------- */
  function cached() { try { var c = JSON.parse(ls('wa198dict') || 'null'); if (c && c.d) { S.d = c.d; S.v = c.v || ''; return true; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return false; }
  function dbOk() { try { return typeof db !== 'undefined' && db && typeof db.rpc === 'function'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  async function refreshDict(force) {
    if (!dbOk()) return false;
    try {
      if (!force && S.d) { var vr = await db.rpc('wa198_dict_v', { p_lang: 'fr' }); if (vr && !vr.error && vr.data === S.v) return false; }
      var r = await db.rpc('wa198_dict', { p_lang: 'fr' });
      if (!r || r.error || !r.data || !r.data.d) return false;
      var fresh = !S.d; S.d = r.data.d; S.v = r.data.v || '';
      ls('wa198dict', JSON.stringify({ v: S.v, d: S.d }));
      return fresh;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  /* the onboarding and Academy content in French (only what is current) */
  var CT = { trainings: 'id', onboarding_template: 'id', academy_paths: 'key', hr_setup_template: 'id', app_config: 'key' };
  function setContent(rows) {
    var m = {}, rev = {};
    (rows || []).forEach(function (r) { var k = r.tbl + '|' + r.row_id + '|' + r.field; m[k] = r.tr; });

  }
  function cachedContent() { try { var c = JSON.parse(ls('wa198content') || 'null'); if (c && Array.isArray(c)) { setContent(c); return true; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return false; }

  }
  /* the pages where this content is edited: a note says the English is the original (the French is shown; a save keeps the English

  function editing() { var h = String(location.hash || ''); return /^#hrcentre\/(plan|training)/.test(h); }

  }
  function frValue(orig, tr) { if (orig && typeof orig === 'object') { try { return JSON.parse(tr); } catch (e) { return orig; } } return tr; }
  function mapRows(t, res) {
    try {
      if (S.lang !== 'fr' || !S.cmap || !res || !res.data) return res;
      var idk = CT[t], rows = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [res.data];
      rows.forEach(function (row) {
        if (!row || typeof row !== 'object' || row[idk] == null) return;
        if (t === 'app_config' && row.key !== 'onboarding_journey') return;
        Object.keys(row).forEach(function (f) {
          var k = t + '|' + row[idk] + '|' + f, tr = S.cmap[k];
          if (tr == null || row[f] == null) return;
          var v = frValue(row[f], tr);
          S.rev[t + '|' + f + '|' + (typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : v)] = row[f];
          row[f] = v;
        });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* the English stays */ }
    return res;
  }
  /* a save never writes the French over the English: a value still equal to the French we showed goes back to the English */
  function unmap(t, patch) {
    if (!patch || typeof patch !== 'object' || !Object.keys(S.rev).length) return patch;
    var one = function (p) { if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return p; Object.keys(p).forEach(function (f) { var v = p[f], k = t + '|' + f + '|' + (v && typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : v); if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(S.rev, k)) p[f] = S.rev[k]; }); return p; };
    return Array.isArray(patch) ? patch.map(one) : one(patch);
  }
  function wrapThen(b, t) {
    if (!b || typeof b.then !== 'function' || b.__w198) return b;
    var orig = b.then; b.__w198 = true;
    b.then = function (ok, bad) { return orig.call(this, function (res) { res = mapRows(t, res); return ok ? ok(res) : res; }, bad); };
    return b;
  }
  function patchDb() {
    if (!dbOk() || db.__w198) return !!(dbOk() && db.__w198);
    var from = db.from.bind(db);
    db.from = function (t) {
      var q = from(t);
      if (!CT[t] || S.lang !== 'fr') return q;
      ['select'].forEach(function (mth) { if (typeof q[mth] !== 'function') return; var o = q[mth].bind(q); q[mth] = function () { return wrapThen(o.apply(null, arguments), t); }; });
      ['update', 'upsert', 'insert'].forEach(function (mth) { if (typeof q[mth] !== 'function') return; var o = q[mth].bind(q); q[mth] = function (p) { var a = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments); a[0] = unmap(t, p); return o.apply(null, a); }; });
      return q;
    };
    db.__w198 = true; return true;
  }
  /* what had no translation, sent now and then (at most 80 a call) */
  async function flush() {
    if (!dbOk() || S.lang !== 'fr') return;
    var keys = Object.keys(S.q).filter(function (k) { return S.q[k] === 1; }).slice(0, 80); if (!keys.length) return;
    keys.forEach(function (k) { S.q[k] = 2; });
    try { await db.rpc('wa198_seen', { p_lang: 'fr', p_keys: keys }); S.sent += keys.length; } catch (e) { /* next time */ }
  }
  /* the person's language, from the database (another device may have changed it) */
  async function syncPref() {
    var em = myEmail(); if (!dbOk() || !em) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_user_prefs').select('lang').eq('email', em).limit(1);
      var row = r && !r.error && r.data && r.data[0];
      if (row && row.lang && row.lang !== S.lang) {
        var once = null; try { once = sessionStorage.getItem('wa198sync'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        if (once === row.lang) return;
        try { sessionStorage.setItem('wa198sync', row.lang); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        await switchTo(row.lang, true);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  async function switchTo(lang, fromPref) {
    lang = lang === 'fr' ? 'fr' : 'en';
    var cover = document.createElement('div'); cover.className = 'w198busy'; cover.textContent = lang === 'fr' ? 'Passage au fran\u00e7ais\u2026' : 'Switching to English\u2026'; document.body.appendChild(cover);
    ls('wa198lang', lang);
    if (!fromPref) { try { var em = myEmail(); if (em && dbOk()) await db.from('hub_user_prefs').upsert({ email: em, lang: lang, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }); } catch (e) { /* this browser keeps it */ } }
    if (lang === 'fr') { S.lang = 'fr'; await Promise.all([refreshDict(true), refreshContent()]); }
    location.reload();
  }

  /* ---------- the account menu: Fran\u00e7ais / English ---------- */
  function menuItem() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu'); if (!m || m.querySelector('.w198lang')) return;
    var so = m.querySelector('#signOutBtn');
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w115item w198lang notranslate'; b.setAttribute('data-w198lang', S.lang === 'fr' ? 'en' : 'fr');
    b.setAttribute('lang', S.lang === 'fr' ? 'en' : 'fr');
    b.innerHTML = (S.lang === 'fr' ? 'English' : 'Fran\u00e7ais') + '<small>' + (S.lang === 'fr' ? 'EN' : 'FR') + '</small>';
    if (so) m.insertBefore(b, so); else m.appendChild(b);
  }
  (function watchMenu() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu');
    if (!m) { setTimeout(watchMenu, 700); return; }
    menuItem();
    new MutationObserver(function () { if (!m.querySelector('.w198lang')) menuItem(); }).observe(m, { childList: true });
  })();
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {

    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();

  }, true);

  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { setTimeout(editNote, 400); });
  setInterval(function () { if (S.lang === 'fr') editNote(); }, 2000);

  /* ---------- boot ---------- */
  if (S.lang === 'fr') {
    try { (window.WA_MEMBERS_NAMES || []).forEach(function (n) { S.names[n] = 1; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

    var after = 0;
    (function signedIn() {
      var ok = false; try { ok = typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email && dbOk(); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
      if (!ok) { if (++after < 400) setTimeout(signedIn, 250); return; }
      try { (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).forEach(function (m) { if (m && m.name) { S.names[m.name] = 1; S.names[String(m.name).split(' ')[0]] = 1; } }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      refreshDict(false).then(function (fresh) { if (fresh || !S.on) { S.on = false; if (MO) MO.disconnect(); begin(); } });
      refreshContent();
      syncPref();
      setInterval(flush, 15000);
    })();
  } else {
    (function signedIn() {

      if (!ok) { setTimeout(signedIn, 400); return; }

  }

  window.w198 = { state: function () { return S; }, same: function (text, en) { text = String(text || '').trim(); if (text === en) return true; if (S.lang !== 'fr' || !S.d) return false; var f = tr(en); return f != null && text === f.trim(); }, tr: tr, core: core, dates: dates, norm: norm, switchTo: switchTo, walk: walk, flush: flush, mapRows: mapRows, unmap: unmap };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v199 Positions: job descriptions and CVs from the HR drive, linked to Applications (03/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (03/10/26): "add other positions with JDs from the human resources shared drive ... add CVs that
   are linked to current positions like Ahmed who started as a volunteer relations coordinator to Operations
   manager. The job descriptions / positions title should be properly linked to the applications module ...
   we should be able to upload CVs to a folder in google drive ... by department ... a folder for all the
   resumes, a single file that is the latest version of the job description, and a folder of older job
   descriptions." His choices: the Hub's eight departments; positions open in the Hub (ClickUp's
   Recruitment list imported once).
   The drive: *Recruitment* > Job descriptions > <Department> > <Position> > Resumes / the JD / Older JDs.
   jd-drive reads it into hub_positions (one row per position folder) and hub_position_files (CVs, older
   JDs); a CV dropped into a Resumes folder after the first read becomes an application (New).
   This wave draws HR centre > Positions (by department, with each position's JD, CVs, applications and
   the people who hold it) and links Applications to positions (filter, card, sheet: JD, CV in Drive).
   ===================================================================== */

  function ago(s) { if (!s) return ''; var d = (Date.now() - new Date(s).getTime()) / 60000; if (d < 2) return 'just now'; if (d < 60) return Math.round(d) + ' min ago'; if (d < 1440) return Math.round(d / 60) + ' h ago'; try { return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function day(s) { if (!s) return ''; if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s))) s = s + 'T12:00:00'; try { return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function cands() { try { return (window.w197 && w197.state().cands) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }

  function peopleOf(id) {

  }
  async function fn(body) {

  async function q(p) { try { var r = await p; return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load(force) {
    if (P.loading) return P.loading;
    if (!force && P.at && Date.now() - P.at < 60000) return P;
    P.loading = (async function () {
      var res = await Promise.all([
        q(db.from('hub_positions').select('*').eq('gone', false).order('title')),
        q(db.from('hub_position_files').select('id,position_id,kind,file_id,name,mime,url,created_time,modified_time,candidate_id,member_id').eq('gone', false).order('created_time', { ascending: false }).limit(2000)),

        q(db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'jd_drive_sync').limit(1)),
        q(db.from('hub_candidate_grades').select('*').order('updated_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5000)),
        q(db.from('hub_candidate_calls').select('*').order('called_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5000))
      ]);
      P.pos = res[0]; P.files = res[1]; P.people = res[2]; P.sync = res[3][0] ? res[3][0].value : null; if (typeof P.sync === 'string') { try { P.sync = JSON.parse(P.sync); } catch (e) { P.sync = null; } }   /* app_config.value is text */ P.grades = res[4] || []; P.calls = res[5] || [];
      P.at = Date.now(); P.loading = null;
      try { if (window.w197 && /^#hrcentre\/applications/.test(String(location.hash || ''))) w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (window.w197 && w197.loadCands) await w197.loadCands(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return P;
    })();
    return P.loading;
  }
  async function privacy() {
    if (P.privAt && Date.now() - P.privAt < 600000) return P.privacy;
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'privacy' }); P.privacy = !!(r && r.link_open); } catch (e) { P.privacy = null; }
    P.privAt = Date.now(); return P.privacy;
  }

  /* ---------- for Applications (wave197 asks) ---------- */
  function roleOf(c) { var p = c && c.position_id ? byId(c.position_id) : null; return p ? p.title : (c && c.role) || 'No role given'; }
  function filterOptions(list) {
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    (list || []).forEach(function (c) { var p = c.position_id ? byId(c.position_id) : null; var k = p ? 'p' + p.id : (c.role ? 'r' + c.role : ''); if (!k || seen[k]) return; seen[k] = 1; out.push({ v: k, l: p ? p.title : c.role }); });
    return out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.l.localeCompare(b.l); });
  }
  function match(c, v) { if (!v) return true; if (v.charAt(0) === 'p') return String(c.position_id) === v.slice(1); if (v.charAt(0) === 'r') return !c.position_id && c.role === v.slice(1) || roleOf(c) === v.slice(1); return c.role === v; }
  function sheetBits(c) {
    /* the positions are not read yet (the sheet opened in the first seconds): read them, then draw the sheet again */
    if (!P.at) { load().then(function () { try { var st = window.w197 && w197.state(); if (st && String(st.open) === String(c.id)) w197.openSheet(c.id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }); return '<label>Position</label><p class="w197note" style="margin:0">Loading the positions\u2026</p>'; }
    var opts = DEPTS.map(function (d) {
      var ps = P.pos.filter(function (p) { return p.dept === d[0]; }); if (!ps.length) return '';
      return '<optgroup label="' + E(d[1]) + '">' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (String(c.position_id) === String(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.title) + (hiring(p) ? '' : (p.stage === 'filled' ? ' (filled)' : ' (not hiring now)')) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    }).join('');
    var p = c.position_id ? byId(c.position_id) : null;
    var cv = c.drive_file_id ? P.files.filter(function (f) { return f.file_id === c.drive_file_id; })[0] : null;
    return (window.w208 ? w208.topBits(c) : '') + callField(c) + gradeField(c) + ivField(c) + '<label for="w199pos1">Position</label><select id="w199pos1" data-w199f="position_id"><option value="">Not linked to a position' + (c.role ? ' (they wrote: ' + E(String(c.role).slice(0, 40)) + ')' : '') + '</option>' + opts + '</select>' +
      '<div class="w199links">' + (p && p.jd_url ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + E(p.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">The job description</a>' : (p ? '<span class="w197pill mut">No JD in Drive yet</span>' : '')) +
      (p ? btn('The position', 'data-w199open="' + p.id + '"', 'sm') : '') +
      (cv && cv.url ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + E(cv.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">CV in Drive</a>' : (c.drive_file_id ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + E(c.drive_file_id) + '/view" target="_blank" rel="noopener">CV in Drive</a>' : (p ? '<label class="w197b sm w199up">Upload their CV to Drive<input type="file" data-w199upc="' + c.id + '" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.odt,.rtf,.txt,.png,.jpg,.jpeg" hidden></label>' : ''))) + '</div>' +
      (window.w209 ? w209.sheetBits(c) : '') + (window.w211 ? w211.sheetBits(c) : '') + (window.w208 ? w208.sheetBits(c) : '');   /* wave209: a volunteer role moves to the Volunteer Manager; wave208: the interview from the transcript archive, and its review */
  }

  /* ---------- grades: each person gives 1 to 5 stars (Steel, 03/10/26: "grade applicants and have that show as well") ---------- */
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myName() { var n = ''; try { n = (USER && (USER.name || USER.full_name)) || ''; } catch (e) { n = ''; } if (n) return n; var em = myEmail(); var m = members().filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0]; return (m && m.name) || em; }
  function gradesOf(id) { return (P.grades || []).filter(function (g) { return String(g.candidate_id) === String(id); }); }
  function avgOf(id) { var gs = gradesOf(id); if (!gs.length) return null; return { avg: gs.reduce(function (a, g) { return a + Number(g.stars || 0); }, 0) / gs.length, n: gs.length }; }
  function starTxt(a) { return '\u2605 ' + (Math.round(a.avg * 10) / 10).toFixed(1) + (a.n > 1 ? ' (' + a.n + ')' : ''); }
  function gradePill(c) { var a = avgOf(c.id); return a ? '<span class="w199st' + (a.avg >= 4 ? ' hi' : (a.avg < 2.5 ? ' lo' : '')) + '" title="Average of ' + a.n + (a.n === 1 ? ' grade' : ' grades') + '">' + starTxt(a) + '</span>' : ''; }
  function gradeField(c) {
    var gs = gradesOf(c.id), mine = gs.filter(function (g) { return lower(g.grader_email) === myEmail(); })[0], a = avgOf(c.id);
    var stars = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(function (n) { return '<button type="button" class="w199star' + (mine && mine.stars >= n ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w199grade="' + c.id + '" data-n="' + n + '" aria-label="' + n + (n === 1 ? ' star' : ' stars') + '">\u2605</button>'; }).join('');
    var others = gs.filter(function (g) { return g !== mine; });
    return '<label>Grade' + (a ? ' <small>' + E(starTxt(a)) + ' average</small>' : '') + '</label>' +
      '<div class="w199grade"><span class="w199stars" role="group" aria-label="Your grade">' + stars + '</span><small>' + (mine ? 'Your grade' + (mine.stars ? ': ' + mine.stars + ' of 5' : '') : 'Give your grade, 1 to 5') + '</small></div>' +
      (mine ? '<input type="text" id="w199gnote" data-w199gnote="' + c.id + '" placeholder="Why this grade (optional)" value="' + E(mine.note || '') + '">' : '') +
      (others.length ? '<ul class="w199files w199glist">' + others.map(function (g) { return '<li><b>' + E(g.grader_name || g.grader_email) + '</b><span class="w199st">' + '\u2605'.repeat(g.stars) + '</span>' + (g.note ? '<small>' + E(g.note) + '</small>' : '') + '<small>' + E(day(g.updated_at)) + (g.stage ? ' \u00b7 at ' + E(g.stage) : '') + '</small></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '');
  }
  async function setGrade(cid, n) {
    var em = myEmail(); if (!em) return;
    var gs = gradesOf(cid), mine = gs.filter(function (g) { return lower(g.grader_email) === em; })[0];
    var c = cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(cid); })[0];
    try {
      if (mine && mine.stars === n) {   /* the same star again clears your grade */
        var d = await db.from('hub_candidate_grades').delete().eq('id', mine.id); if (d && d.error) throw d.error;
        P.grades = P.grades.filter(function (g) { return g !== mine; }); toastS('Your grade is cleared');
      } else {
        var row = { candidate_id: Number(cid), grader_email: em, grader_name: myName(), stars: n, stage: c ? (c.status || 'new') : null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        if (mine) row.note = mine.note || null;
        var r = await db.from('hub_candidate_grades').upsert(row, { onConflict: 'candidate_id,grader_email' }).select('*').single(); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        var saved = (r && r.data && r.data.id) ? r.data : Object.assign({}, mine || {}, row);
        P.grades = P.grades.filter(function (g) { return g !== mine; }); P.grades.unshift(saved);
        toastS((c ? c.name + ': ' : '') + n + ' of 5');
      }
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(cid), field: 'grade', old_value: mine ? String(mine.stars) : null, new_value: mine && mine.stars === n ? '' : String(n), changed_by: myName() }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
      try { w197.paintBoard(); w197.openSheet(cid); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save the grade: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }
  /* ---------- calls: has someone called them? (Steel, 03/10/26: "check whether people in reviewed have been called or not") ---------- */
  var OUTCOMES = [['reached', 'Reached'], ['voicemail', 'Left a voicemail'], ['no_answer', 'No answer'], ['wrong_number', 'Wrong number']];
  function outLabel(o) { var x = OUTCOMES.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === o; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : o; }
  function callsOf(id) { return (P.calls || []).filter(function (k) { return String(k.candidate_id) === String(id); }); }
  function notCalled(c) { return c && c.status === 'reviewing' && !callsOf(c.id).length; }
  function callPill(c) {
    if (!c || ['reviewing', 'interview', 'offer'].indexOf(c.status) < 0) return '';
    var ks = callsOf(c.id); if (!ks.length) return c.status === 'reviewing' ? '<span class="w199ivno">Not called yet</span>' : '';
    var k = ks[0], reached = ks.some(function (x) { return x.outcome === 'reached'; });
    return '<span class="' + (reached ? 'w199called' : 'w199tried') + '" title="' + ks.length + (ks.length === 1 ? ' call' : ' calls') + '">' + (reached ? 'Called' : 'Tried') + ' ' + E(shortDay(k.called_at)) + (reached ? '' : ' (' + E(outLabel(k.outcome).toLowerCase()) + (ks.length > 1 ? ', ' + ks.length + 'x' : '') + ')') + '</span>';
  }
  function shortDay(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function callField(c) {
    var ks = callsOf(c.id);
    return '<label>Calls <small>' + (ks.length ? ks.length : 'none yet') + '</small></label>' +
      '<div class="w199calls"><span>Log a call:</span>' + OUTCOMES.map(function (o) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b sm' + (o[0] === 'reached' ? ' pri' : '') + '" data-w199call="' + c.id + '" data-o="' + o[0] + '">' + E(o[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<input type="text" id="w199callnote" placeholder="What was said (optional, saved with the call you log)">' +
      (ks.length ? '<ul class="w199files">' + ks.map(function (k) { return '<li><b>' + E(outLabel(k.outcome)) + '</b><small>' + E(ivText(k.called_at)) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(k.by_name || k.by_email || '') + '</small>' + (k.note ? '<small>' + E(k.note) + '</small>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : (c.status === 'reviewing' ? '<p class="w197note" style="margin:4px 0 0">Nobody has logged a call yet.</p>' : ''));
  }
  async function logCall(cid, outcome) {
    var nt = document.getElementById('w199callnote'), c = cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(cid); })[0];
    var row = { candidate_id: Number(cid), outcome: outcome, note: nt && nt.value.trim() ? nt.value.trim().slice(0, 500) : null, by_email: myEmail(), by_name: myName(), stage: c ? (c.status || 'new') : null, called_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidate_calls').insert(row).select('*').single(); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      P.calls = [(r && r.data && r.data.id) ? r.data : row].concat(P.calls || []);
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(cid), field: 'call', old_value: null, new_value: outcome, changed_by: row.by_name }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
      toastS((c ? c.name + ': ' : '') + outLabel(outcome));
      try { w197.paintBoard(); w197.openSheet(cid); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not log the call: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }
  function notCalledOnly() { return !!P.notCalled; }

  /* ---------- several applicants at once (Steel, 03/10/26: "select multiple applicants at a time to do bulk operations") ---------- */
  P.sel = {};
  function selIds() { return Object.keys(P.sel).filter(function (k) { return P.sel[k]; }).map(Number); }
  function selBits(c) { return P.selMode ? '<span class="w199sel' + (P.sel[c.id] ? ' on' : '') + '" aria-hidden="true">' + (P.sel[c.id] ? '\u2713' : '') + '</span>' : ''; }
  function bulkBar() {
    var b = document.getElementById('w199bulk');
    var onApps = /^#hrcentre\/applications/.test(String(location.hash || ''));
    if (!P.selMode || !onApps) { if (b) b.remove(); return; }
    if (!b) { b = document.createElement('div'); b.id = 'w199bulk'; b.setAttribute('role', 'region'); b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Selected applications'); document.body.appendChild(b); }
    var n = selIds().length, ms = members().slice().sort(function (a, c) { return a.name.localeCompare(c.name); });
    var stages = [['new', 'New'], ['reviewing', 'Reviewing'], ['interview', 'Interview'], ['offer', 'Offer'], ['hired', 'Hired'], ['declined', 'Declined'], ['withdrawn', 'Withdrawn']];
    var pos = DEPTS.map(function (d) { var ps = P.pos.filter(function (p) { return p.dept === d[0]; }); return ps.length ? '<optgroup label="' + E(d[1]) + '">' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(p.title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>' : ''; }).join('');
    var dis = n ? '' : ' disabled';
    b.innerHTML = '<b>' + n + ' selected</b>' + btn('Select all shown', 'data-w199a="selall"', 'sm') + (n ? btn('Clear', 'data-w199a="selnone"', 'sm') : '') +
      '<select data-w199bulk="status"' + dis + ' aria-label="Move to stage"><option value="">Move to\u2026</option>' + stages.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s[0] + '">' + E(s[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-w199bulk="owner"' + dis + ' aria-label="Handled by"><option value="">Handled by\u2026</option><option value="__none">Nobody</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-w199bulk="position_id"' + dis + ' aria-label="Position"><option value="">Position\u2026</option>' + pos + '</select>' + (window.w211 ? w211.bulkBits(selIds()) : '') +
      btn('Done', 'data-w199a="seloff"', 'sm pri');
  }
  function setSelMode(on) { P.selMode = !!on; if (!on) P.sel = {}; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } bulkBar(); }
  async function bulkApply(field, value) {
    var ids = selIds(); if (!ids.length || !value) return;
    var patch = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: myName() }, label = '';
    if (field === 'status') {
      if (window.w211g) { var g = await w211g.bulkGate(ids, value); if (!g || !g.ids.length) { bulkBar(); return; } ids = g.ids; Object.keys(g.patch || {}).forEach(function (k) { patch[k] = g.patch[k]; }); }   /* wave211: each card needs what its stage needs */
      patch.status = value; patch.stage_at = patch.updated_at; label = 'moved to ' + value;
    }
    else if (field === 'owner') { patch.owner = value === '__none' ? null : value; label = value === '__none' ? 'handled by nobody' : 'handled by ' + value; }
    else if (field === 'position_id') { var p = byId(value); if (!p) return; patch.position_id = p.id; patch.role = p.title; label = 'linked to ' + p.title; }
    else return;
    var verb = ids.length + (ids.length === 1 ? ' application ' : ' applications ') + label;
    if (field === 'status' && (value === 'declined' || value === 'withdrawn') && !window.w211g && !window.confirm(verb.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + verb.slice(1) + '?')) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update(patch).in('id', ids); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      cands().forEach(function (c) { if (ids.indexOf(Number(c.id)) >= 0) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; }); });
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert(ids.map(function (id) { return { entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(id), field: field + ' (bulk)', old_value: null, new_value: String(field === 'position_id' ? patch.role : (patch[field] == null ? '' : patch[field])), changed_by: myName() }; })); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
      toastS(verb.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + verb.slice(1));
      P.sel = {}; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } bulkBar();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not update: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  function sortGrade() { return !!P.sortGrade; }
  function gradeAvg(c) { var a = avgOf(c.id); return a ? a.avg : -1; }
  function barBits() { setTimeout(bulkBar, 0); var n = cands().filter(notCalled).length; return (window.w209 ? w209.barBits() : '') + (window.w211 && w211.barBits ? w211.barBits() : '') + (P.selMode ? '' : btn('Select', 'data-w199a="selon"', 'sm')) + (window.w208 ? w208.barBits() : '') + '<label class="w199sortg"><input type="checkbox" id="w199notcalled"' + (P.notCalled ? ' checked' : '') + '> Not called yet' + (n ? ' (' + n + ')' : '') + '</label><label class="w199sortg"><input type="checkbox" id="w199sortg"' + (P.sortGrade ? ' checked' : '') + '> Best graded first</label>'; }

  /* ---------- interviews: a day and a time (Steel, 03/10/26: "Those in interview stage should have a day and time") ---------- */
  function ivText(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function ivLocal(t) { if (!t) return ''; var d = new Date(t); if (isNaN(d)) return ''; var p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()) + 'T' + p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes()); }
  function cardBits(c) {
    var gp = c ? selBits(c) + gradePill(c) + callPill(c) + (window.w208 ? w208.cardBits(c) : '') + (window.w211 ? w211.cardBits(c) : '') : '';   /* wave208: the interview's review; wave211: texted, said yes, offer sent */
    if (!c || c.status !== 'interview') return gp;
    if (!c.interview_at) return gp + '<span class="w199ivno">No interview time</span>';
    var past = new Date(c.interview_at).getTime() < Date.now();
    return gp + '<span class="w199iv' + (past ? ' past' : '') + '">' + (past ? 'Interviewed ' : 'Interview ') + E(ivText(c.interview_at)) + '</span>';
  }
  function ivField(c) {
    var need = c.status === 'interview';
    if (!need && !c.interview_at && ['reviewing', 'offer'].indexOf(c.status) < 0) return '';
    return '<label for="w199iv">Interview day and time</label><input type="datetime-local" id="w199iv" data-w199iv="' + c.id + '" value="' + E(ivLocal(c.interview_at)) + '"' + (need && !c.interview_at ? ' class="need"' : '') + '>' +
      (need && !c.interview_at ? '<p class="w197note" id="w199ivnote" style="margin:4px 0 0">At the Interview stage: add when it is booked.</p>' : '');
  }
  function askTime() { setTimeout(function () { var i = document.getElementById('w199iv'); if (i) { try { i.focus(); i.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } toastS('Add the interview\'s day and time'); }, 60); }

  /* ---------- Positions page ---------- */
  function host() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w199pos');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w199pos'; h.className = 'w197card w197blk'; h.setAttribute('data-w197s', 'positions'); var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs'); if (tabs) tabs.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', h); else body.insertBefore(h, body.firstChild); }
    return h;
  }
  function card(p) {
    var cvs = filesOf(p.id, 'resume').length, ap = candsOf(p.id).filter(function (c) { return OPEN_C.indexOf(c.status || 'new') >= 0; }).length;
    var ppl = peopleOf(p.id).filter(function (x) { return !x.past; }).map(function (x) { return x.m.name.split(' ')[0]; });
    return '<div class="w199cw"><button type="button" class="w199card' + (hiring(p) ? ' hire' : '') + '" data-w199open="' + p.id + '"><b>' + E(p.title) + '</b>' +
      '<span class="st st-' + E(p.stage) + '">' + E(stageLabel(p.stage)) + '</span>' +
      '<span class="m">' + (p.jd_id ? '<span>JD</span>' : '<span class="no">No JD</span>') + (p.older_count ? '<span>' + p.older_count + ' older</span>' : '') +
      '<span>' + cvs + (cvs === 1 ? ' CV' : ' CVs') + '</span>' + (ap ? '<span class="ap">' + ap + (ap === 1 ? ' application' : ' applications') + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
      (ppl.length ? '<span class="ppl">' + E(ppl.join(', ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</button>' +
      /* wave216c: the job description one click from the card (Drive), outside the card's button */
      (p.jd_url ? '<a class="w199jd" href="' + E(p.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" aria-label="' + E('The job description for ' + p.title) + '">Job description</a>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function pageHtml() {
    var qq = lower(P.q);
    var list = P.pos.filter(function (p) { return (P.all || hiring(p)) && (!P.dept || p.dept === P.dept) && (!qq || lower(p.title).indexOf(qq) >= 0); });
    var nHire = P.pos.filter(hiring).length;
    var unsorted = P.files.filter(function (f) { return !f.position_id; });
    var bar = '<div class="w197bar"><input type="search" id="w199q" placeholder="Search a position" value="' + E(P.q) + '">' +
      '<select id="w199dept" aria-label="Department"><option value="">Every department</option>' + DEPTS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d[0] + '"' + (P.dept === d[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(d[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="w199seg"><button type="button" data-w199a="hiring" class="' + (P.all ? '' : 'on') + '">Hiring now <small>' + nHire + '</small></button><button type="button" data-w199a="all" class="' + (P.all ? 'on' : '') + '">All positions <small>' + P.pos.length + '</small></button></span>' +
      btn('New position', 'data-w199a="new"', 'sm pri') + btn(P.busy ? 'Reading Drive\u2026' : 'Sync with Drive', 'data-w199a="sync"' + (P.busy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'sm') + '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + ROOT_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in Drive</a></div>';
    var warn = P.privacy ? '<div class="w199warn"><b>CVs are personal information.</b> The Job descriptions folder is shared with anyone who has the link, so the Hub will not upload CVs into it. Turn off link sharing on *Recruitment* in Drive (Share, General access: Restricted).</div>' : '';
    var info = '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 12px">' + (P.sync && P.sync.at ? 'Read from Drive ' + E(ago(P.sync.at)) + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + P.pos.length + ' positions' +
      (unsorted.length ? ' \u00b7 <a href="#" data-w199a="unsorted">' + unsorted.length + (unsorted.length === 1 ? ' CV' : ' CVs') + ' in Unsorted Resumes</a>' : '') + '. Each position is a folder in Drive: a Resumes folder, the latest job description, and Older JDs. A CV dropped in a Resumes folder shows up in Applications as New within about 10 minutes.</p>';
    var groups = DEPTS.map(function (d) {
      var ps = list.filter(function (p) { return p.dept === d[0]; }); if (!ps.length) return '';
      ps.sort(function (a, b) { return (hiring(b) ? 1 : 0) - (hiring(a) ? 1 : 0) || a.title.localeCompare(b.title); });
      return '<h3 class="w199dh">' + E(d[1]) + ' <small>' + ps.length + '</small></h3><div class="w199grid">' + ps.map(card).join('') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var empty = !P.pos.length ? '<p class="w197note">No positions yet. Press Sync with Drive to read the Job descriptions folder.</p>' :
      (!list.length ? '<p class="w197note">' + (P.all ? 'Nothing matches.' : 'Nothing is being hired for right now. All positions shows every role.') + '</p>' : '');
    var un = P.showUnsorted && unsorted.length ? '<h3 class="w199dh">Unsorted Resumes <small>' + unsorted.length + '</small></h3><ul class="w199files">' + unsorted.map(fileRow).join('') + '</ul>' : '';
    return '<h2>Positions</h2>' + bar + warn + info + groups + empty + un;
  }
  function paint() { var h = host(); if (!h) return; var a = document.activeElement, qf = a && a.id === 'w199q'; h.innerHTML = pageHtml(); if (qf) { var x = document.getElementById('w199q'); if (x) { x.focus(); try { x.setSelectionRange(x.value.length, x.value.length); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } } try { if (window.w197) { w197.apply(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function show() {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    try { if (window.w197) w197.apply(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (P.at) paint(); else h.innerHTML = '<h2>Positions</h2><p class="w197note">Loading the positions\u2026</p>';
    load().then(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); });
    privacy().then(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); });
  }
  function onPage() { return /^#hrcentre\/positions/.test(String(location.hash || '')); }
  function fileRow(f) {
    var c = f.candidate_id ? cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(f.candidate_id); })[0] : null;
    var m = f.member_id ? members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(f.member_id); })[0] : null;
    var moveSel = '<select data-w199mv="' + E(f.file_id) + '" aria-label="Move to"><option value="">Move to\u2026</option>' + P.pos.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) !== String(f.position_id); }).map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(p.title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + (f.position_id ? '<option value="__unsorted">Unsorted Resumes</option>' : '') + '</select>';
    return '<li><a href="' + E(f.url || '#') + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(f.name) + '</a><small>' + E(day(f.created_time)) + '</small>' +
      (c ? btn(E(c.name) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(c.status || 'new'), 'data-w197c="' + c.id + '"', 'sm') : (m ? '<span class="w197pill">' + E(m.name) + ', on the team</span>' : (f.position_id ? btn('Add to Applications', 'data-w199add="' + E(f.file_id) + '"', 'sm') : ''))) + moveSel + '</li>';
  }

  /* ---------- the position sheet ---------- */
  function sheetEl() { var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w197sheet'; s.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); s.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); document.body.appendChild(s); } s.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Position'); return s; }
  function openPos(id) {
    var p = byId(id); if (!p) return; P.open = p.id;
    var ms = members().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
    var olds = filesOf(p.id, 'old_jd'), cvs = filesOf(p.id, 'resume'), apps = candsOf(p.id), ppl = peopleOf(p.id);
    var s = sheetEl();
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w199a="shut"></div><div class="pn w199pn">' + btn('Close', 'data-w199a="shut"', 'sm x') +
      '<h3>' + E(p.title) + '</h3><p class="role">' + E(deptLabel(p.dept)) + (p.clickup_url ? ' \u00b7 <a href="' + E(p.clickup_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">was in ClickUp</a>' : '') + '</p>' +
      '<label>Stage</label><div class="stg">' + PSTAGES.map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" data-w199st="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (x[0] === 'closed' ? 'cl ' : '') + (p.stage === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<label>Job description</label><div class="w199links">' + (p.jd_url ? '<a class="w197b sm pri" href="' + E(p.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the job description</a>' + (p.jd_public_url && onSite(p) ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + E(p.jd_public_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">The PDF applicants see</a>' : '') + '<small>' + E(p.jd_name || '') + ' \u00b7 updated ' + E(day(p.jd_modified)) + (onSite(p) ? (p.jd_public_url ? ' \u00b7 linked on welfareavenue.com/join and the application form' : ' \u00b7 goes on the website within 15 minutes') : '') + '</small>' : '<span class="w197pill mut">No job description yet</span><small>Put the JD file in the position\'s folder in Drive, then Sync.</small>') + '</div>' +
